tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi -600 017

moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit  tamiḻ vitti ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. ati muḷikkac ceytavar paritimāl kaliñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟimaliyām maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkiḷi nili nāṭṭiya maliyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavi eḻutiyavi aṉittum meymmiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭit tam āyvu matukiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuriyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uriyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavi alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpiyāka avaṟṟi yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉi akara niral tokiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kāliyil, tamiḻ moḻiyiyum tamiḻ maṇṇiyum tamiḻ iṉattiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uriyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅ kaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉi vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkarutti yāṉ urikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi tōṟṟuvippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷi amittār.
tēvanēyar paṭippukaḷ aṉittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷit tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷiyum kaṭṭurikaḷiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉivar kikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya niliyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpati nilip paṭuttum vakiyil avarkkup paṭiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumiyarum toṇṭaru māvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!								iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!							    irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


patippuri
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutiyum celaviṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatit tam paṉmoḻip pulamiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ aṉittiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatit tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻar kaḷukkuk kiṭitta viraccuraṅkam. it tokuppukaḷi veḷiyiṭuvatil perumip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlatti vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar

poruḷātāram
nāṭṭiyāḷvataṟkum pōr ceytaṟkum kuṭikaṭku naṉmi ceytaṟkum aracaṉukkup poruḷvēṇṭum. apporuḷ iṭiviṭātu vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, atu varum vaḻikaḷum niliyāṉaviyāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
araciyal varuvāykaḷ: mūvēntarkku mirunta poruḷ varuvāykaḷ, vari, iyaṟkic celvam. tiṟi, putiyal, piṟaṅkaṭi (vāricu) illāc cottu, kiyuṟi, naṉkoṭi eṉa eḻutiṟattaṉa.
iyaṟkic celvam yāṉiyum muttum poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlvaṉa. kiyuṟi kuṉṟakkuṟavar cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukkuk kaṇṇakiyip paṟṟik kūṟiṉapōtu koṭutta malip poruḷkaḷ pōlvaṉa. (cilap. 25 : 37-54). teyvattirumuṉ kiyuṟiyōṭu celvatu pōṉṟu aracattirumuṉṉum celvatu marapu. naṉkoṭi karikālvaḷavaṉukku.
avanti vēntaṉ uvantaṉaṉ koṭutta
 nivantōṅku marapiṉ tōraṇa vāyil  (cilap. 5 : 103-4).
pōlvatu. piṟa veḷippaṭi.
cēraṉukku yāṉiyum poṉṉum maṇiyum, cōḻaṉukkup poṉṉum vayiramum, pāṇṭiyaṉukku yāṉiyum, muttum, ciṟanta iyaṟkic celvaṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa.
veṭcik kāṉattu vēṭṭuva rāṭṭak
 kaṭci kāṇāk kaṭamā nallēṟu
 kaṭaṟumaṇi kiḷarac citaṟupoṉ miḷirak
 kaṭiya kataḻu neṭuvarip paṭappi
 ....................................................................
 kōṭipala vaṭukkiya poruṇumak kutaviya
 nīṭunili yariyattu...................................................  (puṟam. 202)
eṉṟu kapilar pāṭiyiruppatāl, koṅkunāṭu poṉṉukkum maṇikkum ciṟantiruntami aṟiyappaṭum. koṅkunāṭṭiṟ piṟantu neṭuntolivu ataṉvaḻiyē cellum kāviriyāṟu poṉṉi yeṉappaṭṭatum, tamiḻ nāṭṭup poṉ koṅkup poṉ eṉak cuṭṭappeṟṟatum, kavaṉikkattakkaṉa. koṅkunāṭu muṟkālattiṟ cēraṉukkum piṟkālattiṟ cōḻaṉukkum uriyatāyiruntatu.
cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṭavellip puṟattiluḷḷa kiruṭṭiṇi yāṟṟaṅkari yilum, ataṉ kiḷiyāṉa tuṅkapattirik kariyi lum, vayirac curaṅkaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ulaka oḷimaṇiyāṉa kōki ṉūr vayiram kiruṭṭiṇi yāṟṟaṅ kariyil eṭukkap peṟṟatē.
pāṇṭināṭṭil vacciranāṭu eṉap peyarpeṟṟa oru kaṭaṟkari nāṭu iruntatākat kaḷaviyaṟkāriki eṉṉum nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.
iṟivaki : araciyal varuvāykaḷuḷ niliyāṉatum mutaṉmi yāṉatum kuṭikaḷ celuttum variyē. atu, nilavari, nīrvari, maṉi vari, toḻilvari, āḷvari (Poll tax) vilaṅkuvari, poruḷvari, uyppuvari, maṇavari, taṇṭavari, kōyilvari, koṭivari, ūḻiyappēṟṟuvari, paṭivari, kāṭcivari, karuvūlavari, kācaṭip puvari eṉap palatiṟattatu.
nīrvari, viḷinilaṅkaṭku nīrnilikaḷiṉiṉṟu nīrip pāyccik koḷ vataṟkuc celuttumvari, atu ērikuḷap pāyccalukkuriya tāyiṉ nilinīrp pāṭṭam eṉṟum, āṟṟup pāyccaṟkuriyatāyiṉ oḻukunīrppāṭṭam eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. iṉi, naṉṉīr puṉṉīr eṉavum vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. toḻilvari, uḻavoḻinta piṟa toḻilkaṭku vāṅkappaṭuvatu. vilaṅkuvari, āṭu peṟṟam (pacu) erutu erumi mutaliya vilaṅkukaṭku vāṅkappaṭuvatu. uyppu vari, nilattilum nīrilum poruḷkaḷi uyttaṟkuc celuttuvatu. atu āyam ulku cuṅkam eṉṉum peyarkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum. maṇavari tirumaṇattiṟku vitikkappaṭuvatu, atu arikkāl paṇamākum. taṇṭavari, kuṟṟavāḷikaṭkut taṇṭamāka vitikkap paṭuvatu. koṭivari, aracaṉ antaṇarkkuc ceyyum peruṅ koṭikaṭkut taṇṭappaṭuvatu. ūḻiyappēṟṟuvari, ūravi yār pāṭi kāvalaṉ mutaliyavariṉ campaḷattiṟkākat taṇṭappaṭu vatu. pāṭi kāvalaṉ campaḷatti avaṉē taṇṭik koḷḷa uttaraviruntatu. avaṉ campaḷam, naṉcey viḷivil māviṟku oru kalamum. puṉcey viḷivil māviṟku oru paṇamum, kamukiṟku maram oṉṟukku vīcam paṇamum, karumpu marukkoḻuntu iñci eḷ vāḻi ākiya veḷḷāṇmi yil māviṟku aintu paṇamum, vīṭṭiṟku āṇṭukku iraṇṭu paṇamum, ākum.
variyāṉatu avvattoḻiṟkēṟpa āyam, iṟi, kaṭaṉ, kaṭami, kaṟi, kāṇikki, tīrvi, pakuti, pāṭṭam, pūṭci, pēṟu, makami, makaṉmi eṉap palvēṟu peyar peṟṟatu. avaṟṟukkellām vari eṉpatu potuppeyarākum. ūrttalivaṉ allatu ūraviyār vitikkum vari ūriṭuvarippāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu.
vāṅkappaṭṭa varikaḷ : akkālattuk kuṭikaḷiṭattiṟ palavaki yilum vāṅkappaṭṭa varikaḷāvaṉa : aṅkāṭip pāṭṭam (aṅ kāṭikkūli), accuvari, aṭṭuk kiṟi, aṭikācu, aṭimikkācu, aṇiyiṭuvāṉ vari, atikaraṇattaṇṭam, atikārap pēṟu (atikārappoṉ), arikoḻi, ari naṭṭi, arivāṭpatakku, aruntōṭu, arikkālvāci, aḻakerutu, aḻakerutuk kāṭcikkācu (kāṭciyerutu kācu) aḻukaṟcarakku, aḷḷukkācu, aḷiyiṭuvāṉvari, aṉuppu, ācukavikaḷ kācu (ācīvakkācu), ācavakkaṭami āṭṭiccammātam (āṭṭikkāṇikki), āṇṭeḻuttuttēvi, āttiṟip pāṭṭam, ātirippiḷḷi yār nōṉpu, āḷnel, āṟṟukkuli, āṟṟuppāṭṭam, iṭippāṭṭam (iṭippūṭci, iṭivari), irācākaraṅ kāṇikki, irupatakkaṭṭi, ilāñciṉippēṟu, iṟikāval, iṟiccōṟu, iṉavari (iṉakkācu), īḻappuṉ cey (īḻampūṭci), īḻaṟkaṭivari, ukappārpoṉ (ukavippoṉ), uppāyam (uppukkācu, uppukōc ceyki), upayam (upayamārkkam), uralvari, ulliyakkūli, ulāvu kāṭci, uvaccavari, uḻutāṉkuṭi, uḷvari, uṟupāti, uṟuvukōl, nilaṉkācu, ūcivāci, ūttip pāṭṭam, ūrkkaṇakkar cīvitam (ūrkkalaṉi, ūrk kaḻañcu), ūrccariki, ūrāṭci, ūriṭuvarippāṭu, ūreṭṭu, eṭkaṭami (ekkaṭami), eṭuttukkoṭṭi, eṭivari, erumip poṉ, eḻuvi, ēṇikkāṇam, ērk kāṭi (ērppoṉ, ērk kāṇikki). ēriyāyam, ēlvi, oṭṭitaṟ kaṭami, ōṭṭaccu, ōṭakkūli, kaṭiyaṭikkāy, kaṭiyiṟi, kaṇkāṇi, kaṇkāṇi kaṇakkar mutal, kaṇkūli, kaṇṇālakkāṇam (kaliyāṇak kāṇam), kaṇṇiṭṭukkāṇam (kaṇṇēṭṭuk kāṇam), kaṇakkappēṟu, (kaṇakkilakki), katirkkāṇam, karuvūlavari, kāṭṭāḷ kācu, kāṇavaṭṭam, kāṇiveṭṭi, kāriyavārāycci, kālattēvi, kāval parappu (kāvaṟ pēṟu), kīḻvārap pacci, kucakkāṇam, kuṭanāḻi, kuṭimi (kuṭikkācu, kuṭikkāṇam, kuṭiyiṟi), kutiri vari, kutiri vilāṭam, kumārakkaccāṇam, iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa varikaḷellām, ellāk kālattum ellāviṭattum ellāriṭattum vāṅkappaṭṭaviyalla. vēṟu vari kuṟippaṉapōl tōṉṟum pala peyarkaḷ orē vari kuṟippaṉavākum. ikkālattut toḻil variyeṉappaṭum oṉṟē, akkālattut toḻil toṟum karuvitoṟum vevvēṟu peyarpeṟṟu nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa varipōl tōṉṟiṟṟu. āyiṉum, caṅka kālattu varikaḷiṉum piṟkālattu varikaḷ mikap pala veṉṟum, ikkālattilillāta āḷvari maṇavari tulāpāra vari mutaliya cila vitappu varikaḷ akkālattiruntaṉa veṉṟum, aṟitalvēṇṭum. āḷvari cōḻa nāṭṭil muṉṉiyūr tavirap piṟavūrkaḷil kūṉar kuṟaḷariṭattum vāṅkappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
varip pākupāṭu : varikaḷellām, kīḻiṟippāṭṭam (ciṟuvari) mēliṟip pāṭṭam (peruvari) eṉa irupālāyp pakukkappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. C®¡fHŠR Fku¡ f¢rhz« t©zhu¥ ghiw j£lhu¥ gh£l« KjÈad ÑÊiw¥ gh£lK«, ntiy¡ fhR â§fŸ nkuhK K¤jhtz« j¿¥òlit KjÈad nkÈiw¥ gh£lK« MF«.*
cila varikaḷ kācākavum, cila varikaḷ kūla (tāṉiya)mākavum, cila varikaḷ avviraṇṭiloṉṟākavum, celuttappaṭṭaṉa. kācākac celuttappaṭṭavi kācāyam (kācu kaṭami) eṉṟum antarāyap pāṭṭam eṉṟum, kūlamākac celuttappaṭṭavi mēlaṭiyeṉ ṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa. kācu paṇam poṉ kāṇam eṉa iṟum peyarkaḷellām muṉvakiyiyum, arici nel nāḻi kuṟuṇi patakku eṉa iṟum peyarkaḷellām piṉvakiyiyum kuṟikkum. perumpālum toḻilvarikaḷ paṇamākavum, nilavari iṉamāka vum, celuttappaṭṭaṉa. cekkiṟi taṟiyiṟi kaṭivari mutaliyaṉa āṇṭukku āṟu paṇamākum.
canti aṅkāṭi teru tiruviḻā mutaliyavaṟṟil palavakip poruḷkaḷ viṟkum cillaṟik kaṭikaṭku, nāḷvari vāṅkap paṭṭatu.
nāṭu muḻumikkum potuvāṉa celaviṟkuriya varikaḷ kuṭika ḷellāriṭattum, oru kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattiṟku maṭṭum payaṉ paṭum allatu payaṉpaṭuvatākak karutappaṭum celaviṟkuriya varikaḷ akkuṟippiṭṭa iṭattilum, taṇṭappaṭṭaṉa.
aracaṉāl vitikkappaṭṭavum, ūraviyārāl vitikkap paṭṭavum, aṟamaṉṟattārāl vitikkappaṭṭavum, kōyilatikāri kaḷāl vitikkappaṭṭavum, ciṟṟūrt talivaṉāl vitikkappaṭṭa vum, āka aivēṟu  tokutippaṭṭiruntaṉa akkālattu varikaḷ. aracaṉukkuc celutta vēṇṭiyavi araciṟiyeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
mēṉāṭṭup poruḷiyal nūṟpaṭi, nērvari (Direct tax) nēralvari (Indirect tax) ākiya iruvaki varikaḷum akkālattu vāṅkap paṭṭaṉa. vari koṭuppavaṉiyē tākkum vari nērvari eṉṟum, vari koṭuppavaṉit tākkātu vēṟoruvaṉit tākkum vari nēral vari eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭum. āḷvari maṇavari mutaliyaṉa nēr variyum, viṟpaṉi vari ōṭak kūli mutaliyaṉa nēral variyum, ākum.
nilavari : varikaḷuṭ ciṟantatu nilavari. ataṉāl atu ciṟappāka iṟi allatu araciṟi eṉappaṭum. ataṟkuc ceykkaṭaṉ kāṇikkaṭaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa.
varivitittaṟku, viḷinilam viḷiyānilam eṉa irupālāka ūrnilaṅkaḷ pakuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, viḷiyā nilam, ūrppotunilamum paṭṭappāḻ eṉṉum taricumāka irupakutippaṭṭatu. ivviru pakutiyum iṟiyili nilamāyirun taṉa. ūrppotu nilaṅkaḷ; palvēṟu cērikaḷ uḷḷiṭṭa ūrk kuṭiyiruppu, ūrkkaḷam, aḻintupōṉa vūrākiya nattappāḻ, kaṭitteru, kōyil, kuḷaṅkuṭṭi, maṭam, cattiram, nantavaṉam, manti, kaṉṟumēypāḻ, peruvaḻi, ōṭi, ūrnilattūṭaṟuttup pōṉa vāykkāl, pāṟi, āṟṟuppaṭuki, uṭippu, iṭukāṭu, cuṭukāṭu, iṭukāṭṭiṟkum cuṭukāṭṭiṟkum cellumvaḻi, mutaliyaṉavākum.
viḷinilam, nīrnilam, kolli, kāṭu eṉa mūvakiyāka vakukkap paṭṭiruntatu. ivi, muṟiyē, naṉceyyum iṟivip puṉceyum vāṉāvārik kāṭumākum. naṉceykaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ viḷi viṟkut takkapaṭi palataramākap pākupāṭu ceyyap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
nilavari, tīrvi eṉṟum vāram eṉṟum iru vakiyāyiruntatu. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu oru kuṟippiṭṭa aḷavum, piṉṉatu kaṇṭu mutalil oru pakutiyumākum. tīrvikkuriya nilam tīrvippaṟṟu eṉṟum, vārattiṟkuriya nilam vārappaṟṟu eṉṟum, vārattip paṇamākac celuttum nilam kaṭamippaṟṟu eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. perumpālum, aḷantu kaṇṭumutal kāṇakkūṭiya kūla veḷḷāṇmikku vāramum, aṅṅaṉañ ceyya iyalāta piṟavaṟṟiṟkut tīrviyum, vitikkappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. vāram potuvāy āṟiloru paṅkāyiruntatu. itu toṉṟutoṭṭu vantamuṟiyeṉpatu,
teṉpulattār teyvam viruntokkal tāṉeṉṟāṅ
kimpulattā ṟōmpal tali
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum. vāram āṟiloru pakutiyā yirunta tiṉālēyē atu pakutiyeṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
vari vāṅkuvataṟkup potuvāka nalviḷivē kavaṉikkappaṭṭatu. ari viḷiccalum, naṭṭuppāḻ aḻukic cētam cāvi pūcci veṭṭu nōy aḻivu ākiya aṟuvaki naṭṭikaḷum, varivitikkap peṟṟila. aḻivāvatu, naṉṟāy viḷintum kaḷvarālum pakiva rālum vilaṅkālum aḻiyuṇṭanili.
nalviḷivuk kālattil, vari kaṇṭippāy vāṅkappaṭṭatu. īrāṇṭāka vari celuttātār nilamuḻutum paṟimutal ceyyap paṭṭatu. tañcimāvaṭṭattic cērnta ōrūril, pañcakkoṭu miyāl kuṭipōṉa makkaḷiṉ nilattiṟkāka, ūraviyār ūrppotunilattit kōyiṟku viṟṟu vanta paṇattik koṇṭu, vari celuttiṉar eṉṉuṅ kalveṭṭuc ceytiyi ṉiṉṟu, cila aracar kālattil pañcattiṉāṟ payiriṭappaṭā nilattiṟkum vari vāṅkappaṭṭami aṟiyalām. picirāntiyār pāṭiya kāynel laṟuttu eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭāl (184). nallaracar kālattilum kaṭuntaṇṭalāḷar iruntu, kuṭikaḷi varuttiyami aṟiyappaṭum.
varippaḷuvik kuṭikaḷ tāṅkamuṭiyātapaṭi kaṭuvari vitikkappaṭuṅ kālattil, kuṭikaḷ muṟiyiṭuvatum aracaṉ varivītatti māṟṟi yamippatum uṇṭu. varippaḷuviṉālō pañcattiṉālō ōr ūr muḻutum kuṭipōyiṉ, avvūrukkuriya variyi avvūrik koṇṭa nāṭu ēṟṟuk koḷḷum. avvūr, aṇmiyiluḷḷa teyvattiṟku nāṭu ceḻikkak kāṇikki celuttumāṟu, ōriru celvariṭattil oppaṭikkappaṭum. ittakiya nikaḻcci, māṟavarmaṉ kulacēkaraṉ āṭciyil, tiruccimāvaṭṭattic cērnta marutūril nikaḻntatu.
aracaṉ valiyaṟṟa kālattil, aracaṉukkaṭaṅkāta nāṭṭatikāri kaḷ, tam kaṇkāṇippiṟkuṭpaṭṭa nāṭṭu variyit tām payaṉpaṭuttikkoḷvatuṭaṉ variyiṟanta kaṭuvari vitippatu muṇṭu. aṉṟu kuṭikaḷ uḷḷaturiyatellām viṟṟukkoṭukkanērum.
varitaṇṭum iṭattiṟku nilikkaḷam eṉṟupeyar. talinakaral lāta vūrkaḷilum nakarkaḷilum, taṇṭalāḷarāl taṇṭappaṭṭa kācuṅkūlamumākiya iruvakipporuḷkaḷum, mutalāvatu, nāṭṭuppaṇṭāram eṉṉum ūrp paṇṭārattiṟ cērkkappaṭum. nāṭṭilāyiṉum, tali nakarilāyiṉum, paṇṭāratti mēṟpārppavaṉ paṇṭārak kaṇkāṇi eṉappaṭuvaṉ. poruḷkaḷi aḷappataṟkum niṟuppataṟkum, paṇṭāra nāḻi eṉṟum paṇṭārakkal eṉṟum aracamuttiriyiṭṭa paṭiyum niṟikallum aṅkirukkum. kaḷañciyam eṉṟum karuvūlam eṉṟum paṇṭāram irupakutiyatākum. muṉṉatil kūlamum piṉṉatil kācum cērkkappaṭum. kaḷañciyattiṟkuk koṭṭāram (koṭṭa kāram) eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. āṇṭutōṟum, ūrppaṇṭārat til cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷavaṟṟil celavupōka eñciyavellām, talinakariluḷḷa mūlapaṇṭārattiṟkaṉuppap peṟum.
nāṭṭuppuṟattūrkaḷilum talinakarilum taṇṭappaṭum varikaṭ kellām, talinakariluḷḷa variyatikārikaḷāl vacatiyākak kaṇakku vikkappaṭṭiruntatu.  nilaṅkaḷiṉ taraṅkaḷ  kuṟikkap paṭṭirukkum puttakam taravucāttu eṉṟum, nilavarik kaṇakkik kuṟikkum puttakam varip pottakam eṉṟum, piṟavarikkaṇakkik kuṟikkum puttakam puravuvari eṉṟum, ellā varikaḷiyum kuṟikkum puttakam aṭaṅkal eṉṟum, celuttap paṭṭa varittokiyik kuṟikkum puttakam variyilīṭu eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. varip pottakam variyilīṭu eṉṉum puttakap peyarkaḷ. avaṟṟiṟkup poṟuppāṉa atikārikaḷiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
araciyal varumāṉamum kuṭikaḷ varumāṉamum cērnta nāṭṭu varumāṉatti (National Income)¥ perukkutaṟkuriya poruḷā tāram, akkālattil iyaṟṟal (Organisation), īṭṭal (Collection), kāttal  (Protection),  vakuttal  (Distribution)  eṉṉum nāṉku talippiṟ kavaṉikkap peṟṟatu.
iyaṟṟalāvatu, mēṉmēlum poruḷkaḷ varumvaḻikaḷi amittal  (Organization of resources). poruḷkaḷāvaṉa nel, maruntu, parutti, poṉ, maṇi, kōḻi, āṭu, māṭu, kutiri, yāṉi mutaliyaṉa. avivarum vaḻikaḷāvaṉa. pakivarik koḷḷi yaṭittalum piṟanāṭukaḷik kippaṟṟit tiṟikōṭalum, taṉ nāṭṭiyum kuṭikaḷiyum kāttalum mutalāyiṉa.
īṭṭalāvatu, amikkappaṭṭa varuvāykaḷiṉiṉṟu varum poruḷkaḷi mūlapaṇṭārattiṟ cērttal.
kāttalāvatu, aṅṅaṉañ cērkkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷi, paki var kaḷvar cuṟṟattār araciyalviṉiñar ākiyavar kavarāmaṟ kāttal.
vakuttalāvatu, aṅṅaṉaṅ kākkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷi aṟam poruḷiṉ paṅkaḷiṉ poruṭṭuc celaviṭal. kaṭavuḷ, antaṇar, aṭiyār, vaṟiyār, uṟuppaṟiyar. vaḻippōkkar mutaliyōrkkuc celaviṭuvatu aṟapporuṭṭum; palvakip pulavarkkum, kaliñarkkum, araciyal viṉiñarkkum, paṭikkum, araṇukkum, aracavuṟaviṟkum, pōrukkum celaviṭuvatu poruṭporuṭṭum; tēviyarkkum, ciṟanta vūṇuṭikkum, aṇikalattiṟkum, ici kūttiṟkum, māḷiki ceykuṉṟu ilavantikic cōli mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuñ celaviṭuvatu iṉpap poruṭṭum; ākum.
karikālvaḷavaṉ, cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, mutalām irācarācaṉ, mutalām irācēntiraṉ, mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ mutaliya pēraracar piṟanāṭukaḷi veṉṟu koṇṭa koṇṭiyun tiṟiyuṅ koñcanañcamalla.
araṇmaṉic celavum araciyaṟ celavum pōrc celavum pōka, eñciya poṟkācukaḷum maṇikaḷum karuvūlattiṟ pōṟṟappeṟum, maturiyil kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ karuvūlam, 1200 kōṭit tiṉāram eṉṉum poṟkācukaḷiyum, collāṟ kuṟikkoṇāp paṉmaṇik kuviyalkaḷiyuṅ koṇṭiruntatu.  (pa.ta.ā.)
poḻil
māntaṉ tōṉṟu muṉṉum makkaḷ peruku muṉṉum ñālattiluḷḷa nilamellām maram ceṭi koṭipaṭarntu orē cōliyāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. perunilap pakutikaḷum ciṟunilap pakutikaḷum taṉittaṉi peruñcōliyum ciṟu cōliyumāka iruntaṉa. itaṉāl maṇṇulakamum ataṉ kaṇṭaṅkaḷum nāṭukaḷum poḻil eṉṉum potup peyar peṟṟaṉa. poḻil eṉpatu cōli. poḻital tiraḷutal;  poḻittal tiraṭṭutal. maraṅkaḷiṉ poḻippu allatu tokuppu poḻil. oru pāṭṭiṉ coṟporuḷit tokuttuk kūṟum uriyip poḻippuri eṉṟal kāṇka.
(tōppu, tōṭṭam eṉṉum otta poruṭ coṟkaḷum ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyaviyē. tokuppu - tōppu, tōṭu- tōṭṭam, tōṭu - tiraṭci.)  (col 17)
poḻutu
poḻutu eṉpatu, perum poḻutu ciṟupoḻutu eṉa aṭiyaṭuttukiṉṟu muṟiyē, irumāta aḷavāṉa paruva kālattiyum pattu nāḻikiyaḷavāṉa nāṭpakutiyiyum kuṟikkum.
iḷavēṉil mutuvēṉil kār kuḷir muṉpaṉi piṉpaṉi eṉpaṉa, aṟu perum poḻutukaḷ; kāli naṇpakal eṟpāṭu, māli yāmam vikaṟi eṉpaṉa, aṟuciṟu poḻutukaḷ. (ciṟu poḻutukaḷ āṟum, muṟiyē kāli 6 maṇi mutal nannāṉku maṇiyaḷavukaḷ)
vēḷi eṉpatu, pakalvēḷi irāvēḷi kāli vēḷi māli vēḷi eṉa nāṭpakutiyik kuṟikkum.
nēram eṉpatu, mikak kuṟukiya kālap peyarāy, avaṉ vanta nēram eḻutik koṇṭiruntēṉ, eṉa viṉi nikaḻ ciṟu kālattiyum ivviṉi ceyya orumaṇi nēram cellum, eṉa viṉi nikaḻ kāla aḷaviyuṅ kuṟikkum.
camiyam eṉpatu, nīṇṭatum kuṟukiyatumāṉa kālap peyarāy, oru poruḷ oṉṟaṟkuc caminta allatu pakkuvamāṉa niliyi maṭṭum uṇarttum, e.ṭu: camiyam pārttu vantāṉ. amiyam eṉpatu cantarppam.
cevvi eṉpatu, oruvaṉiṉ maṉam cevviyāṉa allatu icivāṉa nili.
aṟṟam eṉpatu oruvaṉatu viḻippaṟṟa nili.
muṟi eṉpatu, oru variciyil oruvaṉukku oḻuṅkuppaṭi oruviṉi nikaḻum taṭavi (turn) e.ṭu : iru muṟi (two turns) (taṭavi eṉpati vāṭṭi eṉpatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku). 
taram allatu taṭavi eṉpatu, varici yoḻuṅkillāmal nikaḻum viṉi nikaḻccit toki. e.ṭu : iru taṭavi (twice, two times)  (col: 51)



poṟkālam (Gold Age)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000 - 30,000)
tamiḻar varalāṟṟuk kālap pakutikaḷuḷ, paḻaṅ kaṟkālam putuk kaṟkālam eṉṉum iraṇṭum avvak kālattoṭu muṭintu pōṉaviyākum. paḻaṅkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ putukkaṟ kālattilum, putukkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ perumpāṉmiyākap poṟ kālattilum, vaḻaṅkiyirā. āyiṉ, poṟkālam mutaliya piṟkālak karuvikaḷ ulakuḷḷa kālamellām vaḻaṅkum.
poṟkālam cempukkālam uṟikkālam irumpukkālam eṉap piritta tellām, perumpālum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉpāṭṭut toṭakkam paṟṟiyē yaṉṟi muṭivu paṟṟi yaṉṟu. āyiṉum, muṭivu kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppatu, avvakkālattu māḻi perum pāṉmiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kālattiṉ muṭivik kuṟittaṟkē eṉa aṟika,
poṉ aṇikalattiṟkum, cempum uṟiyum (veṇkalamum) kuṭavamum (pittaḷiyum) nīrkkalattiṟkum, irumpu ellāk karuvikaṭkum, eṉṟum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, poṉ mikac ciṟṟaḷavākavum irumpu mikap pēraḷavākavum irukkum.
māḻikaṭkuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa kālaṅkaḷ, mēli yaṟiñar kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟoṭu ovvā. avar tamiḻ nākarikattiṉ toṉmiyiyum muṉmiyiyum aṟiyārātaliṉ, māḻik kālaṅkaḷip piṟpaṭakkūṟuvatil viyappoṉṟu milli. āriyarālum avaraṭiyārālum maṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa vuṇmi veḷippaṭṭapiṉ, mēliyarum eṉ kūṟṟi oppuk koḷvatu tiṇṇam.
poṟkālam eṉa oṉṟu tamiḻakam tavira vēṟen nāṭṭiṟkum iruntatilli. piṟa nāṭukaḷilellām, poṟkālam eṉpatu, aṇiyiyaṟ poruḷil, oḻukkattilum celvattilum kalviyi lum taliciṟanta kālattik (Golden Age) kuṟikkumēyaṉṟi, ceñcoṟporuḷil poṟkaruvikaḷē vaḻaṅkiya kālamoṉ ṟik kuṟikkātu. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuḷḷa palvakit taṉic ciṟappuk kaḷuḷ poṟkālamum oṉṟākum. polivuṟṟatu poṉṉeṉap paṭṭatu. pol - poli - polivu. pol- poṟpu. pollutal = aḻakātal, polital. poṟṟa = aḻakiya, ciṟanta, poṉṉālāṉa. pol - poṉ. pol - polam - polaṉ.
kumari nāṭṭiṟ poṟkālamiruntamikkuc cāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa:
(1) poṉṉiṉ peyar māḻip potup peyarāṉami.
tamiḻarāl mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa māḻi (metal) poṉṉātalāl, ataṉ peyarē piṟkālattiṟ kaṇṭu piṭikkap paṭṭa piṟavaṟṟiṟkum potup peyarāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu :	cempoṉ = cempu. veṇpoṉ = veḷḷi.
		karumpoṉ = irumpu.
tūṇṭiṟpoṉ mīṉviḻuṅki yaṟṟu, eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷaṭiyil (931), poṉ eṉṉuñ col aṭiyiṉṟiyum irumpik kuṟittatu. nāka (īya) maṇal poṉvittu eṉappaṭum.
(2) kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilum poṉ mikutiyāka vaḻaṅkiṉami.
akkālattuk kācellām poṉṉāyiruntamiyāl, kācu poṉ eṉṟum, paṇamuṭippu poṟkiḻi eṉṟum, paṇattaṇ ṭam poṉtaṇṭam eṉṟum, collappaṭṭaṉa. aracar tammip pāṭiya pulavarkku ilakkakkaṇakkiṟ poṟkāci vāri vaḻaṅkiṉar. kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār karikāl vaḷa vaṉip pāṭip patiṉāṟilakkam poṉpeṟṟār. kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉār kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēralippāṭi, nāṟpatilakkam poṉṉum avaṉ āṇṭatiṟ pākamum peṟṟār. kākkipāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷiyār āṭu kōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉippāṭi, aṇikalaṉukkeṉṟu tulām poṉṉum nūṟāyiram poṟkācum peṟṟār. kapilar celvak kaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉippāṭi ōrilakkam poṟkācum oru nāṭum peṟṟār. aricilkiḻār takaṭūreṟinta peruñcēralirum poṟi yippāṭi, toṇṇilakkam (oṉpatu nūṟāyiram) poṟkācum, ariyaṇik kīṭāka amiccurimiyum peṟṟār. peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār kuṭakkō iḷañcēral irumpoṟiyip pāṭi, muppattīrāyiram poṟkācum palvakip paricilum peṟṟār.
mātavipōl taṉ āṭalpāṭali araṅkēṟṟiya nāṭakak kaṇikiya rellārum, āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu poṉ peṟṟaṉar. aracari yaṭuttup pāṭiya pāṇarellārum poṟṟāmariyum, pāṭiṉiyarellārum poṉṉari māliyum, paricākap peṟṟaṉar.
tillic ciṟṟampalam poṟpalakiyāl vēyappaṭṭiruntatu. maturiyampalam veḷḷiyampālam eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntata ṉāl, tilliyampalam aṉṟu poṉṉampalamā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
mūvēntarum koṭimaṭampaṭṭup pirāmaṇap pūcakarkkut tuliniṟip poṉ tāṉañceytu vantaṉar. kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil ittuṇip poṉvaḷam iruntateṉiṉ, talik kaḻakattiṉum muṟpaṭṭu makkaḷ toki mikak kuṟivāyirunta kālattil, ciṟcila karuvikaḷiyēṉuñ ceytu koḷḷap pōtiya poṉṉiruntateṉpatu, nampattakātataṉṟu.
(3) teṉṉāppirikkāvilum āttirēliyāvilum iṉṟum poṉ kiṭikkiṉṟami.
ulakiṉ iṉṟu mikutiyākap poṉ kiṭikkum iṭaṅkaḷ, iraciyā, teṉṉāppirikkā, kāṉaṭā, amerikka oṉṟiya nāṭukaḷ (U.S.A.) eṉṉum nāṉkām.
poṉ kiṭikkum vakikaḷ, tūḷ (dust), maṇal (grin), takaṇi (nugget),  narampu (lode or vein), kalappu (mixture) eṉa aintu.
1850 - il āttirēliyap poṉ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa piṉ, poṟpittar peruntiraḷākap pōy aṅkuccarintaṉar. palaviṭat tiṟ peruntakaṇikaḷ nila maṭṭattiṟku oru nila viralaṅkaṭkuk (inches) kīḻēyē kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. arampa mali maṇal takaṇi (‘Sierra Sanda’ nugget) 1, 117 viraṉiyum, āttam perumāṭṭi (Lady Hotham) 1177 viraṉiyum nalvaravu ayalār takaṇi (Welcome Stranger Nugget), 2000 viraṉikku mēlum, ālatarmaṉ takaṇi (Holtermann Nugget) 200 tulattiṟku  (lb.) mēlum eṭi niṉṟaṉa.
kumarik kaṇṭam āttirēliyāvōṭum teṉṉāppirikkā vōṭum iṇintiruntataṉāl, annaṭuviṭattilum poṟ ṟakaṇikaḷ eḷitāyk kiṭittiruttal vēṇṭum.
muḻukip pōṉa kumarik kaṇṭattic cērnta tamiḻ nilam muḻutum paḻam pāṇṭināṭākum. cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakuti toṇṭi maṇṭalam eṉṟu pirintatu pōṉṟē, cēranāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyum koṅku maṇṭalam eṉap pirintatu. akkoṅkum piṉṉark kuṭakoṅku kuṇakoṅku eṉa irupāṟpaṭṭatu. koṅkunāṭṭilum cōḻa nāṭṭilum ōṭum pērāṟu kāvēri. akkāvēri poṉ kalanta maṇalik koḻittataṉāṟ poṉṉi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
cōḻa nāṭṭil mikutiyākap poṉ kiṭittataṉāl, mutaṟ parāntakaṇ, iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaḷ mutaliya cōḻavēntar palar tillic ciṟṟampalam poṉ vēyntaṉar. iraṇṭām irācarācaṉ, ciṟṟampalam maṭṭumaṉṟip pērampalam māṭa māḷiki kūṭakōpuram mutaliya palavaṟṟiyum, poṉṉā laṇivittut tilliyip poṉvaṇṇamākkiṉāṉ.
ciṟṟam palamun tirupperumpē rampalamum
maṟṟum palapala maṇṭapamum - cuṟṟiya
māḷikiyum pīṭikiyum māṭamuṅ kōpuramum
cūḷikiyu matteruvun tōraṇamum - āḷuṭiyāṉ
kōyil tirukkāmak kōṭṭamu makkōyil
vāyil tiruccuṟṟu māḷikiyum - tūyacem
poṉṉiṟ kuyiṟṟip puṟampiṟ kuṟampaṟuttu
muṉṉiṟ kaṭalkaḷuḷ mūḻkuvitta ceṉṉi  (irācarāca tēvarulā)
araṇmaṉikaḷil aracaṉ taṅkum taṉi māḷikiyum poṉṉāl vēyappaṭṭiruntatu. cuntarac cōḻaṉip poṉmāḷikit tuñciya tēvar eṉṟu kalveṭṭuk kūṟutal kāṇka.
koṅku nāṭṭiṟ kiṭitta poṉ koṅkup poṉ eṉac ciṟap pittuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṉṟum curaṅkattiṟ poṉ ṉeṭukkum kuvaḷālapuram (kōlār) paṇṭik koṅku nāṭṭic cērntatē.
koḻuṅkoṭi yaṟukiyuṅ kuvaḷiyuṅ kalantu
viḷaṅkukatirt toṭutta viriyal cūṭṭip
pāruṭip paṉarpōṟ paḻicciṉar kitoḻa
ēroṭu niṉṟōr ērmaṅ kalamum
eṉṉum cilappatikāra nāṭu kāṇ kāti yaṭikaṭku (132-5) cenneṟ katirōṭē aṟukiyum kuvaḷiyiyum kalantu toṭuttu māliyi mēḻiyilē cūṭṭip pāri iraṇṭākap piḷappārip pōlap pōṟṟuvār toḻap poṉṉēr pūṭṭi niṉṟōr ērip pāṭum ērmaṅkalap pāṭṭumeṉka. eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār uri varintuḷḷār.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akaramutali, itip piṉpaṟṟip poṉṉēr eṉpataṟku, paruva kālattil nalla nāḷil mutaṉ muṟiyāka uḻuṅ kalappi eṉṟu viḷakkaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷatu.
paṇṭi makatanāṭṭūrkaḷil āṇṭu tōṟum mutaluḻavu uḻum pōtu, ūrttalivaṉ poṉṉāṟ ceyta ērip pūṭṭi uḻavar variciyil mutalil niṉṟu, piṟar piṉvara, oru paṭic  cālōṭṭit toṭaṅkivippāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭuvatāl, kumari nāṭṭil ataṟku mūlamāṉa vaḻakkam iruntirukkalā meṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
uruvap paḵṟēriḷañcēṭa ceṉṉi poṟṟēri yuṭi yavaṉāyintāṉ.
polantērmicip polivutōṉṟi
mākkaṭal nivanteḻutaruñ
ceññāyiṟṟuk kaviṉimātō  (puṟam. 4)
eṉṟu paraṇar pāṭutal kāṇka. paṇṭit tamiḻaracariṉ tēr kutiri yāṉikaḷ poṟpaṭikaḷāl aṇiceyyap peṟṟiruntaṉa.
makiḻā tītta iḻiyaṇi neṭuntēr  (puṟam. 123 : 4)
pāṭip peṟṟa poṉṉaṇi yāṉi  ( 177:3)
valampaṭu tāṉi vēntar
polampaṭik kalimā eṇṇu vōrē.  (puṟam. 116)
aracaruṭiya pōr muracum atupōṉṟa piṟa ciṉṉaṅkaḷum poṉṉāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
polaṅkuḻi yuḻiñiyoṭu poliyac cūṭṭik
kuruti vēṭki yurukeḻu muracam  (puṟam.50)
ki.mu.11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum 10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil yūtēyā nāṭṭi yāṇṭa cālōmōṉ aracaṉ  (1015 - 975), oppīr (Ophir) eṉṉum intiyat tuṟimukattiṉiṉṟu varuvitta poruḷkaḷuḷ poṉṉum oṉṟu. attuṟinakar, vēmpāy eṉappaṭum pampāy mānakarkku vaṭakkē 37 kal tolivilirunta, upparā allatu upparakā eṉṟu ārāycci yāḷarāṟ karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. kālṭuvelār atu orukāl pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ kīḻkkariyiluḷḷa uvariyā yirukkalā meṉṟu karutiṉar. etuvāyiṉum, oppīr ōr intiyattuṟinakar eṉpatu uṟuti. cālōmōṉ kālattiṟ cēranāṭu kuccaram (kucarāttu) vari paraviyiruntatu.
toṉṟu toṭṭup poṉ intiyāviṉiṉṟu mēṉāṭukaṭku ērāḷamāka ēṟṟumatiyāki vantataṉāl, cēkkacuppiyar (Shakes-peare) tam paṉṉiraṇṭām iravu (Twelfth Night) eṉṉum nāṭakat til, poṉṉi intiya māḻi (metal of India) eṉṟār (2:5)
(4) kumarināṭṭil poṉ kiṭittami paṟṟiya neṭṭimiyār kūṟṟu.

cennīrp pacumpoṉ vayiriyark kītta
munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē.  (puṟam.9).
itaṉ paḻiyavuri:  - tammuṭiya kōvākiya civanta nīr miyiyuṭiya pōkkaṟṟa paciya poṉṉik kūttarkku vaḻaṅkiya munnīrk kaṭaṟṟeyvattiṟ keṭutta viḻāviṉiyuṭiya neṭiyōṉā luḷa tākkappaṭṭa nalla nīriyuṭiya paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉu māṟṟiṉ maṇaliṉum palakālam
kumari nāṭṭu māntar mutaṟkālattil iyaṟki nīrnilikaḷiyē cārn tiruntataṉālum, āṟillāta viṭamellām aṭarn tiruṇṭa kāṭāyirunta taṉālum, makkaḷ toki mikamikap perumpālum āṟukaḷiyaṭuttē paravi yiruttal vēṇṭum. ātalāl, perumali nāṭāṉa kuṟiñciyiyum kuṟumali nāṭāṉa mulliyiyuṅ kaṭantapiṉ, karaṭum kallum murampum pāṟiyum aruki nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamum mikka maruta nilattiṟkē iyaṟkiyākap paraviyirun tiruppar.
aṅku nellum vāḻiyum pōṉṟa nilittiṇi (tāvara) vakikaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṟkēṟṟa meṉṉilattiyuṅ kaṇṭu, avaṟṟic ceyaṟkiyāka viḷi vikka muṉintu uḻittaṉar. eṉṟum nīr vēṇṭum payirkaḷik karical nilattilum, iṭiyiṭṭu nīr vēṇṭum payirkaḷic cival nilattilum payiriṭṭaṉar. ceṭikoṭi puṟkaḷital, kalleṭuttal, uramiṭutal, paṉmuṟi yuḻutal, kaṭṭiyaṭittal, parampaṭittal (palliyāṭutal), puḻutiyāk kutal, kāyaviṭutal eṉṉum palvakiyil tiruttappaṭṭa nilam cey eṉappaṭṭatu. ceytal tiruttutal, pēraḷavākat tiruttap paṭṭatu naṉcey eṉṟum, ciṟṟaḷavākat tiruttappaṭṭatu. puṉcey eṉṟum, collap paṭṭaṉa. naṉcey uram mikutiyāka vikkappaṭuvatāl vayal eṉṟum, (vi - (vay) - vayal), cēṟu paṭutalāl ceṟu eṉṟum, paḷḷamāyiruntalāl paṇṇi yeṉṟum, pōraṭikkuṅ kaḷañ cērntiruppiṉ kaḻaṉi yeṉṟum, nīṇṭa kālam paṇpaṭuttap paṭṭup paḻamiyāṉapiṉ paḻaṉam eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟatu. nelvayalil uḻumpōtum aṟuvaṭi ceyyum pōtum kiṇippaṟi (uṟumi) koṭṭappaṭṭatu.
nellum vāḻiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟi naṉceyilum, cōḷamum kēḻvarakum pōṉṟavaṟṟip puṉceyilum, varakum cāmiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟi vāṉāvārik kolliyilum, payiriṭṭaṉar. nīrppācaṉa ēntu (vacati) irukkumiṭamellām puṉceyi naṉceyākkiṉar. patiṉeṇ kūlamum eḷḷum iñciyum mañcaḷum paruttiyum piṟavum viḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
nīrppācaṉattiṟku mutalil āṟum poykiyum ākiya iyaṟki nīrnilikaḷiyē cārntiruntaṉar. piṉṉar aviyillāta viṭaṅkaḷil, kaṇṇāṟuṅ kālvāyum ēriyuṅ kuḷamum veṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. avi ellārkkum potuvāyirunta miyālum, kōṭikkālattil vaṟṟip pōṉamiyālum, āṇṭu muḻutum payaṉpaṭumāṟum contavuṭamiyāyirukku māṟum, nīrcurakkumiṭamellām kiṇaṟukaḷiyum veṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. akkālattil makkaḷ toki mikak kuṟivā yiruntamiyālum, nilapparappil mukkāṟ paṅkiṟku mēlum mara maṭarnta aṭaviyā yiruntamiyālum, kōṭi maḻi, kāla maḻi, aṭi maḻi, paṭi maḻi ākiya nālvaki maḻiyum kālantappātu peytu vantaṉa. a(l)kāla vampa maḻiyum orōvoru camiyam peytatu.
kālvāy nīriṟikka iṟipeṭṭi (iṟikūṭi), uḻaṇi (ōṇi) mutaliya karuvikaḷum, kiṇaṟṟu nīriṟikka ēṟṟam, kammāli eṉṉum poṟikaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. am = nīr. am-kam=nīr. ālutal = āṭutal, cuṟṟutal, āl-āli = cuṟṟivarum poṟi. cekkāli, karumpāli mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkuka. iṉṟum, tirukkōvalūrkkum villipurattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa ūrkaḷiṟ cuṟṟukkavaliyāṭutal kāṇka. kammāli - kamali- kavali, ēṟṟam kiyāl iyakkappaṭuvatu. atu kittulā, āḷēṟuntulā eṉa iruvaki, kammāli erutu pūṭṭi iyakkap paṭuvatu.
neṟcōṟu marutanilattil mutaṉmutalākap poṅkappaṭṭatu. col=nel. col-coṉṟi- cōṟu. ciṟṟuṇṭiyākap payaṉpaṭumāṟu, aricippori porikkavum avaliṭikkavum kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. payaṟu vakikaḷi avittum cuṇṭiyum tiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟiṉ paruppāṟ kummāyamuṅ kūṭṭum cāṟuṅ kuḻampum ākkic cōṟṟuṭaṉ kalantuṇṭaṉar. vāḻi, vaḻutuṇi (kattari), veṇṭi, curi, pīrkku, pūcaṇi mutaliya kāykaḷit taṉittum paruppuṭaṉ cērttum, kaṟivakikaḷākac camittaṉar.
ūṉukku, vayalilum vāykkālilum piṭitta mīṉiyum āmiyiyum, vīṭṭil vaḷartta āṭu kōḻikaḷiyum, payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. iṭiyiṭi mullikkuṅ kuṟiñcikkuñ ceṉṟu, tamakku vēṇṭiya vilaṅku paṟavikaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭiyum vantaṉar.
kārattiṟku miḷakiyum, iḷaṅkārattiṟkum cuvikkum veṅkāyattiyum, niṟattiṟkum maṇattiṟkum caḷimuṟippiṟkum mañcaḷiyum, cappeṉṟiruttali nīkkic cuviyūṭṭataṟku umaṇ eṉṉum uvarnilattu uppiyum camiyaliṟ cērttuk koṇṭaṉar.
kuṟiñciyilum mulliyilum perumpālum pālum cūṭṭi ṟicciyumē uṇṭataṉāl, avvuṇaviṟku uvarppup poruḷ tēviyillātiruntatu. mulli nilattil, tiṉi varaku cāmi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ariciyip pāliluñ camittirukkalām. maruta nilattiṟku vantapiṉpē ciṟanta muṟiyiṟ camiyal toṭaṅkiya tāl, poṅkal puḻuṅkal aviyal cuṇṭal tuvaṭṭal puraṭṭal kāyccal poriyal mutaliya viṉikaḷi nīriṟ ceytaṟku, uvarppuc carakkuc cērkka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu, uppu viḷikkum uvarnīrk kaṭalic cārnta neytal nilatti aṭukkumuṉ, umaṇiyē payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum. umaṇ eṉpatu uvarmaṇ, ati mutalil viṟṟavar, piṉpu neytal nilattu uppaḷattil uppu viḷittapōtum, umaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
kaḷarnilat tuppiṟanta vuppiṉic cāṉṟōr
viḷinilattu nelliṉ viḻumitāk koḷvar  (nālaṭi.133)
poriyal mutalil āviṉ neyyilēyē ceyyappaṭṭatu. ataṉālēyē, piṉṉar eḷḷilirunteṭukkappaṭṭa neyp poruḷ eṇṇey (eḷney) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. eḷḷiṉ neyyē mutaṟkālattiṟ peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkiṉatiṉāl, piṟkālattil, āviṉ ney tavira maṟṟellā neyvakikaṭkum eṇṇey eṉpatu potup peyarāyiṟṟu.
camiyaṟku vēṇṭiya neruppu, nāḷtoṟum ñelikōlāl virintu kaṭiyappaṭṭatu. atu vīṭutoṟum iṟavāṇattiṟ cerukappaṭṭiruntatu. kuṟiñcivāṇarum mullivāṇarum vēṭṭikkārarum, ati eṉṟuṅ kiyil vittiruntaṉar.
illiṟic cerīiya ñelikōl pōla  (puṟam. 315 : 4)
kāṉavar pottiya ñelitī viḷakkattu  (puṟam. 247 : 2)
pulleṉ mālic ciṟutī ñeliyuṅ
kallā viṭiyaṉ pōla  (puṟam. 331 : 4)
celvat tōṉṟalōr valvil vēṭṭuvaṉ
toḻutaṉa ṉeḻuvēṟ kikavit tirīi
iḻuti ṉaṉṉa vāṉiṇak koḻuṅkuṟi
kāṉatar mayaṅkiya iḷiyar vallē
tāmvan teytā vaḷavi yoyyeṉat
tāṉñeli tīyiṉ virivaṉaṉ cuṭṭuniṉ
irumpē rokkaloṭu tiṉmeṉat tarutaliṉ  (puṟam. 150 : 7-13)
ivi piṟkālattuc ceyyuḷāyiṉum, muṟkālattu nilimiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
tīppaṭṭa viṭattiṟ kaḷimaṇ iṟukik kaṟpōṟ keṭṭi yāṉatik kaṇṭu, kaḷimaṇṇāṟ pāṉi caṭṭikaḷi vaṉintu cuṭṭuc camiyaṟ kalaṅkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
paruttip pañcāl nūliḻittup palvaki āṭikaḷi neytu koṇṭaṉar. nūl nūṟṟal peṇṭirāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu eṉpati, nuṇṇiya palavāya pañcunuṉikaḷāṟ kivaṉ makaṭūut taṉatu ceyki māṇpiṉāl ōriḻip paṭuttalām, ulakattu nūṉūṟṟaleṉpatu eṉṉum iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷurik kūṟṟālum (nūṟpā. 1),
pañcitaṉ collāp paṉuva liḻiyākac
ceñcoṟ pulavaṉē cēyiḻiyā - eñcāta
kiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
miyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu.
eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ potuppāyira nūṟpāvālum (23), aṟiyalām. vīṭṭiliruntu ceyyum toḻilukku ēṟṟavar peṇṭirē. parutti nūl nūṟṟa peṇṭir paruttip peṇṭir eṉṉap paṭṭaṉar.
paruttip peṇṭiṉ paṉuvalaṉṉa  (puṟam. 125)
kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭiyapeṇ vīṭṭuvēliyuñ ceyyalām;  naṉcey puṉceyk kāṭṭuvēliyuñ ceyyalām. kaṇavaṉi yiḻanta kimpeṇ, taṉ kaṟpik kāttuk koṇṭu piḻikka vīṭṭuvēliyē ceytal kūṭum. attakiya vēli akkālattu nūl nūṟṟalē. ātalāṟ kimpeṇṭir ataṉi mēṟ koṇṭaṉar.
āḷil peṇṭir tāḷiṟ ceyta
nuṇaṅkunuṇ paṉuval pōla  (naṟ. 353)
āṭinecavu irupālārāluñ ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu.
aḻakiṟkum maṇattiṟkum, palvaki naṟumalarkaḷik kaṇṇiyākak kaṭṭiyum māliyākat toṭuttum, āṭavar kaḻuttilum peṇṭir koṇṭi yilum nāḷtoṟum putitu putitāka aṇintu koṇṭaṉar.
marutanilamaṇ keṭṭiyāyiruntataṉāl, uyarnta maṇcuvar eḻuppip peruvīṭu kaṭṭik kūri vēyntu koṇṭaṉar. ūrt talivarāyiruntavar, mullinilattuk karaṭukaḷiluṅ kuṉṟukaḷilu miruntu kaṟkoṇarvittu, kāri (maṇal kalantu cuṇṇāmpuc cāntu) pūcik kalvīṭu kaṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. cuṭumaṭkalan tōṉṟiyapiṉ cuṭumaṭ kalluñ cuṭumaṇ ōṭun tōṉṟiyataṉāl, atikāramuñ celvamuñ ciṟantavar kāripūcic ceṅkal maṉikaḷ kaṭṭi ōṭuvēyntu koṇṭaṉar. ōṭu vēyātu maṭṭamāka mukaṭamitta kārivīṭu maccuvīṭeṉappaṭṭatu. cuṭumaṇ eṉpatu, mutalil, cuṭṭakalam, cuṭṭa ceṅkal cuṭṭa ōṭu ākiya mūṉṟuṟiyuṅ kuṟittatu.
cuṭumaṇ = 1. maṭkalam, (cilap. 14 : 146. arum). 2. ceṅkal. cuṭuma ṇōṅkiya neṭunakar varippil (perumpāṇ. 405). 3. ōṭu. cuṭumaṇ ēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ (cilap. 14 : 146)
cuṭṭa ceṅkal cuṭumaṭ palaki yeṉṟuñ collappaṭṭatu. payiluñ cuṭumaṭ palakipala koṇarvittu (periya pu. ēyarkōṉ. 49).
iṟutiyil ceṅkal eṉṉum peyarē nilittatu. pārvikku naṉṟā yirukkavum vaṇṇa ōviyam variyavum, ceṅkaṟ cuvarellām maṇal kalavāta veṇcāntiṉāl tīṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.
veṇcāntu pūciya kārivīṭu, kūrivēynta maṇ vīṭṭoṭu oppunōkkiya pōtu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiyataṉāl, nakar eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu. nakutal viḷaṅkutal. naku - nakar. nakarmikka vūrum ciṉiyāku peyarāka nakar eṉappaṭṭatu. vīṭṭik kuṟikkum kuṭi eṉṉuñ collum, kuṭimikka vūrik kuṟittal kāṇka. 
(e-ṭu):  maṉṉārkuṭi, kārikkuṭi). im mayakkan tavirttu ūriyē kuṟikka nakari eṉṉuñ col eḻuntatu. nakarkaḷi (kāri vīṭukaḷi) uṭiyatu nakari. periya nakar (ūr) nakaram eṉappaṭṭatu. ikkālattil mānakar eṉpar. aṅṅaṉañ collat tēviyilli. am eṉpatu perumip poruḷ kuṟikkum piṉṉoṭṭu, e-ṭu: nili - niliyam viḷakku- viḷakkam, mati - matiyam = muḻunilā.
nakarkaḷilēyē nākarikan tōṉṟiyataṉāl, nākarikam eṉṉum collum nakar eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟē tirinta mintatu. nakar - nakarakam - nakarikam - nākarikam.
aracaṉ allatu avaṉuṭiya tuṇiyatikārikaḷ vatital, aṟiñarum pulavarum pērāciriyarum vāḻtal, kaṇakkāy paḷḷikaḷum taṉippaṭṭa perumpulavariṉ uyarnilik kalvi niliyaṅkaḷum amintiruttal, perumpālār eḻutappaṭikkat terintavarāyiruntu tiruttamākap pēcutal, ellārun tiruntiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷi mēṟkoḷḷutal, aḻakiya āṭiyaṇi kaḷ uṭal maṟiya aṇiyappaṭutal, tuppuravāṉa kārivīṭukaḷum māṭamāḷiki kaḷum kūṭakōpuraṅkaḷum nērāṉa perun terukkaḷum puticālakaṅkaḷum iruttal, ēntāṉa aḻakiya vūrtikaḷ iyaṅkutal, uyarnta aṟucuvi yuṇṭikaḷ uṇṇap paṭutal, palavaki yuyarnta piṟanāṭṭu aimpula nukarcciyarum poruṭ kaṭikaḷ aṭuttaṭuttiruttal, kaḷavuṅ koḷḷiyum nikaḻāvāṟu ūrkāvalar allum pakaluṅ kāttal, koliyāḷikaḷiyum piṟa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷiyun taṇṭikkum aṟamaṉṟamum, tirukkōvilkaḷum tuṟaviyar maṭamum coṟpoḻivukkūṭamum paṭṭimaṇṭapamum uṇmi, mutaliya ētukkaḷāl, nakaraṅkaḷum nakarkaḷumē nākarikap piṟappiṭamāyiṉa. anākarikaṉi nāṭṭuppuṟattā ṉeṉṟum, paṭṭikkāṭṭā ṉeṉṟum colvatē, iti valiyuṟuttum. nakara mēmpāṭukaḷ nakarkaḷiṟ kuṉṟiyirukku mēṉum nākarikattiṟ kētuvāṉaviyē. nākarikam nakariṟ piṟantateṉiṉ, nakarattiṟ ciṟanta teṉalām. pāṇṭiyaṉ maturi oru nakaram (mānakar) akkālattu nelli (tirunelvēli) orunakar, viki maturi pōṉṟatē paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟut teṉmaturi.
nākarikattiṉ muḷi kuṟiñci nilattilēyē tōṉṟiyatēṉum, atu muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭintatu marutanilattu nakarppāṅkē yeṉṟum, kuṟiñci nākarikamum mulli nākarikamum marutanākarikattiṉ kīḻnilikaḷē yeṉṟum, neytal nili vaṇikavaḷarccikkum celvap perukkiṟkumē ētuveṉṟum, pāli nili nākarika vaḷarcciyiṉṟip paṇpāṭṭiḻipiyē kāṭṭumeṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
maruta nilak kumukāya vāḻvil, orumaṉi maṇamum iṟappuvari piriyā ilvāḻvum perumpāṉmiyāyiṉa. oruvaṉāl maṇañ ceyyappaṭṭamikku aṟikuṟiyākap peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttiṟ poṟṟāli kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. paṉmaṉi maṇamum tīrvi muṟiyum toṭarntaṉavēṉum, tolli mikutiyum paṇpāṭṭiḻipum paṟṟi avi tāḻvākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. ūrttalivaṉum avaṉukku mēṟpaṭṭa aracarum, iṉpac ciṟappu nōkkip paṉmaṉi maṇattiyē kaṭip piṭittārēṉum, ataṉ iḻivi avaratu teyvat taṉmiyāṉa atikāram muṟṟilum maṟittuviṭṭatu. maruta nilattu niliyāṉa kuṭiyiruppum payirttoḻiliṟ kuṭumpak kūṭṭuḻippum, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar kātali vaḷarttu maṇavāḻkkiyi nīṭikkac ceytaṉa. uyirukkiṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭittataṉāl, vayiṟāravuṇṭu iṉpuṟavum, uḻikka iyalātavarkkutavi aṟam vaḷarkkavum, ētuvāyiṟṟu.
poṟkāla marutanila āṭcittuṟiyil, taṉiyūrāṭcipōy, oru pērūrum ataṉic cūḻnta uṭkiṭi pōṉṟa ciṟṟūrkaḷuñ cērnta kūṭṭūrāṭci tōṉṟi, piṉṉar ataṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuṟunila maṉṉarāṭci ēṟpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
taṉiyūrāṭci eṉpatu, elliyaḷavil ikkālattu nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ, periyataṉakkāraṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, ampalakāraṉ, mūppaṉ, talivaṉ, kavuṇṭaṉ, kuṭumpaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum kuṭittalivaṉ āṭci pōṉṟatu. āyiṉ, atikāra aḷavil piṟkālattu vēntaṉ āṭci pōṉṟē kōṉmi (Sovereignty) koṇṭatu. kūṭṭūrāṭci eṉpatu, kirēkka nāṭṭu nakara nāṭu (City State) pōṉṟatu;  ciṉṉūṟūrkaḷum paṉṉūṟūrkaḷuṅ koṇṭatu. ammaṉṉariṉ aracu vīṟṟirukkikaḷellām nakarkaḷ. amiccarum paṭi maṟavarum avarukkut tuṇiyāyiruntaṉar.
maruta nilakkuṭiyiruppup pōṉṟē, mulli kuṟiñci nilak kuṭiyiruppu kaḷum āṭciyil māṟutaṭintaṉa. atāvatu, taṉiyūrāṭci pōyk kūṭṭūrāṭci tōṉṟiṟṟu. paṭittuṇi yiṉmiyāṟ kuṟunila vāṭci tōṉṟavilli. ivvāṭci māṟṟam paṇṭamāṟṟu, viḻāk kāṇṭal, vēṭṭiyāṭal mutaliya toṭarpukaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, marutampōl nākarika vaḷarcciyaṭiya villi. itaṟku nila amippē aṭippaṭik karaṇiyam. ataṉāl, nakaraṅkaḷun tōṉṟavilli;  ūrppeyarkaḷum vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. marutanilattu ūrē ūreṉap paṭṭatu. ur=uṟu. uṟutal poruntutal. ur-ūr, uḻavut toḻilē nilattoṭu porunti niliyāka vāḻa ētuvāyiṟṟu, ataṉāl, ciṟṟūr pērūr mūtūrum āyiṉa. nakaraṅkaḷ perumi paṟṟip pērūr eṉṟum, mutumi paṟṟi mūtūr eṉṟum, collappaṭṭaṉa.
vāṇaṉ pērūr  (maṇi. 3 : 123)
atirāc ciṟappiṉ maturi mūtūr  (cilap. pati. 39)
mullinilattiṟ kālnaṭikaṭkup pulveḷi tēṭi aṭikkaṭi iṭam peyara vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ataṉāṟ kaṭṭi maṇmēṟ kūrivēyntu, uyaramiṉṟit tāḻvāṉa vīṭukaḷik kaṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl annilattūr pāṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. paṭuttal= tāḻtal, tāḻvāyiruttal. paṭu-pāṭu-pāṭi. iṉi, paṭu-paṭi-pāṭi eṉṟumām. vīṭukaḷ ceṟintiruntataṉāl, avvūr cēri eṉavum paṭṭatu.
kuṟiñci nilak kuṭiyiruppu nilampaṟṟik kuṟicci yeṉappaṭṭatu. kuṟiñci - kuṟicci. ciṟuciṟu kūṭṭamāka vāḻntataṉāl ciṟukuṭi yeṉavum paṭṭatu. kuṭiyeṉṟatu kuṭit tokutiyi, kuṭi= vaṭṭamāṉa ciṟu vīṭu.
poṟkāla moḻi, pakucoṉṉili (Inflexional Stage) kaṭantu tokunili (Synthetic Stage) paltokunili (Polysynthetic Stage) eṉṟa nilikaḷum aṭintiruttal vēṇṭum.
muṟiyeṇ	nili	eṭuttukkāṭṭu
1.	(1) aci nili:	ē, uḷ, pal.
	(2) tirinili	yā, uṇ (uṭkoḷ).
		(par-paru-peru).
2.	puṇarnili	perumakaṉ, ceyki, 				nallatu.
3.	koḷuvunili	var +ottu + i= varuttuvi,
		cuḷ-cuṇ+am + pu = cuṇṇa
		ṇampu- cuṇṇāmpu
4.	pakucoṉṉili	(perumakaṉ-) pemmāṉ,
		pirāṉ; ceyki, naṉṟu.
5.	tokunili	ē+oṉ= ēṉ-yāṉ.
		ē+um=ēm-yām.
6.	paltokunili	el+a+um= ellavum-
		ellām, cey+al+oṇ
		+ar+itu+a= ceyya
		voṇṇāta- ceyyoṇāta
		- ceyyoṇā.
aritu - arutu = kūṭātu. ceyyarutu = ceyyak kūṭātu.
putuk kaṟkālattil, peyarviṉi yeṉṉum col vēṟupāṭum, taṉmi muṉṉili paṭarkki yeṉṉum iṭavēṟupāṭum, orumi paṉmi yeṉṉum eṇ vēṟupāṭumē iruntiruntu, poṟkālattil irutiṇiyum aimpālum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. marutanilac coṟkaḷāl colvaḷam perukiṟṟu.
mataviyal vāḻkkiyil, poṟkāla marutanilat tamiḻar, paḻiya kuṟiñci mullit teyvaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṟanta aracaṉiyum teyvamāka vaṇaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
akkālattil, perumpaṇṇikaḷi lellām, tāmāka vantu oṭṭik koṇṭavarum vilikku vāṅkappaṭṭavarumāka, irucār aṭimiyar iruntaṉar. avar tappi ōṭippōṉālum aṭimiyareṉṟu aṟiyappaṭuvataṟku, avar kātil tuḷiyiṭap paṭṭatu.
teyvappaṟṟu mikunta piṟkālattil, āṇṭi mutal aracaṉ vari ellā vakuppārum tammi iṟiyaṭiyār eṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟkut tam kātu kuttit tuḷiyiṭṭuk koṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. attuḷi tūrntu pōkāvāṟē, mutalil ōliccuruḷum piṉṉarp poṉṉōliyum piṟavaṇikaḷum peṇṭir aṇintu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. pāmpaṭam (nākapaṭam), taṇ ṭoṭṭi, aricittaḻuppu, pūccikkūṭu, mēlīṭu eṉṉum aivaki nakikaḷi yaṇitaṟkut tam iḷamiyilēyē kuṇukku eṉṉum kutampiyāl tam kātut tuḷiyip peritāka vaḷarttuk koṇṭaṉar. kumarināṭṭuṭaṉ iṇintirunta pāṇṭi nāṭṭil, ivvaḻakkam iṉṟum iruntu varukiṉṟatu. atu kātu vaḷarttal eṉappaṭum. āṭavar tamcōṇit taṇṭi attuṇi nīṭṭāviṭiṉum, kuḻiyum kuṇṭalamum kuṇṭuk kaṭukkaṉum aṇiyumaḷavu tuḷiyi akalittuk koṇṭaṉar.
uḻavut toḻil varavara vaḷarntu muḻunēra vuḻippiyum vēṇṭiyamiyālum, ataṟkup pala putukkaruvikaḷ vēṇṭi yiruntamiyālum, vāḻkkiyilum nākarikam vaḷaravaḷarap putituputitākap pala tēvikaḷ tōṉṟiyamiyālum, ellārkkum ellā vēliyuñ ceyya nēramilli yeṉṟum;  nēramiruppiṉum, viruppaṅkaḷum tiṟamikaḷum iyaṟkiyākavē makkaṭku vēṟupaṭṭiruppatāl, ellārum ellā vēliyuñ ceyya viyalāteṉṟum;  kaṇṭapōtu, iyaṟkiyākavē paṟpala toḻiṟpātīṭukaḷ vevvēṟu kālattil ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
marutanila vāḻkkiyil, aḷavāṉa ūrtoṟum, mutaṉ mutaṟ kōvil vaḻipāṭu ceyya oru pūcakaṉum, kāval ceytu vaḻakkut tīrkka oru talivaṉum, paṇṭamāṟṟa oru kaṭikkāraṉun tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ, uḻavarkku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷic ceytutavap pakkattuṇiyākap pala toḻilāḷar paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟiṉar. avarip patiṉeṇkuṭi makkaḷ eṉpatu marapu. (ta.va.)
poṟu (1)
poṟuttal = 1, cumattal, civiki poṟuttāṉōṭūrntāṉiṭi (kuṟaḷ. 37), iciyilā yākki poṟutta nilam (239).  2. tāṅkutal. ālattu... neṭuñciṉi vīḻ poṟuttāṅku (puṟanā. 58). 3. aṇital. ammaṇi poṟuttireṉ ṟaṟintāṉ (upatēcakā. uruttirāk.44). 4. nōvu tāṅkutal. 5. tuṉpam poṟuttal. 6. maṉantāḷutal. poṟukki lēṉuṭal pōkkiṭaṅ kāṇēṉ (tiruvāca. 23: 6).  7. oru viṉiyic caṟṟu niṟutti vittal. poṟu, appuṟam uṇlām (u.va.). 
8. kālantāḻttal. oru maṇinēram poṟuttu vantāṉ. 9. nīṇṭa kālan tuṉpappaṭṭu amitiyāyiruttal. 10. oṉṟiṟkup poṟuppātal. atu uṉ poṟuppu. 11. maṉṉittal. 12. iḷakkāraṅkoṭuttal. 12. marakkalam nilantaṭṭutal. tōṇi poṟuttup pōyviṭṭatu. 14. celavu tākkutal. vaṇṭi uṭintu viṭṭāl āyiram urupā uṉṉip poṟuttuviṭum. 15. uvamiyākat takutal. ulampoṟukkalāta tōḷāy  (cīvaka. 402)
poṟukka = nirampa, mūkku muṭṭa, aḷaviṟku miñci. poṟukkap piṭittuviṭṭāṉ. (kocci vaḻakku)
poṟuti = piṟar kuṟṟam poṟuttal.
poṟuppu = oru kaṭami niṟivēṟṟatti allatu viṉit tavaṟṟi ēṟṟuk koḷḷutal.
poṟumi = nīṇṭa kālam oṉṟiṟku amitiyākak kāttiruttal.
poṟi = nīṇṭakālan tuṉpam poṟuttal.
poṟāmi = piṟarākkaṅ kaṇṭu maṉam puḻuṅkutal. poṟuttal eṉṉuñ colliṟku mēṟkūṟiya poruḷkaḷellāvaṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāṉatu cumattal eṉpatē atu uṭampāṟ cumattalum uḷattāṟ cumattalum eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ muntiyatē muṉṉatu.
poṟu eṉṉum tūya teṉcol, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam, tuḷu, kuṭakam, kōttam, tuṭavam mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷil maṭṭumaṉṟi, āriya moḻikaḷ aṉiyavaṟṟilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅku vatu, kīḻvarum eṭuttukkāṭṭu viḷakkattāl aṟiyappaṭum.

kālṭuvelār collarāycci
Glossarial Affinities - West Indo - European Family. “Poru, to sustin to bear, to suffer pationtly, poruppu, responsibility, porumei patience, Comp. Sans. bhri (bhar), to bear; Gothic biran, bar, beram, to bear; Old High German beram, peran; English bear, bore; Old English bearn, - child; Greek phero; Latin fer-o. Tamil distinguishes between this word and pira; to be born.... Latin in like manner distinguishes between par-io and fer-o. whilst the Teutonic tongues make no difference between bear, to sustin, and bear, to bring forth. They constitute one word, from which is formed the past participle to be born or borne and also the noun birth.”
piṟanti eṉṉum tamiḻc colli ‘birth’ eṉṉum āṅkilac col ottiruttalāl, piṟappu viṉiyik kuṟikka vēṟoru collum tiyūttāṉiya moḻikaḷil mutalil vaḻaṅkip piṉpu maṟintirukkalām.
kālṭuvelār, poṟu eṉṉum teṉcoṟku iṉamāṉa āriyac coṟkaḷi, tāmē kaṇṭiruppatu iṅkup perituṅ kavaṉikkattakkatu.
mākkacumullar ārāycciyuṅ kūṟṟum
“No one can help seeing that even amongst the most ordinary words in English there are some which are very much alike in sound. If these words have also some similarity in meaning we are justified in supposing that they may have a common origin.
“Take for instance, such words as, to bear, burden, bier, and barrow. They all have the same constituent element, namely, br; they all have a meaning connected with bearing or carrying. Burden is what is carried; bier, what a person is carried on; barrow, in wheel-barrow, an implement for carrying things.
“No doubt, this is only prima facie evidence. We must not forget that we are dealing with a modern language which has passed through many vicissitudes. In order to institute truly scientific comparisons, we should have in each case to trace these words to their Anglo-Saxon, or even to their corresponding Gothic forms.
“But though it is necessary, before we institue comparisons, always to go back to the oldest forms of words which are within our reach, still for practical purposes it suffices if we kown that such words as bear, burden, bier and barrow have all beeen proved to come from one common source.
“And more than this. As to bear is used in many languages in the sense of bearing children, we may safely trace to the same source such English words as brith, and birn, a child.
“Nay, as the same expression is also used of the earth-bearing fruit, we can hardly be wrong in explining, for instance, bar-ley, as what the earth bears or brings forth. In German Getrcide, M.H.G.  Getregede, literally, what is born, has become the name of every kind of corn. If we go back to Anglo-Saxon, we find boer-lic for barley, in which lic is derivative, while bere by itself meant barley. In Scotland more particularly bear continued to be used for barley and a coarse kind of barley is still called bear-barley. Barn also receives the explanation from the same quarter. For barn is contracted from bere-oern, which means barley-house, or, as also called, bere-flor.
“But now we shall be asked, What are those mrysterious syllables? What is, for instance, that bar, which we discovered as the kernel of ever so many words?
“These syllables have been called roots, That is, of course, nothing but a metaphorical expression........ In burden, den is formative; in birth, th is formative; in birn, n is formative. In barn, too, n is formative, but it is different from the n in birn, becuse it is really a contraction of oern. Bere-oern meant a place for bar-ley, just as horsern meant a place for horses, a stable, sleepern, a sleeping place.
“There remin therefore bar with a variable vowel, and this we call a root, or an ultimate element, of speech, becuse it cannot be analysed any further.
“This root bar, however, is not an English root. It existed long before English existed, and we find it agin in Latin, Greek, Celtic, Slavonic, Zend, and Sanskrit, that is, in all the languages which form what is called the Aryan family of speech. As this root bar exists in Latin as fer, in Greek as pher, in Celtic as ber, in Slavonic as ber, in Zend as bar, and in Sanskrit as bhar, it is clear that it must have existed before these languages separated, and that, as you may imagine, must have been a very, very long time ago”.  (Three Lectures on the Science of Language, pp. 20 - 23.)

ceyaṟpeyar vaṭivu : (Gerundial form)
poṟu-ttal, (ce. kuṉṟā. vi.). 1. cumattal, to bear, sustin. civiki poṟuttāṉō ṭūrntā ṉiṭi (kuṟaḷ. 37). iciyilā yākki poṟutta nilam (kuṟaḷ. 239). 2. tāṅkutal. to prop, to support, ālattu... neṭuñciṉi vīḻpoṟuttāṅku (puṟanā. 58). 3. aṇital, to put on. ammaṇi poṟuttireṉ ṟaṟintāṉ (upatēcakā. uruttirāk. 44), 4. uṭaṉ koṇṭiruttal, to continue, to possess, as one’s own body. poṟukki lēṉuṭal pōkkiṭaṅ kāṇēṉ (tiruvāca. 23 : 6). 5. tuṉpam poṟuttal. to endure, suffer. 6. kuṟṟam poṟuttal, to bear with, to put up with. 7. iḷakkāraṅ koṭuttal, to indulge to allow. 8. maṉṉittal, to excuse, forgive, pardon. tammi yikaḻvārp poṟuttal tali (kuṟaḷ. 151). 9. piṟa karuttu vēṟupāṭṭi allatu mata vēṟupāṭṭip poṟuttuk koḷḷutal, to tolerate. 10. poṟuppu (uttaravātam) ēṟṟal, to take resposibility, to be accountable for. 11. kālan tāḻttal, to delay, to postpone. 12. uvamiyākap peṟutal, to be similar. ulakam poṟukkalāta tōḷāy (cīvaka. 402). 13. aḻuttutal, to press heavily, as a load.
(ce. kuṉṟiya vi.) 1. poṟumiyāyiruttal, to be patient, exercise forbearance. 2. viṉiyiṭi niṉṟu koḷḷutal. to stop, wait halt in doing anything. 3. vili koḷḷutal, to cost, as an article; to be spent or expended on. 4. tōṇi taṭṭippōtal, to run aground, as a vessel, to strand. 5. māṭṭik koḷḷutal, to become fixed, jammed, wedged in (W.). 6. kaṭamiyākak cumarutal, to devolve upon, as a duty responsibility expense. 7. iṇaṅkiyiruttal, to consent yield, comply with. 8. mikuntiruttal, to be excessive. 9. kaṉattal, to be heavy, weighty.

“Atlas .... (Atlas-antos (1) Greek god of the older family. who held up pillars of universe; (2) the mountin in Libya regarded as supporting the heavens)”
“Atlantic... 1. Pertining to mount Atlas in Libya; hence applied to sea near western shore of Africa, & later to whole ocean between Europe and Africa on east & America on west.... (f. L. f. Gk. Atlantikos. f. Atlas)” eṉṟu erutantuṟic ciṟṟakaramutali (the concise oxford dictionary) kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.
poṟiyaṉ = pe. 1. cumappavaṉ. puṉṉilip pavattuk kellān tāṉoru poṟiyaṉāki (upatēcakā. civapuṇ. 344); bearer, sustiner. 2. maliyaracaṉāṉa cēraṉ. yāṉik kaṭumāṉ poṟiya (puṟanū. 53); Cera king, as lord of the mountinous region in the Tamil  country. 3. poṟumikkum poṟikkuñ ciṟanta tarumaputtiraṉ; Dharmaputra, the eldest of the the Pandavas, as embodiment of patience.
kuṟumpoṟi = pe. ciṟumali (piṅ.), variyaka naṇṇik kuṟum poṟi nāṭi (patiṟṟup. 74 : 7).
kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ = mullinilat talivaṉ (iṟi. 1 : 18) chief of the sylvan or pastral tract.
irumpoṟi = pe. cērar paṭṭap peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu (patiṟṟup. 89 : 9). e-ṭu : cēramāṉ kaṇikkālirumpoṟi; a title of the Cera kings.
poṟiyāḷaṉ = pe. 1. poṟumicāli 2. tarumaputtiraṉ (piṅ.).
poṟiyāṭṭi = 1. poṟumiyuḷḷavaṉ. curuṅkum maruṅkuṟ perum poṟi yāṭṭiyi (tirukkōvi. 353); patient woman.  2. kāvu (pali) koṭukkum pūcārip peṇ. kāṉappali nērkaṭavuṭ poṟiyāṭṭi vantāḷ. (periyapu. kaṇṇap. 65); priestess who offers animals to god in sacrifice.
poṟiyilāṉ = pe. 1. poṟumiyillātaṉ, impatient person. 2. vēṭaṉ, savage hunter.
poṟinili = pe. maṉatti oru vaḻippaṭuttutal (tāraṇi. t.); concentrated attention in yogic meditation.
viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar : (participal noun or appelativetive verb).
poṟuttār puviyāḷvar poṟuttār nāṭāḷvar, poṅkiṉār kāṭāḷvar (paḻamoḻikaḷ). paci poṟukkātavaṉ (e.ma.), one who can not bear hunger.
kūṭṭuccol : (compound words)
e-ṭu :irumpoṟi, kuṟumpoṟi, neṭumpoṟi, poṟiyilāṉ.
iṇimoḻi : (words in pirs)
e-ṭu :aṟiyum poṟiyum, pe.
toṭar moḻi : (phrase)
e-ṭu :poṟuppaṟṟataṉam, pe.
marapu vaḻakku : (idioms)
poṟuttiru-ttal (ce. kuṉṟiya viṉi.) To wait patiently.
poṟuttukkoḷ-ḷutal. (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) To bear with.
poṟuttup pō-tal : (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) 1. tōṇitaṭṭutal to ran aground. 2. māṭṭik koḷḷutal. to be stuck or jammed in. ce. kuṉṟiya vi. toṭarntu poṟuttal, to continue. to tolerate.
poṟuppuk kaṭṭu-tal : (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). 1. poṟuppēṟṟutal (uttaravātap paṭuttutal), to put responsibility on 2. īṭukāṭṭutal, to tender as security. 3. muṟṟuvittal to accomplish (W.).
poṟiyiruntāṟṟu-tal : (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) tuṉpam poṟuttalik kaṭippaṭittal, to bear with patience poṟiyirun tāṟṟuyeṉṉuyirum pōṟṟi ṉēṉ (kamparā. cuntara. urukkāṭṭu. 11).
poṟiyuyir-ttal (ce.kuṉṟā vi). 1. marakkalam carakkiṟakkutal, to be unloaded, as a vessel. 2. iḷippāṟumāṟu cumiyiṟakkutal, to disburden and rest tuṟituṟi tōṟum poṟiyuyirt toḻuki (poruna. 239). 3. makap peṟutal. peruntēvi poṟiyuyirtta kaṭṭil (naṉ. 269, uri); to be delivered of a child.
piṟaviṉi (cusal verb)
1. poṟukkavikkiṟēṉ, poṟukkavittēṉ, poṟukka vippēṉ.
poṟukkavi-ttal = (ce. kuṉṟā vi.). 1. cāravittal to place, lean aginst. 2. mikucumiyēṟṟutal, to overload 3. poṟup pēṟṟutal, to impose a duty or expense upon 4. poṟuttuk koḷḷac ceytal, 5. tōṇi taṭṭac ceytal to run aground, as a vessel.
2. poṟuppikkiṟēṉ, poṟuppittēṉ, poṟuppippēṉ.
poṟuppi-ttal = (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) 1. cumattutal, to cuse to rest on. 2. muṭṭukkoṭuttal, to prop; to sustin (W). 3. poṟukkumāṟu ceytal. tiruvaṭiyip poṟuppikkumavaḷ taṉcolvaḻi varumavaṉip poṟuppikkac colla vēṇṭāviṟē (aṣṭātaca mumuṭcup) to cuse to bear. 4. poṟuppuk kaṭṭutal, to put responsibility on.
(5) mūlamum tiripum (Original and derivation)
pul - pol, poru - poṟu
pullutal = poruntutal. pollutal = poruntutal. porutal = poruntutal. poṟuttal = poṟuntutal, poruntit tāṅkutal, cumattal.
	o. nō :	muṭṭutal = poruntutal, poruntit tāṅkutal. muṭṭuk koṭuttal eṉpatu tāṅkutal allatu tāṅkavittal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
(6) iṉaccoṟkaḷ (congnates and allied words)
tiraviṭam
maliyāḷam - poṟu(kka), kaṉṉaṭam - poṟu. kuṭakam - pori, kōttavam - por, tuṭavam - pir, tuḷuvam - pute = (cumi.) load, burden. kōṇṭi - pukuttāṉā = kaṉattal to high heavily.
OE., OS., OHG ber (an), E. bear, ON ber (a), Goth bir (an) L. fer (re) 
Gk. pher (ein). Skt bhar. LL port (are), to carry. port eṉpatiṉiṉṟu tirintaviyē porter, portage, portable, portative mutaliya taṉiccoṟkaḷum, import, export, comfort, deport, purport, rapport, report, support mutaliya muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟa coṟkaḷum fortfire, fortfolio, fortmanteau mutaliya kūṭṭuccoṟkaḷum.
bar allatu bhar eṉṉum āriya vērccol paṟṟi For instance. the root bar or bhar particularly if we minde the words derived from Latin ferre and adopted English such as, for instance, fertile, far (barley), farina, barley-flower, reference, deference, conference, difference, inference, preference, transference and all the rust, would more than a hundred English words. We should not want therefore more than a hundred such roots to accounts for 10,000 words in English.” (p.26).
“Thus birth was originally bhar, to bear plus a domonstrative element in English th which localises of bearing here and there”.
“The sanskrit bi-bhar-mi shows us the same root reduplicated, so as to express continous action and followed by as a personal demonstrative bearing comes to mean, I bear.
The English bear-able is a compound of bear with the Roman suffixable, the Latin abilis, which expresses fitness.” (p. 27)
eṉṟu mākkacu mullar taṉ moḻinūl paṟṟiya muc coṟpoḻivukaḷ (Three Leatures on the Science of Language) eṉṉum pottakattil varintiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
orucila mēli moḻi nūlārāycciyāḷar piratar (brother) eṉṉum āṅkila muṟippeyariyum bar (bear) eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu tirittu, kuṭumpattit tāṅkupavar eṉṟu poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. aḵtuṇmiyāyiṉ, poṟuttār eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟku aḵtu iṉamāyirukkum.
iṉi, ki.pi. 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa cīvakacintāmaṇiyil
karuṅkaṭal vaḷantarak kariyum paṇṭiyum  (cīvaka. 62)
eṉṟu uyttal allatu ēṟṟiccellutal eṉṉum poruḷil āḷap peṟṟirukkum karital eṉṉum tūya tamiḻccol, kēri (carry) eṉṉum āṅkilac colli oliyilum poruḷilum kuṟittiruppatu āḻntu kavaṉikkattakkatu. kari eṉpatu paḻaṅkaṉṉaṭattilum kara eṉṉum vaṭiviluḷḷatu. āṅkila akaramutalikaḷ carry eṉṉum colli vaṇṭiyik kuṟikkum kār (car) eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. āṅkilar varukikku āṟu nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭa kari eṉṉuñcol āṅkilattiṉiṉṟu vantirukka muṭiyātu.
ME. & ONF carre f.L. carrus. cum four wheeled vehicle, f. celt karrom OS, Olr. O Welsh karr, E. car.
iṅṅaṉam nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭi āriyaccoṟkaḷ tamiḻc coṟkaḷōṭu ottiruppatuṭaṉ tamiḻilēyē vērkaḷik koṇṭiruppatāl avvoppumi taṟceyalāka nērttirukka muṭiyātu.
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
āriyattil vaḻaṅkum tamiḻccoṟkaḷuḷ poṟu eṉpatum oṉṟu. mēliyāriyattilum kīḻiyāriyattilum vaḻaṅkum vaṭivukaḷi nōkkiṉ, tiyūttāṉiyak kuṭumpattic cērnta āṅkilaccol tamiḻccoṟku mika nerukkamākavum, ilattīṉac collum kirēkkac collum caṟṟut tirintum camaṟkirutac col mikat tirintum iruppatik kāṇalām.
okaram ekaramākat tirivatu iyalpē.
e-ṭu : coruku - ceruku. immuṟippaṭi poṟu eṉpatu āṅkilattilum ilattīṉilum kirēkkattilum ber, fer, pher eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. camaṟkirutattil bhar eṉṟu okaram akaramākat tirintuḷḷatum iyalpēyāyiṉum aḵtu eṭuppoliyum mūccoliyum kūṭi mikat tirintuḷḷami kāṇka. attiripē kalavit tamiḻil pari eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
Port eṉṉum ilattīṉac col poṟuttal eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyariṉiṉṟu tirintirukkalām.
tiruttam
ceṉṉi yakaramutaliyil bhar eṉṉum camaṟkirutac colloṭu oppunōkkumāṟu ēvi, attiripiyē mūlampōṟ kuṟippākak kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟākum.
pōṭṭi vaki
ikal : pakimiyāl iṭumpōṭṭi;
ical : viḷiyāṭṭāka iṭum pōṭṭi;
vīmpu : vampiṟku iṭum pōṭṭi;  (col: 59)
pōlit tamiḻppaṟṟu
ulakiṟ pala poruḷkaḷ pōliyākavuḷḷaṉa. potuvāka, veḷip pārviyil pōlip poruḷkaḷē uṇmip poruḷkaḷi ṉum polintu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. ip potuviyalpu pōlit tamiḻp paṟṟāḷarkku vilakkaṉṟu.
	(1) ayaṉmoḻip peyar
āriyar varumuṉ tamiḻar peyarellām tūya tamiḻc coṟka ḷākavē yiruntaṉa eṉpatic colla vēṇṭuvatilli. avar vantu āyiram āṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭṭa kaṭikkaḻakap pulavar nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar peyaruḷḷum toṇṭē (oṉpatē) muḻumiyākavō pakutiyākavō vaṭacollā yiruntaṉa. ak kālattil moḻiyārāycci iṉṟuḷḷa vakiyilum aḷavilum illi. āriyamum pirākirukatamuṅ kalanta vētamoḻiyum, ataṉoṭu tamiḻkalanta camaṟkirutam eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyum, tēvamoḻiyeṉa muṟṟilum nampappaṭṭaṉa. aṅṅaṉamiruntum āriyaroṭu paḻakiya tamiḻp pulavaruḷ aintiloru pakutiyiṉarē vaṭacoṟpeyar tāṅkiṉar. āriyar toṭarpu ciṟitumaṟṟa nāṭṭuppuṟat tamiḻar peyar perumpālum tūya tamiḻc collākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉiṉum, aṉṟiliruntu 1916ām āṇṭu vari, vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻp pēccilum ilakki yattilum tamiḻiṉ toṇṭi neriyumaḷavu ciṟitu ciṟitāy mēṉmēlum toṭarntu kalantu vantatiṉāl. tamiḻar peyarum an nilimi yaṭintaṉa.
tamiḻarkku naṟkālamāka, 1916ām āṇṭu tavattiru maṟimali yaṭikaḷ vaṭacoṟkaḷi aṟavē kaḷintu, tamiḻppayir mīṇṭum taḻittōṅkumāṟu ceytaruḷiṉārkaḷ. aṉṟiliruntu taṉit tamiḻ  kallāp potumakkaḷiṭiyum kaṭukip paravi varukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, innilimikku muṟṟum māṟāka, talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyarum tamiḻkkāvalarum tamiḻa amiccarum vaṭacoṟ peyar tāṅkivaruvatu viyakkattakka vēṭikkic ceytiyē. avar vaṭacoṟ peyar tām tāṅkuvatu maṭṭumaṉṟit tam makkaṭkum pērappiḷḷikaṭkum iṭṭu āriyamarapi aḻiyātu kāttu varukiṉṟaṉar.
pērā. paritimāṟ kaliñaṉ pirāmaṇarāyiruntum taṉittamiḻukku vittūṉṟit tam peyarit tūy tamiḻc collāka māṟṟik koṇṭār. tamiḻp peyar tāṅkātavar uṇmit tamiḻp paṟṟuṟṟuḷḷavarāka irukkavē muṭiyātu.
im mūvakiyārum. ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭu amiccar, tāmum tam kāṉmuḷiyarum tamiḻap peyar tāṅkāk kuṟṟatti maṟikka oruvaḻi kaṇṭupiṭittirukkiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu araciyal aluvalakaṅkaṭkum tām taṅkiyirukkum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaṭkum tamiḻp peyariṭuvatē. aiyaṟivuḷḷa arimāvum varimāvum karimāvum narimāvum mutaliya vilaṅkukaṭkum tamiḻp peyar cūṭṭiṉ avi immiyum etirkkātu ēṟṟuk koḷḷum. aṅṅaṉamirukka, ōraṟivuyirumaṟṟa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ amitiyāy ēṟkātu eṉ ceyyum? iṉi. kaṭṭaṭaṅkaṭkup peyariṭuvatil ōr ēntum (vacatiyum) uḷḷatu. vilaṅkāyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ kaḻuttiṟ kaṭṭit toṅkaviṭṭa peyarp palakikaḷic citittuviṭalām allatu eṅkēṉum vīḻttiṭalām. kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷō perumaḻi peytāloḻiyap peyar aḻiyap peṟā; nilanaṭukkam uṇṭāṉāloḻiyap palaki citiyappeṟā.
iṉi, nakarap pēriyaṅkikaḷil tirukkuṟaḷ eḻutivippatu allatu tirukkuṟaṭpalaki āṇiyaṟintu vippatum, tirukkuṟaḷ aṟiviyō, tamiḻp paṟṟiyō, avaṟṟil ēṟic celvārkku uṇṭākkiviṭātu. muṉṉum piṉṉum niṟkum allatu irukkum āḷ teriyātavāṟu ciṟitu nēram nerukki niṉṟu. iṟaṅkumiṭam ceṉṟu cērvatik kavaṉippatilēyē kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyiruntu ēntākac cīṭṭu vāṅkavum iyalāta vaḻip pōkkar, tirukkuṟaṭ palakiyirukkumiṭaṅ kaṇṭu vācittu ataṉ poruḷuṇarntu ataṉpaṭi naṭappar eṉpatu, veṇṇey taṭavik kokkup piṭippati nampuvatiṉuṅ kēṭāṉatē.
oṟuttārk korunāḷi yiṉpam poṟuttārkkup
poṉṟun tuṇiyum pukaḻ  (kuṟaḷ 156)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷip paṭittup poruḷuṇarntiruntāl, ceṉṟa āṇṭu (1968) māṇavarkkum ceṉṉi mānakarap pēriyaṅkit toḻilāḷarkkum, iṭi nikaḻnta caccaravu nērntēyirātu. kuṟaḷ neṟiyik kik koḷḷāmaṟ kuṟaḷip pārttāl maṭṭum pōtumeṉiṉ, ovvoru kaṭṭaṭattilum cuvarukkoru kuṟaḷō kuṟaḷatikāramō eḻuti. ciṟukaṭṭaṭaṅ kaḷil ōrīriyaliyum peruṅkaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷil ōrirupāliyum māperuṅ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷil muppāliyum muṭittuviṭalām. mēlum, taṉippaṭṭavar pēriyaṅkikaḷilum māṭṭuvaṇṭi kutiri vaṇṭikaḷilum ovvoru kuṟaḷ aṭṭi toṅka viṭalām.
tiruvaḷḷuvar kaḷneṟiyik kaṇṭippatu pōṉṟē kavaṟāṭṭi yuṅ kaṇṭikkiṟār. kavaṟāṭṭut taṉmiyuḷḷa cīṭṭāṭ ṭellām kavaṟāṭṭē.
2. kalavi naṭippēccu
iṟṟit tamiḻiṟ kalantuḷḷa ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷellām ovvoru collākat tamiḻiṟ pukuntavi allatu pukuttap paṭṭaviyē. avi pukuttappaṭumuṉ avaṟṟāl iṉṟu vaḻakku vīḻttappaṭṭuḷḷa tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷellām ellārkkum eḷitāyp poruḷ viḷakkiṉavē. avaṟṟiṉ iṭattil iṉṟu eḷituṇar poruṭcoṟkaḷ pōl vaḻaṅkum ayaṟcoṟ kaḷum toṭakkattil arituṇar poruṭcoṟkaḷāyiruntaṉavē. ātalāl, eḷiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷi nīkkiviṭṭu ariya vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷip pukuttutal kūṭāteṉṟu kūṟuvār, aṟivārāycciyillātavarum tamiḻp pakivarumē yāvar. orunāḷukku ōr ayaṟ colli vilakkiṉālum ōrīrāṇṭiṟkuḷ oruvar taṉittamiḻ pēcap payiṉṟu koḷḷalām. ovvoruvarum nūṟṟiṟku nūṟu tūymiyākap pēcāviṭiṉum iyaṉṟavari pēciṉāṟ pōtum!
(3) vaḻunaṭip pēccu
iṉi. tūya tamiḻiyum ilakkaṇap piḻiyiṉṟit tiruntiya vaṭiviṟ pēcuvatē kaṟṟōrk kaḻakām.
kalvik kaḻaku kacaṭaṟa moḻital. eṉṟār ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyar. paṇṭik kālattiṟ kumarināṭṭiṟ potu makkaḷum tiruttamāyp pēci vantaṉar. iṉṟu pulamakkaḷum piḻiyiṉṟip pēcuvatilli. koccit tamiḻi virumpum kāmil cuvalepil eṉṉum cekkōcilāvakkiyat tamiḻaṟiñarum tam tamiḻ varalāṟṟilakkaṇac coṟpoḻivukaḷ (Lectures on Historical Grammar of Tamil) eṉṉum āṅkilac cuvaṭiyil, eḻuttiṟkum muṟippaṭṭa pēcciṟkum payaṉpaṭuttappeṟum ilakkiya naṭit tamiḻi, tamiḻnāṭṭut taliciṟanta kalviyāḷarum vaḻakkamāṉa uriyāṭṭil orupōtum kiyāḷvatilli. palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻp pērāciriyar oruvar tam maṉiviyāroṭu allatu makkaḷoṭu pēcum pōtu colkiṟēṉ eṉṟu colvatilli, colrēṉ eṉṟē colkiṟār. tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar oruvar tam naṇparoṭu pēcumpōtu, illā viṭṭāl eṉṟa vaṭivattiyaṉṟu, illāṭṭa eṉṟa vaṭivattiyē payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟār. (pak. 5-6) eṉṟu iṟṟit tamiḻp pērāciriyariyum eḻuttāḷariyum paḻittirukkiṉṟār. vaḻunaṭiyip pōṉṟē ilakkaṇa naṭiyum vaḻakkamāyiṉ eḷitāvatē. cila coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻuvaṭivam uṇmiyil tiruntiya vaṭivattiṉum palukkal (uccarippu) aritāyirukkum. āyiṉum, vaḻakkaṅ karaṇiyamāka eḷitāka vāykku varum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tañci vaṭṭārattil eṇpatu eṉṉum colliṉ empaḷatu eṉṉum tiruntā vaṭivam eḷitāka vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
	(4) ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutalit tiruttam
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali, vēṇṭu meṉṟē tamiḻ camaṟkirutattiṉ kiḷi eṉṟu ayalār karutumāṟu, tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itil, akki, accaṉ, accu, appam, āppam, am (ām, ampu) ampalam, arakku, araṅku (araṅkam), aracu (aracaṉ), arattam, avi (aviyam) āciriyaṉ (āciriyam) āṇi, āyiram, ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam, uvaṇam (cuvaṇam), uvami, uru (uruvu, urupu, uruvam). ulaku (ulakam), aiyaṉ mutaliya āyirakkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭit tamiḻc coṟkaḷ āriyac coṟkaḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pērā. parō, pērā. emaṉō ākiya mēṉāṭṭup pērāciriyar iti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatiṉālēyē, tam tiraviṭa oppiyal akaramutaliyiyum kuṉṟak kūṟalākavum vaḻuppaṭavun tokuttuḷḷaṉar. inti tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukuvataṟkum kaṭanta īrulakat tamiḻ mānāṭukaḷum tamiḻp paḻippu mānāṭukaḷāka muṭintamikkum, ceṉṉip pa.ka.ka.ta. akaramutaliyē karaṇiyam. itit tiruttumāṟu araciṟku ettuṇiyō tamiḻp pērāciriyarum tamiḻ maṉṟaṅkaḷum ettuṇiyō muṟi vēṇṭiyum. aḵtu immiyum poruṭpaṭuttavilli.
(5) kaṭanta 2ām ulakat tamiḻmānāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭut talinakaril, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ vāḻnta iṭattil naṭantum kōlālampūr mānāṭṭiviṭak kēṭāka muṭintatu. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ vaḻiyiṉar vilakkappaṭṭaṉar. vaṅkanāṭṭup pirāmaṇar moḻittuṟit talivarāyiṉar. tilliyil naṭuvaṇāṭṭut tuṟiyic cērnta pirāmaṇaroruvar, tamiḻeḻuttum tolkāppiyamum acōkaṉ kalveṭṭiṟkup piṉ ki.mu. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiṉaveṉa acciṭṭa kaṭṭuri paṭittār. pireñcukkāra roruvar, paṇṭikkālattil camaṟkirutamē intiyap potumoḻiyāyiruntateṉṟum, ataṉvaḻi vantatē tamiḻeṉṟum, kaṭṭuri paṭittār. tiru. kāmil cuvelapilār maṟimali yaṭikaḷiyum nāvalar cōmacuntara pāratiyiyum puraṭcippāvalar pāratitācaṉiyum moḻittuṟip paṇpāṭaṟṟavarāka vaṉmiyākak kaṇṭitta kaṭṭuriyoṉṟi acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉār. tamiḻnāṭṭu mup palkalikkaḻakat talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyarum itit taṭukkavilli. tamiḻaracum kaṇṭikkavilli. mānāṭu perumpālum āṅkilattilēyē naṭipeṟṟatu.
(6) tamiḻnāṭṭu araciṉar pēriyaṅkip pōkkuvarattuttuṟi vaṇṭikaḷilum aluvalakaṅkaḷilum, tamiḻk kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷum toṭarkaḷum puṇarccip piḻiyāka eḻutap paṭṭuḷa.
(7) kallūrikaḷiṟ kaṟpikkappaṭum aṟiviyal tuṟip pāṭaṅkaṭkuriya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ muṟṟum taṉittamiḻil moḻi peyarkkappaṭa villi.
(8) vaḻakkaṟṟu varum tamiḻccoṟkaḷit tokukkavum, ici nāṭakam kaṇiyam maruttuvam mutaliya tuṟikkuṟiyīṭukaḷi āriyar varumuṉ ṉiruntatupōl tūya tamiḻākkavum, kōyil vaḻipāṭṭit tamiḻil naṭipeṟuvikkavum, iṉṉum oru muyaṟciyum ceyyappeṟavilli. āyiṉum, tamiḻ ariyaṇi yēṟiviṭṭatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(9) taṉittamiḻ ārāycci nūlkaṭkum tamiḻ varalāṟṟu nūṟkum taṉittamiḻ ēṭukaṭṭum araciyalutavi oruciṟitumilli.
(10) maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār vaḻiyiṉar tamiḻttoṇṭu ceyya āvalākak kāttiruntum avar puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭu, avar irukka vēṇṭiya iṭattiṟku arikuṟiyāṉa takuti yuṭiyavarē amarttappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
(11) tamiḻiṉ peyarāl araciyaṟkaṭci vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟatē yoḻiya, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkāṉa takunta vaḻi ētum kiyāḷap peṟavilli.
ivaṟṟiyellāṅ kavaṉiyātu intiyetirppuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭattuvatum tiruvaḷḷuvar īrāyira āṇṭuviḻāp paṟṟi aṟikki viṭuvatum, vaṇika vaḷarcciyum peyar viḷamparamum nōkkiyaṉavē yaṉṟi vēṟalla. ayaṉmoḻip peyari māṟṟātu tamiḻppaṟṟuk kāṭṭuvatellām. peṟṟōrkku ōraḷavu utaviṉum avarip piṟariṭattu maṟṟōrākak kāṭṭi maṟippatu pōṉṟatē.  (teṉmoḻi kumpam 1969.)

uruvam.
