vikkappaṭṭa āṇ peṇ uruvam.
	pommi	:	ciṟu patumi.
	pāvi	:	kaṉavaḷavillāta tōṟpāvi allatu kaṇpāvi.
(col. 41).

makkaḷ
makkaḷ taṅkaḷip payirukku allatu marattiṟku oppākak karutik koḷvatu vaḻakkam kaṭavuṭ kāppik karuti, ‘maratti naṭṭavaṉ taṇṇīrviṭa māṭṭāṉā?  eṉṟu kūṟuvatum, maṇa makkaḷi āyiraṅkālattup payir eṉṟaḻippatum ulaka vaḻakku col.
makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku matam muṭṭukkaṭṭiyā?
mata veṟiyum ataṉā lēṟpaṭum pōrumē, makkaḷ makiḻcciyāy vāḻvataṟkum amitiyāy muṉṉēṟuvataṟkum taṭiyāy niṟkiṉṟaṉa. matam taṉṉaḷavil makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku muṭṭuk kaṭṭi yaṉṟu. tamiḻar ellāt tuṟiyilum tali ciṟantu cīruñ ciṟappumāka vāḻntatu, kumarināṭṭu mata vāḻkkik kālamē. iṉṟum aṟiviyal kammiyat tuṟikaḷil tali ciṟantu tiṅkaḷi yaṭinta amerikkar, kaṭavuḷ nampikki yuḷḷavarē.
oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum eṉṉum uṇmiyi yuṇarntu, mānta rellārum kaṭavuḷiṉ makkaḷāṉa uṭaṉ piṟap peṉṟu karuti, yātum ūrē yāvaruṅ kēḷir eṉṟu aṉpoṭu kūṭi vāḻvataṟkē matam ēṟpaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, māntar tam maṉam pōṉavāṟu matattit tirittu, tammoṭu māṟupaṭṭa karuttiṉarip pakittu, avari ṉiṉṟu pirintu pōvatum avaroṭu poruvatum, veṟiyāṉa matattiṉ viḷivē yaṉṟi neṟiyāṉa matattiṉ viḷivaṉṟu. paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu kūṭi vāḻnta intiyarum pākkittāṉiyarum, pakivarāyp pirintu pōṉamikku, mataveṟiyē karaṇiyam.
makkaḷ akak karaṇa vaḷarccik kēṟṟavāṟu, matam munnilip paṭṭuḷḷatu. avaṟṟuḷ uyarnta niliyāṉa kaṭavuṭ camayattik kaṭip piṭippiṉ, kōvil kuḷamō, uruva vaḻipāṭō tēviyilli. ataṉāl, pāl ney mutaliyaṉa pāḻāvatum araciṟiyāṉa potup paṇac celavum ēṟpaṭā.
uruva vaḻipāṭṭi virumpiyavarum, taṉippaṭṭavarākavō palar kūṭiyō, vīṇ celavu ceyyātu vaḻipaṭṭu, viṭumuṟi nāḷilum vēliyillā nāḷilum tam contap paṇattic celaviṭṭu viḻāk koṇṭāṭi, piṟarip pakikkā tiruppiṉ, ataṉāluṅ kēṭilli. ākavē, iv viru vakiyālum, matattāl makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkut taṭiyilli yeṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻ nāṭṭip poṟutta variyil, makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku muṭṭuk kaṭṭiyā yiruppatu, piṟavik kulap piriviṉiyē yaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. itu intiyāviṟkē ciṟap pāṉa kumukāyak koṭu nōy. pirāmaṇaṉ taṉṉi nilat tēva ṉeṉṟum taṉ ilakkiya moḻiyit tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum colli ēmāṟṟi, matat tuṟiyuṭ pukuntu, kōvil vaḻipāṭṭi yum, iru vakic caṭaṅku naṭāttattiyum taṉ kulat toḻilākak koṇṭataṉālēyē, makkaḷuṭaṉ matamuṅ keṭṭa taṉṟik kaṭavuḷ nampikkiyā laṉṟu. in nilimi eṉṟum nilittaṟ poruṭṭē, tamiḻaruḷ tuppuravil uyarntavaṉ vīṭṭilum tāṉ taṇṇīruṅ kuṭippatilli yeṉṟu taṉṉi ellārkkum mēlāka vuyarttiyum, tamiḻarkku oṟṟumiyum uramum orumitta muṉṉēṟṟamum ēṟpaṭāvāṟu, avari nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa ēṟṟat tāḻvuḷḷa piṟavik kulak kūṇṭukaḷuḷ aṭittum, vit tirukkiṉṟāṉ pirāmaṇaṉ. ākavē, tamiḻari muṉṉēṟṟu taṟku, pirāmaṇiyatti matatti ṉiṉṟu muṟṟilum pirippatē aṟiñar mēṟ koḷḷat takka ceytiyām. matat tuṟiyiluḷḷa kuṟṟaṅ kuṟikaḷi nīkkātu matattiyē oḻikka vēṇṭumeṉ patu, cōṟṟi luḷḷa kaṟkaḷip poṟukkātu cōṟṟiyē koṭṭi viṭuvatu pōṉṟatē. cōṟu muḻuvatiyum kalleṉṟō maṇa leṉṟō oruvaṉ karutuvā ṉāyiṉ, atu avaṉ puṟak kaṇṇiṉ allatu akak kaṇṇiṉ kōḷāṟē yākum.
matattiṟ kuṟṟaṅ kaḷivatu eḷitaṉṟu; ellārkkum iyalvatu maṉṟu. paranta kalviyum āḻnta ārāycciyum paṇpāṭum poṟu miyum uḷḷavarē, matattic cīrtirutta muṭiyum. mūvāyiram āṇṭāka īṇṭi yuḷḷa aḻukkuk kuṉṟi orē tali muṟiyil akaṟṟi viṭa muṭiyātu.
iṉṟum tamiḻaruṭ perum pālār kaṭavuḷ nampikki yuḷḷa varātalāl, kaṭavu ḷilli yeṉṟu collac colla, atu āriya mēmpāṭṭiṟkum ēmāṟṟiṟkum araṇ ceytatēyākiṉ ṟatu. māntaṉ maṉatti iṟukap piṇikkak kūṭiya paṟṟukkaḷuḷ, mikunta valimi yuṭiyatu matap paṟṟu allatu kaṭavuṭ paṟṟē. tamiḻaṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭuk kaṇṭa matatti āriyaṉatu eṉiṉ, āriyaṉukku uyarvum tamiḻaṉukkut tāḻvum ēṟpaṭuvatōṭu, uṟitta matap paṟṟuḷḷa tamiḻaṉuk kirukkum āriya aṭimit taṉam iṭumput taṉamāka iṟukavē ceykiṉṟatu. ataṉāl āriyaṉ cuṟaṇṭalum atikarikkiṉṟatu. itu kaṇṇārak kāṇum ceyti.
māntaṉ puṟa nākarittiṉum ciṟantatu aka nākarikam. ataṉ viḷivē palvēṟu paṇpāṭṭaṟiviyalkaḷ. avaṟṟuḷ talimiyāṉatu mataviyal allatu meyp poruḷiyal eṉṟē, mēliyarālum karutap peṟukiṉṟatu.
aṉpilā rellām tamakkuriyar aṉpuṭiyār
eṉpum uriyar piṟarkku  (71)
eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ maṟi.
aṉpuñ civamum iraṇṭeṉpar aṟivilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟikilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟintapiṉ
aṉpē civamāy amarntiruntārē.  (257)
eṉpatu tirumantira maṟi.
iṅṅaṉam uyarnta tēvika aṟatti valiyuṟuttum mataviyal, pakuttaṟiviṟku māṟāṉa teṉṟu entap pakuttaṟivāḷaṉ colla muṭiyum?
kattarikkā yaṟukka vāṅkiya katti kaḻut taṟukkuṅ karuviyā yiruppiṉ, atu kaḻut taṟuttāṉ kuṟṟamā? kattiyiṉ kuṟṟamā? māntari yellām kaṭavuḷiṉ makkaḷākki uṭaṉ piṟap paṉpāl oṉṟupaṭ ṭiṉpuṟṟu vāḻac ceyyum uyarnta mataviyali, oru taṉṉalak koḷḷik kūṭṭam tām vāḻavum, piṟar tāḻavum payaṉpaṭuttiṉ, ak kuṟṟam eṅṅaṉ mataviyalic cārum?
iṉṟu kaṭavu ḷilli yeṉṟu colvārum koḷvārum, tamakkuc camamāṉavarum tammuṭaṉ uṇṇum takuti yuṭiya varumāṉa vakuppāruṭaṉum maṇavuṟavu kalavātu, āriyaṉ vakutta vaḻiyilēyē niṉṟu, eṉṟum tam piṟavik kulattuḷḷēyē koḷvaṉi koṭuppaṉi ceytu koḷvatu, eṅṅaṉam pakuttaṟivuc ceyalākum? itaṉālum, matam makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟat tiṟku muṭṭukkaṭṭi yaṉṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟa taṉṟō? paḻiyōriṭam paḷakōriṭam.
āriyam eṉṟa pēru millāta kumarināṭṭilum, kaṭavuḷ nampikki yillātavar cila riruntaṉar. pirāmaṇa vakuppillāta piṟanāṭukaḷilum, kaṭavuḷ nampikkiyillāta pala rirukkiṉṟaṉar. itu piṟavik kuṇaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
kaṭavuḷum maṟumiyum illi yeṉṟu kūṟum ulakiyam (lōkāyata) eṉṉum matam, kaṭik kaḻakak kālattil tamiḻakat tiliruntimi, pāṅkuṟum ulōkā yatamē puttam eṉṉum maṇimēkali yaṭiyāl (27:28) aṟiyappaṭum. im mataviyali nūlāka virit turittavaṉ cārvākaṉ (Ca#rva#ka).
accamē kīḻkaḷa tācāram eccam
avāvuṇṭēl uṇṭām ciṟitu  (kuṟaḷ. 1075)
eṉpatu uṇmi yātalāl, ōraḷavu nalloḻukkattiṟkut tūṇṭukōlāka irukkum vakiyilēṉum, matattāl naṉmi yuṇṭeṉṟu karuti, matattiṉ peyarāl uṇmiyil tīṅku ceyyum piṟavik kulap piriviṉiyiyē, aṟavē oḻittal vēṇṭum.
makkaḷ vāḻkkimuṟi
nāṭṭuvāḻkkinili : ikkālattiṟpōlac carittira nūlkaḷ muṟ kālattil eḻutappaṭāviṭiṉum, mutaṟṟamiḻariṉ vāḻkki muṟi akapporuṭ ceyyuḷkaḷil, ciṟappākak kōviyil, mikak kāvalākap pōṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. makkaḷ evvaḷavu nākarikamaṭiyiṉum, ataṉāl avaratu naṭiyuṭi koḷki evvaḷavu māṟiṉum, paṇṭimuṟipaṟṟiyē eṉṟum pāṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kōvikku oru marapuḷḷatu. atu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉappaṭum. atāvatu, uḷḷatum illatuṅ kalantu iṉiyatākavē yirukkum nāṭaka muṟiyum, iṉiyatum iṉṉā tatuṅ kalantu uḷḷatākavē irukkum ulakiyal muṟiyum oruṅkē taḻuviya nūṉeṟi vaḻakkākum.
kuṟiñci nāṭṭaracaṉ makaḷukku uṭiyum aṇiyum taḻiyākavē kōviyiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu, mikap paḻaṅkālattu iyalpik kuṟippatākum. ikkālattiṟ karāccip paṭṭaṇiyum oru peṇṇikkuṟittuk kōvi pāṭiṉum, paṇṭit taḻiyē talivaṉ kiyuṟiyākak kūṟappaṭuvataṉṟik karāccippaṭṭu kūṟappaṭātu. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
paṇṭittamiḻar kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉa aintu vakiyāṉa nilattiṟ kuṭiyiruntaṉar. ivi aintiṇi yeṉappaṭum. ivi nilittiṇiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa (iṭava ṉāku peyar). ivaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷuḷ, pāli marutam eṉṉum iraṇṭum marappeyarkaḷ; ēṉiya pūppeyarkaḷ.
pāli eṉpatu piṟanāṭṭi luḷḷatupōṉṟa vaṟaṇṭa maṇal nilamaṉṟu. kuṟiñcikkum mullikkum iṭiyiluḷḷa nilam pālimarac ciṟappāl pāliyeṉappaṭṭatu. atu mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil vaṟaṇṭum maṟṟak kālattil ceḻittumirukkum.
vēṉalaṅ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
ṟāṉalan tirukat taṉmiyiṟ kuṉṟi
mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟimiyiṟ ṟirintu
nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
pāli yeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum  (cilap. 11:12-16)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka. pāliyiṉ mutu vēṉiṟkāla niliyē, piriviṟkuriyatāka akapporuṭ ceyyuḷ kaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
makkaḷ aintiṇi nilattiṟkup pirintupōṉatu, kuṟiñciyiṉiṉṟu pōvatum, ayalnāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu vantu kuṭi pukuntu pōvatumāka iruvaki. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu makkaṭperukkāl paṭippaṭiyāy nikaḻvatu; piṉṉatu terintukōṭalāl orē camiyattum nikaḻak kūṭiyatu. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatē tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻnta teṉka.
kuṟiñciyil maṭṭum makkaḷirunta kāla muṇṭu. atu māntaṉ tōṟṟattiṟku aṭutta niliyākum. tamiḻnūlkaḷ tōṉṟiyatu marutattil nakaram tōṉṟiyapiṉpātaliṉ, kuṟiñciyilmaṭṭum makkaḷirunta toṉṉili appōtu maṟintupōyiṟṟu. ataṉāṟ kuṟikkappaṭavilli. āyiṉum atik karuttaḷavi yāṉ aṟintukoḷḷalām.
māntaṉ tōṟṟam āṇum peṇṇumāyttāṉiruntirukka vēṇṭum. avariyē ātam ēviyeṉṟu kiṟittumatamum ilām matamuṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
irumutu peruṅkuravariṉiṉṟum pala makkaḷ tōṉṟiya piṉ, kuṟiñciyil iṭampōtāmal, cilar mullikkuc ceṉṟaṉar.
mutaṟkālattil uṇavu tēṭuvatē māntar toḻilāyiruntatu. kuṟiñciyil kāykaṉikaḷip paṟittum vēṭṭiyāṭiyum uṇṭu vanta makkaḷ, iyaṟkiyāy viḷiyum maravuṇavu pōtāmi yālum, vēṇṭiyapōtellām ūṉuṇavu kiṭiyāmi yālum, ceykiyāyp payirpaccikaḷiyum vilaṅkukaḷiyum vaḷarkkattoṭaṅkiṉar. itaṟku maramaṭarnta kuṟiñci vacati yāyirāmiyāl mullikkuc ceṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl koṭiya vilaṅkukaṭkum ōraḷavu tappiṉar.
māntaṉ mutaṉmutalāy vaḷartta vilaṅku āvē. ā eṉpatu mā eṉpataṉ mey nīkkam. mā eṉṟu kattuvatu māveṉappaṭṭatu. mā-māṉ-māṭu. mā eṉpatu ṉakara meyyīṟu peṟṟu, āviṟkiṉa māṉa māṉi uṇarttiṟṟu. ā eṉpatu māṭu eṉpatupōla mutalāvatu potuppeyarā yiruntu. piṉpu peṇpālukku variyaṟukkappaṭṭatu. mutalāvatu vaḷarkkap paṭṭa vilaṅku mā(ā) vātaliṉ ataṉ peyar vilaṅkiṉattiṟ kellām potuppeyarāyiṟṟu. ā eṉpatu ṉakara vīṟupeṟṟu āṉ eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
maravuṇavum ūṉuṇavum makkaṭku vēṇṭiyavāyuḷḷamiyiṉ, uḻavum āvōmpalum oruṅkē tōṉṟiṉaveṉṉalām. āvāṉatu pāl tantatuṭaṉ uḻaviṟku vēṇṭiya kaṉṟukaḷiyum īṉṟatu. pulveḷikaḷil māṭukaḷi mēyttuk koṇṭu, ciṟitu puṉceyp payirkaḷiyum viḷittuk koṇṭaṉar mulli nilattār.
māṭu paṟiyarālmaṭṭum tiṉṉappaṭu kiṉṟatu. mēṉāṭukaḷil ati ellārum uṇkiṉṟaṉar.
mullinilattiṟ pōtumāṉa nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamumillā miyāl, makkaḷ aṭuttāṟpōl, mīṉip peritum virumpi ṉavar neytalnilattiṟkum kūlattip peritum viruppiṉavar marutanilattiṟkumākap pirintupōyiṉar.
pālinilavāṇarkku mutuvēṉilil uṇavu kiṭiyāmiyāl, aṉṟu avar āṟalikkavum cūṟi koḷḷavum nērntatu.
aintu nilattilum māntar paraviyapiṉ, kuṟiñcinilattār kuṟi colvatāl kuṟavar eṉṟum, pālinilattār pōrt toḻil ceytu maṟam (vīram) ciṟantirun tamiyiṉ maṟavar eṉṟum, mullinilattār kuṟiñcikkum marutattiṟkum iṭiyilirunta miyāl iṭiyar eṉṟum āvik kāttalāl āyar eṉṟum, marutanilattār uḻavic ciṟappāyc ceytatāl uḻavar eṉṟum, neytal nilattār paṭavut toḻil ceytamiyiṉ paṭavar allatu paravar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭṭār.
paṭaku paṭavu. ka-va, pōli. paṭavar-paravar-paratavar. ṭa-ra, pōli. paṭavar cenniṟamā yiruppatāl cempaṭavar eṉappaṭṭār. paratavar virittal tiripu.
nakaravāḻkki nili
māntar marutanilattiṟku vantapiṉ niliyāyk kuṭiyirukkat toṭaṅkiṉar; ataṉāl kuṭiyāṉavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. nili yāyk kuṭiyiruntatiṉāl, maruta nilattūrkaḷ mūtūrum pērūrum nakarum mānakaru māyiṉa. marutanilattūrkaḷ piṟa nilattūrkaḷiṉum palavakiyiṟ ciṟantiruntamiyāl, ūr eṉṟē yaḻikkap paṭṭaṉa.
ūr nakar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, mutalil taṉi vīṭṭiyum, piṉpu, vīṭṭut tokutiyāṉa ūriyum kuṟittaṉa. nakar eṉpatu piṟkālattil pērūrukku variyaṟukkappaṭṭatu.
niliyāṉa vāḻkkiyālum, māntar perukkālum, mutaṉ mutal nakarattilēyē nākarikam ciṟappāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. nākarikam eṉṉum collum nakaram eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantatē.
araciyal
māntar niliyāyuḷḷa iṭattil āṭporuṭ pātukāp piruppatum. niliyillāta iṭattil aviyillātiruppatum iyalpu. marutanilattil māntar niliyāyiruntamiyiṉ, āṭ poruṭ pātukāppiṟku mutalāvatu kāvalum, piṉpu ūrāṇmi nāṭṭāṇmikaḷum, ataṉ piṉ araciyalum tōṉṟiṉa.
kuṭikaḷi oru mantipōṉṟum aracaṉi ataṉ mēyppaṉ pōṉṟuṅ karutiṉar. ataṉāl, aracaṉ kōṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ kiyil, araciṟku aṭiyāḷamāka oru mēyppaṉ kōluṅ koṭukkappaṭṭatu. atu nērāyiruntami paṟṟic ceṅ kōl eṉappaṭṭatu. ceṅkōl cemmiyāṉa aracāṭcikku aṭiyāḷam. 
kō = pacu. kō + aṉ = kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kō(ṉ). kōṉ eṉṉum col āveṉṉum poruḷil āriya moḻikaḷiṟ peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
Swed. - Dan. ko, Du. koe, Skt. go, Ger. kuh, Ice. kyr. Irish - Gael. bo, L. bos, Gk. bous.
kō eṉṉuñ col, āveṉṉum poruḷil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkā miyālum, āriyattil vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālum ati āriyaccol eṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. pacuvik kuṟikka, ā, peṟṟam eṉa ēṉiyiru coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālēyē, kō eṉṉum col ap poruḷil vaḻakkaṟṟateṉka.
kōviyuṭiyavaṉ kōṉ. aṉ īṟṟil akaram tokkatu. o.nō: yāvar - yār.
kōvaṉ, kōṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iyaṟporuḷil iṭi yaṉiyum, uruvakap poruḷil aracaṉiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
kōvaṉiri mīṭṭaṉaṉ (cīvaka. 455) eṉpatil iṭiyaṉiyum, kōvaṉum makkaḷum (cīvaka. 1843) eṉpatil aracaṉiyum kōvaṉ eṉṉuñ col kuṟittatu.
kōṉ kōṉār (uyarvuppaṉmi) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iṭiyarkkuk kuṭippeyarāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kōṉ eṉṉum col, ceṅkōṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷil aracaṉik kuṟittatu.
aracaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil, kōṉ eṉṉum peyarē īṟu keṭṭuk kō eṉṟākum. talikkaḻakak kālat taracar peyar ceṅkōṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉatiyum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālattaracar peyar (pālipāṭiya peruṅ) kaṭuṅkō, iḷaṅkō (aṭikaḷ) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉatiyum, kō eṉṉum peyar pacupporuḷuk kēṟpatiyum kōṉ eṉṉum peyar aracaṉuk kum iṭiyaṉukku maṉṟip pacuvuk kēlāmiyiyum nōkkuka.
kō eṉpati, ā mā eṉpavaṟṟōṭu cērttu,
āmā kōṉav vaṇiyavum peṟumē  (naṉ. 248)
eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār kūṟiyatu tavaṟākum.
kō eṉṉum peyar peṟṟattiṟku vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉamiyiṉ, kōṉ eṉpatiṉ īṟukeṭṭa vaṭivam, aracaṉik kuṟikkum pōtu mayakkattiṟ kiṭamilli.
tā eṉṉum col āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉum, tamiḻukku eṅṅaṉam uriyatō, aṅṅaṉamē kō eṉṉuñ collum uriya teṉka.
toḻiṟperukkamum kulap pirivum
marutanilattil mutalāvatu uḻavar eṉṉum orē vakuppār iruntaṉar. piṉpu, muṟiyē vaṇikam araciyal tuṟavu eṉpaṉa paṟṟi, av vakuppiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōṉavar, vāṇikar aracar antaṇar eṉappaṭṭār. uḻavar mutaliya nāṟpālum piṟkālattil ēṟṟattalimi muṟiyil talimāṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. uḻavar kaṭiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭamiyiṉ kaṭiyar eṉappaṭṭār.
kol, necavu mutaliya kittoḻilpaṟṟip piṉpu uḻavar kulattiṉiṉṟē palar pirintaṉar.
uḻavar piṟarinōkka, vēḷāṇmiyil (upacārattil) ciṟantiruntami yiṉ vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭār. avaruḷ vaṟiya rāyiṉār uḻutuṇpārum celvarāyiṉār uḻuvittuṇpāru māyiṉar. ivarē niṟampaṟṟi muṟiyē karuṅkaḷamar allatu kārāḷar eṉṟum, veṇkaḷamar allatu veḷḷāḷar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭu pavar. kaḷattil vēli ceypavar kaḷamar.
karuṅkaḷamarum oḻukkam, ūṇ, iṭam, paḻakkavaḻakkam mutaliyaṉapaṟṟip piṟkālattiṟ paṟpala kulamāyp pirintu pōyiṉar. iṅṅaṉamē piṟakulattārum.
nakarattil araciyal tōṉṟiṉapiṉ tiṇimayakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu. mulli nilattilirunta iṭiyarum pālinilattilirunta kaḷḷar maṟavarum, neytala nilattilirunta cempaṭavarum, kuṟiñcinilatti lirunta kuṟavarum nakarattiṟku vantu tattam toḻilic cey vārāyiṉar. avaruṭ kaḷḷarum maṟavarum muṟiyē cōḻaṉukkum pāṇṭiyaṉukkum paṭiñarāyiṉar. paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ veṟṟic ciṟappiṟku ivviru kulamum peruṅkāraṇam.
kaḷḷar taṉit tamiḻarāyiruppavum, avarip pallavareṉṟu ōr ayal vakuppārākak kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar cilar. pallavar eṉpār. ki.pi. 4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyum, cōḻanāṭṭi allatu toṇṭi maṇṭalatti yāṇṭa ōr aracak kuṭumpattārēyaṉṟit taṉik kulattiṉarallar. avaruṭiya kuṭikaḷum paṭiñarum tamiḻarē. avarukkut taṉimoḻiyum taṉimatamumilli. tamiḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷum mataṅkaḷumē avarkkiruntaṉavum. celvamum maṟamum paṭitta evaṉum, oru paṭiyit tiraṭṭikkoṇṭu oru nāṭṭik kippaṟṟa eṉṟum iṭamuṇṭu. 
pallavarkkirunta paṭṭap peyarkaḷum, toṇṭiyaṉ eṉpatum oṉṟu. pallavarkkum paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpē, cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattiṟkut toṇṭimaṇṭalam eṉṟum, ataṉ aracaṉukkut toṇṭimāṉ eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkiṉami perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyā laṟiyappaṭum. avvāṟṟuppaṭit talivaṉip pāṭiya kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār, karikāl vaḷavaṉiyum pāṭiyuḷḷār. karikāl vaḷavaṉ kālam ki.pi. 1 ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. ākiyāl, putukkōṭṭi aracarit toṇṭimāṉ eṉṉum paṭṭam paṟṟip pallava marapiṉarākak kūṟutal poruntāteṉka. mēlum toṇṭimāṉ eṉpatu taṉittamiḻc collātaluṅ kāṇka.
vaḻipāṭum matamum
vaḻipāṭāvatu oru ciṟuteyvattiyēṉum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷi yēṉum vaṇaṅkum vaṇakkam. matamāvatu vīṭupēṟu karuti muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷi yaṭiyum peruneṟi.
mati + am = matam. matittal - karututal. kaṭavuḷippaṟṟiya matippu matamākum. matattiṟkuc camayam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. cami + am = (camiyam) - camayam. camital - pakkuvamātal. peṇṭir pūppaṭitaliyum arici cōṟātaliyum camital eṉṟu colluvatum, pakkuvamātal eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē. matam āṉmākkaḷi vīṭu pēṟṟiṟkup pakkuvappaṭuttalāl camayam eṉappaṭṭatu. camiyam = pakkuvamāṉa vēḷi, vēḷi, camayam pakkuvamākkum neṟi allatu koḷki. vēḷiyik kuṟikkum camiyam eṉṉum col, matattik kuṟittaṟku aikāram akaramāyiṟṟu. oru col poruḷ vēṟupaṭutaṟku eḻuttu māṟuvatu, oru colliyal nūl neṟimuṟiyākum.
kā : paḻimi (toṉmi) - paḻami (purāṇam); mutaliyār - mutaliyōr.
noṭippaḻami, paḻami pēcutal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.  (ta.ma.)
makaṭpāṟ kāñci
oru maṟakkuṭip peṇṇi maṇakka virumpiya vēntaṉ, akkuṭiyār ataṟkiciyā viṭattu, perumpaṭi yoṭu ceṉṟu avaroṭu pōr purivatuṇṭu. ammaṟavar tam kuṭiyiṉ māṉattik kāttaṟku añcātu etirppar. itu makaṭpāṟ kāñci eṉappaṭum  (ta.ti.11).
makaḷ
makaḷ - mahilā
maḻa - maḻavu - makavu. maḻa - maka - makaṉ, makaḷ.
makaḷ = 1. putalvi. nalkūrntār celva makaḷ (kalit. 56)
2. peṇ. āya makaṇīyāyiṉ (kalit. 107).
3. maṉivi. maṉakkiṉi yāṟku nīmaka ḷāyatūum (maṇi 21:30).
vaṭavar kāṭṭum mah eṉṉum mūlam makiḻ, makiḻvi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.  (va.va: 212).
makaṉ
makaṉ iḷamik karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa col.
ul - mul - mel - meṉmi. in. mulāyam = melliya. piṟappu allatu tōṟṟak karuttiṉiṉṟu, muṉvaral, muṉmi, iḷami, ciṟumi, meṉmi, aḻaku mutaliya karuttukkaḷ kiḷikkum.
mel - E. mellon, soft, L. mollis, soft, E. mollify, to make soft.
mellutal = kaṭiṉamāṉa vuṇavip pallāl arittu melitākkutal.
o.nō : L. mol. to grind, to make soft by grinding L. mola, E mill, building with machinery for grinding corn.
E. molar, f. L. molaris, mammal’s back teeth serving to grind.
E. muller, tool used for grinding powders on slab, upper part of the millstone, f. ME. mulour f. mul, to grind. L. mola, millstone.
mel - meli - melivu.
mel - melaku. melkutal = melitātal.
mel - melu - melukku = meṉmi.
mel - metu - va. mrutu, E. smooth, OE. smoth, smethe, smeeth, ka.metu.
metu - mettu. metteṉal = metuvāyiruttal.
metteṉavu = meṉmiyāṉa col, kuṇam, ūṟu, poruḷ. veṭṭeṉavu metteṉavi vellāvām (mūturi, 33).
mettu - metti = metuvāṉa irukki, paṭukki, taliyaṇi.
melukku - metukku. metukkiṭutal = meṉmiyātal meṉmiyā yiruttal.
metu - metuvu - metuku. metu - metuppu.
mel - mella = metuvāka. mella - meḷḷa = piya.
mel - mellam - meḷḷam.
mul - muṉ - muṉṉi = kāṭṭil iyaṟkiyāka viḷiyum ciṟu payaṟṟuvaki.
muṉ - muṉi = yāṉikkaṉṟu. muṉiyuṭik kavaḷam pōla (kalit. 360).
muṉ - miṉ - L. min, small.
iv vērccolliṉiṉṟu piṉvarumāṟu pala coṟkaḷ tirintuḷḷaṉa.
mince (to cut small), miniature, minicab, minify, minim, minimal, minimalise, minimum, minion, minish (diminish), minister (servant, one who is small), ministerial, ministration, ministrant, ministrative, ministry, minimize, minor, monority, minus, minuscule, minute, minutia.
muṉ - muṉaku - muṉaṅku. muṉakutal (muṉaṅkutal) = metuvāka vāykkuṭ collutal, muṇu muṇuttal.
o.nō : ānu., nu. aṭiyan, vaṭi ayamana ṭaṭiṟa aracaaraca.
mul - muḷ - muḷu.
muḷutal = arici ventu muṇṭi varutal. cōṟu muḷuntu varukiṟatu.
muḷ - muḷi. muḷital = muḷittal. viti iṉṉum muḷiya villi (u.va.)
muḷittal = 1. tōṉṟutal. oṉṟāy muḷitteḻuntu (tiruvāca. 10:8). 2. eḻutal. kāli ñāyiṟu katir virittu muḷippa (maṇi. 8:18).
ma. muḷi. ka. moḷe. te. molatcu.
muḷiyaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ. muḷiyāṉ = ciṟu kuḻanti, inta muḷiyāṉ pēcci yār kēṭkiṟatu? (w).
muḷi = 1. vittiṉiṉṟu veḷippaṭuvatu. vittiya veṇ muḷi (aiṅkuṟu. 29). 2. iḷami. muḷiyami tiṅkaḷ (kamparā. kumpaka. 16). 3. marakkaṉṟu. ataṉṟāḷ vaḻiyē muḷiyōṅ kupu (cīvaka. 223). 4. muḷiyāṉ. 5. makaṉ (piṅ.). 6. mūṅkil muḷi. 7. puṇmuḷi. 8. mūlamuḷi. 9. tiriki muḷi. 10. katavukkuṭumi. 11. kuṟuntaṟi.
ma. muḷa, tu. muḷe, ka. moḷe, te. molakka.
muḷ - mur - muru - muruku = 1. iḷami (tivā). 2. aḻaku (piṅ.). 3. eḻucci (tivā.). 4. murukaṉ. aruṅkaṭi vēlaṉ murukoṭu vaḷii (maturik - 611). 5. teyvam. muruku meyppaṭṭa pulitti pōla (puṟanā. 259). 6. vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭṭu. murukayarntu vanta mutuvāy vēlaṉ (kuṟun. 362). 7. tiruviḻā. murukayar pāṇiyum (cūḷā. nāṭ. 7). 8. tirumurukāṟṟup paṭi. muruku porunāṟu (taṉippā.).
muruku - murukaṉ. ma. murukaṉ, ka. muruka (g).
muru - muṟu - muṟi. muṟital = tuḷirttal. muṟinta kōla mukiḻ muliyār (cīvaka. 2358).
muṟi = 1. taḷir. muṟimēṉi (kuṟaḷ. 1113). 2. koḻuntili. iliyē muṟiyē taḷirē tōṭē (tol. mara. 88).
muḷ - muṇṭu. muṇṭutal = vittiṉ muḷi muḷuttal allatu muṭṭutal.
muḷ - muṭṭu = iḷam piñcu. muṭṭukkāy = muṟṟāta iḷanīr.
muṭṭuk kurumpi = ciṟu kurumpi.
muṭṭu - moṭṭu = 1. arumpu. moṭṭaṟā malar (tiruvāca 29:8). 2. tēriṉ kūmpu. māmoṭ ṭoṭintu ... māṉṟēr citiya (pārata. nāṉkā nāḷ. 24).
moṭṭu - moṭṭi. moṭṭip payal = maṇamākāta iḷiñaṉ, iḷavaṭṭam. (potuvāka iḻivuk kuṟippu).
muḷ - muṇ - muṇa. muṇamuṇattal = metuvāka vāykkuṭ pēcutal.
muṇ - muṇu. muṇu muṇuttal = metuvāka vāykkuṭ pēcutal.
muṇu - muṇukku. muṇukku muṇukkeṉal = ciṟu kuḻavi ciṟitu ciṟitākat tāyppāluṇṇutal.
muṇamuṇa - moṇa moṇa. moṇa moṇa veṉal = vāykkuṭ collutal. cuṟṟivantu moṇa moṇeṉṟu collu mantiram ētaṭā (civavāk. pā.)

vilaṅkoṭu makka ḷaṉiyar ilaṅkunūl
kaṟṟārō ṭēṉi yavar.  (kuṟaḷ. 410)
ceviyiṟ cuviyuṇarā vāyuṇarviṉ mākkaḷ
aviyiṉum vāḻiṉu meṉ.  (. 420)
makaṉ eṉṉum teṉcol, kōtiya (gothic) moḻiyil maku (magus) eṉṉum, kēliya (Gaelic) moḻiyil mak (mac) eṉṟum, tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
“Mac (mak) A Gaelic word signifying son, and prefixed to many surnames, as Mac Donald, Mac Grigor, &c. It is synonymous with Son in names of Tentonic origin ... It is allied to Goth magus, a son, fem. magaths (G. magd, a mid)”. I.D.E.L. Vol. III. p. 91.
Gadhelic (Gaelic), a. Of or pertining to that branch of the Celtic race which comprises the Erse of Ireland, the Gaels of Scotland and the Manx of the Isle of Man.
- The Imperial Dictionary of the English Language, Vol. II, p. 352.
Goth, n. One of an ancient Teutonic race of people, first heard of as inhabiting the shores of the Baltic. - Do. p. 413.
Mac eṉṉum kēliyac col Mc eṉṟu kuṟukiyum vaḻaṅkum.
e.ṭu. : Mc Adam. 
makaṉ - māṉ - maṉ = māntaṉ.
maṉpatu = makkaṭ ṭokuti. maṉpatu maṟukkat tuṉpaṅ kaḷi vōṉ (paripā. 15:52).
maṉpatu - maṉpati = 1. makkaḷiṉam. maṉpati kākkuniṉ purimi (puṟanā. 210). 2. paṭimakkaṭ ṭokuti. maṉpati peyara (patiṟṟup. 77:3).
maṉ - Teut. man, Skt, manu.
“MAN, a human being. (E). ME. man, Chucer, C.T.I. 43. A.S. mann, also mon; Grein, ii. 105 + Du man; Ice. madr (for manur); also man; Dan mand (with excrescent d); Goth, manna; G mann; (the G. mensch = mannich, i.e. mannish human). Allied to Skt. manu, Vedic manus -, a man. B. connected by some with Skt. man, to think. See Mind. But it is unlikely that the orig Sense could have been thinker.
Skeat’s Etymological Dictionary of the English Language, p. 358. 
maṉ - va. manu.
manu, thinking creature, man, Manu.
manuṣa, a man.
manuṣī, a woman
manuṣya, human, manly; human being, man.
manuṣvat, as a man.
manvantara (nt), age of a Manu.
mānava, descended from or belonging to Man or Manu.
mānavaya, to act like man.
mānavīya, descended or derived from Manu.
mānuṣya, human nature or condition.
- Monier William’s Sanskrit - English Dictionary : Man eṉṉum āṅkilac colliyum manu eṉṉum camaṟkirutac colliyum muṟiyē mind. mana eṉṉum coṟkaḷōṭu toṭarpu paṭutti thinking animal, thinking creature eṉṟu mēliyar cilar poruḷ kūṟuvar. atu tavaṟu.
iṭic cērppu
muku - mukku - iruteru kōṇampaṭak kūṭum mūkkup pōṉṟa mūli. mukkuvēṟu; muṭukku (cantu) vēṟu.
mukku - mukki = āṟu tirumpum allatu īrāṟu kūṭum iṭattu mukkup pōṉṟa mūli.
mukku - mūkku = 1. mukattiṉ muṉ nīṇṭuḷḷa uṟuppu. 2. paṟavi yalaku. 3. yāṉit tutikki. 4. kalaṅkaḷiṉ vāyil mūkkuppōl nīṇṭuḷḷa uṟuppu. 5. ili nuṉi. 6. koṇṭik kaṭalip payaṟṟiṉ muṉi, 7. vittiṉ muḷi. 8. vaṇṭippāriṉ mūkku vaṭivāṉa muṉippakuti.
ma. bj., tu. mūkku, ka. mūku (g).
mūkku - mukkaṉ = 1. eṭuppāṉa mūkkuḷḷavaṉ. 2. mīṉkotti.
makuṭam
makuṭam - makuṭa ( t|) mukuṭa ( t|)
mukam - mukaṭu = mūkkuppōṉṟa kūriyucci.
mukaṭu = 1. ucci. mukaṭu tumittaṭukkiya paḻampal luṇaviṉ (perumpāṇ. 246).
2. vīṭṭiṉ ucci.
3. vāṉamukaṭu. vāṉeṭu mukaṭṭi yuṟṟaṉaṉ (kamparā. maruttu. 30).
4. tali. mukaṭūr mayirkaṭinta ceykiyārum (tēvā. 936 : 10).
5. uyarvu. muṉimi mukaṭāya mūvā mutalvaṉ (cīvaka. 1609).
mukaṭu - (mukaṭam) - makuṭam = uccimayir muṭi, maṇimuṭi, tēr muṭi, oruporuṭ pala pāṭṭup potu muṭivu.
iṉi, mukiḻ - mukiḻam - mukuḷam - mukuṭam - makuṭam = moṭṭup pōṟ kūmpiya maṇimuṭi eṉṟumām.
māmoṭṭoṭintu ... māṉṟēr citiya  (pārata. nāṉkā nāḷ. 24).
pērā. parō. iccollit teṉcolleṉṟē kūṟutal kāṇka. (The Sanskrit Language, p. 381).  (va.va.)
mukuṭam piṟi vaṭiviṉateṉṟum, kirīṭam kuvintateṉṟum, muli mummuṉiyateṉṟum mā. vi. a. kūṟum.  (va.va: 212-213).
maṅkalam
maṅkalam - maṅkala
maṅku - maṅkal - mañcal = maṅkalāṉa niṟam. anniṟak kiḻaṅku (mañcaḷ). mañcal - mañcaḷ.
o.nō : poṅku - poñcu, iṅkē - iñcē (kocci).
maṅkal - maṅkalam = 1. mañcaḷāl allatu mañcaḷ nīrāl kuṟikkappeṟum naṉṉilimi.
maṅkala makaḷiroṭu māli cūṭṭi  (puṟam. 332)
2. tirumaṇam. maṅkala vāḻttup pāṭal (cilap.).
3. tirumaṇattāli.
maṟṟinal laṇikaḷ kāṇuṉ maṅkalaṅ kātta maṉṉō  (kamparā. urukkāṭṭu. 35).
4. maṅkala nikaḻcci. ciṟanta cīrtti maṇṇu maṅkalamum (tol. 1037).  (va.va.)
5. maṅkalac ciṉṉam.
eṇ maṅkalamum maṅkalam patiṉāṟum.
6. maṅkala vaḻakku (naṉ. 267).
7. naṉmi.
maṅkala meṉpa maṉimāṭci  (kuṟaḷ. 60)
maṅkalam - maṅkaḷam.  (va.va: 213)
maṅkalam - maṅkaḷam. vaṭavar - kāṭṭum mūlam. maṅk = cel, iyaṅku. pakuttaṟivuṭiyār kaṇṭu teḷika.  (ti.ma. 751).
maṅkala vaḻakku
oruvar tuṉpuṟum pōtu, avari āṟṟit tēṟṟuvatum avaroṭu tāmum cērntu tuṉpuṟuvatum, uyarntōr iyalpu. kaṇṇaṉṉa kēḷiri iḻappatum, eri pōṉṟa vaṟumiyāl vāṭuvatum peruntuṉpamām. attuṉpaṅkaḷi avaṟṟiṟkuriya collāṟ kuṟippiṉum avaṟṟāl tuṉpuṟuvōrkkut tuṉpam perukumeṉṟu karuti, iṉpamum naṉmiyum kuṟikkum coṟkaḷāl avaṟṟik kuṟittu vantaṉar paṇṭi mēlōr. itaṉi maṅkala vaḻak keṉpar ilakkaṇavāciriyar.
cettāṉit tuñciṉāṉ eṉṟum, cāvip perum piṟitu eṉṟum, cuṭukāṭṭi naṉkāṭu eṉṟum, tāliyaṟutalit tāli perukiṟṟu eṉṟum vaṟumiyi nirappu eṉṟum, vaṟumip pāṭṭi nal kūrtal eṉṟum kūṟuvatu maṅkala vaḻakkām.
tuñcutal tūṅkutal. nirappu niṟivu, nal kūrtal naṉmi mikutal. iṉi, cāvuk kētuvāṉa naccuyirikaḷiyum maṅkalac collāṟ kuṟippatu vaḻakkam. e.ṭu. nalla pāmpu.  (col. 112.)
maci
maci - maṣi
mattu - matti. mattittal = mattiṉāṟ kaṭital, kaṭintu kaḷi allatu kūḻākkutal.
matti - mati. matittal = mattiṉāṟ kaṭivatupōṟ kiviralāṟ kuḻittal.
kuḻantikkuc cōṟṟi matittu ūṭṭu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
mati - maci. macittal = kuḻittal. macital = kuḻital.
maci - macaku = vikkōṟ kariyōṭu viḷakkeṇṇey kalantu macitta (kuḻitta) vaṇṭi mi.
eḻutu miyum iṅṅaṉamē vēṟu poruḷi macittu amikkap peṟum.
macittu miyi viḷḷa veḻuti  (patiṉo. tiruvālaṅ. mūt. 2)
maci = eḻutumi. macikalan teḻutappaṭṭa (cūḷā. tūtu. 83).
macikkūṭu, macikkuppi eṉpaṉa ulaka vaḻakku.
maci - mayi - mi.
karuniṟattik kuṟikkum mi eṉṉum colvēṟu. māl - mā - mi.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlam maṣ = citi, cētappaṭuttu. mā. vi. a.
ic colliṉ kīḻ, “(Prob. invented to serve as the source of the words below)” eṉṟu kuṟittiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
maṣi eṉpatu mēliyāriya vaḻiyāy vaṭamoḻi peṟṟa teṉ col tiripukaḷuḷ oṉṟākum.
OE masc, E mash, mess, Dan mask, Sw. maska, Sc. mask, MLG mesch, MHG meisch, G misch.  (va.va. 213-214)
macir
macir - cmacru (i.vē.).
mi = karuniṟam. mi - mayir = kariya muṭi.
o.nō : ai - ayir, ti - tayir, pi - payir, vi - vayir.
mā. vi. a. “(of unknown derivation, but of. sÝman)”.
“sÝman, n. the body, Nir.; the mouth, L. (both meanings prob. invented to explin sÝmasÝa#na and sÝmasÝru)” eṉṟu kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va.214).
mañcu viraṭṭu
ēṟu taḻuval eṉṉum paṇṭi vaḻakkamē iṉṟu kaḷḷar maṟavariṭic callikkaṭṭu eṉṟum mañcu viraṭṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.  (ta.ti.5)
maṭam
maṭuttal = uṇṇutal. maṭu - maṭi = uṇavu. maṭittoḻil = camiyal toḻil. maṭiyaṉ = camippōṉ.
maṭippaḷḷi = camiyalaṟi allatu camiyal maṉi. maṭi nūl = camiyaṟkali nūl.
maṭu - maṭam = aṟavuṇavuccāli, tuṟaviyarum irappōrum uṇṭu taṅkum iṭam, camayac cārpāṉa tuṟaviyar viṭuti, tuṟaviyar kallūri.
tamiḻakattiṟ pērūrtoṟum maṭamuṇṭu. atil paṇṭāram paratēci eṉṉum āṇṭikaḷum irappōrum ayalūr eḷiyārum taṅkuvar. āṇṭikaḷ kūṭi maṭaṅkaṭṭiṉāṟpōl. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. āṇṭimaṭam eṉpatu peruvaḻakku.
celvap peṟṟōr, tamakkaṭaṅkātu tammiviṭṭup pirintu ceṉṟa makkaḷ pallāṇṭu kaḻittut tammūr maṭattiṟku vantu taṅkuvārāyiṉ, tam uṟavik kāṭṭikkoḷḷātē avarkku iravil nalluṇavaḷippatu vaḻakkam. ataṟku irāmaṭam ūṭṭutal eṉṟu peyar.
vaṭamoḻiyil maṭam eṉṉum teṉcol maṭa (mat%|ha) eṉṟu tiriyum. ataṟku,
“a hut, cottage, (esp.) the retired hut (or cell) of an ascetic (or student), ..... a cloister, college (esp. for young Brahmans), temple .....” eṉṟu mā. vi. a. poruḷ kūṟum. itaṟku maṭ (mat|h) eṉpati mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar. atu mūlamaṉṟu  eṉpati,
“(prob. invented for the word below) eṉṟu mā. vi. a. kūṟuvatiṉiṉṟu terintu koḷka. vaṭavar kūṟum mūlapporuḷ varumāṟu :-
tātu pāṭam - to dwell or to be intoxicated. 
vōpatēvar - to grind.  (va.va.)
maṭalēṟṟam
mutupaḻakkālattil, oru kaṭuṅkātalaṉ allatu kātaṟpittaṉ avaṉ kātaliyi maṇattaṟku avaḷ peṟṟōr iciyāviṭiṉ, avaḷip peṟutaṟku maṭalēṟṟam eṉṉum uyirccētattiṟ kiṭa māṉa oru vaṉmuṟiyik kiyāḷvatuṇṭu. atu ikkālattuc cattiyākkirakameṉṉum pāṭu kiṭappup pōṉṟatu.
maṭalēṟat tuṇinta kātalaṉ, nīrccēli oṉṟēyuṭuttu uṭam pelāñ cāmpaṟ pūci erukkamāli yaṇintu, taṉ kātaliyiṉ ūr naṭuvē tavaniliyilamarntu, avaḷ uruvi varinta oru tuṇiyik kiyilēnti, ati uṟṟu nōkkiya vaṇṇamāy vāḷāviruppaṉ. ataṉik kaṇṭa avvūrār nī āyvu (cōtaṉi) tarukiṉṟāyā? eṉakkēṭpar. avaṉ tarukiṉṟēṉ eṉiṉ, paṉaṅkarukku maṭṭiyāṟ ceyta oru poyk kutiri yiṉ mēl avaṉiyēṟṟit teru neṭuka iḻuttuc celvar. aṅṅaṉam iḻukkum pōtu, avaṉ uṭampiṟ karukkaṟuttuk kāyam paṭṭaviṭamellām veḷuttut tōṉṟiṉ avaṉukku avaṉ kātaliyi maṇamuṭittu vippar; allākkāl viyār.  (ta.ti. 8,9)
maṇṭalam
maṇṭalam - maṇṭala
mul - muṉ - muṉi = vil. mul - vil. o.nō : muḻuṅku - viḻuṅku.
mul - mur - muri. murital = vaḷital. muri - mūri = vaḷivu.
mur - muru - muruku = piṟipōl vaḷinta kātaṇi.
muru - muṟu = muṟuku. muṟukutal = vaḷital, tirukutal.
muṟuku - muṟukku = tirukal, tirukiya tiṉpaṇṭam. muṟukku - muṟukkam.
muṟu - muṟṟu - muṟṟuki = cūḻki.
muṟu - muṟi = vaḷivu, taṭavi (turn). muṟi - miṟi = vaḷivu.
muṟu - muṟi. muṟital = vaḷital. muṟi - maṟi. maṟital = maṭaṅkutal.
mul - (muḷ) - muṇṭu = uruṭci, tiraṭci, muṇṭu - muṇṭi = uruṇṭi, muṭṭi. muṇṭi viḷipaḻam (patiṟ. 60:6).
muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭi. muṭṭu - muṭṭāṉ - tirunīṟṟuruṇṭi.  (va.va.)
muḷ - (muṇ) - muṇam - muṇaṅku. muṇaṅkutal = uḷvaḷital.
muṇam - muṭam = vaḷivu, kāl vaḷivu. muṭam - muṭavu - muṭavaṉ.
muṭam - muṭaṅku, muṭaṅkutal = vaḷital. muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku. muṭakku - maṭakku. muṭakkam - maṭakkam.
muṭam - muṭanti = vaḷintatu. muṭam - (muṭal) - muṭali = kuṟaṭu, uruṇṭi.
muṭi - muṭiccu = vaḷittuk kaṭṭiyatu, marat tirukal. muṇṭum muṭiccum eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = vaḷital. maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭi yiṭal = muḻaṅkāli maṭakkutal.
maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam, nāṭṭuppakuti, kālappakuti, nūṟpakuti, vaṭṭavaṭivam, vaṭṭamāyc cuṟṟivaruki, vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ.  (va.va.)
koṅku maṇṭalam, toṇṭi maṇṭalam eṉpaṉa toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakku.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali. maṇṭalittal = vaṭṭamātal, vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟivarutal, īṟu toṭaṅkiyil (antātiyil) īṟum mutalum oṉṟittu varutal.
maṇṭali = vaṭṭamāṉa poṟikaḷuḷḷa pāmpu.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam = 1. vaṭṭam. 2. vaṭṭamāṉa iṭam.
parippuc cūḻnta maṇṭilamum  (puṟam. 30)
3.	vaṭṭamāṉa katiravaṉ. pakalcey maṇṭilam  (perumpāṇ. 442).
4.	vaṭṭamāṉa tiṅkaḷ. ceyvuṟu maṇṭilam  (kalit. 7).
5.	vaṭṭamāṉa kaṇṇāṭi. miyaṟu maṇṭilam (maṇi. 25. 137).  (va.va.).
6.	vaṭṭamāṉa ñālam. kaṭalcūḻ maṇṭilam (kuṟun. 300).
7. 	ñālappakutiyāṉa nāṭu, nāṭṭuppakuti.
maṇṭilat tarumiyum  (tol. po. 41).
8. 	kūttiṉ maṇṭila nili.
9. 	vaṭṭamāyōṭuki.
celavoṭu maṇṭilañ ceṉṟu  (pu.ve.12, veṉṟip. 14).
	10.	vaṭṭamāyōṭuṅ kutiri (piṅ.). maṇṭilaṅ koṭpu (iṉṉā. 35).
	11. 	aḷavaṭi. maṇṭila yāppum (tol. 1372).
12.	ceyyuḷiṉ ellāvaṭiyum aḷavottu varutal.
e.ṭu. nili maṇṭila vāciriyam, maṇṭilam (viruttam).
maṇ - maṇi = nāḻiki vaṭṭil, maṇiyaṭikkum vaṭṭamāṉa veṇkalat taṭṭu, maṇiyeṉṉuṅ kāla aḷavu, uruṇṭi, uruṇṭiyāṉa viti.
ic coṟku vaṭamoḻiyil ittakiya varalāṟumilli; mūlamumilli.  (va.va. 214-216)
maṇa nāḷaṉṟē maṇamakkaḷik kūṭṭutal
cila peṟṟōr, maṇattiṉ nōkkattiyē uṇarātu nīṇṭa kāla māka iṉpantuyttu veṟutta taṅkaḷip pōṉṟē iḷa maṇa makkaḷiyuṅ karutik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. kaḷa voḻukka iṉpam vāykkāta maṇamakkaṭkellām maṇanāḷiṉpamē ciṟantatu eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum. mēlum maṇanāḷiṟ kūṭāmiyāl, oḻukkakkēṭu, aiyuṟavu, matiyāmi, aṉpiṉmi, nīṭukūṭi vāḻāmi mutaliyaṉa irucārum ēṟpaṭutaṟkiṭamām.  (ta.ti.59).
maṇamakkaḷ
kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyumāka oruvarukkoruvar vāḻkkit tuṇivarāy illaṟam toṭaṅkum iruvarum, tirumaṇattaṉṟu maṇamakkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. avviruvariyum pirittuk kūṟuṅkāl, maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷ eṉṟum, maṇavāḷaṉ maṇavāṭṭi eṉṟum, peṇ māppiḷḷi (maṇavāḷappiḷḷi) eṉṟum, peṇ piḷḷi eṉṟum, kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. maṇamakaṉip piḷḷi eṉpatu vaṭārkkāṭu vaḻakku.  (ta.ti.muṉ. vi).
kulaveṟi mella mellac cuṟṟaveṟiyi uṇṭu paṇṇiṟṟu oru muṟi kārap peṇṇukku allatu āṇukku, eṅkē vēṟiṭattil maṇam muṭintuviṭukiṉṟatō eṉṉum accattiṉāl, piḷḷip paruvattilēyē avarkku maṇam ceytu vippatu vaḻakkamāy viṭṭatu. cilar toṭṭiṟ kuḻantikkum maṇam ceytu vittatuṇṭu. atu toṭṭil maṇam eṉappaṭṭatu. orutti kaṉṉiyāy iṟantāl tīkkatiyaṭivāḷ eṉṉum āriyak koḷkiyum, iḷami maṇattiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.  (ta.ti.37).
maṇāṭṭuppeṇ
maṇāṭṭup peṇ eṉpatu muṟiyē māṭṭuppeṇ nāṭṭup peṇ eṉa maruvi, marumakaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil, tañci vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.  (ta.ti. muṉ. vi).
maṇi (1)
maṇi - maṇi (i.vē.)
maṇṇutal = kaḻuvutal; maṇ - maṇṇi - maṇi = kaḻuvap peṟṟa oḷikkal. maṇṇi yaṟipa maṇinalam (nāṉ maṇi. 5).
vaṭamoḻiyil iccol akaramutalikaḷil maṭṭum uḷateṉṟu mā. vi. a. kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va. 216).
maṇi : maṇṇutal = kaḻuvutal. maṇ - maṇṇi - maṇi = kaḻuvappeṟṟa oḷikkal.
maṇṇi yaṟipa maṇi nalam  (nāṉmaṇi. 5)
maṇi - maṇi.
maṇi (2)
maṇi - māṇi = 1. kuṟaḷvaṭivam. (tiv. periyāḻ. 1 : 4 : 1, vā.).
2. ciṟuvaṉ.
3. māṇavaṉ.
karumāṇiyā yiranta kaḷvaṉē  (tiv. iyaṟ. 2:61)
māṇi - māṇ = 1. kaṟkuñ ciṟuvaṉ. māṇāki viya maḷantatuvum (tiv. periyati. 8:10:8).
2. kuṟaḷaṉ. kuṟumāṇoruvaṉ taṟkuṟiyākak koṇṭāṭum (tēvā. 164:5).
māṇi - māṇavaṉ. o.nō : paḷḷippiḷḷi, E. pupil.
iṉi, maṇ - māṇ - māṇi eṉṟumām.
vaṭamoḻiyilum māṇava eṉṉuñ coṟku iḷiñaṉ, kuṟaḷaṉ eṉṉum poruḷkaḷē uḷa.
māṇavaṉ - māṇavakaṉ - māṇākkaṉ.  (va.va. 228).
maṇi vaki
kūlam nel pul (kampu) mutaliya tāṉiyam; payaṟu avari uḻuntu mutali yavi; kaṭali vērkkaṭali koṇṭikkaṭali; mutaliyavi viti kattari miḷakāy mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vittu; kāḻ puḷi kāñciri mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vittu; koṭṭi mā paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vittu; tēṅkāy teṉṉiyiṉ vittu.
avari tuvari mutaliya payaṟukaḷ mutiri eṉṟu peyar peṟum. 
vittu eṉpatu muḷikka vikkum ellā maṇikaṭkum
potup peyarām. (kaṭaliyum payaṟṟil oruvakiyē; kaṭiyap paṭuvatu kaṭali; vaṟuttalum kaṭitalokkum). (col: 70).
mattam (i.vē.)
mattam - matta
matam - mata (i.vē.)
matu - matu (dh) - i.vē.
muttutal = poruntutal, cērtal, kalattal.

mattam (2)

mattaḷam

mottutal = vīṅkutal, paruttal. mottam = parumaṉ, muḻumi, potu, kūṭṭuttoki. mottu = uṭaltaṭippu, matittaṭippu, maṭami.

mattaḷikaṉ

martalika eṉṉum vaṭivam mā. vi. a. vil illi. ceṉṉip pa.ka.ka.ta.a. vil maṭṭum uḷatu.  (va.va.217).
mataṅkam
mataṅkam - mrutaṅka
mataṅkam eṉpatu mattaḷattiṉ ciṟumi. iṭakkaṇṇiyum valakkaṇṇi yum toṭarntu taṭṭuvatāl ēṟpaṭum oliyiṉiṉṟu ippeyar peṟṟatu.
mataṅkamoṭu tuntupi ... muḻaṅkavē  (tiruvāta. pu. kaṭavuḷ. 1).
mrutaṅka eṉṉum col piṉvarumāṟu iruvakiyuḷ oṉṟil tōṉṟiyirukkalāmeṉṟu mā. vi. a. kūṟum.
(1) mrutam + ka = aṭikkumpōtu celvatu (“going about while being beaten”)
ipporuḷil ka (ga) eṉpatu kam (gam) eṉpataṉ kaṭik kuṟi.
(2) martala - mrutaṅka.
āriyar varumuṉpē tamiḻ muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉpatum mataṅkam āṭṭiṟkum pāṭṭiṟkum uriya icittamiḻk karuvi yeṉpatum, aṟika.  (va.va.217).
mataccīrtiruttam
1. tirukkōvil tamiḻ vaḻipāṭu
kiṟittavam ki.pi. 3 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu urōma nakarattiṉiṉṟu pirit tāṉukkuṭ pukuttappaṭṭatu. atip pukuttiyavar maṟimoḻi ilattīṉamā yiruntataṉāl, kōvil vaḻipāṭu am moḻiyilē yē naṭipeṟṟu vantatu. āyiṉ, 16 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, mārṭṭiṉ luttar eṉṉum cerumāṉiyar, pōppāṇṭavariṉ atikāratti yetirttup peruṅ kiḷarcci ceytu cīrtirut tatti yēṟpaṭuttiya piṉ, ellā nāṭukaḷilu muḷḷa cīrtiruttak kiṟittavak kōvilkaḷil, avvan nāṭṭut tāymoḻiyilēyē vaḻipāṭu naṭantu varukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam, airōppiyarum āṅki larum, tam urimi yuṇarcciyāl, ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu ayaṉ moḻi vāyilāka vantamatattiyum, tam tāymoḻi matamāka māṟṟik koṇṭaṉar.
tamiḻarō, tamiḻ nāṭṭil tamiḻarāl tōṟṟuvikkap paṭṭa mata vaḻipāṭṭi, orupōtum, vaḻakkil illāta, oruvarkkum teriyāta ayal nāṭṭu ilakkiya moḻiyil naṭipeṟuvittu, ceviṭar pōl niṉṟu mīḷkiṉṟaṉar.
civaṉiyamum māliyamum tūya tamiḻa mataṅkaḷā yiruntum, aṉpāṉa tantiyuṭaṉ avaṉ arumi makkaḷ neñcu kalantu nēraṭiyāyp pēci iṉpuṟa muṭiyāvāṟu, iṭiyil ōr ayalāṉ niṉṟu taṭuttu, avarkaḷ koṇṭu vanta tiṉpaṇṭaṅ kaḷiyum tāṉē vāṅkik koṭuttu, avarkaḷ tantiyip pōṟṟip pukaḻvatu pōlum, tanti avarkaḷi vāḻttuvatu pōlum, tāṉē maṉappāṭañ ceytu koṇṭaṉavum avar kaṭkup poruḷ teriyātaṉavumāṉa cila toṭarkaḷic colli, ataṉpiṉ avarkaḷē vilakik koḷḷumāṟu ceyvatupōl, kōvililuḷḷa paramat tirut tantiyum aruṭ kaṭalumāṉa iṟivaṉuruviṟku, tamiḻar tāmē tam aṉpārnta neñcu kaṉinta vaṇakkattit tam tāymoḻiyil terivittu vaḻipaṭṭup paṭittu, pēriṉpap peru makiḻcci yip peṟa muṭiyāvāṟu, vaṭanāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu vanta pirāmaṇaṉ iṭiniṉṟu, paṭipput tēṅkāyi vāṅkit tāṉē yuṭittu, tamiḻarkkut teriyāta, taṉakkun teḷivāka viḷaṅkāta, aric ceyaṟki ilakkiya naṭimoḻiyākiya camaṟkirut til tāṉ uruppōṭṭatic colli, añcalakattilum vippakattilum (Bank) variyakattilum paṇañ celuttiyavar tirumpuvatu pōl vaḻipaṭṭavarit tirumpac ceyvatu, ettuṇi ēmāṟṟum tuṇiccalumāṉa tīc ceyalām! āṅkilap paṭṭak kalviyarum aṟiviyal ārāycciyāḷarum itaṟku iṇaṅki yiruppatu, ettuṇi iḻi takavāṉa aṭimit taṉamām!
tamiḻar iruvakic caṭaṅkukaḷiyum kōvil vaḻi pāṭṭiyum tamiḻilēyē naṭipeṟac ceytal vēṇṭum.
potuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷil, avit talivar ‘ammiyappaṉ tiruvaṭipōṟṟi! eṉṟē olikka. aviyiṉar ‘arakara (aravara) mātēvā! eṉṟu ārpparikka.
2. pal vakuppuk kōviṟ pūcakar
potuk kalvi, ciṟappuk kalvi, tamiḻp paṟṟu, teyva nampikki, pulāṉ maṟuttal, tuppuravu, oḻukkam ākiya eḻuvakit takutiyum uḷḷavari, ellā vakuppi ṉiṉṟum pūcakarākat terinteṭuttup paṇi yāṟṟuvittal vēṇṭum. itaṟku māṟā yiruppavari aracu taṇṭittalum vēṇṭum.
3. vaḻipāṭṭil vīṇ celavu niṟuttam
kōvilkaḷiṟ paṭikkappaṭum uṇavup poruṭkaḷ, vaḻipaṭu vārkkum pūcakarkkum irappōrkkum ēḻikaṭkum payaṉpaṭum. āviṉ, pāl muḻukku, neyyāṭṭup pōṉṟavi oruvarkkum payaṉpaṭā. iṟivaṉukkum ēṟkātu. makkaṭ ṭokip perukka mum vēli yillāt tiṇṭāṭṭamum poruḷ vili yuyarvum vaṟumiyum vaṟaṭciyum mikka ikkālattil, ēḻi makkaḷ kuṭikkak kūḻumiṉṟip paciyum paṭṭiṉiyumāyk kiṭantu varuntu kiyilum, taṟkoli ceytu varukiyilum, uyiraṟṟa ōruruvat tiṉ mēṟ kuṭaṅ kuṭamāyp pālik koṭṭupavaṉi, iṟivaṉ aṭiyāṉ eṉṉātu koṭiyāṉ eṉṟē karutuvaṉ. iṟi vaṉukku eṇṇillā ellā vulakum eṉṟum contam. avaṉukku oru poruḷum tēvi yilli. avaṉē ellārkkum ellāvaṟṟiyum aḷippavaṉ. avaṉ virumpuvatu maṉam oṉṟē. ati muḻumiyum aḷikka muṭiyātavar, pūcakarkkum ēḻi kaṭkum palvakip paṇi ceyyum potu makkaṭkum payaṉ paṭumāṟu, tat tamakku iyaṉṟavaḷavu palvakip poruḷkaḷik kāṇikki yākap paṭikkalām.
4. uruvilā vaḻipāṭu
tuṟavaṟattārkku maṭṭumaṉṟi, aṟivu viḷakkam peṟṟa illaṟat tārkkum uruvilā vaḻipāṭē ukantatām. uruva vaḻipāṭṭi ṉāl, iṟivaṉiṉ eṅkum niṟinta taṉmi uṇarappaṭātu pōm. atōṭu, avaṉatu aḷaviṭappaṭāta perumikkum atu iḻukkākum.
ulakiluḷḷa uyiriṉaṅkaḷuḷ uyarntatu māntaṉiṉam. ātalāl, taliciṟanta vakiyilum, māntaṉ vaṭivil tāṉ iṟivaṉik kāṭṭa muṭiyum. atuvē avaṉ māṭcimikku iḻukkā yirukka, pakuttaṟi villā yāṉi vaṭiviṟ paṭivamamittu vaṇaṅkuvatu ettuṇip paḻippākum! oruvar paṭattit tavaṟāka variyiṉ, avarukku ettuṇic ciṉam eḻukiṉṟatu! māntaṉukkē aṅṅaṉa māyiṉ iṟivaṉukku eṅṅaṉamirukkum!
kaciṉi makamatu kaṟ paṭimikaḷi yuṭittup poṟ paṭimikaḷi urukkuvāṉ; caṇpakak kaṇ nampi ceppup paṭimiyit tiruṭik kāṭu mēṭāy iḻuttuc ceṉṟu viṟpāṉ, paṭimi oṉṟuñ ceyyātu.
viḷḷuvamō cīrāci vīṭuviṭṭuk kāṭutaṉil
naḷḷiruḷiṟ ceṇpakakkaṇ nampiyāṉ - meḷḷavē
āṭeṭukkuṅ kaḷvarippō lañcā temikkaricaṟ
kāṭutoṟu mēyiḻuttak kāl.
oruvaṉatu teyvap paṭimiyē avaṉ vīṭṭup paṭikkallāka vantu amiyiṉum amiyalām.
illaṟa makkaṭku uruva vaḻipāṭu iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉpar cilar. kiṟittavarum makamatiyarum uruvilā vaḻipāṭṭiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkutal kāṇka.
tuṟavaṟam uyarnta aṟivu neṟi yātalāl paṭṭiṉattārum tāyumāṉavarum pōṉṟa uṇmit tuṟaviya rellām, uruva vaḻipāṭṭi aṟiyāmic ceyalākavē karutiṉar.
iṟivaṉukku neñcak kōyilē iṉiya teṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvarum tirumūlarum kaṇṭaṉar.
malarmici yēkiṉāṉ māṇaṭi cērntār
nilamici nīṭuvāḻ vār  (kuṟaḷ. 3)
uṭampiṉi muṉṉam iḻukkeṉ ṟiruntēṉ
uṭampiṉuk kuḷḷē yuṟuporuḷ kaṇṭēṉ
uṭampuḷē yuttamaṉ kōyilkoṇ ṭāṉeṉ
ṟuṭampiṉi yāṉirun tōmpukiṉ ṟēṉē.  (tiruman. 705)
paṭamāṭak kōyil pakavaṟkoṉ ṟīyil
naṭamāṭak kōyil namparkkaṅ kākā
naṭamāṭak kōyil namparkkoṉ ṟīyil
paṭamāṭak kōyil pakavaṟka tāmē  ( 1821)
matatti yoḻittu viṭṭāṟ pirāmaṇiyam aṭiyōṭoḻiyu meṉṟu karuti, kaṭavuḷilli yeṉṟu cilar colli varukiṉṟaṉar. uruva vaṇakkatti oḻittu viṭṭālē pirāmaṇiyam oḻintu pōmeṉṟu uṟutiyāyc collalām.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil uruvilā vaḻipāṭu patiṉeṇ cittar kālattil tōṉṟiya teṉṟum, kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum, tavaṟākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ulakil mutaṉ mutal kaṭavuḷi uṇarntavar tamiḻaṟivarē. kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum col, kumari nāṭṭil talik kaḻakak kālattilēyē tōṉṟiya tākum. muntu nūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa eṇṇip pulan tokukkappaṭṭa tolkāppiyattil, kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ col āḷappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
āriyar vantapiṉ, kaṭavuṭ camayam maṟikkap paṭṭatu; kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum colliṉ poruḷum kuṟikkap paṭṭatu.
5.  intu matam eṉṉātu teṉmatam eṉal
1.	intu eṉpatu vaṭanāṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟiṉ peyarāṉa cintu eṉpataṉ tiripu.
	cintu = ōr āṟu, atu pāyum nāṭu. cintu eṉṉum peyar pāracīkattil hintu eṉat tirintatu. piṟkālattil atiṉiṉṟu intiyā eṉṉum peyar kirēkkattil tōṉṟiṟṟu.
	vēta āriyar mutaṉ mutal cintu veḷiyil taṅki yiruntataṉāl, hintu eṉpatu nāḷaṭivil nāvalan tēyam muḻumikkum peyarāyiṟṟu.
	āriyar tam vēḷvi matattoṭu tamiḻa mataṅkaḷiyum iṇittu, ak kalaviyit tamateṉṟu kāṭṭi, intiyā muḻutum paravi intiyar ellārum ak kalavi matattit taḻuvumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭataṉāl, hintu eṉṉum tēcap peyar, at tēca makkaḷiyum am makkaḷ matattiyum kuṟikkalāyiṟṟu. atu tamiḻil intu eṉat tirintatu.
2. 	intu matam eṉpatu, oru taṉi matamaṉṟi āriyamum tamiḻamuñ cērnta kalavi matam.
3. 	intu matat teyvaṅkaḷāṉa muttiru mēṉikaḷuḷ, paṭipput toḻilaṉāṉa nāṉ mukaṉ (piramaṉ) eṉṉum āriyat teyvatti, tamiḻar toṭakkan toṭṭu oppuk koḷḷa villi. avaṉ civaṉiyē ēmāṟṟiṉa paṭumōcap poyyaṉ eṉṟum, ōṅkārap poruḷ teriyātu murukaṉāṟ kuṭṭup paṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum, katikaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
4.	civaṉ eṉṟum tirumāl eṉṟum iruvēṟu peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum orē iṟivaṉ, kāpput teyva meṉṟum aḻipput teyva meṉṟum iru vēṟu teyvaṅkaḷākak kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷāṉ.
5.	mut toḻil iṟivaṉāṉa civaṉ allatu tirumāl, orē toḻil talivaṉākak kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷāṉ.
6.	orē iṟivaṉi iru vēṟu teyvamākak kāṭṭiyatāl, civaṉ periyavaṉā, tirumāl periyavaṉā eṉṉum matap pōr mūṇṭuḷḷatu.
7.	poyyum puraṭṭumāṉa pala katikaḷum koḷkikaḷum kūṟṟukkaḷum, āriyat toṉmaṅkaḷilum koṇ muṭipiyali lum meyp poruḷiyalilum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
8.	āriyaṉ nilat tēvaṉ eṉṟu uyarttap paṭṭum, tamiḻaṉ cūttiraṉ eṉṟu tāḻttap paṭṭum, uḷḷaṉar.
9. 	intu matattāl tamiḻap paṇpāṭu aḻikkap paṭukiṉṟatu.
10. intu matattāl tamiḻ vaḻipāṭu etirkkap paṭukiṉṟatu.
ātalāl, iṉimēl, civa neṟiyāṉ taṉṉic civaṉiyaṉ eṉṟum, tirumāl neṟiyāṉ taṉṉi māliyaṉ eṉṟumē, kuṟittal vēṇṭum. iru neṟikkum potumi kuṟikka virumpiṉ, teṉ matattāṉ allatu tamiḻa matattāṉ eṉṟu kuṟittal vēṇṭum.
intu uyarnilip paḷḷiyeṉṟum, intuk kallūri yeṉṟum, tamiḻ nāṭeṅkum irukkum kalvi niliyap peyarkaḷit teṉṉavar uyarnilip paḷḷi, teṉṉavar kallūri yeṉṟu māṟṟal vēṇṭum. mūtta tali muṟiyic cērnta periyōr  perumpālum aṭimiyarāyum pēṭiyarāyum iruttalāl, iḷantali muṟiyic cērnta māṇavarē peruṅ kiḷarcci ceytu im māṟṟattic ceytal vēṇṭum. intu eṉṉuñ col āriya aṭimit taṉattik kuṟittalāl, urimit tamiḻar accollāl tammik kuṟittal peru māṉakkēṭāṉa ceyalākum.
mutaṟkaṇ, centamiḻp pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉ tali nakarākiya tirunelvēliyil uḷḷa, tiraviyam tāyumāṉavar intuk kallūriyiṉ peyari, tiraviyam tāyumāṉavar teṉṉavar kallūriyeṉṟu nellit tamiḻa māṇavar māṟṟi, ēṉik kalvi niliya māṇavarkkum nalvaḻi kāṭṭuvārāka.
6. ellā matamum taṉittamiḻ pōṟṟal
kaṭavuḷ puṟak kaṇṇāṟ kāṇap paṭāmiyālum, matam maṟumiyi nōkkiya tātalālum, makkaḷ maṉappāṉmi palvēṟu vakippaṭṭiruppataṉālum, uḷnāṭṭu mata meṉṟum veḷi nāṭṭu matameṉṟum vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭātu karuttu vēṟu pāṭṭiṟku iṭan tantu, taṉit tamiḻip pōṟṟuvatiyē ellā matattārum kaṭippiṭittal vēṇṭum.
matatti aḻikka muṭiyumā?
kaṭavuḷ nampikki yillāta taṟperumi vēntarum, neṟi tappiya aṟiviya lārāycciyāḷarum, potuvuṭimik koḷkiyiṉarum, kaṭavuṭ koḷkiyi oḻikkat tammāl iyaṉṟavari muyaṉṟu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. mataviyal muṟṟum maṉattip poṟutta tātaliṉ, ati evarum aḻikka viyalātu cākkiya nāyaṉār pakivar pōṉṟu civap paṭimiyik kallālaṭittē, civaṉaṭiyā rāyiṉār eṉiṉ, vēṟu eṉṉa collavirukkiṉṟatu! 
mēlum, potuvuṭimi iṟivaṉuk kēṟṟatē. makka ḷellārkkum uṇa veṉiṉ, tanti makiḻattāṉē ceyvāṉ! aṅṅaṉamē paramat tantiyum.
irantum uyirvāḻtal vēṇṭiṉ parantu
keṭuka vulakiyaṟṟi yāṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 1062)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. kiṟittavar etir nōkkum āyiravāṇṭu aracāṭciyum potuvuṭimi vakiyil tāṉ iyalum.
kaṉavu kāṇātu uṟaṅkum nēram tavira, maṟṟellā nērattilum - iruppiṉum, naṭappiṉum, vēli ceyyiṉum uriyāṭiṉum uṇṇiṉum - iṟivaṉi niṉikkavum vaḻuttavum vēṇṭavum iyalu mātaliṉ, maṉam uḷḷavari matatti oruvarālum aḻikka muṭiyā teṉa aṟika.  (ta.ma.)
matam eṉṉum col varalāṟu
āṟu, neṟi, koḷki, matam, camayam eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟuṭ piṉṉiraṇṭum vaṭamoḻi yeṉṉum camaṟkirutattilum vaḻaṅkuvatālum, ammoḻi piṟamoḻiyi ṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷāta tēvamoḻi yeṉṉum nampikki iṭikkālat tamiḻaruḷḷattiṟ patikkap paṭṭiruntataṉālum, avviru collum vaṭacollē yeṉṟu iṉṟum karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
muttutal = 1. poruntutal allatu cērtal. tiru muttu āram = tirumakaḷ cēru māram; kaḻi pōy viṇ muttum eṉṟāṟpōla. (cīvaka. 504, nac. uri) 2. meṉmiyākat toṭutal. 3. muttamiṭutal. putalvar pūṅkaṇ mutti (puṟam. 41).
muttu - muṭṭu. muṭṭutal = 1. vaṉmiyākat toṭutal, mōtutal. tuḷḷit tūṇ muṭṭumāṅ kīḻ (nālaṭi. 94). 2. muṭṭit tāṅkutal. 3. muṭital. muṭṭaṭi yiṉṟik kuṟivu cīrttākiyum (tol. po. 435). 1. muṭṭu.
takaram meṉmiyiyum ṭakaram vaṉmiyiyum uṇarttum. o.nō: ottu - oṭṭu, kuttu-kuṭṭu. muṭṭu = tākku, poruttu, muṭivu.
muṭṭu - maṭṭu - muṭivu, aḷavu. muttu - mattu = aḷavu. mattu - matti - mati.
matittal = 1. aḷaviṭutal. maṇviḻintu vāḻnāṇ matiyāmi (tirikaṭu. 26). 2. karututal. āṭali matittāṉ (kantapu. tiruviḷi. 1). 3. poruṭ paṭuttutal. maṇṇāḷvāṉ matittumirēṉ (tiruvāca. 5:12).
mati = aḷaviṭappaṭṭa paṇṭam. e.ṭu. ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumati.
mati - matippu = 1. aḷaviṭuki. 2. poruṭ paṭuttuki, kaṇṇiyap paṭuttuki. umiyic cāla matippoḻinta vallamarar māṇṭār (tēvā. 1014 : 7). 3. karuttu. maṇṇula kāḷu matippi yoḻittē (piramōt. 8 : 99). tōrāyam. 5. kaṇṇiyam. te. matimpu.
mati - matam = 1. peritāka matippatu. 2. karuttu. 3. koḷki. neṟimuṟi. 5. camayam. tatta mataṅkaḷē yamivatāka. (tiruvāca. 4. 52). va. mata. (mata).
mata eṉṉum camaṟkirutac colli maṉ eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu tirippar. maṉ eṉpatiṉiṉṟu, māṉ, mā, māṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟakkumē yaṉṟi mati allatu matam eṉṉum col tōṉṟātu.
kaṭitalik kuṟikkum mati eṉṉum viṉic collum, mattu eṉṉum, vaṭiviṉiṉṟē tirintiruttali nōkkuka. muttu - mottu - mattu = mottiyāka virukkum kaṭikaruvi.
mattu - matti, mattittal = mattiṉāṟ kaṭital. matti - mati. matittal = 1. kaṭital. mantaraṅ koṭu matitta nāḷ cētupu. caṅkara. 20). 2. kaṭital pōl viralā laḻutti maciyac ceytal .. mati - maci. macital = kaṭintatu pōṟ kaḷiyātal. kuḻantikkuc cōṟṟi matittu ūṭṭu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
mattu eṉṉum peyari manta (mantha) eṉṟum, mattittal eṉṉum viṉiyi mat (math) eṉṟum, camaṟkirutattil tirittirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟiṟku vēr tamiḻilēyē yaṉṟi vaṭamoḻiyi lilli.
aḷattaṟ poruḷ kuṟitta mete eṉṉum āṅkila viṉic col kavaṉikkat takkatu. E mete (to measure), OE, OS metan ON meta, Goth. mitan, OHG mezzan.
E. meter = person or thing that measures. Metron (measure) eṉṉum kirēkkac col māttiri eṉpataṉ tiripu. mā - māttiram - māttiri - va. mātrā. māttal eṉṉum viṉi tamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
eḻuvaki matamē uṭaṉpaṭal maṟuttal
piṟartam matamēṟ koṇṭu kaḷivē
tāa ṉāṭṭit taṉātu niṟuppē
iruvar māṟukōḷ orutali tuṇivē
piṟarnūṟ kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭal ēṉip
piṟitoṭu paṭāaṉ taṉmataṅ koḷalē
eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāviṉiṉṟu (11), oru poruḷ allatu neṟimuṟi (principle) iṉṉavā ṟiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu matittuk koḷvatē matam eṉṟum, camayam matameṉṉum peyar peṟṟatum ivvakiyilēyē eṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
teyvam uṇmi yiṉmi, teyvattiṉ taṉmi, teyvattāl ēṟpaṭum naṉmi, teyvattiṟkum māntaṉukkum uḷḷa uṟavu, maṟumi yuṇmiyiṉmi, maṟumiyil irukkum vāḻvunili ākiyavaṟṟip paṟṟi iṉṉavāṟirukku meṉṟu oruvar matittuk koḷvatē, potuvāka matameṉṟu collap paṭuvatākum.
teyvamum maṟumiyum puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇappaṭāta poruḷkaḷātalālum, makkaḷiṉ maṉappāṉmi vevvēṟu vakippaṭṭiruppataṉālum, matamāṉatu, ovvoruvarum tattam aṟiviṟkum cūḻnilikkum ēṟṟavāṟu matittuk koḷvatākavē yuḷḷatu.
teyvam uṇṭeṉṟu koḷvatu pōṉṟē, illiyeṉṟu koḷvatum, matamākum. ākavē, nampu matam nampā matam eṉa matam iruvakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum pala tiṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āyiṉ, nampu matap pirivukaḷē mikap paṟpala tiṟattaṉavā yuḷḷaṉa.  (ta.ma. : va.va.)
matam tōṉṟiya vaki
1. accam
kāykaṉi kiḻaṅku mutaliya iyaṟki viḷiporuḷiyum vēṭṭiyāṭiya vilaṅku paṟavi yiṟicciyiyum uṇṭu vāḻntu, anākarika niliyilirunta muntiyal māntar; tīyum iṭiyum pōṉṟa pūta iyaṟkikkum, pāmpu pōlum naccuyirikkum, iṟantō rāvikaṭkum avaṟṟuḷ oru cāraṉavāṉa pēy kaṭkum, añciya accamē teyva vaṇakkatti allatu ciṟuteyva matatti mutaṟkaṇ tōṟṟuvittatu. kollun taṉmiyuḷḷa ellāvaṟṟiyum teyvamākak koṇṭu, avi tammik kollāvāṟu iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyumākiya uṇavup poruḷkaḷip paṭittum, irutiṇi yuyirikaḷiyum kāvu koṭuttum, vantaṉar.
nākarikam mutirntuḷḷa ikkālattum, immiyilum maṟumi yilum nērakkūṭiya tuṉpaṅkaṭku añcuvatē, palvēṟu mata voḻukkaṅkaṭkum perumpālum aṭippaṭik karaṇiyamā yirukkiṉṟatu.
accattiṉāṟ paṭippatellām tīmivilakkalik kuṟik kōḷākak koṇṭatu.
2. muṟkāppu
māntaṉ vāḻkki palvakit tuṉpam niṟintataṉāl, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tappuvataṟkut toṉṟu toṭṭup pātukāpput tēṭikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. uṭamikaḷiyum uyiriyum kāppataṟku muṉpu kāvaṟkāraṉum piṉpu aracaṉum ēṟpaṭṭaṉar. āyiṉ, makkaḷāl taṭukka muṭiyāta koḷḷi nōy, pañcam, kaṭuṅkāṟṟu, peruveḷḷam mutaliya tuṉpaṅkaḷit taṭuttaṟkum nīkkaṟkum, māntariṉattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa cila maṟivāṉa teyvaṅkaḷē āṟṟaluḷḷavi yeṉṟu karuti, aṉivarum avaṟṟi vaṇaṅkavum vaḻipaṭavum talippaṭṭaṉar. attuṉpaṅkaḷ at teyvaṅkaḷiṉ cīṟṟattāl nērvaṉa veṉṟum nampi, avaṟṟiṟku añciṉar. ātalāl, muṟkāppu accatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatākum. 
tīyum pāmpum pēyum eṉṟumuḷḷaṉa. koḷḷinōy, pañcam mutaliyaṉa orōvoru kālattu nikaḻvaṉa.
3. naṉṟiyaṟivu
palvēṟu tīṅkukaṭkum accaṅkaṭkum peritum iṭantarum kāriruḷip pōkki, uyirvāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavu tēṭavum iṉattāruṭaṉ uṟavāṭavum, pēraḷavāka utavum katiravaṉiyum ciṟṟaḷavāka utavum tiṅkaḷiyum; uṇṇak kāykaṉiyum taṅkat taṇṇiḻalum kuṭiyirukka uṟiyuḷum utavum palvakip paḻumaraṅkaḷiyum; iḷami mutal mutumi vari ellārkkum iṉṉuyirt tīmpāl utavum āviyum, iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavaṟṟiyum; teyvamākap pōṟṟiyatum vaṇaṅkiyatum naṉṟiyaṟivu paṟṟiyatākum. avvaki vaṇakkattik kuṟitta kālantoṟum toṭarntu ceyvatu, mēṉmēlum naṉmi peṟalik kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭatākum. vaṇakka mellām paṭippoṭu kūṭiyatē.
4. pārāṭṭu
iṉattik kākkap pakivaruṭaṉ pulipōṟ porutupaṭṭa, uyirīki (pirāṇattiyāki) yākiya taṟukaṇ maṟavaṉukkum, maḻi varuvittum, paḻukkak kāycciya poṉṉik kiyilēntiyum, tīyōric cāvittum, ciṉattāl ūri yerittum, uṭaṉ kaṭṭi yēṟiyum, kaṭuṅkaṟpik kātta pattiṉip peṇṭiṟkum; kal naṭṭi viḻā veṭuttatu pārāṭṭup paṟṟiya tākum.
teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyu maḻi.  (kuṟaḷ. 55)
eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟu, orucila nikaḻccikaḷiyēṉum cāṉṟākak koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
paṭṭavaṉ kallum pattiṉik kallum iṉattār allatu payaṉ peṟṟavar naṭṭuc ciṟappuc ceyvatu, naṉṟiyaṟiviyum orumaruṅku taḻuviyatē.
tīyāṉatu, kolluntaṉmiyāl accattiṟkum, iruḷ pōkkiyum uṇavucamikka vutaviyum koṭuvilaṅkukaḷi veruṭṭiyum kuḷirakaṟṟiyum naṉmi ceyvatāl naṉṟiyaṟiviṟkum, uriyatāyiṟṟu.
5. aṉpu
irutiṇip pakiyiyum aḻittum, uṇaviṟku vaḻivakuttum, naṭuniliyāka āṭci ceytum, kuṭikaḷi aravaṇittuk kātta aracaṉ iṟanta piṉ, avaṉukkup paṭimiyamittup paṭittu vaṇaṅkiyatu aṉpu paṟṟiyatākum. itiṉiṉṟē, viṇṇulaka vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) vaṇakkam tōṉṟiṟṟu.
6. karutukōḷ
mutaṟkālattil kuṟiñci nilattilēyē vāḻnta māntar, piṉṉar ēṉi nilaṅkaḷilum paraviyapiṉ, avvannilattiṟ kēṟpa ovvoru teyvan tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ, ovvoru perunilattiṟkum pērāṟṟiṟkum perun toḻilukkum perunaṉmip pēṟṟiṟkum, kātaṟpaṇpiṟkum, cātaltīṅkiṟkum ovvoru teyvam iruppatākak karutappaṭṭatu.
kaṇṇāṟkāṇum iyaṟkik kūṟukaḷum nikaḻccikaḷum tōṟṟaṅkaḷum āvikaḷum viṉikaḷumaṉṟi, maṉattālēyē paṭittuk koḷḷum teyvaṅ kaḷellām karutukōḷiṉ viḷivēyākum.
nīrūṭṭiyum nīrāṭuvittum uṇavu viḷittum palvaki yutavum āṟṟi naṉṟiyaṟivu paṟṟi vaṇaṅkuvatu vēṟu; ataṟkut taṉit teyvamuṇṭeṉṟu karutuvatu vēṟu.
7. aṟivuvaḷarcci
māntaṉ nākarikamaṭintu aṟivu vaḷarntu paṇpāṭuṟṟa piṉ, maṟumiyum kaṭavuḷuṇmiyum kaṇṭu, palvēṟu mataṅkaḷiyum camayaṅkaḷiyum vakuttatu aṟivu vaḷarcciyākum.
makkaḷ maṉappāṅkum aṟivuniliyum palvēṟu vakip paṭṭiruppatāl, mata camayaṅkaḷum palvēṟāyiṉa.
aṟivuvaḷarcci yeṉṉum peyarukku muraṇāka, palvēṟu mūṭap paḻakkaṅkaḷum koḷkikaḷum mataṅkaḷiṟ kalantirup patuṇmiyē. avi perumpālum paḻaṅkālat taṉmiyālum taṉṉalakkārariṉ cūḻcciyālum ēṟpaṭṭavi. ikkāla aṟivu nilikkēṟpa, avaṟṟi iyaṉṟavari nīkkik koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
matamum camayamum perumpālum nampikiyip poṟuttaṉa. (ta.ma.)
mati(1)
mati - mati
mati = 1. aḷavu, aḷavīṭu.
2. aḷantaṟiyum aṟivuk karaṇam.
matinuṭpam nūlō ṭuṭiyārkku  (kuṟaḷ. 636)
3. pakuttaṟivu. matiyili maṭaneñcē (tiruvāca. 5: 33).
4. aṟivu. matimi cālā maruṭki nāṉkē (tol. po. 255).
vaṭavar maṉ (karutu) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu matam, mati eṉṉum coṟkaḷit tirippar. maṉ eṉpatu vēṟuvaḻic collāyiruppa tōṭu tūyatamiḻccol eṉpatu,  (va.va.218)
mati (2)
mati : muttutal mella muṭṭutal. muṭṭu  maṭṭu = aḷavu. muttu  mattu = aḷavu. mattu  matti  mati. matittal = aḷaviṭutal. aḷantaṟital. mati = aḷantaṟiyum akakkaraṇam, aḷaviṭap paṭum poruḷ, pakuttaṟivu, aṟivu. mati - mati, miti (va.).

mēṟkāṭṭiya eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu potuvāka ar īṟu paṉmiyiyum ār īṟu uyarvup paṉmiyiyum uṇarttum eṉa aṟintu koḷka.
kumaraṉ eṉṉum teṉcol kum eṉṉum aṭippiṟantu. kūṭṭattiṟkut takuntavaṉ allatu tiraṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil iḷiñaṉiyē kuṟittatu. itaṉ peṇpāl vaṭi vāṉa kumari eṉṉum collum itē poruḷil iḷiñiyik kuṟittatu.
kum - kummutal = kūṭutal. tiraḷutal. kum - kummal = kuviyal.
kum - kumi - kuvi - kuvi, kuvāl, kuvivu, kuvavu.
kumi - kumiyal - kuviyal, kummiruṭṭu = tiṇinta kāriruḷ.
kuvavuttōḷ = tiraṇṭatōḷ.
kum - kumpu - kumpal = kūṭṭam, kumputal = kūṭutal.
kumpu = kūṭṭam.
kum - kumar = tiraṇṭa iḷami, iḷami kaṉṉimi aḻiyāt taṉmi.
kumar - kumaraṉ, kumari.
kuṟiñcinilat teyvamāṉa murukaṉum pālinilat teyvamāṉa kāḷiyum, eṉṟum iḷamiyar eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē muṟiyē kumaraṉ, kumari eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kāḷiyiṉ peyarālēyē, mūḻkip pōṉa paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭut teṉkōṭi maliyum vaṭakōṭi yāṟum kumariyeṉap peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. kumari maliyiṉ peyarālēyē, mūḻkip pōṉa teṉkaṇṭattik kumarikkaṇṭam eṉkiṉṟōm. kumarimali yirunta kālam, āriyar eṉṟa iṉamē tōṉṟāta toṉmutu paṇṭik kālamākum.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tamiḻaṉ toṉmiyi muṟṟum maṟittut tamiḻi vaṭamoḻiyiṉ vaḻiyatākak kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, piṉvarumāṟu kumaraṉ, kumariyeṉṉum iru coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭiviyum poruḷiyum tirittum mikuttum uḷḷaṉar.
kumaraṉ - kumāra = kuḻanti, piyaṉ, iḷiñaṉ, makaṉ (irukku vētam).
kumari = kumārī = ciṟumi, pattiliruntu paṉṉīrakavip paṭṭavaḷ, iḷiñi, makaḷ (atarvavētam).
iv viru coṟkaḷiyum āriyac collākak kāṭṭumāṟu mūlattiyum kīḻvarumāṟu tirittuḷḷaṉar.
ku + māra = eḷitāka iṟappatu.

kumaraṉ kumari eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaṭacolleṉṟu nīkkiviṭiṉ, vaṭa moḻiyāḷar kūṟṟi ottukkoṇṭatākavum, kumarimali mūḻkiyatu āriya varukikkup piṟpaṭṭatākavumē muṭital kāṇka.

eṉṉum tirukkuṟaṭkē eṭuttukkāṭṭām. orukāl, tirumati eṉpataṉ īṟṟi aṟivup pulaṉik kuṟikkum teṉcollākak karutik koṇṭaṉar pōlum! aṟivut tiṟaṉik kuṟikkum mati eṉṉum teṉcol vēṟu; peṇpāluṇarttum matī eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyīṟu vēṟu.

mati viḷakkam
colliyal (Etymology) moḻinūluḷ (Philology) aṭaṅku mēṉum, pala coṟkaḷ pala moḻikaṭkup potuvāyirutta lāṉum, oru moḻic col vevvēṟu vakiyil piṟa moḻic ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, moḻiyārāycci ceyyātār collārāycciyil iṟaṅkutal takātu. moḻiyārāyccikku moḻi nūlilakkiyak kalvi maṭṭumaṉṟi palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa aṟivum coṟṟokuti yaṟivum vēṇṭum.
poruṭpāṭunōkkic coṟkaḷi.
(1) oru poruṭ pala col (Synonyms).
(2) pala poruḷoru col (Homonyms).
eṉa iru vakiyākap pakuttaṉar paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkaṇiyar. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē, oru poruṭcol, pala poruṭ col eṉak kuṟukkiyum vaḻaṅkalām. oru poruṭ col eṉpatu orēyoru poruḷuḷḷa col eṉṟum poruḷpaṭiṉum oru poruṭ palacol eṉpatu orumikku ēṟkāmiyāṉum. iraṭṭuṟaṟ coṟkaḷ pala iṭamnōkkip poruḷ vēṟu pāṭuṇarttalāṉum, oru poruṭcol eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭṭiṟku iraṭṭuṟaṉmi iḻukkākāteṉka.
iṉi, pala poruṭ collum.
(1) oru vērp pala poruṭcol (Homonymy).
(2) pala vērp pala poruṭcol (Polysemy).
eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. kaṭu eṉṉum col. mikuti, vaṉmi, vali, virivu, kāram, koṭumi mutaliya pala poruḷi orē vērp poruḷaṭiyil uṇarttutalāl oru vērp pala poruṭ collum; māṇṭāṉ eṉṉum col, māṇ, māḷ eṉṉum iru vēṟu viṉippakutikaḷiṉ paṭarkki āṇpāl iṟanta kāla muṟṟātaliṉ pala vērp pala poruṭ collum; ākum.
oru poruṭ collum iṅṅaṉamē oru vēruṭimiyum pala vēruṭimiyumpaṟṟi iru tiṟappaṭum.
mati eṉṉum col, paruttal, kaṭital, koḻuttal, mataṅkoḷḷutal, mayakkam, kaḷ, yāṉi, nilā, iṉimi, karuvāppaṭṭi, aḷavu, matippu, aṟivu, aciccol mutaliya poruḷkaḷil oru vērp pala poruṭ collāṉa teṉ collum, peṇpāl aṭi moḻiyīṟāka variṉ, māṉ eṉṉum tam īṟṟiṉiṉṟu tirinta vaṭa collum ākum. iv viraṇṭiṟkum evvakit toṭarpumilli. ivaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu varumāṟu :
muttu - mattu - matti - mati. u - a (aḷḷit tiripu).
muttutal = cērtal. (cīvaka. 504). mukattoṭu mukam cērtal.
pala poruḷkaḷ allatu kūṟukaḷ oṉṟucēriṉ tiraṭci yuṇṭākumātalāl, cērtaṟ karuttil tiraṭcik karuttut tōṉṟum.
cērē tiraṭci  (tol. col. 363)
muttu - mutti = tiraṭci (cūṭā), cōṟṟuruṇṭi.
mutti - motti = parumaṉ, uruṇṭi.
motti - mōtti = parutta veḷḷāṭṭukkaṭā.
muttu - mottu - mottam = tiraṭci, muḻumi.
muttu = tiraṇṭa viti, maṇi, kiḷiñcilil viḷiyuṅ kal, koppuḷam.
muttu - muttam = periya muttu, ic col vaṭamoḻiyil mukta veṉṟu tiriyum. am perumip poruḷ īṟu.
muttu - mattu = tiraṇṭa tayir mutaliyaṉa kaṭikaruvi. mattu - mattam āymakaḷ mattam piṇitta kayiṟu (kalit. 110).
mattu - matti, mattittal = kaṭital.
matti - mati. matittal = kaṭital, viralāl allatu kiyāl nacukkip pacipōlākkutal.
mati - maci. macital = pacipōlātal (ta.vi.)
macittal = paci pōlākkutal (pi.vi.)
mattu, mattam eṉpaṉa manta (mantha) eṉṟum; matti, mati eṉpaṉa mat (math) eṉṟum, maci eṉpatu maṣ eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
mattam = erumikkaṭā, iccol vaṭamoḻiyil matta eṉat tiriyum. potuvāka, ōr iyaṅkutiṇi uyiriyiṉ uṭampu paruttalāl koḻuttalum mataṅkoḷḷutalum uṇṭākum.
matittal = 	1. paruttal, upputal.
			2. koḻuttal cerukkutal
matittetir tevvā (iraku. mīṭci 37)
			3. mataṅkoḷḷutal.
matitta kaḷiṟṟiṉiṉ  (kamparā. pañcacē. 56)
mati = matayāṉi. kōṇ matittiṭar kiṭantaṉa, (kamparā. nākapāca. 136)
mati - matam = mikuti. perumi, valimi, vaḷam, cerukku, matanīr.
matam - mataṉ = mikuti, valimi, cerukku.
mattam = matam, mattamā = yāṉi, mattam - matam eṉṟumām.
mattaṉ = koḻuttavaṉ. matta ṉirāvaṇaṉ kotittāṉ (irāmanā. uyut. 44).
mattam, mattaṉ eṉpaṉa matta eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. 
matam - matar. matarttal = mikutal, ceḻittal, koḻuttal, mataṅkoḷḷutal, cerukkutal.
pala poruḷkaḷ allatu vaḻikaḷ allatu karuttukkaḷ kalattalāl, kalakkamum mayakkamum uṇṭākum.
mattittal = kalattal, maruntu kalattal.
mattam = mayakkam. mattam - matam - mataṉ = kalakkam.
o.nō : kula - kulavu - kalavu, kalavutal = kalattal kulavutal = kūṭutal. kula - kala - kalaṅku - kalakku - kalakkam. muya - muyaṅku - muyakkam. muyaṅkutal = taḻuvutal, kalattal. muya - maya - mayaṅku - mayakku - mayakkam = kūṭṭam, kalakkam. mayattal = mayaṅkutal. mayantuḷēṉulaka vāḻkkiyi (aruṭpā. apayattiṟaṉ. 14).
mayakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu, uṇarviṉmi (mūrcci) veṟi, kōṭṭi
(pittiyam), kāmam ākiya karuttukkaḷ tōṉṟum.
mattam = mayakkam, kuṭiveṟi, kōṭṭi, ūmatti.
mattam - mattaṉ = matimayaṅkiyavaṉ, kōṭṭi piṭittavaṉ.
mattam - matam = kuṭiveṟi, kōṭṭi.
mattu = ūmatti, matattal = mayaṅkutal.
mattu, mattam, mattaṉ eṉṉum im muccollum vaṭamoḻiyil matta eṉat tiriyum; mata eṉpatu mada eṉat tiriyum.
cerukku. yāṉi matam ākiya iru karuttum mayakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākavuṅ koḷḷalām. o.nō: kaḷi-kaḷiṟu. kaḷittal = kaḷḷuṇṭu veṟittal, mayaṅkutal.
nilavoḷiyāl kōṭṭi yuṇṭākumeṉṟu paṇṭik kālattil kīḻ nāṭṭilum mēlnāṭṭilum oru karuttiruntatāl, tiṅkaḷukku mayakkan taruvatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟi oru peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu.
mattu - (matti) - mati: allatu matam - mati = tiṅkaḷ.
Luna (Latin) = tiṅkaḷ, Lunacy (Eng.) = kōṭṭi.
mati = mātam (matiyāl aḷakkappaṭum kāla aḷavu).
mati - mātam - māsa (va.).
matip peyariṉiṉṟē mātap peyar uṇṭākum. tiṅkaḷ = mati, mātam, (Moon-month). mati eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyil tiṅkaṭ poruḷ illi. iyalpākat takaram cakaramākat tiriyumēyaṉṟic cakaram takaramākat tiriyātu. ātalāl, mātam eṉpatu teṉ collātal teḷivu. āyiṉum, āriya mayakkāl tamiḻar pētuṟuvatu avar pētimiyē. ēṟkeṉavē tiṅkaṭ col matiyiyum oru kiḻamiyiyum kuṟittalāl, ati mēlumoru poruḷil vaḻaṅki mayakkutal vaḻuvākum.
mayakkan tarum kaḷ allatu tēṉ iṉittalāl, mayakkak karuttil matuk karuttum tōṉṟum.
mattu - maṭṭu = tēṉ, kaḷ.
mattu - matu - matur - maturi - maturam.
matu - matam = tēṉ. mataṅkamaḻ kōti (cīvaka. 2584).
matu = tēṉ, kaḷ, iṉimi, atimaturam.
matu - mati = atimaturam.
maṭṭu eṉpatu madya eṉṟum, matu eṉpatu madhu eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
iṉi, muttu eṉpataṉ valvaṭivāṉa muṭṭu eṉṉum col, oru poruḷ iṉṉoru poruḷi muṭṭutalāl aṟiyappaṭum iyakka muṭivi allatu vaḻi elliyi uṇarttutalāl, cērtal allatu poruntutaṟ karuttil elli allatu aḷavukkaruttum tōṉṟum. mukattāṟ poruntutal muttutalum taliyāṟ poruntutal muṭṭutalum ātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ, meṉmi vaṉmi kuṟikka, muṟiyē takara ṭakaram vantaṉaveṉa aṟika. iṅṅaṉamē muṭṭiyāl mutukil aṟital kuttutalum maṇṭiyil aṟital kuṭṭutalum ātal kāṇka. āyiṉum, mutaṟkālattil takarac collē ṭakaraccoṟ poruḷiyuṅ kuṟittatu.
muṭṭutal = muṭital.
muṭṭu - maṭṭu = muṭivu, elli, aḷavu, maṭṭu - maṭṭam.
(mattu) - (matti) - mati.
matittal = aḷaviṭutal, uḷḷattāl aḷaviṭutal, uyarvākak karututal, karuttākak koḷḷutal.
matippu = ēṟattāḻak kaṇippu (kaṇicam), uyarvu.
mati = matippu, aḷaviṭappaṭṭa paṇṭam (ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumati), aḷantaṟiyum pakuttaṟivu, aṟivuppulaṉ, mati - matam = koḷki camayam, nūṟkoḷki.
mati eṉṉum col muṉṉili paṭarkki yaciniliyāy varuṅkāl, aḷavu eṉṉuṅ karuttil atu pōtum eṉṟēṉum, aṟivu eṉṉum karuttil atu aṟivākum eṉṟēṉum poruḷpaṭum. paḻañcēra nāṭākiya kēraḷattil, mati eṉpatu pōtum eṉṉum poruḷil iṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
matam (camayam) eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil mata eṉa vaḻaṅkum. aḷavīṭu eṉṉum karuttoḻittu, itukāṟuṅ kūṟiya ellāp poruḷkaḷilum, mati eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpuṭiya mata, matam, mataṉ, matar mutaliya coṟkaḷ tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. muḻukip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattoṭu pala ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa paṇṭiyilakkiyattoṭu pala ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, maṟintupōṉamiyāl, pala coṟkōvikaḷiṉ, iṭiyilirunta aṇṭukaḷi iṉṟu kāṭṭamuṭiyavilli.
makaṉ eṉṉum col varumoḻiyākavum īṟākavum varuṅkāl māṉ eṉṟu maruvum.
e.ṭu. : marumakaṉ - marumāṉ, perumakaṉ - perumāṉ. iṅṅaṉamē tirumakaṉ eṉpatu tirumāṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
tiru eṉpati vaṭamoḻiyār srī eṉṟu tirittuk koṇṭatāl tirumāṉ eṉpatu srīmāṉ eṉat tirintatu. itaṉic cīmāṉ eṉa eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta col lākkiṉar tamiḻar. tiruvaraṅkam - srīraṅkam - cīraṅkam eṉṉum tiriviyum nōkkuka.
cīmāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl cīmāṭṭi yātalāl, srīmāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl srīmāṭṭi yum tirumāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl tirumāṭṭiyum ātal vēṇṭum. tiruvāṭṭi eṉpatu tiruvāḷaṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl marumakaṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl marumāṭṭi eṉṟum perumāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl perumāṭṭi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. kali marumāṭṭi (tiruviḷi, tirumaṇa 62). perumāṭṭi (tivā) perumāṉ - pirāṉ perumāṭṭi - pirāṭṭi ivi marūuc citivu.
vāṉ eṉṉum īṟṟiṉiṉṟu vat, vatī eṉṟu muṟiyē āṇpāṟkum peṇpāṟkum tirittuk koṇṭatu pōṉṟē, māṉ eṉṉum īṟṟiṉiṉṟum mat, matī eṉṟu tirittuk koṇṭaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ātalāl, peṇpāṟ peyarkkut tirumati eṉṟu aṭikoṭukkātu tirumāṭṭi eṉṟu koṭuttal vēṇṭum. srīmāṉ srīmatī eṉpaṉa vaṭa col vaṭivukaḷ. srīmatī eṉpatu eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collākum pōtu tirumati eṉṟākum. iv vaṭivi vilakka vēṇṭum.
maturi
maturi - maturā (dh). mati - matiri. o. nō kuti - kutiri.
mati - matiri - maturi = matikkulattavaṉāṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ mutal talinakar (teṉmaturi), kaṇṇaṉ āṇṭa vaṭamaturi, ap peyar peṟṟa kaṭikkaḻakap pāṇṭiyar talinakar (viki maturi).
civaperumāṉ tam caṭimuṭiyiluḷḷa tiṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu matuvip poḻinta iṭam (viki) maturi eṉappeṟṟatu eṉṟum, matu eṉṉum aracaṉ āṇṭatiṉāl matupura eṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭapeyar matrā (vaṭa maturi) eṉat tirintateṉṟum, kāraṇaṅkāṭṭuvar.  (va.va.230).
maturit tamiḻk kaḻakam
tamiḻmoḻiyum ilakkiyamum nākarikamum paṇpāṭum varalāṟum pōṟṟutal paṟṟi, varukiṉṟa puttāṇṭu vāḻttup pōl 1981 caṉuvarit toṭakkattil, pāṭalmikka kūṭaluṟṟa māṭa maturiyil paṉimali yeḻucciyum kumarimali muḻukkum kaṇṇetiril nikaḻntāṟpōl kāṭṭavirukkum tirippaṭap piṭippum.
ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟillic ciṟṟam palattumeṉ cintiyuḷḷum
uṟivā ṉuyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāyntavoṇ ṭīntamiḻ
eṉṟu māṇikkavācakar pāṭiya tamiḻ vaḷarkkuṅ kaḻakam oṉṟu niṟuvum tiṭṭamum, ulakam pārāṭṭumaḷavu taliciṟantaṉavākum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, kaṉavupōl tōṉṟi maṟiyum tirippaṭattiṉum, naṉavāka nilittu niṉṟu nāṭṭiṟku nalam payakkum kaḻaka niṟuvaṉam cālac ciṟanta teṉpatic colla vēṇṭuvatilli.
orē kaḻakam
paṇṭit tamiḻakam cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum mūvēntaka mākap pirintu, kumarimalimutaṟ paṉimali vari paraviyirunta tēṉum, pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum orē nāṭṭil, ovvoru kālat tilum orē kaḻakam iruntu vantatu. tamiḻulakeṅkaṇumuḷḷa talimip pulavarellām atiṟ kalantiruntaṉar.
taliyiṭi kaṭiyeṉa mukkaḻakam iruntatākac collap paṭiṉum, pāṇṭināṭṭilēyē pāṇṭiyarē kaḻakam niṟuvi vantatāl, uṇmiyil am mūṉṟum oṉṟē. kaṭalkōḷāṟ pāṇṭiyarāṭci iṭiyīṭupaṭṭatiṉālēyē, mūvēṟiṭattil mūvēṟu kālattil kaḻakam niṟuva nērntatu. iru kaṭalkōḷum nikaḻntirāviṭiṉ, talikkaḻakam eṉṉum oṉṟē iṟutivari toṭarntiruntirukkum.
paḻam pāṇṭināṭākiya kumarināṭu, tamiḻ tōṉṟiya nilamāta lālum, ayaṉmoḻi vāṭi immiyum vīcāta centamiḻ nāṭātalālum, pāṇṭiyarkkut tāymoḻip paṟṟut tatumpi vaḻintataṉālum, tamiḻmakaḷē kalimakaḷumāyiruntata ṉālum, aṟivi vaḷarttal aracaṉ kaṭamiyātalālum, pāṇṭiyarkkut tamiḻi vaḷarttup pōṟṟik kākkum kaḻakam niṟuvātiruttal iyalavilli.
17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta taḷavāy irakunāta cētupati yeṉṉum ciṟṟaracar paṉṉiru pulavarip pōṟṟik kākkavum, in nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattil pālavanattam vēḷ pāṇṭit turittēvar pulavar iruvarip pōṟṟik kākkavum nūṟṟu varkku mēṟpaṭṭavari āṭṭi viḻāviṟ kūṭṭavum āṟṟalarā yiruntirukkavum, pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉ intiyā aḷavu nilapparappuḷḷa kumarināṭṭi oru kuṭikkīḻ āṇṭa pāṇṭi yaṉukkut tamiḻppaṟṟum, pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉmarip pōṟṟik kākkum celvac ciṟappum iruntirāteṉṟu koḷvatu, ettuṇi iraṅkattakkatākum!
talikkaḻakattil akattiyarum tolkāppiyarum iruntatilli. talikkaḻakak kālam ki.mu. tōrā 10,000 - 5,000. akattiyar tamiḻakam vanta kālam ki.mu. tōrā. 1200. tolkāppiyar kālam ki.mu. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu tamiḻiṉ mutupaḻan toṉmiyālum, varalāṟṟaṟivum kālavārāycciyum iṉmiyiṉālum, mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟil muṉṉavariyum piṉṉavariyum orukālattavarāka mayakkiviṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl, kaḻakamē yiruntatilli yeṉpatu kāṭṭik koṭukkun taṉmiyittāṉ kāṭṭum.
nakara aṭippaṭiyilum, māvaṭṭa aṭippaṭiyilum, mānila aṭippaṭiyilum, nāṭṭu aṭippaṭiyilum, palvēṟu tamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷit tōṟṟiyiruppati aṟavē nīkkiviṭṭu, paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭut tamiḻk kaḻakam pōṉṟu orē ulakap potu māperun tamiḻk kaḻakam niṟuva vēṇṭum. ataṉ kiḷikaḷ palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷil irukkalām.
nila varampiṉmi
tamiḻar pala nāṭukaḷilum tīvukaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyiruppatāl, tamiḻulakam aṉittiyum taḻuviya tamiḻk kaḻakamāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
toki varampiṉmi
talikkaḻakap pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar; iṭik kaḻakap pulavar aimpattoṉpatiṉmar, kaṭikkaḻakap pulavar nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar. nilapparappiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu pulavar toki vēṟupaṭṭiruntatu. maṟṟappaṭi, civaṉiyap pulavar eḻuvar, tirumāliyap pulavar eḻuvar, camaṇap pulavar eḻuvar, puttap pulavar eḻuvar, āriya matap pulavar eḻuvar, ulakiyal matap pulavar eḻuvar, matamaṟṟa pulavar eḻuvar eṉṟō, vēṟu vakiyākavō pulavar toki amiyavilli. takutiyuṭiyavar aṉivarum iṭampeṟal vēṇṭum.
pulavar takuti
oru tamiḻppulavar kallūriyil māṇavarākap payiṉṟō taṉiyāka oru perum pulavariṭam kaṟṟō, palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ naṭatti varum tērveḻutit tēṟip pulavar paṭṭam peṟṟavarkkuḷ, nuvalāciriyarākavō (pōtakāciriyarākavō), nūlāciriyarāka vō, uriyāciriyarākavō, itaḻāciriyarākavō, ārāycciyā ḷarākavō pallāṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟi avaravar tuṟiyil atikāri yeṉa aṟiñarāl oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭavarē, tamiḻppulavar kaḻaka vuṟuppiṉarākat takkār.
ilakkaṇa aṟivum pulavar paṭṭamum iṉṟiyamiyāta aṭip paṭit takutiyākum. āṅkilattiṟ paṭṭam peṟṟavariyē oppuk koḷḷum muṟiyit tamiḻilum kaṭippiṭittal vēṇṭum. mēṭip pēccāḷarākavō itaḻāciriyarākavō iruntāl maṭṭum pōtātu. ilakkaṇa aṟiviṟkup palkalik kaḻakap pulavar paṭṭamē cāṉṟātal vēṇṭum. tērveḻutātu peṟum kaṇṇiyap (Honorary) paṭṭamum cāṉṟākātu.
variciyaṟital
pulamic cāṉṟāṉa palvēṟu tiṟaṅkaḷuḷ, kaṭikkaḻakac ceyyuṭkum paṉuvaṟkum uri variyum āṟṟal cālac ciṟantatu. nāvalar vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭārukkup piṉ aṭuttapaṭiyāka at tiṟamiyuṭiyār. mutuperum pulavar urivēntar auvi cu. turicāmippiḷḷiyē. avarit talivarākak koṇṭu pulavarit tērnteṭuppatu taliciṟanta vakiyākum.
pulamik kavarip pulami terital
pulamik kavarkkē pulaṉām - nalamikka
pūmpuṉa lūra potumakkaṭ kākātē
pāmpaṟi yumpāmpiṉ kāl.  (pā. 7)
avarukku aṭuttavar mutuperum pulavar vēṇukōpālap piḷḷiyē.
tamiḻppulavaruḷ, viyāpuri (Heretic School) vaḻiyiṉar eṉṟum, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ vaḻiyiṉar (Orthodox School) eṉṟum, iru muraṇpaṭṭa kūṭṭattār uḷar. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ pōl muṟṟum taṉittamiḻē vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum eṉpati evarum maṟukkalām. āyiṉ, uṇmiyāṉa varalāṟṟi oruvarum maṟukkavō maṟikkavō māṟṟavō oṇṇātu. ātalāl, camaṟkirutattiliruntu tamiḻ vantateṉṟum, aṭippaṭit tamiḻccoṟkaḷellām camaṟkirutameṉṟum, pāṇiṉīyattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭatu tolkāppiyam eṉṟum, cintuveḷi nākarikam āriyara teṉṟum, irukku vētattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatu tamiḻ eṉṟum, tamiḻar vaṭamoḻip parata cāttirattiṉiṉṟu naṭamum nāṭakamuṅ kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar eṉṟum, koccit tamiḻiyum centamiḻpōṟ koḷḷavēṇṭu meṉṟum, acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmiyeḻuttiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkut tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻi yeṉṟum, pirāmaṇar nilattēvar eṉṟum, tirukkōyil vaḻipāṭu vaṭamoḻiyilēyē naṭipeṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, intiyi etirttal kūṭāteṉṟum, tamiḻar veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantavar eṉṟum, piṟavāṟun tamiḻukku māṟākavum kēṭākavum col vārum eḻutuvārum ellām tamiḻkkaḻakattil iṭampeṟat takkārallar.
tamiḻi muḻuttūymiyākap pēcāviṭiṉum, iyaṉṟavari tūymiyākap pēca muyala vēṇṭum: tamiḻppeyarun tāṅka vēṇṭum.
ulakap potumi
iṉa kula mata iṭa kaṭci vēṟupāṭiṉṟit takutiyoṉṟē paṟṟi, ellāruñ cērtaṟkēṟṟa kaḻakamā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
iruppiṭam maturi.
naṭimuṟi
paltuṟippaṭṭa tamiḻārāycciyē tamiḻkkaḻakattiṉ talimip paṇiyāyiruttal vēṇṭum. paṇṭit tamiḻkkaḻakaṅkaḷum ceyta paṇi ituvē. avar tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷap paṭṭa maturi yeṉpa. avar tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapuram eṉpa. avar tamiḻārāyntatu uttara maturiyeṉpa, eṉṟē mukkaḻakap paṇiyumpaṟṟi mukkaḻaka varalāṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. ciṟivāṉ ... uyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāynta voṇṭīntamiḻ eṉṟē māṇikkavācakarum pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka.
ārāyccit takuti, matinuṭpam, paranta kalvi, naṭunilimi, añcāmi, taṉṉalamiṉmi, meyyaṟi yavā eṉṉum āṟām. ivvāṟum illār pōliyārāycciyār, poruḷīṭṭavē vallār.
ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉamum ik kaḻakamum oṉṟākavē yirukkalām.
urivēntar auvi cu. turicāmip piḷḷi maturiyilēyē yiruppatāl, aṭikkaṭi ceṉṟu kaḻaka ārāycciyi mēṟpārttu varalām.
nāṭāṭcit talivar (mutalamiccar) pāṇṭiyaṉpōl eṉṟum niṟuvaṉat talivarāyiruppār.
āṇṭutōṟum āṭṭiviḻāvum araṅkēṟṟamum nikaḻalām. araṅkēṟṟamum paricaḷippum paṇṭi nāṭpōṉṟē naṭantēṟal vēṇṭum. kaḻakattil araṅkēṟiya nūlē nāṭṭil ulavutalum nilavutalum vēṇṭum.
celavu poruḷ varuvāykaḷ
tamiḻnāṭṭaracu otukkīṭu. naṭuvaṇaracu otukkīṭu, tiraviṭanāṭukaḷiṉ otukkīṭu, peruñcelvar naṉkoṭi, tamiḻnāṭṭut tirumaṭaṅ kaḷiṉ tiruttāṉam, tiruppati vēṅkaṭavar tiruvīki, palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ paṇavutavi, oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṉ kalviyaṟiviyaṟ paṇpāṭṭuk kaḻaka (UNESCO) oppuravuttoki, palkalikkaḻaka nalkik kuḻu (U.G.C.) nalki mutaliyaṉa kaḻakac celaviṟkup poruḷtēṭum vaḻivakikaḷām.
kaḻakap peyar
mutaliru kaḻakaṅkaḷum kaḻakam, kūṭal, toki, avi, maṉṟam mutaliya coṟkaḷālēyē kuṟikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa. ak kālattil āriyam eṉṉum pērum tōṉṟavilli. caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭacol ki.pi. 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē camaṇaroṭu tamiḻakam pukuntiruttal vēṇṭum.
patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa parañcōti muṉivar, tam tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattil, kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu eṉṟu pāṭiyiruppatāl, iṉṟum kaḻakam eṉṉum colliyē āḷalām.
vaṭamoḻiyiṟ caṅkam eṉṉum col samka (samga) eṉṉum vaṭivupeṟum. ati samgha eṉṟum tirittu samhaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripākak kūṟuvar. samka eṉṉuñ coṟku uṭaṉcellutal allatu oṉṟucērtal, kūṭutal eṉpatē poruḷ.
kummutal = kūṭutal, kuvital, kum - kummal (kuviyal) - L. cumulus, heap. um - kum - kumpu - kumpal.
kummaliṭu - L. cumulate.
kum - L. cum - E. cum, com.
L. cum (kum), Gk. sum (sum), Skt. sam (sam) = uṭaṉ, kūṭa. E. sym.
ē(ēku) - yā - i. jā. fh(ga#); E. go, Skt. gam, to go.
Skt. gam - ga, samga = to go together, to unite.
Samgha = combination, collection, assembly, association, society.
itaṉāl, caṅkam (samka) eṉṉum vaṭacollum teṉcolliṉ tiripē eṉṟu terintukoḷka. ākavē, kaḻakam eṉṉum collē irumaṭip poruttamākum.  (ce.ce.)
mantiram
mantiram1 - mantra (i.vē.).
muṉṉutal = karututal. muṉ - maṉ. maṉ + tiram (tiṟam) = mantiram. eṇṇattiṉ tiṇmiyiṉāl. eṇṇiyavāṟu niṟivēṟum eṇṇam allatu kūṟṟu allatu pāṭṭu mantiramām.
eṇṇiya eṇṇiyāṅ keytupa eṇṇiyār
tiṇṇiya rākap peṟiṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 666)
mantiram = 1. eṇṇam (piṅ.).
2. cūḻviṉi 3. cūḻviṉiñar (mantirimār) avi.
maṉṉavaṉ ṟaṉakku nāyēṉ mantirat tuḷḷēṉ  (kamparā. urukkāṭ. 31)
3. koṇmuṭipu (cittānta) mantira nūl.
pōkamiku mantiramā maṟiyoṉṟu  (tirumuṟi kaṇ. 26)
4. cāvippuk kūṟṟu.  (va.va.)
mantiram - mantiri = aracaṉukkuc cūḻviṉit tuṇiyā yiruppavaṉ.
mantirakkāraṉ = cāvippu mantiram paṇṇupavaṉ (mantiravāti).
mantiram - mantiri. mantirittal = (ce. kuṉṟāviṉi) 1. mantirattāl aṭakkutal.
3. mantirattāl ūḻkutal (tiyāṉittal).
mantirippār maṉattuḷāṉi  (tēvā. 590 : 4)
4. kūṭiccūḻtal.
makaṭpēci mantirittu  (tiv. nāyc. 6,3).

cirattiṉañ cuṟṟapiṉṉi mīṇṭiṭa mantirippār  (pirapōta, 5,7)  (va.va.)
mantirap poruḷvayiṉ āakunavum  (tol. 932)
niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa.  (. 1434)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyamum,
niṟimoḻi māntar perumi nilattu
maṟimoḻi kāṭṭi viṭum  (kuṟaḷ. 28)
eṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷuṅ kūṟuvatāl, tirumantiram pōṉṟa maṟi nūlkaḷ akkālat tiruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
nāṟpeya relli yakattavar vaḻaṅkum 
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvum (1336) ati valiyuṟuttum, vāymoḻi yeṉṟatu mantiratti. eṇṇattiṉ tiṇmiyē allatu maṉṉum tiṟamē mantiramātalāl, maṉ eṉṉuñ collum mantirattik kuṟikkum (piṅ.).  (va.va: 221-222: ti.ma. 751).
mayir
mayir : mi = karumi. mi - (may) - mayir = kariya muṭi. o. nō: ai - ayir, pi - payir, uy - uyir, cey - ceyir, ceyirttal = civattal, ciṉattal. mayir - macir - cmacru (va.).  (ti.ma.751).
mayil
mayil - mayūra
mi = karuniṟam. il = iṭam, kaṇ. mi - mayi + il = mayil = kariya allatu nīlap pīlik kaṇkaḷi yuṭiyatu.
mayil tamiḻakak kuṟiñci nilap paṟavi; kuṟiñci nilat teyvamākiya murukaṉūrti.
vaṭavar aiyuṟṟuk kāṭṭum mūlam mā2 = oli (to sound).  (va.va. 222, 223).
marakkala vaki
puṇi - nīril mitakkum kaṭṭi.
teppam - palamitappuk kaṭṭikaḷiṉ cērkki.
kaṭṭumaram - irukaṭiyum vaḷinta marakkaṭṭu.
tōṇi - tōṇṭappaṭṭa maram pōlvatu.
ōṭam - vēkamākac cellum taṭṭiyāṉa tōṇi.
timil - tiraṇṭa mīṉ paṭaku (tiṇṭimilvaṉ paratavar puṟam. 24).
paḵṟi - paṉṟi pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷa tōṇi.
parical - vaṭṭamāṉa piramput tōṇi.
ampi - vilaṅku mukam allatu paṟavi mukam pōṉṟa mukappiyuṭiya marattōṇi.
paṭaku - pāykaṭṭiya tōṇi.
nāvāy - nīrik koḻittuc cellum pōrk kalam.
kappal - palapāy kaṭṭi vaṇikac carakkēṟṟic cellum peruṅkalam.  (col. 44)
marakkuraṅku viḷiyāṭṭu
(komparacaṉ kuḻiyaracaṉ)
āṭṭiṉ peyar : ciṟuvar marattiṟ kuraṅkupōl ēṟi viḷiyāṭum āṭṭu marak kuraṅku eṉpatām. itu pāṇṭi nāṭṭil komparacaṉ kuḻiyaracaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār toki : iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvārellārum oru marattarukē oru vaṭṭak kōṭṭukkuḷ niṉṟukoṇṭu ovvoruvaṉāy iṭak kāli maṭakkit tūkki, ataṉ kavaṭṭūṭu oru kalliyāvatu kucci yāvatu eṟivar. kuṟinta tolivu pōkkiyavaṉ, piṟar maratti lēṟip piṭikkac coṉṉa piṉ avarip piṭittal vēṇṭum. piṭikkiṟavaṉ marattilēṟumpōtu, cilar marattiṉiṉṟum kutittu vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ pōy niṉṟu koḷvar. cilar kiḷikkuk kiḷitāvi āṭṭaṅ kāṭṭuvar. vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ pōy niṟkumuṉ yārēṉum toṭappaṭṭuviṭṭāl, avaṉ aṭutta āṭṭiyil piṟari muṉ coṉṉavāṟu piṭittal vēṇṭum. marakkiḷikaḷi lirukkumpōtu komparacaṉ kuḻiyaracaṉ eṉṟu pāṇṭi nāṭṭuc ciṟuvar tammik kūṟikkoḷvar.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : kuraṅkukaḷiṉ ceyaliṉiṉṟu iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : maramēṟap payilutal iv vāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
marappaṭṭi vaki
paṭṭi puḷi vēmpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ paṭṭi; ciṟāmpu teṉṉi paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ paṭṭi; maṭṭi allatu taṭi vāḻi yiṉ paṭṭi. (paṭṭi eṉṉum peyar vāḻit taṭikkum vaḻaṅkum).  (col. 71).
marapiyal
1. vaḻakkiyal vaki
coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum vaḻaṅkivaruvatu vaḻakku.
atu, iyalpu vaḻakku, takuti vaḻakku eṉa irupāṟpaṭum.
iyalpu vaḻakku
1. ilakkaṇamuṭiyatu,
2. ilakkaṇappōli,
3. marūu,
eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
ilakkaṇantavaṟāta collum coṟṟoṭarum ilakkaṇa muṭiya (ilakkaṇa) vaḻakkām.
e.ṭu. yāṉi, yāṉi maliyil vāḻum.
ilakkaṇam ciṟitu tavaṟiyum ilakkaṇamuṭiyatu pōla vaḻaṅkum collum coṟṟoṭarum,ilakkaṇap pōli vaḻakkām.
e.ṭu. viciṟi - civiṟi (eḻuttumuṟi māṟṟu)
kōvil - kōyil (uṭampaṭumeym māṟṟu)
nakarppuṟam - puṟanakar (coṉmuṟi māṟṟu)
coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarum maruvi vaḻaṅkuvatu marūu vaḻakkām. maruvutalāvatu citital allatu kuṟukutal.
e.ṭu. vāḻnaṉ - vāṇaṉ.
		tiruccirāppaḷḷi - tirucci.
takuti vaḻakku
1. iṭakkaraṭakkal, 2. maṅkalam, 3. kuḻūukkuṟi eṉa mūvakippaṭum
aviyil collattakāta iṭakkarāṉa poruḷi allatu karutti aṭakki (aḵtāvatu maṟittu)c colvatu, iṭak karaṭakkal vaḻakkām.
e.ṭu. paṉṟiyoṭu cērnta kaṉṟum pavvī tiṉṉum.
amaṅkalamāṉa karutti maṅkalac collālum coṟṟoṭa rālum kuṟippatu, maṅkala vaḻakkām.
e.ṭu. tuñciṉāṉ
		iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭi niḻalaṭintāṉ
		iyaṟki yeytiṉāṉ			   (cettāṉ)
		tirunāṭalaṅkarittāṉ
		perumpiṟitu (cāvu) uṟṟāṉ
piṟarkkup pulaṉākātavāṟu, oru kuḻuvār maṭṭum tamakkuḷ maṟiporuḷavāka vaḻaṅkum kuṟikaḷ allatu coṟkaḷ, kuḻūuk kuṟiyām.
e.ṭu. karuntali (1/4)
iru kuraṅkukki (mucumucukki)
kuṟippu : oru nāṭṭil oru pakutikkē ciṟappāyuriya tici vaḻakkiyum, uyarntōrallātār vaḻaṅkum iḻi vaḻakkiyum, ayaṉmoḻi taḻuviya ayalvaḻakkiyum, kaṭṭurikaḷilum nūlkaḷilum vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu.
tici vaḻakku 
e.ṭu.	tañci 	vaṭārkkāṭu 	kōvi
		keṭṭup 	tārivārntu 	kūṭappōyiṟṟu
		pōyiṟṟu 	pōyiṟṟu 	(kāṇāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu)
ticivaḻakku avvavviṭattiṟku ēṟkum; nāṭu muḻumikkum ēṟkātu.
iḻi vaḻakku 
veḷuttuvāriviṭṭāṉ, kampiyi nīṭṭiviṭṭāṉ, antap paruppu iṅkē vēkātu.
ayal vaḻakku
piḻi 			tiruttam
viḷiyāṭṭil naṉṟāyc	naṉṟāy viḷiyāṭiṉārkaḷ
ceytārkaḷ
payiṟciyeṭukka vēṇṭum.	payiṟci ceyyavēṇṭum
kūṭṭattiṟ paṅkeṭuttuk	kūṭṭattic cērntu naṭattuṅkaḷ koḷḷuṅkaḷ.
iṅkuk kūṟiya ayalvaḻakku āṅkilamoḻiyit taḻuviyatu.
cila coṟkaḷ, ulaka vaḻakkil oru vakip poruḷum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil oruvakip poruḷum tarum. kaṭṭurikaḷil, iṭattiṟkēṟpa iruvakiyuḷoṉṟit taḻuvalām.

2. peyarccol
1. paṇpuppeyarkaḷ
karumi = perumi, valimi, paḻimi, koṭumi, kīḻmi, tīmi.karuṅkaṇ = kaṇṇēṟu (tiruṣṭitōṣam).
karuṅkantu = neṟkaḷattil polikku aṭutta viḻum patar.
karuṅkaṟi = vaḷamāṉa cati.
karuṅkāṭu = cuṭukāṭu.
karuṅkāy = iḷampākku, koñcaṅkuṟiya muṟṟiya kāy.
karuṅkuṇam = tīkkuṇam.
karuṅkūttu = iḻitaraka kūttu, anākarikak kūttu.
karuṅki = vēliyil aṭippaṭṭuk karutta ki, valiya ki, kolluṅki.
karuñcarakku = kūlam (tāṉiyam), cārāyam.
karuñcār = iṭuppupporuttu (Hip-joint).
karuñcey = naṉcey.
karuñcēvakam = peruvīracceyal.
karuntamiḻ = karukalāṉa tamiḻ.
karuntaṟi, karunilam = pāḻnilam, payaṉpaṭāta nilam.
karuntali = pōril iṟakkum tali, muṭivu.
karuntaṉam = peruñcelvam, poṉ.
karuntāḷ = aṟupaṭṭa tāḷaṭi.
karuntiṭṭi, karuntiṭar = periya mēṭu.
karuntoḻil = uṟutiyāṉa vēlippāṭu, kaṭuvuḻippu kolittoḻil.
karunaṭam = iḻitara naṭam.
karunākku = tīya nākku.
karunākkaṉ = kañcaṉ, tīyaṉ.
karunāṭakam = iḻitara nāṭakam, anākarika nāṭakam.
karunāḻiki = iravu.
karunāḷ = amaṅkala nāḷ, tīnāḷ.
karunīlappiṟappu = nākanilikkuc ciṟitu mēṟpaṭṭa piṟavi.
karunūl = cūṉiya (ceyviṉi) nūl.
karuppu = pēy, pañcam.
karumpāṭu = ēriyil allatu kuḷattil karikkum nīrukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa nilam.
karumpiṟappu = narakappiṟavi.
karumakaḷ = caṇṭāḷi.
karumakaṉ = caṇṭāḷaṉ, kollaṉ.
karumāṉam = cūṉiyavitti.
karumēṉi = pontuṭampu, (tūlacarīram)
karuvali = mikunta palam.
karuvāḷi = matinuṭpaṉ.
karuviṉi = pāvam, tīviṉi.
karuvūlam = pokkiṣavaṟi.
kārviṉi = pāvattoḻil, tīcceyal.
kāraṟivu = mayakkam poruntiya aṟivu.
cemmi = cevvi, nērmi, oḻuṅku, tuppuravu, aḻaku, perumi, ciṟappu, naṉmi.
ceṅkaḷam = pōrkkaḷam.
ceṅkāy = paḻukkum paruvakkāy.
ceṅkāyppuṇ = paḻukkāta puṇ.
ceṅkuṇakku = nērkiḻakku.
ceṅkuttu = nēr kuttu.
ceṅki = koṭikki.
ceṅkōṭu = ceṅkuttāṉa mali.
ceṅkōl = nērmiyāṉa āṭci, nērāṉa kōl.
ceñca, ceñcevē = nērāka, muḻutum.
ceñcam, ceñcu = nērmi, carimi (Correctness) muḻumi.
ceñceyal =nērmiyāṉa ceyal.
ceñceviyar = celvar.
ceñceḻippu = peruñceḻippu, ērāḷam.
ceñcol = tiruntiya col, veḷippaṭiccol.
ceñcōṟu = aracaṉ taṉporuṭṭup pōril iṟakkak karutiya vīraṉukkuk kālamellām aḷikkum cōṟu.
ceññāṉi = ciṟanta ñāṉi.
centaṭṭu = antarattaṭṭu, taṉmēl viḻum aṭiyit taṭukki, ēviṉa kāriyattit taṭṭuki.
centamiḻ = ilakkaṇattamiḻ, tūyatamiḻ.
centalippu = ceḻippu.
centūkku = nērākat tūkkuki.
centoṭi = mōṉiyetukiyillāta yāppu.
cennaṭi = cevviyanaṭi.
cenniṟuvutal = naṉṉeṟiyil niṟuttutal.
cenneṟi = cevviya vaḻi, naṉṉeṟi.
cennilam = pōrkkaḷam.
cempatti = uṇmiyāṉa aṉpu.
cempākam, cempāti, cempāl nērpāti.

cemporuḷ = nērporuḷ, uṇmipporuḷ, ciṟanta poruḷ, kaṭavuḷ, aṟam.
cemmal = talimi, valimi, talivaṉ, iṟivaṉ.
cemmāttal = iṟumāttal, mikamakiḻtal.
cemmoḻi = naṟcol, tiruntiyamoḻi.
nīlam
nīlaṉ = koṭiyavaṉ
nīli = koṭiyavaḷ
pacumi = iḷami īram, kuḷircci, aḻaku, iṉimi, putumi, vēkāmi.
