piñcēṟu = cāṇam.
poṉmi = polivu, aḻaku, viḷakkam.
poṟpa = polivupeṟa.
veṇmi = veṟumi, veḷippaṭi, aṟiviṉmi, kaḷḷa miṉmi, uḷḷīṭiṉmi.
veṇkaṭaṉ, veṇṇilikkaṭaṉ = īṭukāṭṭātu vāṅkuṅ kaṭaṉ.
veṇkāval = veṟuṅkāval (Simple imprisonment).
veṇki = toḻilceytu paḻakāta ki.
veṇcōṟu = veṟuñcōṟu.
veṇṭēr = kāṉal (Mirage).
veṇṇilam = veṟuntari, maṇaltari.
veṇṇōvu, veṇṇōkkāṭu = piḷḷippēṟṟiṟkumuṉ uṇṭākum veṟu vētaṉi.
veṇpatam = iḷañcūṭṭuppatam.
veṇpāṭṭam = muṉpaṇamiṉṟi viṭuṅ kuttaki.
veṇpuḻukkal, veṇpuḻukku, veṇpuḻuṅkal = iḷampuḻukkal, iḷampuḻukkalarici.
veṇmaṭṭak karuttu = mēleḻunta vārittīrmāṉam (Superflcial judgement).
veṇmaṭṭam = mēleḻuntavāri, nuṇukkamiṉmi.
veṇmaṭṭavēli = nuṇukkamillāta vēli, mēleḻunta vāriyākac ceyyum vēli.
veḷvīccu = āṭamparappēccu.
veḷveṭi = kuṇṭu illāta tōṭṭā.
veḷḷanti = kaḷḷaṅkavaṭaṟṟavaṉ.
veḷḷaṭi = cātāraṇam.
veḷḷaṭi veruṭṭu = peṭṭimiraṭṭu.
veḷḷaveḷi = paraveḷi.
veḷḷaṟivu = avivēkam.
veḷḷiṭi = kōṭiyil maḻipeyyātu iṭikkum iṭi etir pārātu tiṭumeṉavarum tuṉpam.
veḷḷiṭi, veḷḷiṭimali = mikatteḷivāyiruppatu.
veḷḷitu = veḷippaṭiyāṉatu.
veḷḷili = veṟṟili.
veḷḷuyir = tūya āṉmā.
veḷḷuruṭṭu = veṟṟaccuṟuttam.
veḷḷuḻavu = ciṟitu īramuḷḷa nilattil uḻum uḻavu.
veḷḷeḻuttu = eḻuttu viḷakkamāyt teriyāmikkē tuvāṉa pārvikkuṟi (Long sight).
veḷḷeṉa = atikāliyil, iruṭṭumuṉ, kuṟitta kālattiṟku muṉpē.
veḷḷēṭu = veṟṟēṭu.
veḷḷēṟaṉ = paḻutāṉa irumpu (Scrap iron).
veḷḷi = kaḷḷamaṟṟavaṉ.
veḷḷikkavi = poruṭceṟivillāta pāṭṭu.
veḷḷikkōṭṭi = payaṉilpēccu, payaṉilapēcum aṟivilār kūṭṭam.
veḷḷiccol = eḷiyacol, tiruntāccol.
veḷḷittamiḻ = eḷiya naṭittamiḻ.
veḷḷittaṉam = kaḷḷamiṉmi.
veḷḷinōkku = kaḷḷamaṟṟa pārvi.
veḷḷippuṇ = ūṉveḷuttu eḷitilāṟāta puṇ.
veḷḷippūccu = tavaṟṟi maṟikki.
veḷḷippēccu = veḷippaṭiyāṉa pēccu, kaḷḷamaṟṟa pēccu.
veḷḷipūcutal = tavaṟṟi maṟittal.
veḷḷimakaṉ = mūṭaṉ.
veḷḷimati = aṟiyāmi, aṟivukkuṟivu.
veḷḷimaḻi = ciṟu maḻi.
veḷḷimaṉam = kavaṭaṟṟa maṉam.
veḷḷokkal = kuṟṟamaṟṟa iṉam, celvamuḷḷa kiḷiñar.
veḷḷōkkāḷam = umiḻnīri maṭṭum veḷiyēṟṟum kumaṭṭal.
veḷḷōṭṭam = pututtēri mutaṉmutalāka ōṭṭip pārttal payaṉpaṭuttuvataṟku muntiya nōṭṭam (parīṭci).
veḷḷōli = eḻutappaṭāta ōli.

āṇpāṟkuc ciṟappuppeyar peṟāta uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṉ āṇpā lellām, āṇ eṉṉum aṭimoḻi peṟṟa potup peyarāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum.
e.ṭu. āṇkaraṭi, āṇpalli.
ii.  peṇpāṟ peyar

iii. iḷamippeyar

potuvāka, muṭṭiyiṭupavaṟṟiṉ iḷamippeyar kuñcu eṉpatum, nēraṭiyāka īṉupavaṟṟiṉ iḷamippeyar kuṭṭi eṉpatumākum.
piḷḷikuṭṭi kaṭikkuṭṭi mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷil, kuṭṭi eṉṉum peyar makkaṭkum uriyatākum.
cila uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṉ iḷamippeyar, ētēṉum ōr aṭi moḻi peṟṟa potuppeyarākavē vaḻaṅkum.
e.ṭu. narikkeḷiṟu, arittavaḷi, kuṭṭiviḷā, piḷḷi viḷātti.

e.ṭu. paṉṟikkāraṉ, paṉṟimēycci.
3. viṉiccol
1. icikkaruvi viṉikaḷ
tōṟkaruvikaḷ : kañcirāttaṭṭiṉār, mataṅkam (mirutaṅkam) taṭṭiṉār, taval aṭittār, paṟipaṟintār, tappu aṭittār, muracu aṟintār, koṭṭukkoṭṭiṉār.
tuḷikkaruvikaḷ : kuḻal ūtiṉār; nātacuram ūtiṉār, icittār; caṅku ūtiṉār, vāyvittār, kuṟittār; kompu ūtiṉār, vāyvittār, kuṟittār.
narampukkaruvikaḷ : kiṉṉari (Fiddle) iḻuttār, icittār, eḻuviṉār; yāḻ (vīṇi) eḻuviṉār, uḷarntār, muraṉṟār, icittār; tampurā mīṭṭiṉār; kōṭṭu eḻuviṉār, icittār; cittār icittār, eḻuviṉār.
kañcakkaruvikaḷ : cālar aṭittār, taṭṭiṉār; mukarciṅku aṭittār; cappaḷākkaṭṭi aṭittār, taṭṭiṉār; kiṟikkaṭṭi aṭittār.
palavakik karuvikaḷ
iyam iyampiṉār; icikkaruvikaḷi icittār, iyakkiṉār.
2. uṇṭi viṉikaḷ
uṇpaṉa : cōṟu uṇṭār, cāppiṭṭār; cāppāṭu cāppiṭṭār; uṇavu uṇṭār.
tiṉpaṉa : palakāram tiṉṟār, ciṟṟuṇṭi aruntiṉār.
parukuvaṉa : taṇṇīr kuṭittār; kāppi kuṭittār; mōr kuṭittār, uṟiñciṉār.
kāppicāppiṭṭār, taṇṇīrcāppiṭṭār eṉak kūṟutal vaḻuvām.
3. maḻi viṉikaḷ
maḻitūṟiṉatu, maḻipeytatu, maḻipoḻintatu (corintatu), maḻi koṭṭiṉatu, (ūṟṟiṉatu).
ivviṉikaḷ, muṟiyē, mēṉmēlum valitāyp peyyum nilikaḷik kuṟikkum.
4. ciṉi viṉikaḷ
i. aṭiviṉikaḷ 
aṭikkīḻppaṭutal = oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉuṭiya āṇik kuḷḷātal.
aṭicāytal = aṭiyil niḻalcāytal.
aṭittirumputal = aṭiyil niḻalcāytal, uccimēṟ katiravaṉ mēṟku nōkkic cāytal.
aṭitoṭutal = piṟarpātantoṭṭu āṇiyiṭutal.
aṭinakartal = iṭamviṭṭup peyartal.
aṭippaṭutal = kīḻppaṭital.
aṭipiṭittal = aṭiccuvaṭu paṟṟippōtal, toṭarutal, tuppaṟital.
aṭipārttal = niḻalaḷantu poḻutaṟital.
aṭipiḻittal = neṟitavaṟinaṭattal.
aṭipiṟakkiṭutal = piṉvāṅkutal, tōṟṟōṭutal.
aṭipeyartal, aṭipeyarttal = kāleṭuttuvittal.
aṭiyiraṭṭittal = muṉcellāmal niṉṟa iṭattilēyē kālittūkki vittal.
aṭiyilēyuṟital, aṭiyuṟital = vaḻipaṭutal, aṭiniḻalil niṟṟal, oruvaṉuṭiya pātukāppiṟkuḷḷātal.
aṭiyoṟṟutal = piṉpaṟṟutal.
aṭiviḷakkutal = pātaṅkaḻuviyupacarittal.
ii. kaṇviṉikaḷ
kaṇkalattal = oruvariyoruvar pārttal, etirppaṭutal, kaṇkaḷavukoḷḷutal = tāṉ piṟaṉippārppati avaṉ kāṇātavāṟu tāṉ avaṉip pārttal.
kaṇkaṉalutal = kōpattāṟ kaṇcivattal.
kaṇkāṭṭiviṭutal = kaṇcāṭiyāl ēviviṭutal, 
kaṇkeṭacceytal = aṟintiruntu tīmiceytal.
(e.ṭu.) vēṭṭikkāraṉ taṉ kūṭṭāḷiyik kaṇkeṭac cuṭṭāṉ).
kaṇkeṭappēcutal = kaṇṭoṉṟu collutal.
kaṇcāttutal = aṉpōṭu pārttal.
kaṇcāytal = aṟivutaḷartal, aṉpukuṟital.
kaṇcuruṭṭutal = aḻakiṉāl vacīkarittal, oruvaṉukkut tūkkamayakkamātal.
kaṇcuḻalutal = viḻicuḻaṉṟu mayaṅkutal.
kaṇceṟiyiṭutal = muḻutum paravi aṭittuk koḷḷutal.
kaṇṭu kaḻittal = veṟuppuṇṭākum vari nukarntu vilakkutal, tāṉē camittuc cuvi allatu uṇṇum ārvam keṭutal.
kaṇṭukāṇutal = kavaṉamāyppārttal.
kaṇṭupāvittal = oruviṉiyip piṉpaṟṟic ceytal (To imitate an action).
kaṇṇakaṟṟutal = tuyilnīṅki viḻittal.
kaṇṇaṭital = payiriṉ kuruttu veḷittaḷḷāmal niṉṟu pōtal, pāl mutaliyaṉa vēṟupaṭṭukkeṭutal;
(e.ṭu. kaṇṇaṭinta pāl).
kaṇṇaḻittal = patavuri kūṟutal.
kaṇṇaṟutal = aṉpukuṟital, naṭpukkulital, nīṅkutal.
kaṇṇāṟcuṭutal = kaṇṇēṟu (tiruṣṭi) paṭappārttal.
kaṇṇārvittal = kaṇṇukku iṉpamūṭṭutal.
kaṇṇiṟṟal = etirniṟṟal.
kaṇṇukkukkaṇṇātal = mika arumiyāṉa uṟavātal.
kaṇṇūṟukaḻittal, kaṇṇeccilkaḻittal = tiruṣṭitōṣam pōkkutal.
kaṇṇeṟital = kaṭikkaṇṇāṟ pārttal.
kaṇṇoṭṭutal = kaṇ tūkkaniliyaṭital.
kaṇṇōṭutal = virumpiyaporuḷmēṟ pārvicellutal, iraṅkutal.
kaṇtiṟattal = uruva ōviyaṅkaṭku viḻiyamittal, parukumāṟu iḷanīric cīvutal, cilanti allatu koppuḷam nuṉiyil uṭital, vāṉam veḷivāṅkutal, aṟivu uṇṭākutal, kalvi kaṟpittal.
kaṇteṟittal = peruveḷiccattāṟ kaṇṇoḷi maḻuṅkutal.
kaṇpacattal = tuṉpattāṟ kaṇṇiṉ niṟam māṟutal.
kaṇpaṭutal = tūṅkutal, tiruṣṭipaṭutal.
kaṇparital = mūṭṭaṟutal.
kaṇpaṟital = kaṇṇoḷi kuṟital.
kaṇpituṅkutal = vēlikkaṭiṉattāl varuttamikutal.
kaṇputital = aṟivukeṭutal.
kaṇmalartal = viḻittal.
kaṇmāṟutal = tōṉṟi uṭaṉē maṟital, nilitāḻtal, aṉpu kuṟintu puṟakkaṇittal.
kaṇmukiḻttal = kaṇmūṭutal, tūṅkutal.
kaṇmūṭutal = iṟattal.
kaṇvaḻukkutal = kaṇkūcutal.
kaṇvaḷartal = tūṅkutal.
kaṇvāṅkutal = kaṇṇik kavartal, kavaṉippu nīṅkutal, tūrviyeṭuttal.
kaṇviṭutal = tuḷiyuṇṭātal.
kaṇvitumputal = kātalar oruvariyoruvar virintu kāṇa virumputal.
iii. kaḻuttuviṉikaḷ
kaḻuttiruntuviṭutal = pārattāṟ kaḻuttu amuṅkutal. (e.ṭu. avaṉukkuk kaḻuttu iruntuviṭṭatu).
kaḻuttiṟ kaṭṭutal = valintu poṟuppāḷiyākkutal.
kaḻuttukkoṭuttal = taṉ varuttam pārāmaṟ piṟar kāriyatti ēṟṟu niṟṟal, vāḻkkippaṭutal.
kaḻuttumuṟittal, kaḻutti muṟittal = varuttutal.
kaḻuttikkaṭṭutal = viṭātu nerukkutal.
iv. kātu viṉikaḷ
kātaṟuttal = āvaṇatti (pattiratti) aṟutiyākat tīrttuk kiḻittal.
kātilaṭipaṭutal = oru ceyti aṭikkaṭi kēṭkappaṭutal.
kātukiḻittal, kātukiḷḷutal = āvaṇattit tīrttuk kiḻittal.
kātukuttutal = tāḷ mūliyil tuḷiyiṭutal, vañcittal.
kātuperukkutal, kātuvaḷarttal = kātuc cōṇiyiluḷḷa tuḷiyip peritākkutal.
kātik kaṭittal = kātōramāka mukattivittu maṟiporuḷ collutal, kōṭcollutal.
v. kāl viṉikaḷ
kālkiḷartal = ōṭutal, paṭiyeṭuttuc cellutal.
kālkeñcutal = kāl ōyntu mēṟkoṇṭu naṭakkamuṭiyā tiruttal.
kālkoḷḷutal = viḻāviṟkut toṭakkam ceytal, cili cetukkak kaṟkoḷḷutal, paravi iṭatti yaṭittal.
kāl cāytal = keṭutal, aḻital.
kāl cīttal = kōḻipōṟ kālāṟ kiḷittal, tuṭittup pōkkutal.
kāl tāṅkutal = kāliyiḻuttu naṭattal.
kāl piṉṉutal = nōyiṉāṟ kālkaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu kuṟukkātal, muṭṭikkāl taṭṭutal,
kāl muḷittal = kuḻanti naṭakkat toṭaṅkutal.
(e.ṭu. kuḻantikkuk kāl muḷittuviṭṭatu).
kāl vāṅkutal = kāl vaḻukkutal, uyirmey neṭiṟkuṟi varital.
kāl vārttal = koṭikkālil āṭi mātam akatti vitiyi ūṉṟutal.
kāl viṭutal = kulavuṟavu nīṅkutal.
kāl viḻuntupōtal = pakkavātattāṟ kāl acivaṟṟup pōtal.
kālāṭṭutal = necavut toḻil ceytal.
kālāṭutal = muyaṟciyāṟ celvañ ciṟantiruttal.
(e.ṭu. avaṉukku iṉṟu kālāṭukiṉṟatu).
kālāṇi yaṟutal = uḷḷaṅkāl uraṅkeṭutal, valimi celvam mutaliyaṉa kuṟital.
kālāṟutal = naṭi ōyntu iḷippāṟutal, kālil aratta vōṭṭam uṇṭāka ulāvutal.
kāliṟaṅkutal = maḻikkāliṟaṅkutal, ariyāṭiyiṉ aṭi viḷimpu tāḻtal.
kālūṟutal = vaḻippōkkuk kuṟiyākak kālil tiṉavuṇṭātal, piḷḷikaṭku ōṭiyāṭa viruppuṇṭātal, kaṇavaṉukkup paṇam varaṟ kuṟiyāka maṉivikkuk kālil tiṉavuṇṭātal.
kālūṉṟutal = pantaṟkāl nāṭṭutal, maḻikkāliṟaṅkutal, nilipeṟutal.
kālic cuṟṟutal = toṭarntu paṟṟutal.
kāloṭṭutal = kuṟivic carippaṭuttutal.
kālōṭutal = oru kāriyattil muyaṟciyuṇṭātal.
akalakkāl vittal = aḷavukkumiñciya iṭaṅkaḷik kippaṟṟutal allatu koḷḷutal.
vi. ki viṉikaḷ
kikkōraṇi kāṭṭutal = iṟakkun taṟuvāyiliruttal.
kikalattal = taḻuvutal kūṭutal, caṇṭiyiṭutal.
kikavittal = kiccāṭiyāl aṭakkutal, apayamaḷittal. kikāṭṭutal = tiṟami kāṭṭutal.
kikāṇutal = aṉupavattil aṟital.
kikeṭutal = vaṟumiyaṭital.
kikoṭuttal = tuṉpattil utaviceytal, kikulukkak ki nīṭṭutal.
kikōttal = naṭpucceytal.
kiccuḻippaṭutal, kiccuḻiyātal = cariyāka vitiyāmiyāl payir cilaviṭattiṟ kūṭṭamāka vaḷartal. 
kicalittal = koṭi ōytal, vēḷāṇmi (upacāram) oḻital.
kicōrtal = kiviṭṭuppōtal vaṟumiyaṭital.
kitali vittal = peruntuyaraṭital.
kitaḷartal = vaṟumiyaṭital.
kitarutal = tuṉpattil utavutal.
kitāṅkutal = kiyāl tāṅkutal, kēṭaṭiyāmal kāttal.
kitāḻtal = celvanili taḷartal.
kitiruntutal = kiyeḻuttu viṉittiṟami mutaliyaṉa tiruntutal.
kitīṇṭutal = aṭittal, tīyanōkkoṭu toṭutal.
kituṭittal = viṭṭu nīṅkutal.
kitūkkiviṭutal = nīril aḻuntuvōri eṭuttuk kāttal, vaṟumiyil allatu tuṉpattiṟ kāttal.
kinniṟuttutal = niliniṟuttutal, aṭakkutal.
kinimirtal = vaḷarntu paruvamaṭital.
kinīṭṭutal = aṭittal, koṭuttal, irattal, tiruṭutal.
kinīḷutal = aṭikkavō, koṭukkavō, tiruṭavō ki nīḷutal.
kinaṉittal = piṟar vīṭṭil paciyāṟṟikkoḷḷutal.
kināṭṭutal = kiyeḻuttiṭutal.
kinekiḻtal = kitavaṟaviṭutal.
kinoṭittal = celvanili keṭutal.
kinoṭu noṭuttal = kaṇṭatellāvaṟṟiyum toṭutal.
kippāṭupaṭutal = kiyāl uḻittal.
kippiṭittal = maṇañceytal.
kippiṭiyāyp piṭittal = nēriṟ kiyāṟ piṭittal, kiyuṅ kaḷavumāyp piṭittal.
kipārttal = utavi nāṭutal,
kipūcutal = uṇṭa kiyik kaḻuvutal.
kipōṭṭuk koṭuttal = kipōṭṭu oppantatti uṟutippaṭuttal.
kimmaṟittal = kiyāl taṭuttal, kikavittu vilakkutal.
kimmikutal = aḷavukaṭattal.
kimukiḻttal = kikūpputal.
kimāṟutal = oruvar kiyiṉiṉṟu maṟṟoruvar kikkuc cellutal, vēliyāḷ māṟutal, kaṭci māṟutal, oḻukkam māṟutal.
kiyaṭittal = kiyaṭittu uṟuti tarutal.
kiyaṭippaṭutal = oruporuḷ pala ki kaṭantu cellutal.
kiyaṭittal = piṟarkiyil oppuvittal.
kiyamarttutal = kiyiṟciki kāṭṭi amitiyāyirukkac ceytal, kiyāṟciki kāṭṭi āṭkaḷi amarttutal.
kiyayartal = kicōrtal, nilimi tāḻtal.
kiyarik koḷḷutal = vāri arittukkoḷḷutal.
kiyaḻital, kiyaṟutal = ceyalaṟṟu varuntutal, oṉṟuñ ceyyāmal varuntutal.
kiyaḷittal = oppaṭittal.
kiyiḷittal = celvanili taḷartal.
kiyuṇṇutal = piṟar kiyil uṇṭu vāḻtal.
kiyik kaṭṭutal = oṉṟum ceyyātapaṭi taṭuttal.
kiyik kaṭittal = naṭṭamātal.
kiyik kuṟukkutal = celavik kuṟittal, kañcaṉātal.
kiyōṭutal = vēkamāka eḻututal.
kiyutirkkoḷḷutal = viṭṭu vilakumpaṭi kiyacittuk kuṟippiṭutal.
kivaṟṟutal, kivaṟaḷutal = poruḷaṟutal, koṭi ōytal.
kivarutal = oṉṟic ceyyak kiyeḻuki, tērcci peṟutal.
kivāraṅ koḷḷutal = atikāratti mēṟkoḷḷutal, kūtti niṟuttum poruṭṭuk kiyi uyaraveṭuttu vīcutal.
kivitirttal = maṟuppu, accam mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟittuk kiyacittal.
kivirittal = iṉmi kuṟittal, koṭi maṟuttal.
vii. cevi viṉikaḷ
cevikkēṟutal = kēṭṭaṟkiṉiyatā yiruttal.
cevikkoḷḷutal = kēṭṭal.
cevicāyttal = iṇaṅkik kēṭṭal.
cevi tiṉṉutal = maṟiporuḷāka ōtutal.
cevippaṭutal = kēṭkappaṭutal.
cevimaṭuttal = kēṭṭal.
ceviyaṭittal = yāṉipōlak kātāṭṭutal.
ceviyaṟivuṟuttutal = nallaṟivu pukaṭṭutal.
viii. tali viṉikaḷ
talikkaṭṭutal = aṉpāy naṭattutal, pātukāttal, oppuvittal.
talikkūṭutal = oṉṟucērtal, niṟivēṟutal.
talikkēṟutal = pittavēkam talikkēṟutal, cerukku mutirtal.
talikki tarutal = kiyāl taḻuvi aṉpu kāṭṭutal.
taliccellutal = ceṉṟu kūṭutal.
taliciṟattal = mikat talimiyātal.
talicuṟṟiyāṭutal, talicuṟṟutal = iṟumāppuk koḷḷutal, cerukkuṇṭātal.
tali taṭumāṟutal = kalakkamaṭital, cīrkulital.
talikulukkutal = uṭaṉpāṭu ikaḻcci mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟikkat taliyacittal.
talitoṭutal = taliyittoṭṭu āṇiyiṭutal.
taliniṉṟoḻukutal = talimiyāka niṉṟu allatu aṇuki niṉṟu paṇiceytal.
talippaṭutal = kūṭutal, etirppaṭutal, mēṟkoḷḷutal, talimiyātal, toṭaṅkutal.
talippōkutal = muṭivuvari cellutal.
talimayaṅkutal = āḷneruṅkutal, kalantiruttal.
talimēṟkoḷḷutal = mikac ciṟappāyēṟṟal, poṟuppēṟṟal.
taliyaraṅkēṟutal = taṉ kalvit tiṟamiyi mutaṉ mutal aviyōrkkuk kāṭṭutal.
taliyaḷi ceytal = mukamalarntu iṉcoṟ collutal, ciṟanta aṉpu ceytal.
taliyit taṭavutal = vañcittal, ēmāṟṟip poruḷ peṟal. 
talivaḻittal = taliccavaram paṇṇutal, aḷakkumpaṭiyiṉ taliyit taṭṭutal.
ix. nākku (nā) viṉikaḷ
nākkuccātal = nākkuc cuviyuṇarcciyi iḻattal.
nākkut taṭṭutal = tikkippēcutal, pūṭṭiṉ nākkuk keṭutal.
nākkut taṭittal = nāmarattuppōtal.
nākkut tāḷampōṭutal = uṇavukoḷḷa āvalāyiruttal, aḷavukaṭantu nakiyāṭutal (parikacittal).
nākkut tiruntutal = uccarippu tiruttamāyiruttal.
nākkut teṟikkap pēcutal = periyōrip paḻittal.
nākku nīṭṭutal = aḷavukaṭantu pēcutal.
nākku nīḷutal = aṭakkamiṉṟip pēcutal.
(e.ṭu.) avaṉukku nākku nīḷkiṉṟatu.
nākkup puraḷutal = col tavaṟutal.
nākku vaḷittal = paḻittal.
nākku vāṅkip pōtal = iṟumpūtiṉāl (aticayattiṉāl) pēcamuṭiyātapaṭi nā uḷḷiḻukkappaṭutal, tākattāl nā vaṟaḷutal.
nākku vāṅkutal = nāvi uḷḷiḻuttal, kaḷittuppōtal, kaḷikkap paṇṇutal.
nākku viḻutal = nākku timirvātappaṭutal.
nākkoṭṭutal = nāvaṟaḷutal.
nāvacittal = pēcutal.
nāvaṭittuppōtal = pēcamuṭiyāmaṟ pōtal.
nāvuḻaṟṟutal = poṟāmippaṭṭup pēcutal.
x. pal viṉikaḷ
palluppaṭutal = vacimoḻi allatu cāpam palittal.
pallik kaṭittukkoṇṭu ceytal = kaṭṭāyattiṟkāka oṉṟi veṟuppuṭaṉ ceytal.
xi. maṇṭi viṉikaḷ
maṇṭi tuḷḷutal = cerukku mikutal.
maṇṭi vaṟaḷutal = eṇṇey muḻukkillāmal taliyerital, matikeṭutal.
xii. muka viṉikaḷ
mukaṅkāṭṭutal = kāṭci koṭuttal.
mukaṅkāṇutal, mukaṅkoḷḷutal = kaṭṭi uṭivataṟkumuṉ muṉi vittal.
mukaṅ kuṟāvutal = tuṉpattāl mukam poliviḻattal.
mukañciṉṉam pōtal = veṭkattāl mukaṅ karukutal.
mukañceytal = nōkkutal, tōṉṟutal, muṉṉātal.
mukattilaruppa miṟaṅkutal, mukattilarumputal, mukattiṟ karikkōṭiṭutal = mīci muḷittal.
mukantarutal = paṭcaṅ kāṭṭutal.
mukantiruttutal = vāṭṭan tavirttal.
mukampukutal = aruḷukkāka etirceṉṟu niṟṟal.
mukam vaḻittal = mukaccavaram ceytal.
mukamaṟuttal = kaṇṇōṭṭamiṉṟip pēcutal.
mukamātal = uṭaṉpaṭutal.
mukamukam pārttal = mukatti mukam pārttal.
mukamuṟital = kaṇṇōṭṭaṅ keṭutal, veṟuppaṭital.
mukamoṭṭutal = cēvalkaḷic caṇṭikku etirmukamākkutal.
xiii. vayiṟṟu viṉikaḷ
vayiṟṟiliṭṭuk koḷḷutal = peruntuyarāl uṭampu naliyac ceytal.
vayiṟṟit tiṟattal = karukkoḷḷumāṟu kaṭavuḷ aruḷtal.
vayiṟṟip perukkutal = uṇṇum aḷavi atikappaṭuttutal, nirampa vuṇṇutal.
vayiṟaṭittal = uṇṇum viruppam illātiruttal, malaṭāyiruttal, karuttarikkum taṉmiyaṟṟuppōtal.
vayiṟu kaṭittal, vayiṟu kiṇṭutal, vayiṟu kiḷḷutal = pacinōvu mikutal.
vayiṟu kaḻuvutal = arumpāṭupaṭṭu uṇavutēṭip piḻittal.
vayiṟu vāyttal = makappeṟutal.
vayiṟu vāḻtal = vēṇṭiya uṇavu uṭiyavaṉātal, uyir vāḻtal.
xib. vāy viṉikaḷ
vāy tiṟattal = puṇkaṭṭi uṭital, pēcattoṭaṅkutal.
vāypōkkutal = eḷitil vākkuk koṭuttal.
vāymaṭuttal = uṭkoḷḷutal.
vāy malartal = pēcutal.
vāymuṭṭup pōṭutal = maṟiporuḷi veḷiyiṭātiruk kumāṟu kiyūṭṭuk koṭuttal.
vāymūttal = pēcciṟ ciṟattal, pēcamuntutal.
vāymūḻttal = vāymūṭutal, pēcātiruttal.
vāymoḻital = apimantirittal.
vāyvaḻaṅkutal = uṇṇutal.
vāyviṭutal = olittal.
vāyāvi pōkkutal = koṭṭāvi viṭutal, vīṇpēccup pēcutal.
vāyilē pōṭutal = pēcavoṭṭātu kuṟukkiṭṭup pēcit taṭiceytal.
4. oruporuṭ pala coṟkaḷ
orē poruḷpaṟṟip pala coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkalām; allatu vaḻaṅkuvatākat tōṉṟalām. aviyellām parupporuḷil ottaṉavēyaṉṟi, nuṇporuḷil ottaṉavalla. avaṟṟiṉ nuṇporuḷaṟintu avvap poruḷil vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum.
e.ṭu. kocu eṉpatu, kaṭṭukkaṭi nīrilum, iṉippupporuḷ kaḷilum moyttukkoṇṭiruntu, makkaḷik kaṭikkāta oru ciṟṟī vaki. oluṅku eṉpatu, cākkaṭiyilum catuppu nilaṅkaḷilumiruppatāy, kocuviṉum nīṇṭu paruttatāy, makkaḷiṉ kurutiyi uṟiñcik kuṭikkum oru ciṟṟī vaki.
vāval (vuvāl) eṉpatu, maraṅkaḷil toṅkik koṇṭiruntu kaṉikaḷiyuṇṭu vāḻum peru vuvāl, turiñcil eṉpatu, cantu pontukaḷiṟ pukuntiruntu, iravil ūrukkuḷ paṟantu tirintu, ciṟu pūccikaḷip piṭittut tiṇṇum ciṟu vuvāl.
collutalik kuṟikkap pala coṟkaḷ uḷa, avaṟṟiṉ nuṇporuḷ varumāṟu :
collutal oruvaṉiṭam potuvāka oṉṟit terivittal.
pēcutal oru moḻiyik kiyāḷutal.
kūṟutal oṉṟik kūṟupaṭutti allatu vakippaṭuttic collutal.
cāṟṟutal mukkiyamāṉa ceytiyip potumakkaṭ kaṟivittal.
moḻital ovvoru colliyum teḷivāy collutal.
eṉṉutal oruvaṉ kūṟṟit terivittal.
kiḷattal oru poruḷik kuṟippiṭṭuc collutal.
paṉṉutal pala poruḷkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟiyum taṉittaṉi kuṟittuc collutal.
nuvalutal nūṟporuḷi nuṇukkamākac collutal.
urittal nūlukku uri kūṟutal allatu oṉṟi viḷakkic collutal.
navilutal oru colli allatu coṟṟoṭarip palakālum ōtip payilutal.
pakartal oru poruḷiṉ viliyic collutal.
noṭittal kati allatu varalāṟu collutal.
cepputal oru viṉāviṟku viṭi collutal.
aṟital oṉṟi urakka allatu valiyuṟac collutal.
māṟutal uriyāṭṭil māṟi māṟic collutal.
viḷḷutal oṉṟi veḷiviṭṭuc collutal.
viḷamputal oṉṟip palarkku aṟivittal.
pukalutal oṉṟi virumpic collutal.
icittal oṉṟik kōvipaṭa allatu oḻuṅku paṭac collutal.
kaḻaṟutal kaṭintu collutal.
iyamputal iṉṉiciyuṭaṉ collutal.
karital aḻutukoṇṭu allatu iraṅkic collutal.
iṅṅaṉamē ēṉic coṟkaḷiṉ nuṇporuḷiyum aṟintu kaṭippiṭikka.
5. iṇimoḻikaḷ (Words in Pairs)
akkam pakkam pārttup pēcu; akkut tokku illātavaṉ (akati); akaṭavikaṭamāyp pēcukiṟāṉ; añcilē piñcilē aṟiyavēṇṭum; aṭakkavoṭukkamāyirukkiṟāṉ; aṭaṅki yoṭuṅkiyiru; aṭaraṭi paṭaraṭiyāy (kaṭumpōrāy, peruṅ kuḻappamāy)k kiṭakkiṟatu; avaṉ ivaṉukku aṭitaṇṭam piṭitaṇṭam (muṟṟilum aṭimip paṭṭavaṉ); aṭukiṭi paṭukiṭiyāyk kiṭattal; (niṉittatip peṟumaḷavum oruvaṉatu vīṭṭiṉmuṉ paṭuttuk kiṭattal, pāyum paṭukkiyumāyk kiṭattal;) aṭippum aṇippumā yirukkavēṇṭum; aṭuppum tuṭuppumāy (camittukkoṇṭu) irukkiṟa nēram; aṭiyalum viṭiyalum kuruṭarukkilli; aṇṭa piṇṭam otta iyalpu ṭiyaṉa; aṇṭi vīṭu aṭutta vīṭu pakiyāyirukkakkūṭātu; aṇṭi yayalellām tēṭip pārttēṉ; antiyuñ cantiyum pūci naṭakkiṟatu; ayarttatu maṟantati eṭuttuk koṇṭupō; aratēci (akatēci) paratēci kaṭku uṇavaḷikka vēṇṭum; arici tavaci viliyēṟiviṭṭatu; aruvuruvākiya iṟivaṉ; avaṉuṭiya arumi perumi teriyavilli; vēliyi arikuṟiyāy niṟuttiviṭṭāṉ; alluñ cillumāy (ciṟu ciṟu tokiyāy)p paṇam celavāki viṭṭatu; allum pakalum pāṭupaṭukiṟāṉ; alli tolli yellām tīra vēṇṭum; aluṅkik kuluṅkip pōyiṟṟu; alukkik kulukkik koṇṭuvantāṉ; aluttup puluttu vantiruntāṉ; alittuk kulittuk keṭuttuviṭṭāṉ; aḻintoḻintu pōyiṟṟu; aḻukiyuṅ kaṇṇīrumāy vantu cērntāṉ; aḻuṅkip puḻuṅki (poṟāmi mikku)c cākiṟāṉ; aḻuttan tiruttamāyp paṭittirukkiṉṟāṉ; aḷḷāṭit taḷḷāṭi naṭakkiṉṟāṉ; aḻutu toḻutu vāṅki viṭṭāṉ; aṟṟa kuṟṟam (kēṭu paḻutu) pārkka āḷilli; aṟamaṟam aṟintu oḻukavēṇṭum; aṟukkap poṟukkap pāṭupaṭṭum kuṭikkak kañciyilli; aṭuttu maṭuttuk kēṭkavēṇṭum.
ākkamuṅkēṭum aṉivarkkum uṇṭu; ākkiyarittup pōṭukiṟavaḷ maṉivi; āṭṭum pāṭṭumāyirukkiṟatu avaṉ vīṭu; āṭalpāṭal kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum kaḷittāṉ; kāti āṭṭi alittu varukiṟāḷ; tūṇi āṭṭiyacittup pārttēṉ; āṭippāṭic ceṉṟāl avaṉ paricaḷippāṉ; āṭiyacintu naṭakkiṉṟāṉ; āṭiyaṇi yalaṅkāramum vēṇṭum; āṭikkum kōṭikkum vaṟṟāta kiṇaṟu; āṇṭāṉ aṭimi eṉṟa vēṟu pāṭilli; ātāḷipātāḷiyāy (āravāramāy) irukkiṟatu; ātiyantam illātavar kaṭavuḷ; āyntōyntu pārttu naṭpucceyyavēṇṭum; nēṟṟiravu kavuṇṭarukku āyiṟṟup pōyiṟṟu eṉṟu kiṭantatu; avaṉ āyiṟṟā pōyiṟṟu eṉṟu araṭṭiṉāṉ; ālē pūlē eṉṟu alappik koṇṭiruk kiṉṟāṉ; ūrmuḻutum āḻumpāḻumāy (aḻintu)k kiṭak kiṉṟatu; āḷum tēḷum aṟṟa iṭam; avaṉi āṟṟit tēṟṟi vi; āṟa amara (poṟumiyāy)k kāriyañ ceyya vēṇṭum; āṉikkum pūṉikkum uḷḷa tūram.
icaku picakāy naṭakkiṉṟāṉ, akappaṭṭuk koṇṭāṉ; iṇṭum iṭukkum kiviṭṭup pārttāṉ; iyaṅku tiṇi nilittiṇi (tāvara caṅkamam) ākiya iruvakic cottum uṇṭu; iyalum ceyalum ottirukkum; iḻuppum paṟippumāyk kiṭakkiṉṟatu; iḷittuk kaḷittup pōṉāṉ; iṉṉār iṉiyar eṉṟu avaṉukkilli.
īki yirakkam (īvirakkam) irukkavēṇṭum; īṭum eṭuppum (oppum uyarvum) aṟṟavaṉ; īṭucōṭu illātavaḷ; īyeṟumpu moykkum.
uṭinaṭiyāl uyarvu tāḻvu aṟiyappaṭum; uṇṭiyuṟiyuḷ vacati iṅkuṇṭu; uṇṭuṭuttu vāḻavēṇṭum; uruṭṭum puraṭṭum eṉṉiṭam palikkātu; uruṇṭu tiraṇṭu irukkum uruḷikkiḻaṅku; uruvum tiruvum otta kātalar; uḷḷatu uriyatellām viṟṟuviṭṭāṉ; uṟṟār uṟaviṉar utavuvar.
ūṇ uṭi ciṟakkavēṇṭum; ūṇ uṟakkam oḻintu vēliceytāṉ; ūtiyamum iḻappum vaṇikattiṟkiyalpu.
ekkacakkamāy (ēṟamuṭiyāmal) irukkiṟatu maram; ekkacakkamāy (tappamuṭiyātapaṭi) māṭṭikkoṇṭāṉ; ekkāḷamum ēṭāciyumāy (parikācamāyp) pēcukiṟāṉ; eypaṭi eṟipaṭi (atira catiram) koṇṭu porutārkaḷ.
ēṅkit tēṅkit tavikkiṟāṉ; ēṭṭikkup pōṭṭiyāyc ceykiṟāṉ; ēṭākōṭam (ēṟumāṟu) paṇṇukiṟāṉ; ēmamum cāmamum kūttāṭukiṉṟaṉar; ēḻi pāḻi piḻikka vēṇṭum; ēṟṟattāḻvu camutāyattil irukkakkūṭātu; ēṟṟat tāḻcci yāyirukkiṟatu viṭṭam; ēṟiṟaṅkap pārkkiṉṟāṉ.
oṭṭi yularntu pōyiṟṟu vayiṟu; oṭṭu uṟavu aṟṟuppōy viṭṭatu; oṇṭi caṇṭiyāyk kāṭṭuvaḻi pōkak kūṭātu; oṉṟāy maṉṟāy (vēṟupāṭiṉṟik kalantu)k kuṭiyirukkiṟārkaḷ.
ōṭṭamum naṭiyumāyp pōyc cērntōm; ōṭṭi uṭical pōṭṭuvikkum aṟi; ōṭṭi cāṭṭi yiruntāl koṭu; ōṭiyāṭit tirikiṟāṉ; ōyvu cāyvu orunāḷum eṉakkilli.
carakku kaṅkukariyaṟṟuk kiṭakkiṉṟatu; kañcitaṇṇīr avaṉukkuc cellavilli; kaṭṭiyu muṭṭiyumāy uḻutu pōṭṭirukkiṉṟāṉ; etaṟkum oru kaṭṭumaṭṭu (aḷavu) vēṇṭum; kaṭā viṭikaḷāl poruḷi viḷakkiṉār; kaṇṭatu kaṭiyatellām collakkūṭātu; kaṇṭavaṉ kaṭiyavaṉellām vantu cāppiṭṭāṉ; kaṇakkuvaḻakkillāmal koṇṭupōṉāṉ; kaṇṭatuṇṭamāy naṟukkiviṭuvēṉ; kaṇṇīruṅ kampaliyumāyk kālaṅkaḻikkiṟōm; kaṇṇum karuttumāyp pēṇa vēṇṭum; kattikkataṟic coṉṉēṉ; kantal kūḷamāy (kantara kōlamāy)k kiṭakkiṉṟatu; kappuṅkavarumāyk kiḷittirukkiṉṟatu; kantalum pottalum uṭuttikkoṇṭu tirikiṉṟāṉ; karaṭu muraṭāṉa tuṇi; kari tuṟi teriyavilli; kalluṅ karaṭumāṉa vaḻi; kalluṅ karampumāyk kiṭakkiṉṟa nilam; kalvi kēḷvikaḷiṟ ciṟantavaṉ; kaliyāṇaṅ kāṭcikkup pōka māṭṭāṉ; kikālkaḷ kaḻukku moḻukkeṉṟu (paruttuk koḻa koḻattu) irukkiṉṟaṉa; kaḷaṅkamaḷaṅkamaṟṟup pēca vēṇṭum; kaḷḷaṅkavaṭillātavaṉ; kaṟpum poṟpum uṭiyavaḷ; kaṟṟōrkkum maṟṟōrkkum viḷaṅkum naṭi; kaṉṟu kayantali (piḷḷikuṭṭi); kaṉṟukāli varum nēram; kaṉavō, naṉavō?
kākkaṉum pōkkaṉum (ayalāṉum vaḻippōkkaṉum) koḷḷi koṇṭu pōṉārkaḷ; piḷḷikaḷ kāccuppūc ceṉṟu (kāccumūcceṉṟu) kattikkoṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ; eṅku pārttālum kāṭuñ ceṭiyumāy irukkiṉṟatu. kāṭumēṭāy alintu tirikiṉṟāṉ; kātilē kaḻuttilē oṉṟumilli; kāmā cōmā (kaṉṉāpiṉṉā) eṉṟu kattik koṇṭiruk kiṉṟāṉ; payirkaḷ kāyntu karukiviṭṭaṉa; kāracāramaṟṟa (matikēṭāṉa) piḷḷikaḷ; kāṉāṉ kōṉāṉ eṉṟu nātāṅkik kaṭukkaṉ toṅkukiṉṟatu.
kiṭṭattaṭṭa (ēṟakkuṟiya); kiṭṭamuṭṭa avaṉi vara viṭātē; kiṇṭik kiḷaṟi (kiṇṭik kiḷittu) vittuviṭṭatu; kiṇaṟu kuṭṭi yiruntāl kuḷikkalām; kiḻaṅkeṭṭi (kiḻaṭu keṭṭi) vīṭṭiliruppatu nallatu.
āṭu kīrikuḻi tiṉṉum; kīriyum pāmpum pōlap paki.
kuñcuṅ kuḻuvāṉumāy irukkiṟatu avaṉ kuṭumpam; kuṭṭi kuṟumāṉ ellām vantuviṭṭaṉa; kuṇṭakkamaṇṭakkamāy avaṉik kaṭṭit tūkkikkoṇṭu pōṉārkaḷ; tari kuṇṭuṅ kuḻiyumāy irukkiṉṟatu; kuṇaṅkuṟṟam yārukku muṇṭu; kuṇaṅ kuṟikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭikka vēṇṭum; kātil kuttaluṅ kuṭiccalumāy irukkiṉṟatu; ariciyik kuttik koḻittu ākka vēṇṭum; vīṭuvācal kuppi kūḷamāyk kiṭakkiṟatu; kumpakkuḻiya aḷantāṉ; kumpik kotittu niṟkiṉṟāḷ maṉivi; kumpiṭṭuk kūttāṭi vāṅkiṉāṉ; kulaṅkōttiram pārttup peṇkoḷvar; kulamuṅ kuṇamum otta kātalar; kuḻanti kuṭṭi peruttavaṉ; kuḷaṅ kuṭṭiyil taṇṇīrilli; kuṟṟaṅ kuṟikaḷ iruntāl poṟuttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum; kuṟṟa naṟṟam pārkkak kūṭātu; kuṟukkum neṭukkum carikikkari irukkiṉṟatu; kuṟukkum maṟukkum tirikiṟāṉ.
kūṭṭanāṭṭam ippōtu naṭakkiṟatilli; kūṭṭik kumittu vittāṉ; kūṭṭik kuṟittup pēcuvāṉ; kūṭak kuṟiya iruntāl col; kūṭamāṭa vēli cey; kūṭik kulāvit tirikiṉṟaṉar.
keñcikketaṟik kēṭṭāṉ.
kēṭpār kēḷviyilli; kēṭpārum mēyppāru maṟṟut tirikiṉṟāṉ; kēḷvimuṟi illi; kēḷuṅ kiḷiyum keṭṭārk killi.
kiyuṅ kaḷavumāyp piṭikkappaṭṭāṉ; kiyuṅkālum vittukkoṇṭu cummāyirukkamāṭṭēṉ eṉkiṟāṉ; kiyuṅ kālumāy (oṉṟumillāta vaṟiyavaṉāy) vantu cērntāṉ; kiyumeyyumāyk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.
kokku kuruvi ellām pōyviṭṭaṉa; koñcik kulāvip pēcukiṉṟaṉar; koñca nañcamāvatu mīttuvikka vēṇṭum; koṭṭukuḻal illāmal caṭaṅku naṭantatu; koṭivaḻiyil kaliyāṇañ ceyvar; koṭukkal vāṅkal (koṭi vāṅkal) nirampa irukkiṉṟatu; koṇṭāṉ koṭuttāṉ iṭattilē neṭunāḷ taṅkakkūṭātu; koṇṭu koṭuttu vāḻavēṇṭum; vaṟiyavar kottaṭimi kulavaṭimiyāy vāḻa nērntatu; kāyik kottik kutaṟi vittirukkiṉṟāṉ; kottōṭē kuliyōṭē koṇṭu pōṉāṉ; maram koppuṅ kuḻiyu māyt taḻittirukkiṉṟatu; koḷvaṉi koṭuppaṉi avarkaḷiṭiyilli.
kōṭṭi kottaḷamellām kāval ceyyappaṭṭaṉa; terukkaḷ kōṇalmāṇalāyirukkiṉṟaṉa; kōyil kuḷam kaṭavuḷi nampātavaṉukkilli; kōluṅ koṭukkumāyk kaṭṭikkoṇṭu tirikiṉṟāṉ; kōḷkuṇṭuṇi colkiṟavaṉi nampakkūṭātu.
caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaṭku uṭpaṭṭu naṭakkavēṇṭum; caṇṭu caruku arikkiṉṟāḷ; caṇṭi caccaravu irukkakkūṭātu. caṇṭi calliyattiṟku (caṇṭi cāṭikku)p pōkamāṭṭāṉ; canti catukkaṅkaḷiṟ cili niṟuttappeṟum; cantu pontellām tēṭippārttāṉ; cappuñcavarum vāṅkikkoṇṭu vantiruk kiṉṟāṉ; tōl cavaṇṭu tuvaṇṭu kiṭakkiṉṟatu; mā(māvu) caḷittup puḷittup pōyiṟṟu; caḻivu neḷivu illāta peṭṭiyāy vāṅku.
cākkuppōkkuc collakkūṭātu; avaṉ cākap piḻikkak kiṭantāṉ; avaṉ kuṟṟañ ceytālum cāṭi māṭiyāy viṭṭuviṭu; cuvar cāyntu carintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. vēliyic cāyalāy māyalāy (cālāmālā)c ceytu vittirukkiṉṟāṉ; avaṉic cāviḻavu taḷḷivittirukkiṉṟatu.
cikkaṭimukkaṭiyāyk kiṭakkiṟatu nūl; uṭampellām cikkuñ ciraṅkumāy irukkiṉṟatu; ciṭṭi ciraṭṭi yellām taṇṇīr ūṟṟivittirukkiṉṟatu; cōṟṟic cintic citaṟic cīraḻikkiṉṟāṉ; cintumaṇi citaṟumaṇi yellām poṟukkik koṇṭāḷ; ciṟutaṉam cīrāṭṭu (cītaṉam cīrāṭṭu) avaḷukku nirampak kiṭittatu; ciṉṉatu ciṟiyatiṟku oṉṟuṅ kāṭṭakkūṭātu; vaṇṭi ciṉṉapiṉṉamāyc citintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
cīṭṭunāṭṭup pōṭṭu vittirukka vēṇṭum; cīttup pūtteṉṟu cīṟiṉatu pāmpu; avaṉic cīrāṭṭip pārāṭṭi vaḷarttārkaḷ; cīriyar pūriyar (mēlōr kīḻōr) cīruñ ciṟappumāy vāḻntiruntāṉ; cīruñ ceṭṭumāyp piḻikka vēṇṭum; cīviccikkeṭuttuc caṭi piṉṉiṉāḷ; cīvic ciṅkārittu varukiṉṟāḷ; cīṟic ciṉantu viḻuntāṉ.
cuḷḷi cuppal poṟukki erikkiṉṟāḷ; paṭukkiyic cuṟṟic curuṭṭik koṇṭupōyviṭṭāṉ; cuṟṟu muṟṟum pārttāṉ.
ceṭi koṭi pōṭṭāl kāy kāykkum; ceṭi cetti yellām akaṟṟavēṇṭum.
cottu cukam avaṉukku oṉṟumilli; coḷḷi coṭṭi ētāvatu colluvāṉ.
taṭṭāmal taṭukkāmal (taṭṭāmal muṭṭāmal) tārāḷamāy vācalvaḻi koṇṭupōka muṭiyumā? maṭkalaṅkaḷit taṭṭikkoṭṭi vāṅkavēṇṭum; taṭṭuttaṭaṅkaliṉṟip pēciṉāṉ; taṭṭuttaṭumāṟi viḻuntāṉ; taṭṭuttaṭiyiṉṟip pēcukiṟāṉ; taṭṭumuṭṭu vāṅka vēṇṭum; taṭuttu maṭuttuc colla āḷilli; taṭi viṭi (āṭcēpa camātāṉam) nikaḻntaṉa; taṇṇīr vennīr koṭukka āḷvēṇṭum; tattit tottic cuvaril ēṟiṉāṉ; tappittavaṟi intappakkam varātē; tapput tavaṟumāyp pēcukiṟāṉ; taṭaṅkal maṭaṅkaliṉṟic cellalām.
tāṅkit taṭukki avaḷik kūṭṭikkoṇṭu vantāṉ; tāy piḷḷi (uṟaviṉar) oruvarum varavilli; tāyum cēyum nalam.
tiṇṭu taliyaṇi pōṭṭup paṭuttuk koṇṭāṉ; tiṇṭu muṇṭu (tillumullu) paṇṇukiṉṟāṉ; kompu tirukalmuṟu kalāy irukkiṉṟatu.
tuṭṭuttukkāṇi kiyilirukka vēṇṭum; maram tuṇṭu tuṇukkāka irukkiṟatu; mēciyiṉiṉṟu viḻunta tuṇṭu tuṇakkikaḷi nāy tiṉṟatu; pōrkkaḷattil tuṇṭun tuṇiyu māka uṭalkaḷ kiṭantaṉa; tuṇimaṇikaḷip pōṟṟi vikka vēṇṭum; piyaṉ cuṭumaṇalil tuḷḷit tuṭittu viṭṭāṉ.
tūcituppaṭṭi (tūci turumpu); neṟkaḷattit tūrttut tuṭittuviṭṭārkaḷ.
tēkkitteviṭṭip pōṉārkaḷ viruntiṉarellām; āṭṭit tēṭiyōṭip piṭikka vēṇṭum; tēr tiruviḻāviṟku ērāḷamāṉa pēr varuvārkaḷ; cantaṉakkaṭṭi tēyntu māyntu pōyviṭṭatu.
tokuttum vakuttum collavēṇṭum; toṭuttu maṭuttuc ceytumuṭikka vēṇṭum.
tōṭṭanturavu ērāḷamāka avaṉukkuṇṭu; tōlum elumpumāyp pōṉāṉ.
nakinaṭṭu aṇintiruntāl matippārkaḷ; naṅki nāttūṇāḷ kuṟicollātapaṭi naṭantukoḷḷa vēṇṭum; naccum piccum eṉakku vēṇṭām; nayapayattāl āḷavēṇṭum; naritiri varumuṉṉamē nalviṉi ceyya vēṇṭum; nallatu pollatu naṭantāl nālupērkkuc cāppāṭu kiṭikkum; nalam polam iraṇṭum aṉupavikka vēṇṭum; nalintum valintum poruḷ koḷḷak kūṭātu; naṉṉiyuṅ kuṉṉiyum (naṉṉi kuṉṉi) niṟiya vantiruntaṉa.
nāṭṭilum kāṭṭilum makkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar; nāṭit tēṭik kaṇṭupiṭikka vēṇṭum; nāṭu nakarellām itē pēccu; nāṇikkōṇi naṭakkiṉṟāḷ; nāyum pēyum pōṉṟa tīyamakkaḷ; nāḷuṅkiḻamiyum pārttuc ceytālum varuvatu vantē tīrum; nāḷuṅ kōḷum nammi eṉṉa ceyyum?
nirantu kalantu pēci muṭivu ceyyuṅkaḷ; nilapulam avaṉukku nirampavuṇṭu; nīṭṭi nimirntu cāyntu koṇṭāṉ.
nīkkuppōkku (iṭiveḷi, ōyvu oḻivu;) illi.
neṭṭi kaṭṭi (kūṭutal kuṟital) iruntālum cariyeṉṟu pōkavēṇṭum.
nēruṅ kūrumāy (neṭṭiyum kuṭṭiyumāy)k kāyi aṟuttup pōṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
nintu piyntu pōyviṭṭatu tuṇi; iñciyi niya naṟuṅkat taṭṭip pōṭu.
noṇṭi caṇṭikku (noṇṭi nuṭattiṟku) utava vēṇṭum.
nōy nompalam (nōvu noṭi) vantāl kavaṉikka āḷilli.
paṅku pākam kēṭkiṉṟāṉ; paṭṭatu keṭṭatellām coṉṉāṉ; paṭṭam patavikaṭku ācippaṭukiṉṟāṉ; paṭṭi toṭṭi yellām cuṟṟippārkka vēṇṭum; paṭṭiṉiyum paciyumāyk kiṭantu varuntukiṉṟāṉ; paṭṭup paḻakit teriyavēṇṭum; pampi paṟaṭṭiyāyt taliyi vittuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar; pammip patuṅkit tirikiṉṟāṉ; payaṟu pacci naṉṟāy viḷintirukkiṉṟaṉa; payir pacci naṉṟāy viḷiyavilli; makkaḷiṉam palkip peruki varukiṉṟatu; paḻakka vaḻakkam naṉṟāyirukka vēṇṭum; paḻi pāvaṅkaṭku añcavēṇṭum; koṭi paṟṟip paṭarntu celkiṉṟatu.
pāṅku paricaṉi (naṉṉaṭi) yaṟintu oḻukavēṇṭum; ennēramum pāṭṭum paṭippumtāṉ; pāyum paṭukkiyumāyk kiṭakkiṉṟāṉ; pālum tēṉum ōṭum tēcam; pālum paḻamum uṇṭu vaḷarntavaṉ.
piyttup piṭuṅkiviṭuvārkaḷ; piḷḷi kuṭṭi yillātavaṉ; piṟappu vaḷarppu vevvēṟu ūr; ceṭiyuṅ koṭiyum piṉṉip piṇintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
pul pūṇṭu maḻikkālattil muḷikkum.
pūcci poṭṭi (pūccipoṭṭu) vaḻiyiliruntālum irukkum; pūvum piñcumāyirukkiṟatu pīrkku.
peṇṭu piḷḷikaṭkut tēṭivikka vēṇṭum; peṟṟatu piṟantatu piṟkālattil utavum.
pērum pukaḻum nāṭeṅkum paraviyuḷḷatu.
pokkum poṭiyum kāṟṟil paṟantuviṭum; poṅkip porittup pōṭuvatu yār? poṭṭup poṭiyellām nīkkiviṭa vēṇṭum; cōli muḻutum pontum putarumāy irukkiṉṟatu; poyyum puliyum (poyyum puḷukum) collit tirikiṉṟāṉ.
pōkkiri cākkiriyuṭaṉ cērakkūṭātu; avaṉukkup pōkku pukal oṉṟumilli; avarip pōṟṟip pukaḻntu pēciṉārkaḷ.
makki (maṅki) maḻuṅkippōṉa katti; maṭṭu matippu irukkavēṇṭum; maṇ maṉi yellām viṟṟuviṭṭāṉ; vīṭu maṇṇum matilumāyk kiṭakkiṉṟatu; mantira tantiram vallavaṉ; mayaṅkit tiyaṅki niṟkiṉṟāṉ; mayakkamum tiyakkamumāyk kiṭakkiṉṟāṉ; maramaṭṭi yiruntāl erikkalām; maruntu māyam terintavaṉ; malikkum maṭuvukkumuḷḷa tūram; maḻi taṇṇīr aṅku uṇṭā? iṅku maḻiyun taṇṇīrumāy irukkiṉṟatu.
mācu maṟu aṟṟavaṉ; māṭu kaṉṟu vittirukkiṉṟāṉ; māṉa īṉam ciṟitum avaṉukkilli.
miccam mikutiyic cērttuvi; miṉukkit taḷukkit tirikiṉṟāḷ.
mukkalum muṉaṅkalum etaṟku? mukkit takki mūṭṭiyit tūkkiviṭṭāṉ; yāṉi muṭṭi mōtik katavi muṟittu viṭṭatu; viṟaku muṇṭum muṭiccumāyk kiṭakkiṉṟatu; kuḻantiyi mutti mōntu aṇittuk koṇṭāḷ; nilattil muḷḷuṅ kurukkum muḷittuviṭṭatu; kāṭeṅkum muḷḷum muṭiyumāyk kiṭakkiṉṟatu; muṟṟa muṭiya iruntu coṟpoḻivik kēṭka vēṇṭum.
mūccup pēccu illi; mūli muṭaṅkiyiṟ pōy uṟaṅkukiṉṟāṉ; mūli muṭukkellām tēṭip pārttēṉ; āyutaṅkaḷellām mūḻiyuṅ kāḷiyumāyk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
vakitokiyāyp pēcavēṇṭum; vantatu pōṉatip pūcavēṇṭum; vantaṉi vaḻipāṭu naṭantuvarukiṉṟatu; vampu tumpu vēṇṭā; vaḻituṟi teriyavilli; vaḻivaki collikkoṭu; kuḷam vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭu pōyviṭṭatu.
vāṭṭañcāṭṭamā yirukkiṟāṉ piyaṉ; aṅkaṇam vāṭṭañcāṭṭamāyirukka vēṇṭum; katiri vāṭṭi vatakkit tiṉṟārkaḷ; valiyavar eḷiyavari vāṭṭi vatittu varukiṉṟaṉar; malar vāṭi vataṅkippōyviṭṭatu; avaṉ nilattiṟku vāykkāl varappu illi; varuvatu vāykkum kikkum eṭṭavilli; vāykkuṅ kikkumāyirukkiṟa nēram; vāri vakirntu ucciyeṭukka vēṇṭum; vāḻvilum tāḻvilum kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar pirital kūṭātu; vāṉakamum viyakamum ataṟku īṭalla.
viṭṭakuṟi toṭṭakuṟi muṭikka vēṇṭum; viṭēṉ toṭēṉ eṉṟu toṭarntāṉ; viṇṇukkum maṇṇukku muḷḷa tūram; vitivilakku aṟintu oḻukavēṇṭum; viruntu vēṟṟu vantāl vēḷāṇmi ceyya vēṇṭum; viruppu veṟuppu illāmal oru poruḷi ārāya vēṇṭum; vīṭuṅ kuṭittaṉamumāy avaṉi iruttivittārkaḷ; vīṭum viḷakkumāy iruppatu nallatu; vīracūramāyp pēciṉāṉ.
veṭki viṟittup pōṉāṉ; veṭṭuṅ kuttumāyk kiṭakkiṉṟatu; veṭṭuppaḻi kuttuppaḻi nērntuviṭum; cōṟu ventu virintuviṭṭatu; veḷḷiyuñ coḷḷiyumāyp pōkavēṇṭum.
vērum tūrum kaṟiyāṉ arittuviṭṭatu; vērttup pūttu (vērttu viruviruttu) ōṭivantāṉ; vēliveṭṭi yillātavaṉ.
6. maraput toṭarmoḻikaḷ (Idiomatic Phrases and Expressions)
akattu makiḻcci mukattu nikaḻcciyāka, akappaṟṟu puṟappaṟṟuk kaḷi aṟavē oḻittu, aṭakku vāraṟṟa kaḻukkāṇiyāy, aṭi taṇṭam piṭi taṇṭamāy aṭimippaṭṭu, aṭitali taṭumāṟi, aṭimutal muṭivari, aṭimi mutal aracaṉvari, aṭiyaṟṟa marampōl paṭimēl viḻuntu, aṭuppaṅkariyil āmpipūppa, aṇṭapiṇṭa aṉittup poruḷkaḷum (aṉittuyir kaḷum), amiḻtiṉumiṉiya tamiḻ(moḻi), arituṇar poruḷa vaṟṟi eḷituṇar poruḷavākki, arikkāciṟkum vaḻiyillā mal, aḻutapiḷḷiyum vāy mūṭum atikāram, aṉpuḷḷa araca ṉum aṟivuḷḷa amiccaṉum, aṟamutal nāṉkum tiṟamuṟa āṟṟi.
ākkavaḻippāṟṟal (cāpāṉukkiraka cakti) aṭintu, ākkavum aḻikkavum vallavarāy, ācikāṭṭi mōcañ ceytu, ākāyat tāmariyum āmi mayirkkampalamum, āṭiyaṇiyalaṅ kāraṉāy; āṭiyiṉṟi vāṭiyiṉ melintu kiyatukoṇṭu peyyatu pottik kālatu koṇṭu mēlatu taḻīi, āṇṭi mutal aracaṉvari, āṇṭil iḷiyaṉāy aṟivil mutiya ṉāy, ārumaṟṟa attavaṉakkāṭu, ālamuṇṭa nīlakaṇṭaṉ, āḷuk kēṟṟa vēṭamuṅ kālattiṟkēṟṟa kōlamum, āṟṟil orukāl cēṟṟil orukāl vittu.
iṭṭaṭi nōka eṭuttaṭi koppuḷikka, iṭam poruḷ ēval, iṭṭatu caṭṭam vittatu varici, intiraṉ mutaliya iṟiyavar patamum antamiliṉpat taḻivil vīṭum, immikkum maṟumikkum iṉpam payappatāy, immi maṟumi naṟkatikaḷ (vīṭu), iyaṉṟavari muyaṉṟu, irukiyiṉumēntit taliyil tāṅkik kaṇṇiloṟṟi, irutōṇiyiṟ kālvittu, irupakaṭṭoru cakaṭu, irumiyāl nērntu (upayāṉu cammatamāy), iruviṉi yoppu malaparipākam, ilavukātta kiḷipōl ēmāṟi, ilimaṟi kāypōl maṟintukiṭanta, ilakkaṇa vilakkiyam viḷakkamāyk kaṟṟu, iṟivaṉukkē veḷiccam, iṟutivariṉum uṟutikūṟi, iṉṉōraṉṉa. īrṅki vitirāta ivaṟi (ulōpi).
uccantaliyiliruntu uḷḷaṅkāl vari, uccimēl pulavar koḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, uṭalkaraṇa vulakaviṉpaṅkaḷ (taṉukaraṇa puvaṉapōkam), uṭal poruḷ āvi mūṉṟiyum oppuvittu, uṇṭa vīṭṭiṟku iraṇṭakam ceyyāmal, uppiṭṭavari uḷḷaḷavum niṉintu, uppukkum uḻiyāmal ūrcuṟṟit tirintu, uyir oṭuṅki uṭal naṭuṅki, uyirukkuyirāy, urappiyuṅkaṉittum eṭuttum olikkiṉṟa (vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷ), uruvuntiruvuṅ kulamuṅ kuṇamum ottavarāy, ulakavaḻakku ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṟkoppa, ulakamellām orukuṭikkīḻ āṇṭu, uvarkkaṭalaṉṉa celvar, uḷḷaṅkāl veḷḷelumpu tōṉṟa, uḷḷaṅki nellikkaṉipōl, uḷḷatik koṇṭu nallatip paṇṇi, uḷḷum puṟampum ottu, uḷḷoṉṟu puṟampoṉṟu pēcāmal, uṟṟunōkki ūkittaṟintu, uṟṟōr makiḻa maṟṟōr pukaḻa, uḷmatam ilmatam (ātikam nātikam).
ūṇuṟakkamoḻintu, ūrār uṭamikkup pērācikoṇṭu, ūrār uṭamikkup pēyāyt tirintu, ūrār pakikkum tīrāp paḻikkum āḷāy, ūrukkuḻittu ūtāriyāyp piḻittu.
eṅkeḻileṉ ñāyiṟemakku eṉṟiruntu, ecciṟkiyāṟ kākki viraṭṭātavaṉ, eṭṭāta paḻattiṟkuk koṭṭāvi viṭṭu, eṭutta kāriyam iṭi yūṟiṉṟi iṉitu muṭitaṟporuṭṭu, eṭuppār kippiḷḷiyāy, eṭṭiraṇṭum aṟiyāta, eṇcāṇuṭampu orucāṇākak kuṉṟi, eṇṇattoliyātu ēṭṭilaṭaṅ kātu, eṇṇukkum eṭṭā iṟivaṉ, ennāṭṭiṉu miṉiya teṉṉāṭṭil, ellārkkum nallavaṉāy, ellāmvalla iṟivaṉ, eḻumiyum vaḻuvāta uḻuvalaṉpu, eṉṟit toṭakkattār.
ērāḷamāyum tārāḷamāyum, ēḻām narakiṟkum kīḻām narakam, ēḻi pāḻi kaḷi vayiṟṟilaṭittu vāyil maṇṇippōṭṭu, ēṉōtāṉō eṉṟirāmal. aiyantiripaṟak kaṟṟu.
oṭṭiya vayiṟum ularnta utaṭumāy, oru kaṇṇil eṇṇeyum oru kaṇṇiṟ cuṇṇāmpum taṭavāmal oru pakkam pālum oru pakkam nīrum oḻuka, olkāp perumit tolkāppiyar, oṉṟukkum paṟṟāta nāyēṉ.
ōṭum poṉṉum oppa niṉikkum, ōluṭaṉ āṭippāl uṭaṉ paruki.
kaṭaṉmaṭi tiṟantāṟpōlak kavipāṭa vallavarāy, kaṭṭiya ṇittu ucci mōntu, kaṭinteṭutta kaḻiperumaṭiyaṉ, kaṭaṉvāṅki uṭaṉvāṅki uṭalittēṟṟi, kaṇkaṇṭa teyvam, kaṇkavar kaviṉpeṟu kaṭṭaṭam, kaṇṭatē kāṭci koṇṭatē kōlam eṉṟu, kaṇṭu kēṭṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟiyum aimpulaṉum, kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭatik kiyāṟ ceytu, kaṇṇīr vāra meymmayir cilirppa, kaṇṇīrviṭṭuk kataṟiyaḻutu, kaṇṇukkuk kaṇṇākavum uyirukku yirākavumiruntu, kaṇṇik kavarntu pārviyip paṟittu uḷḷattik koḷḷi koṇ ṭatu, kaṇmaṇipōṟ kāttu, kaṇmūṭip paḻakkamellām maṇ mūṭippōka, kaṇmūṭittaṉamāyk kālaṅ kaḻittu, katiravaṉun tiṅkaḷumuḷḷa kālamellām, kayalviḻiyuṅ kuyil moḻiyum, kari pari tēr kāl mutaliyaṉa (rata kaja turaka patāti) karuvilē tiru vuṭiyār, kalviyaṟi voḻukkaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟantu, kaṟṟōrkkē yaṉṟi maṟṟōrkku viḷaṅkāmal.
kāṭiṭiyiṭṭum nāṭiṭiyiṭṭum, kāṭuvāveṉa vīṭu pōveṉa, kāmavekuḷi mayakkaṅkaḷ, kāriyattiṟ kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyiruntu, kālā liṭṭatik kiyāṟ ceytu, kālāliṭṭatit talimēṟ koṇṭu, kāṟṟiṉuṅ kaṭukicceṉṟu, kālaṉum añcum kaṭuṅ kaṇ maṟavar.
kuṭikkak kūḻukkum kaṭṭak kantikkum vaḻiyaṟṟu, kuṇṭuṅ kuḻiyuṅ tiṇṭun tirucum, kumarimutal imayam vari, kuḻi koṇṭu kōḻiyeṟiyum vāḻkkiyar, kuṟṟattit taḷḷik kuṇattik koṇṭu, kuṟukuṟu naṭantu kutali moḻintu.
kūriya aṟivum cīriya oḻukkamum, kūṟṟiṉum koṭiyavar, kūṟṟattik kiyāl viḷikkum koṭumaṟavar.
keṭṭuk kīḻi vaḻiyāki.
kikaṭṭi vāy putittu, kikkeṭṭiṉatu vāyk keṭṭāmal, kimmāṟu karutāmal.
koṭākkaṇṭaṉukku viṭākkaṇṭaṉ, koṭiyāri nīkki aṭiyārik kāttal, koṭuppārum aṭuppārumiṉṟi, koṇṭimēṟ kāṟṟaṭikka (kavaliyiṉṟi), kollatteruvil ūci viṟkiṟavaṉ.
kōṭākōṭic cūriyavoḷiyuḷḷa, kōṭiyuntēṭik koṭimaramum naṭṭi.
caṭṭaṅkaḷ kaṟṟum paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟṟum, catura vētaṉip paṭṭuc campātittu, cantaṉa kaḷapakācaṟi (katūri), campaḷamum umpaḷamum peṟṟu.
cāturiyamāyum māturiyamāyum pēci.
cintiyumoḻiyuñ cellānilimittākaliṉ, ciṟukakkaṭṭip peruka vāḻntu, ciṉṉāṭ palpiṇic ciṟṟaṟiviṉar.
cuviyoḷi yūṟōci nāṟṟam.
ceyvaṉa ceytu tavirvaṉa tavirntu.
coṟcurukkamum poruṭperukkamum, coṟcuvi poruṭ cuvikaḷiṟ ciṟantu, coṉmatiyum taṉmatiyum (coṟputtiyum cuyaputtiyum) illāmal, coṉṉayamum poruṇayamum, coṉṉōkkum poruṇōkkum toṭinōkkum naṭinōkkum.
cōṟṟukkuc celavum pārukkup pāramumāy.
taṭṭukkeṭṭut taṭumāṟi, taṭṭut taṭiyiṉṟit tārāḷamāyp pēcavalla, taṭpaveppa nili (cītōṣṇa titi), tamiḻnāṭu ceyta tavap payaṉākat tōṉṟi taliyāl vantatik kālāl taḷḷi, taliyuṅ kālun teriyātu tammuḷ mayaṅki, taviṭupoṭi yāyt takartteṟintu, tavitta muyali aṭippavaṉ, taḷarnaṭi naṭantu maḻali moḻintu, taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuri yāḷaṉ, taṉṉuyirpōl maṉṉuyireṇṇun taṇṇaḷiyāṉ.
tāṉṟōṉṟit tampirāṉ.
tiṟampaṭavum tēmpaṭavum pēci.
tuṇikkut tuṇiyāyt toṇṭikkuḻikku viṉiyāy, turumpit tūṇākkip pēṉip perumāḷākki, tuḷḷit tirikiṉṟa kālattil tuṭukkaṭakkip paḷḷikku viyāmal.
teṟku vaṭakkut teriyātavaṉ, teṉṟal vīcittēṉ corintu vaṇṭu pāṭum vaḷamarakkā.
tēṉiṉumiṉiya tīñcol, tēṉiṉumiṉiya teṉmoḻi.
tokuttum vakuttum virittum (toki vaki virivāy), toṭakkam naṭuviṟuti, tolkāppiyattum tolkāppiyattum palkāṟ payiṉṟu, tolkāppiyattip palkāṟ payiṉṟu.
tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāyiruntu.
naṭiyuṭi pāvaṉi, nari tiri mūppu, nallatik koṇṭu allatit taḷḷi, nampā matam (nātikam) pēci nāttaḻumpēṟi.
nāṭṭukku nāṟkātam, nāṭavittuk kēṭuceytu, nāṭiyip piṭittu nalla coṟcolli, nāttaḷarntu vāy kuḻaṟi, nānayamum nāṇayamum, nāyoṉṟu collap pēyoṉṟu colla, nālvētam āṟu cāttiram patiṉeṇ purāṇam aṟupattu nāṉku kaliñāṉam, nāḷikkoru tiṟamum vēḷikkoru niṟamum, nāḷoru mēṉiyum poḻutoruvaṇṇamum.
niṉṟu neḷittu nīṭṭi nimirntu.
nīrcūḻnta nilavulakam, nīṟupūtta neruppu.
nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam, nuṉippul mēyccalāy.
nūṉmati tuyppiṟku (curutiyukti yaṉupavaṅkaṭku)p porunta.
neñcārap pukaḻntu vāyāra vāḻtti, neṭuñcāṇ kiṭiyāy nilattil viḻuntu, neṟṟiyiṉ vērvi nilattilviḻap pāṭupaṭṭu.
nōyum noṭiyum pāyumpaṭukkiyumāyk kiṭantu.
pakaleṉṟumillāmal iraveṉṟumillāmal pāṭupaṭṭu, paki naṭpu ayal eṉṉum muttiṟattum ottu, paṭṭapāṭum keṭṭa kēṭum, paṭṭimāṭupōṟ kaṭṭuk kāvaliṉṟic cuṟṟittirintu, paṭinālum puṭicūḻa, paṭippukkāp paḻippu, paṇattip paṇameṉṟum kācik kāceṉṟum pārāmal, paruttamēṉiyuṅ karutta kaṇkaḷum, paruntuṅ kiḷiyum pāṅkāy vāḻa, paluṭaṉ uṟṟuc col uṭaṉkaṟṟu, pallip piṭuṅkip paruntāṭṭa māṭṭi.
pārtta kaṇṇum pūttuppōy.
piṟappup piṇimūppuc cākkāṭu.
pulavarkkum viḷaṅkāpporuḷ, pulaṉintum poṟikalaṅki, puliyum māṉum orutuṟi yuṇṇa, puliyaṉum virumpāp puṉpulāl yākki.
poyyum puliyum puṉicuruṭṭum
pollāp puḻumali nōyppuṉ kurampi
pollāri nīkki nallārik kāttal (tuṣṭanikkiraka ciṣṭa paripālaṉam), poṉṉē pūvē kaṇṇē kaṉiyē eṉṟu pukaḻntu, poṉṉoru taṭṭilē pūvoru taṭṭilē vittup pōṟṟi.
makattuvam poruntiya akattiya muṉivar, maṇimantira maruntu, mayilāṭak kuyilpāṭa maṇaṅkamaḻum malarccōli, maliyilakku, maliviḻuṅki makatēvaṉ, maṉamoḻi meykaḷ (maṉōvākkuk kāyaṅkaḷ), maṉṉuyirit taṉṉuyirpōl eṇṇi, maṉampōṉa pōkkellām pōy.
māṭamāḷiki kūṭakōpuram, māṉaṅkeṭṭa maḻuṅkal.
muyaṟkōṭum kutirikkompum, muṉṉukkuppiṉ muraṇpaṭa.
mēleḻuntavāriyākap paṭittu.
moṭṭit talikkum muḻaṅkāṟkum muṭipōṭṭu
yārukku vanta viruntō eṉṟiruntu.
vaṭamoḻiyilum teṉmoḻiyilum vallavarāy, vaṭikaṭṭiya muṭṭāḷ, vayiṟoṭṭi vāypularntu, varam vāṅki vantavaṉ, vaḻituṟi teriyāmal, vaḻimēl viḻi vittu.
vāykkeṭṭiṉatu vayiṟṟukkeṭṭāmal, vāḻnāḷi vīḻ nāḷāka (vīṇāḷāka)k kaḻittu, vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyāy vanta, vāṉuṟavōṅki vaḷampeṟa vaḷarntu.
viṇṇōr pukaḻa maṇṇōr makiḻa, vitittaṉa ceytu vilakkiyaṉa oḻittu, vilāppuṭikka vuṇṭu.
veṭṭaveḷiccam paṭṭappakalāy viḷaṅka, veṇcāmari vīci ālavaṭṭam parimāṟi, veḷḷiṭimali, veḷḷiṭi malipōl teḷḷitil teriya, veṟuvāyc colvīrar.
vētākama purāṇētikācaṅkaḷ.  (col.)
marapu 
pala talimuṟiyākat toṭarntu varum kulattoṭarcci koṭiyum maramum pōl nīṇṭum toṭarntum iruttaliṉ ataṉik koṭi eṉṟum, koṭivaḻi eṉṟum marapu eṉṟum kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
vittum vērum aṭiyum kiḷiyum kuccuṅ koḻuntumākat toṭarn tōṅkum marampōla mēṉmēl toṭarntu cellum kulat toṭarcci marapu eṉappaṭṭatu.
toṭarntu varum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum col vaḻak kāṟṟiyum marapu eṉpatu muṇṭu. tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa marapiyal eṉṉum iyaṟpeyar ipporuḷ paṟṟiyatē.  (col. 3).
maravaki
āṇmaram kāyātatu; peṇmaram kāyppatu; kōḷiveḷip paṭiyāyp pūvātu kāyppatu; vayiram viḷinta maram; veḷiṟu viḷiyātamaram.
mari
mari - mru (i.vē.)
maṭi - mari. o. nō. kaṭi - kari.
mari + aṇam = maraṇam. itu oru tiraviṭac col.
L. n. ori (mori).  (va.va.223).
maruttuvamuṟi uṇavu
paccarici cūṭuṇṭākkum ātalāl veppanāṭṭiṟku ēṟkāteṉṟu puḻuṅkal arici ākkappaṭukiṉṟatu. atic cama taṇṇīr vittuc camittal vēṇṭum. cuvi mikuttaṟku (ariciyi)t tīṭṭumpōtu nīkkum taviṭṭit taṉiyākak koḻukkaṭṭi piṭittut tiṉpatu vaḻakkam. vāḻikkāy vaḷi mikuppateṉṟu piñcu niliyiṟ camikkappaṭum.
cīṉiyavari eṉṉum kottavari pittamikuppateṉṟu mutalil avittu iṟukkappaṭum.
kāṟal uṇṭākkum kāykaṭkup puḷi cērkkappaṭum.
kāyac carakkākak kūṭṭuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ mañcaḷ neñcuc caḷiyi muṟikkum, kottu malli pittatti yaṭikkum; cīrakam vayiṟṟuc cūṭṭit taṇikkum; miḷaku toṇṭik kaṭṭit tolik kum; pūṇṭu vaḷiyakaṟṟi vayiṟṟaḷiccali niṟuttip pacimikukkum. veṅkāyam kuḷircciyi uṇṭākkik kurutiyit tūymip paṭuttum. peruṅkāyam vaḷiyi veḷiyēṟṟum. iñci pittatti oṭukkik kāyccalik kaṇṭikkum. tēṅkāy nīrk kōvi eṉṉum taṭumatti (mūkkuc caḷiyi) nīkkum; kaṟivēppili maṇa mūṭṭi uṇavu viruppi uṇṭākkum. iviyellām cērtta tuvaram paruppuk kuḻampum cīrakam pūṇṭu kalanta miḷaku nīrum, cūṭṭit taṇittuc cerimāṉa āṟṟali mikukkum mōrum kūṭiya muppaṭiyaṟ cōṟṟik koḻumippaṭuttik kuṭaṟpuṇ āṟṟum neyyōṭum uṭalukku urañceytu kaḻimācuk kaṭṭi (malapantatti) nīkkum. kīriyoṭum, kuḷirccitantu pittam pōkkum elumicci ūṟukāyoṭum aṟucuvippaṭa uṇṭa tamiḻaṉ aṟṟatu pōṟṟi uṇiṉ nōy uṇṭākātu. (ti.ma.942).
maruntu
maruttuva vērum taḻiyum avaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭa kalavikaḷum potuvākac ciṟappāṉa naṟumaṇa muṭimiyāl maruntu eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉa. maru eṉpatu naṉmaṇam. marukkoḻuntu eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkiyum, maruvār koṉṟi (tēvā. 530; 1) eṉṉum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiyum nōkkuka.
maru - maruntu. ciṟanta maruntut taḻikaḷ ellām malikaḷil iruppatālum, malikaḷil vāḻum cittarē avaṟṟip paṟṟiya aṟivil tērcci peṟṟiruntatālum, tamiḻaka maruttuva akara mutali maliyakarāti eṉṟum tamiḻa maruttuvam citta maruttuvam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.  (ti.ma. ati. 95).
marumam
marumam - marmaṉ (i.vē.)
maruvutal = taḻuvutal. maru - mār - mārpu - mārpam = taḻuvum uṟuppākiya neñcu. mār = mārpu. ka. mār.
ippātakaṉ māriṉeyva ṉeṉṟu  (kamparā. irāvaṇaṉ vati. 192)
ūrilē kaliyāṇam, mārilē cantaṉam  (paḻamoḻi)
mārpu = neñcu. kaḷḷaṟṟē kaḷvaniṉ mārpu (kuṟaḷ. 1288).
mārpam = neñcu. poṉṟuñcu mārpam (cilap. 11:64).
maru - marumam = 1. mārpu. marumatti ṉeṟivēl (kamparā. kiyaṭi. 11).
2. maṟiviṭam, 3. maṟiporuḷ (irakaciyam).
4. uyirnili. eṅku marumat tiṭikkuḷippa (pu. ve. 7.23).
o.nō. L arca = chest, arcanum = mystery, secret. vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlam mru = mari. marmaṉ = maraṇattiṟkētuvāṉa iṭam, cētappaṭattakka uṟuppu, uyir nāṭi nili.
mari eṉpatu teṉcoṟ ṟiripu.  (va.va.223).
mallaṉ
mallaṉ - malla
mul - mal = 1. parumi. 2. valimi (piṅ.).
3. maṟpōrt toḻil.
mallalit teḻuntu vīṅki  (cīvaka. 268)
4. maṟpōr ceypavaṉ.
malloṭu kañcaṉun tuñcaveṉṟa maṇivaṇṇaṉ  (tiv. periyati. 11 : 2 : 3)  (va.va. 233-234)
mal - mallaṉ = maṟpōr ceyvōṉ.
maṟattoṭu mallar maṟaṅkaṭanta  (pu.ve. 9 : 4)
mal - mallam = maṟpōr. mal = valimi. mallal = valimi.
mul - mol - molu. molu molu - motu motu.
molu molu, molōr eṉpaṉa moyttal allatu tiraḷutaṟ kuṟippukkaḷ. mol - (moḷ) - moy = nerukkam, kūṭṭam, tiṇukkam, perumi, valimi, pōr.
mal = malku. malkutal = kūṭutal, mikutal, niṟital. mal = kūṭic ceyyum pōr.
mal - mali. malital - porutal.
vaṭavar mal = (mal, mall vittiru, koṇṭiru, uṭami. koḷ) eṉṉuñ colli mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar.
malli - malli, malliki - mallikā.  (va.va.)
malar cūṭal
niri kavartal, niri mīṭṭal, paṭiyeṭuttal, muṟṟukiyiṭal, matil kāttal, porutal, ūṉṟipporutal, veṟṟi koṇ ṭāṭal mutaliya pōrviṉi kaṭku ākkantaruvatākak karutiyē muṟiyē veṭci, karanti, vañci, uḻiñi, nocci tumpi, vāki malarkaḷi muṉṉit tamiḻar cūṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum. cila taḻikaḷum pūkkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ maruttuva āṟṟal paṟṟiyum aṇiyap paṭṭirukkalām.  (col. 20).
maliyam
maliyam - malaya
mali - maliyam = potiyamali. ōṅkuyar maliyat taruntavaṉ urippa (maṇi. 1:3).
am perumip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu. aikāram akaramāṉatu. camaṟkiruta vaṭivip piṉpaṟṟi. o.nō: niliyam - nilaya, vaḷiyam - valaya.  (va.va.234)
maliyāḷam
maliyāḷap peyar
kēraḷam, maliyāḷam eṉpaṉa taṟpōtu maliyāḷa nāṭṭiyum, aṅku vaḻaṅkum moḻiyiyum kuṟikkum peyarkaḷām.
kaḻaka (caṅka)k kālattil muttamiḻ nāṭukaḷil oṉṟāyirunta cēranāṭṭiṉ mēlpākamē ippōtu maliyāḷa nāṭāyiruk kiṉṟatu. kīḻppākam koṅkunāṭum (kōyamputtūr, cēlam, kōṭṭakap pakutikaḷ), kaṅkanāṭum (cēlam micūrc cīmip pakutikaḷ) ākum. maṟaṉ, tiṟaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ muṟiyē maṟal, tiṟal eṉṟu tirintāṟ pōla, cēraṉ eṉṉum peyarum cēral eṉap pōliyākip piṉpu aṉ īṟupeṟṟuc cēralaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
e.ṭu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ; kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nār muṭiccēral, takaṭūr eṟinta peruñcēral, kuṭakkō iḷañcēral.
teṉṉavaṉ cēralaṉ cōḻaṉ  (tiruvācakam)
ceṟumā vukikkum cēralaṉ kāṇka  (tirumukappācuram)
cēralaṉ eṉṉum peyar muṟiyē kēralaṉ, kēraḷaṉ eṉa maru viṟṟu, im marūu vaṭivaṅkaḷ paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ōriṭattuṅ kāṇappaṭavilli; pintiya nūlkaḷiltām kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl ivi piṟkālattaṉa eṉpatu teḷivu.
ca-ka, pōli o.nō: cīrtti-kīrtti, cempu - kempu (ka.) la-ḷa, pōli, o.nō: cetil-cetiḷ, vēli-vēḷi.
pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar pāṇṭiyam eṉa īṟu tirintu pāṇṭiyaṉ ceytikaḷik kuṟittāṟpōla, kēralaṉ kēraḷaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, kēralam kēraḷam eṉa īṟu tirintu cēra nāṭṭiyum, an nāṭṭu moḻiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
maliyāḷa nāṭṭelli
mēṟkut toṭarcci (kuṭa) malikku mēṟkē, vaṭakkil maṅkaḷūri (maṅkaḷapuram)liruntu teṟkil tiruvaṉantapuram varikkum, teṉkaṉṉaṭam malapār kocci tiruvitaṅkōṭu (tiruvāṅkūr) eṉṉum nāṉku cīmikaḷil tāymoḻiyākap pēcappaṭuvatu maliyāḷam.
teṉṉi marattiṉ vaṭamoḻip peyarākiya nāḷikēram eṉpatu, kēram eṉa mutaṟkuṟiyāyp piṉpu kēraḷam eṉa virintu am maram mikutiyāy vaḷarum maliyāḷa nāṭṭik kuṟitta teṉpatu poruntak kūṟum poymmik kūṟṟākum. vaṭa moḻikkum vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkkum uyarvum ataṉāl tamiḻuk kum tamiḻarkkum iḻivum kaṟpikkappaṭṭa purāṇak kālattil, vaṭamoḻit toṭarpi uyarveṉa mayaṅkiya maliyāḷa nāṭṭār, kēraḷam eṉṉum peyarkku vaṭamoḻi mūlattiyum, kēraḷa nāṭṭiṟkup paracurāmakṣēttiram eṉṉum putuppeyari yum makiḻcciyāy ēṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. paracurāmarkku muntiyē vaḻaṅkiya peyar cēranāṭu eṉpatum, avar iṟutik kālattil tavañ ceyta iṭamākak kūṟiyiruppatu cēranāṭṭiṟku vaṭakki luḷḷa kaṭaṟkarip pakuti yeṉpatum aṟital vēṇṭum. (paṇṭic cēranāṭu kūrccaram (kujarat variyil toṭarntirunta tālum, vaṭanāṭṭu āriyar teṉṉāṭṭilum vantu kuṭi pukunta tālum, paracurāmar tavañceyta iṭattiṟku ēṟpaṭṭa paracurāma kṣēttiram eṉṉum peyar avviṭattōṭu toṭarnta teṉpākattiyum piṟkālattil taḻuvalā yiṟṟu. intu (cintu) eṉṉum vaṭanāṭṭup pakutiyiṉ peyar teṉṉāṭṭiyum taḻuviṉāṟpōla.
patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil oru nampūtirip pārppaṉarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa tākat terikiṉṟa kēraḷōtpatti eṉṉum purāṇattil, pirāmaṇar pañcāpiṉiṉṟu paracurāmarāl teṉkaṉṉaṭattiluḷḷa kōkarṇattil mutal mutal kuṭi yēṟṟap paṭṭaṉar eṉṉum ceyti kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iti S. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār tamatu tamiḻārāycci (Tamil Studies) eṉṉum nūlil (pa. 348) maṟuttu, pirāmaṇarik kōkarṇattil kuṭi yēṟṟiyavaṉ kaṭampa marapiṉariṉ mutalvaṉāṉa mayūravarmaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ kālam ki.pi. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti yeṉṟuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
maliyāḷam eṉṉum peyar aṇmiyil tōṉṟiyatākum. cēranāṭu malināṭātalāl cēranāṭṭāṉ maliyāḷi yeṉap paṭṭāṉ. mali + āḷi = maliyāḷi. āḷi = āḷ. mutalāḷi. toḻilāḷi, vaṅkāḷi, paṅkāḷi mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka. maliyāḷiyiṉ nāṭum moḻiyum maliyāḷam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
o.nō : vaṅkāḷi - vaṅkāḷam.
cēranāṭu ki.pi. 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari centamiḻaracarum cen tamiḻp pulavarum tikaḻnta centamiḻ nāṭāyiruntatu. (naṉṉūṟ ciṟappuppāyirattil kuṇakaṭal kuṭakam kumari vēṅkaṭam, eṉunāṉ kelliyiṉiruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ eṉṟu kūṟiyirutta lāl 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil cēranāṭu koṭuntamiḻ nāṭāyirun tami peṟappaṭum). aiṅkuṟu nūṟum patiṟṟuppattum ākiya kaḻaka nūlkaḷum, cilappatikāram ākiya kaḻaka maruviya nūlum, puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyum, cēramāṉ perumāṉ nāyaṉāriṉ ātiyulāvum, kulacēkara āḻvāriṉ perumāḷ tirumoḻiyum maliyāḷa nāṭeṉappaṭum cēra nāṭṭil eḻunta centamiḻ nūlkaḷē.
patiṉālām nūṟṟāṇṭil (ki.pi. 1320) vīrarākava cakkaravartti yāl poṟikkappaṭṭa kōṭṭayam ceppēṭṭu moḻi vātil (vāyil) oṇṭāyil (uṇṭākil), eḻunnaḷḷi (eḻuntaruḷi) mutaliya cila maliyāḷat tiripukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya tamiḻē. atē nūṟṟāṇṭil (ki.pi. 1350) iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kaṇṇaccap paṇikkar irāmāyaṇa moḻiyum ittakiyatē.
e.ṭu. koṇṭalin nēriruṇṭu curuṇṭu nīṇṭoḷi vārntu tiṅṅum, kuntaḷa pāramōṭu mukil kulattiṭa miṉṉal pōlē, puṇṭarīkēkṣaṇan narikep polintavaḷa cītaconnāḷ.
itil, kuntaḷapāram puṇṭarīkēkṣaṇaṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭē vaṭacoṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷḷum kuntaḷam eṉpatu kūntal - eṉṉum teṉcol tiripu; vārntu - vārnnu tiṇuṅkum - tiṅṅum. iṭi - iṭa. arukē - arike.
pōrttukkīciya viṭittoṇṭar (Missionaries) mutaṉmutal maliyāḷak kariyil vantiṟaṅkiṉar eṉṟum, maliyāḷa nāṭṭu moḻiyit tamakku muntiya arapiyar pōla malapār eṉṟaḻittaṉar eṉṟum, kīḻkariyilum ilaṅkik kariyilum vaḻaṅkiya moḻi (tamiḻ) ati yottirukkak kaṇṭu atiyum ap peyarāl aḻittaṉar eṉṟum avarkaḷ maliyāḷak kariyiluḷḷa ampalakkāṭṭil 1577 allatu 9 il mutaṉ mutal tamiḻeḻuttil acciṭṭa puttaka moḻiyi malavār allatu tamiḻ (Malavar or Tamil) eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar eṉṟum kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār kūṟukiṉṟār. (muṉṉuri pak. 10-13).
itaṉāl, 16 ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari maliyāḷa nāṭṭumoḻi koṭuntamiḻā yirunta teṉṟum, ataṉ piṉṉarē vaṭamoḻik kalappāl maliyāḷamākat tiriyat toṭaṅkiṟṟeṉṟum aṟiyalām. maliyāḷam mikat teḷivāyp pirintupōṉatu 17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil (ki.pi. 1650) āriyaveḻutti vakuttum, peru vāriyāy āriyac coṟkaḷiyum vikutikaḷiyum pukuttiyum, cēranāṭṭu moḻiyic cititta tuñcattu eḻuttaccaṉālēyē.
1860 ām āṇṭiltāṉ mutal maliyāḷa ilakkaṇamum eḻuntatu. 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari maliyāḷiyar tamiḻiyum kaṟṟuvantaṉareṉṟum ataṉ piṉputāṉ av vaḻakkam aṭi yōṭu viṭappaṭṭateṉṟum mutiyōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
maliyāḷa nāṭṭil pārppaṉaroḻinta maṟṟak kulattā rellām tamiḻar allatu tiraviṭarē. kuyavar, paṇikkar, pāṇar, āyar, īḻavar, kavuṭar (kavuṇṭar) mutaliya pala kulattiṉar tamiḻ nāṭṭilum uḷḷaṉar. nāyar, nāyāṭi, vāriyar, ceṟumaṉ mutaliya kulattiṉar maliyāḷattiṟkuc ciṟappāyirunta tālum, avar kuṭippeyarellām taṉit tamiḻē. tāḻntatu uyarntatu eṉṟu muṟiyē poruḷ tarum kiḻakku mēṟku eṉṉum ticippeyarkaḷ kuṭamalik kīḻnāṭṭiṟkē ēṟṟavi. ivi maliyāḷattilum vaḻaṅkuvatu maliyāḷanāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭup pakutiyē yeṉpatiyum maliyāḷiyar tamiḻ marapiṉarē eṉpatiyum uṇarttum. piṟkālattil mēṟkut ticikkup paṭu ñāyiṟu eṉa oru peyari avarkaḷ puṉintu koṇṭālum, atuvum taṉittamiḻē eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
maliyāḷa nāṭṭu allatu cēranāṭṭut tuṟimukaṅkaḷ paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil peruṅkaṭal vāṇika niliyaṅkaḷākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ceṅkōṟ, kuṭṭuvaṉ toṇṭi  (aiṅ. 178)
............................................... cēralar
cuḷḷiyam pēriyāṟṟu veṇṇuri kalaṅka
yavaṉar tanta viṉimāṇ naṉkalam
poṉṉoṭu vantu kaṟiyoṭu peyarum
vaḷaṅkeḻu muciri yārppeṉa  (akam. 148)
kalantanta poṟparicam
kaḻittōṇiyāṟ karicērkkuntu
malittāramum kaṭaṟṟāramum,
talippeytu varunarkkīyum
puṉalaṅ kaḷḷiṉ polantārk kuṭṭuvaṉ
muḻaṅkukaṭal muḻaviṉ muciri yaṉṉa  (puṟam. 343)
iyaṟṟērk kuṭṭuvaṉ varupuṉal vāyil vañci  (pat. 3)
maliyāḷa nāṭṭiluḷḷa pala civanakaraṅkaḷum viṇṇakaraṅ kaḷum nāyaṉmārālum āḻvārālum pāṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kōkarṇam tirucceṅkuṉṟūr (kollattiṟ karukiluḷḷatu) eṉpaṉa apparālum campantarālum 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, tiruvañcikkaḷam cuntararāl 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, nēriṟ pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tirumuḻikkaḷam, tirunāvāy, tiruvallavāḻ eṉpaṉa tirumaṅkiyāḻvār 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil nēriṟ ceṉṟu kaṇṭavi. ivaṟṟuṭaṉ tiruvaṉantapuram, tiruvaṇ parikāram, tirukkāṭkari, tiruppuliyūr, tirucceṅ kuṉṟūr, tiruvaṇvaṇṭūr, tiruvattaru, tirukkaṭit tāṉam, tiruvāṟaṉ viḷi eṉpaṉa nammāḻvārāl (ki.pi. 920) kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vittuvakkōṭu kulacēkarāḻvārāl 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ pāṭappaṭṭatu (tamiḻārāycci, pa. 347) mēṟkūṟiya tirunakaraṅkaḷ 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari centamiḻ niliyaṅkaḷā yiruntirā viṭṭāl pāṭal peṟṟa nakaraṅkaḷāyirā eṉpatu tiṇṇam.
maliyāḷattiluḷḷa paḻimiyāṉa ūrp peyarkaḷellām iṉṟum taṉittamiḻāyē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. kōṭu (kōḻikkōṭu - Calicut), cēri (taliccēri), kuḷam (erṇākkuḷam), puram (aṅkāṭi puram), nāṭu (valiyanāṭu), ūr (kaṇṇaṉūr), kuṉṟam (pūṅkuṉṉam), kā (kaṉṉaṅkāvu), kāṭu (pālikkāṭu - Palghat), kuṭi (cākkuṭi), vācal (paḷḷivācal), aṅkāṭi (parappaṉaṅkāṭi), tōṭṭam, pāṭi, tuṟi (tiruppuṉittuṟi), kuṟicci, ēri, kari (koṭṭārak kari), kaḷam (tiruvañcik kaḷam), iruppu mutaliyaṉa taṉittamiḻ ūrp peyarīṟukaḷām. kōṭu = mali. kā= cōli.
paḻañcēranāṭṭiṉ kīḻppakutiyiṉ teṉpākam (koṅku nāṭu) iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭā yirukkiṉṟatu; vaṭapākattiṟ kaṉṉaṭam pukuntuḷḷatu.
13 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa vēṇāṭu, pūḻi nāṭu, kuṭṭa nāṭu, kuṭa nāṭu, mali nāṭu eṉpaṉa iṟṟi maliyāḷa nāṭṭup pakutikaḷāyuḷḷaṉa.



pūḻiyaṉ utiyaṉ koṅkaṉ poṟiyaṉ
vāṉavaṉ kaṭṭuvaṉ vāṉa varampaṉ
villavaṉ kuṭanāṭaṉ vañci vēntaṉ
kollic cilampaṉ kōti kēraḷaṉ
pōntiṉ kaṇṇikkōṉ porunit tuṟivaṉ
cēraṉ maliyamāṉ kōc cēraṉ peyarē
eṉpatu tivākaram
ivaṟṟuḷ koṅkaṉ, kollic cilampaṉ, poruni (āṉ poruni)t tuṟivaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, kōyamputtūr cēlam ākiya iru kōṭṭakaṅkaḷum cēranāṭṭic cērntaṉa eṉpati viḷakkum. koṅkaṉ = koṅku nāṭṭaracaṉ, koṅku nāṭu kōyamputtūrk kōṭṭakap pakuti. kollimali cēlaṅ kōṭṭakattic cērntatu. cērar kuṭiyil oru kiḷiyiṉarāṉa atikamāṉ marapiṉar cēlattic cērnta takaṭūri (tarmapuriyi)t talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭaṉar.
kuṟṟiyalukarattip puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭal, titiyip pakka meṉṟal mutaliya paḻantamiḻ vaḻakkaṅkaḷ iṉṟum maliyāḷa nāṭṭil tāṉuḷḷaṉa.
toṉṟutoṭṭuc cēranāṭṭu melittal tiripu coṟkaḷ cila cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭukaḷilum iruvaki vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. tirinavum (tol. 83) paḻaṉi, (puṟam 113) paḻuṉiya (maṇi. 328) aṟiyuṉaṉ (puṟam 134) (tirikiṉṟa tirikuṉṉa - tiriyuṉṉa - tiriyuṉa - tiriṉa - tirina) paḻuttu - paḻuṉṉu - paḻuṉṉu - paḻuni - paḻuti, makiḻkiṉṟāṉ - makiḻuṉṉāṉ - makiḻunaṉ - makiḻnaṉ. vāḻkiṉ ṟāṉ - vāḻuṉṉāṉ - vāḻunaṉ - vāḻnaṉ - vāṇaṉ. iṉi, tiriyum - tiriyuṉ + a = tiriyuṉa - tiriṉa - tirina eṉṟumām. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum, ṉakarattiṉum nakaram muntiṉateṉak koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu. āṅkaṉam - aṅkaṉam - aṅṅaṉam - aṉṉaṇam.
āō vākum peyarumā ruḷavē
āyiṭaṉ aṟital ceyyu ḷuḷḷē  (tol. 680)
eṉṟu kūṟiyatum cēranāṭṭiṟkē ciṟappāy ēṟkum.
ivaṟṟāl, paṇṭi muttamiḻ nāṭṭut toṭarpiyum, cēranāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟukaḷ pālīṟu peṟṟatiyum aṟiyalām.
.......... vaṇṭami ḻikaḻnta
kāyvēṟ ṟaṭakkik kaṉakaṉum viciyaṉum
ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉṟaṉ ciṉavalip paṭutalum
eṉṟu cilappatikārattilum,
vaṭatici yelli yimaya mākat
teṉṉaṅ kumariyo ṭāyiṭi yaracar
muracuṭip peruñcaman tatiya vārppeḻac
colpala nāṭṭit tolkavi ṉaḻitta
pōraṭu tāṉip polantārk kuṭṭuva  (patit. 43)
eṉṟu patiṟṟuppattilum pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷa cēra nāṭṭuc cīriya maṟam iṉṟu āriya aṭimippaṭṭu aṇuvaḷavut tamiḻuṇarcci yiṉṟik kiṭappatu niṉikkuntōṟum neñcip puṇpaṭuttu vatāyirukkiṉṟatu.
maliyāḷam tirintataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ
1.	cēranāṭu perumpālum malittoṭarāl taṭukkappaṭṭup piṟa tamiḻ nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ perun toṭarpu koḷḷātiruntami.
2.	12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭōṭu pāṇṭiya marapum 13 ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭōṭu cōḻa marapum cēra marapuṭaṉ maṇavuṟavu niṟuttiyami.
3.	vaṭamoḻikkum vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkkum teyva uyarvu kaṟpikkap paṭṭamiyum varampiṟanta vaṭacoṟ kalappum.
4.	miku maḻiyāl maliyāḷiyarkku mūkkoli ciṟantami.
5.	maliyāḷiyar muṉṉōriṉ centamiḻ nūlkaḷik kallāmi.
6.	maliyāḷiyariṉ olimuṟic cōmpal.
maliyāḷam tirinta muṟikaḷ
1.	muṟṟuviṉikaḷ pāl kāṭṭum īṟiḻattal. e.ṭu. vantāṉ - vantu.
2.	melittal tirivu. e.ṭu. eḻuntu - eḻunnu, aṅku - aṅṅu, kaḻuku - kaḻuṅṅu. kuñci - kuññi, vīḻntu - vīṇu.
3.	nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉ kiṉṟatu eṉṉum iṭinili uṉṉu eṉat tirital. e.ṭu: ceykiṉṟa - ceykuṉṉu - ceyyuṉṉu.
4.	vēṟṟumi yuruput tirivu. e.ṭu. atiṉukku - (atiṉku) - atiṉṉu, uṭiya - uṭē - ṭē - ṟē.
5.	pōlit tirivu. e.ṭu. narampu - ñarampu, ceymmiṉ - ceyviṉ.
6.	koccit tirivum tokuttalum, e.ṭu: uḷḷa - oḷḷa, mali - mala, akaṟṟuka - akattuka iru - iri, puṟā - prāvu, kaṉā - kiṉāvu, vēṇṭum - vēṇam, ceyyavēṇṭum - ceyyēṇṭu, pōka vēṇṭum - pōkēṇam.
7.	ṟakara rakara vēṟupāṭiṉmi. e.ṭu: uṟavu - uravu.
8.	nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattiṟkup patilāka vāṉ pāṉ īṟṟu viṉiyeccaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tiripukaḷum vaḻaṅkal. e.ṭu: kuṭippāṉ = kuṭikka, naṭappāṉ - naṭakkāṉ = naṭakka, varuvāṉ = vara.
9.	ciṟappuc coṟkaḷ potup poruḷil vaḻaṅkal. e.ṭu. veḷḷam (putup perukku nīr), = nīr. mūri (kiḻerutu) - erutu, nōkku (kūrntu pār) = pār.
10.	coṟkaḷi orumaruṅku paṟṟiya poruḷil vaḻaṅkal. e.ṭu: atē = ām, vaḷarē = mika, mati = pōtum; valiya = periya.
11.	oruporuṭ pala coṟkaḷil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkāta oṉṟi vaḻaṅkal. e.ṭu. col (ta.) - paṟi (ma.) kūppiṭu (ta.) viḷi (ma.).
12.	āy eṉṉum viṉiyecca vīṟu āyiṭṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkal. e.ṭu: civappāy - civappāyiṭṭu.
13.	neṭuñcuṭṭu vaḻakku vīḻāmi. e.ṭu: āyāḷ (av āḷ), īmūri (im mūri).
14.	ēval viṉikaḷ perumpālum viyaṅkōḷ vaṭivil vaḻaṅkal. e.ṭu. tā-tarika, tara.
15.	tuṇi viṉi vēṟupāṭu. e.ṭu: paṟiyuvāṉ kaḻiyum (colla muṭiyum). paṟiyuvāṉ kaḻiyuṉṉilla (collamuṭiyātu).
16.	ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu mūviṭattum vaḻaṅkal. e.ṭu: ñāṉ, nī, avaṉ - pōkum.
17.	ṟaṉṉakaram tannakaramāka eḻutappaṭal.
18.	vaḻakkaṟṟa col vaḻaṅkal. e.ṭu: kinīṭṭak kācu, tampirāṭṭi.
19.	putuccoṟkaḷ. e.ṭu: mēṭi = vāṅku, veṭippi = tuppuravākku.
20.	viṉimarapu vēṟupāṭu e.ṭu: muṭṭa puḻuṅṅuka (avikka), viḷakku keṭukka (aṇikka), muṭṭuk kuttuka (koṭukka), kāluṟa muṟikka (veṭṭuka), maṭaṅṅi (tirumpi) varuka.
kuṟippu: cēranāṭṭuk koṭuntamiḻākiya maliyāḷattiluḷḷa iruvakit tamiḻccoṟkaḷiyum vaḻakku vīḻttaṟkum maliyāḷattār tamiḻc coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉkoṇṭu ataṉāl avarkkum tamiḻarkkum toṭarpu ēṟpaṭātapaṭiyum, āriyar vaṭacoṟkaḷi mikutiyāyk kalantu maliyāḷattik keṭuttut tamiḻukku mika ayaṉmippaṭuttiviṭṭaṉar.
maliyāḷac col varicikaḷ
1. peyarc coṟkaḷ
		taṉmi	muṉṉili	paṭarkki 	taṟcuṭṭu
orumi	ñāṉ	nī	avaṉ, ivaṉ	tāṉ
				avaḷ, ivaḷ, 
				atu, itu
paṉmi	nām 	niṅkaḷ	avar, ivar	taṅṅaḷ
		nammaḷ 	ava, iva	tāṅṅaḷ
		ñaṅkaḷ	tāṅkaḷ
2. viṉāp peyar
		āṇ	peṇ	potu 	aḵṟiṇi
orumi :	evaṉ	evaḷ	ār	etu, yātu
paṉmi :		evar	yār	entu, eva
			yāvar	yāva
eṉṉatu (ta.) = entu (ma.)
3. muṟip peyar
appaṉ, amma, ammāyi (pāṭṭi), makaṉ, makaḷ, marumakaṉ, marumakaḷ, pēraṉ mutaliya muṟippeyarkaḷ centamiḻc coṟkaḷākum.
mūttaccaṉ, mūttappaṉ, pērappaṉ; mūttamma, mūttacci; valiyaccaṉ (periyappaṉ), valiyappaṉ, iḷiyappaṉ kuññaccaṉ (ciṟṟappaṉ), peṇṇappaṉ (māmaṉ). peṇṇamma (māmi), keṭṭiyavaṉ (kaṇavaṉ), keṭṭiyavaḷ (maṉivi) mutaliya muṟippeyarkaḷ koṭun tamiḻc coṟkaḷākum.
jyēṣṭaṉ, jyēṣṭatti, aṉujaṉ, aṉujatti mutaliya vaṭacoṟ peyarkaḷ vaḻakkūṉṟiyatāl, aṇṇaṉ, akki, tampi, taṅki mutaliya centamiḻp peyarkaḷ maliyāḷattil vaḻakku vīḻntaṉa.
acci, appacci eṉṉum orupāṉ muṟippeyarkaḷ centamiḻ nāṭṭil cila kulattāriṭi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iviyum ivaṟṟiṉ āṇpāl vaṭivaṅkaḷāṉa accaṉ, appaccaṉ eṉpaṉavum oruṅkē maliyāḷa nāṭṭiltāṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kuṟippu : attaṉ - accaṉ, atti - acci, ta-ca, pōli.
4. makkaṭ peyar
aṭiyār, appakkāraṉ, appaṭṭaṉ, āḷ, āḷ kolli, iṭayaṉ, irappāḷi, iḷiyavaṉ, uṭayavaṉ, ūmaṉ, eḷiyavaṉ, kaṇiyāṉ, kaḷḷaṉ, kaṉṉiyāḷ, kuṭṭi (piḷḷi), kuṭiyaṉ, kuruṭaṉ, kūlikkāraṉ, kollaṉ, cakkāḷ (cekkāṉ), ceviṭaṉ, ceṟiyavaṉ (ciṟiyavaṉ), taccaṉ, tāntōṉṉi (tāṉtōṉṟi), tūtaṉ, tōḻaṉ, nallaṉ, pāṇaṉ, pital (piyaṉ), pōkkiri, maṭiyaṉ, mantiri, malayāḷi, mutalāḷi, mūppaṉ, vaṇṭikkāraṉ, vampaṉ, valiyavaṉ, vaḻippōkkaṉ mutaliyaṉa.
paṟayuṉṉavaṉ ceyyuṉṉavaṉ mutaliya viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarkaḷum, kāṇi tiṉṉi mutaliya ceyvāṉ peyarkaḷun tamiḻ muṟiyi lamintaviyē.
5. vilaṅkup peyar
āṉa (āṉi), āṭu, eruma (erumi), oṭṭakam, kaḻuta (kaḻuti), veruku, karaṭi, kaṉṉu (kaṉṟu), kālikaḷ, kāḷa (kāḷi), kutira (kutiri), kuraṅṅu (kuraṅku), nari, nāy, paṉṉi (paṉṟi), puli, pūcca (pūci = pūṉi), māṉ, muyalu, mūri, kuṟukkaṉ (kuṟunari).
6. paṟavip peyar
īcca (ī), kaḻuka (kaḻuku), kākka (kākki), kuyil, kūmaṉ (kūki), kokku, ceṅṅāli (ceṅkāli = puṟā), tārāvu (tārā), paruntu, piṭakkōḻi (peṭṭik kōḻi), prāvu (puṟā), mayil, vaṇṭu, vāval, tatta (tatti = kiḷi), kiḷi.
7. ūrvaṉavaṟṟiṉ peyar
irimpu (eṟumpu), ōntu (ōnti), eli, kīri, cuṇṭeli, tēḷ, pāmpu, puḻu, ñāṅkūḻ, cital.
8. nīr vāḻvaṉavaṟṟiṉ peyar
ñāṇṭu (naṇṭu), tavaḷa (tavaḷi), mīṉ, ayila (ayiri), āral, mutala (mutali) mutaliyaṉa.
9. marañceṭip peyar
avara (avari), āl, iñci, uḻuṉṉu (uḻuntu), eḷ, ēlam, karumuḷaku, kīra (kīri), ceṭi, ceṟu payaṟu (ciṟupayaṟu), cēmpu, cēṉa (cēṉi) teṅku, nel, paṭōl (puṭali), payaṟu, parutti, paṉa (paṉi), pilāvu (palā), pul, puḷi, pukiyila (pukiyili) maram, maññaḷ (mañcaḷ), mā, mutira (mutiri = kāṇam), muḷḷaṅki, vaḻutuṇaṅkāy (= kattarikkāy), vāḻa (vāḻi), veṇṭakki (veṇṭikkāy), veḷḷari, veṟṟila (veṟṟili), paṭṭāṇip payaṟu, uruḷakkiḻaṅku, muntiriṅṅa (muntiriki), ñāṟu (nāṟṟu), kaḷa (kaḷi), cūral = pirampu, vēmpu, ñāṇal, ñāval (nāval) mutaliyaṉa.
10. karuvippeyar
aram, āṇi, īrccavāḷ, uralu, ulakka (ulakki), ēttam (ēṟṟam), uḷi, katti, kattiri, karaṇṭi, kōṭṭu, (koṭṭu), kōṭāli, cakku (cekku), cāṭṭa (cāṭṭi), cuttika (cuttiki), cuḻikkuṟṟi, cūlam, tuṭuppu, turutti, nukam, maḻu, muḷ, mūla maṭṭam, vāḷ, vil, kuṇṭal (tūṇṭil), naṅkūram mutaliyaṉa.
11. aimpūtap peyar
nilam, veḷḷam, tī, kāṟṟu, viṇṇu.
12. ulōkap peyar
irimpu (irumpu), īyam, urukku, cempu, takaram, tuttanākam, piccaḷa (pittaṉi), poṉ, vajram (vayiram), veḷḷi.
13. ūrtip peyar
ōṭi, ōṭam, kappal, tēr, tōṇi, paṭaku, vaṇṭi mutaliyaṉa.
14. uṇavup peyar
ari (arici), appam, ira (iri), iṟacci (iṟicci), uppu, uṟa (uṟi mōr), ūṇ, eṇṇa (eṇṇey), kaññi (kañci), kaṭuku, kaṟi, cōṟu, tayir, taviṭu, tīṉ, tēṉ, ti (ney), palahāram (palakāram), pāl, macāla (macāli), maruṉṉu (maruntu), muṭṭa (muṭṭi), muḷaku taṇṇi (miḷaku taṇṇīr), mōr, veṇṇa (veṇṇey) mutaliyaṉa.
15. āṭiyaṇip peyar
uṭuppu, kampiḷi (kampaḷi), kāl caṭṭa, kāluṟa (kāluṟi), kuppāyam, kuṭutta (kuṭittuṇi), cilampu, cerippu (ceruppu), talakkeṭṭu, talappāvu (talippāki), paṭṭu, pāvāṭa (pāvāṭi), puṭava (puṭavi), mōtiram, mūkkutti, vaḷa (vaḷi) mutaliyaṉa.
16. taṭṭumuṭṭup peyar
aṇa (aṇi), kayiṟu, kalam, kiṇṭi (keṇṭi), kuṭa (kuṭi), kaṭṭi, cākku, toṭṭi, palaka (palaki), pāy, mēca (mēci), viḷakku, cīppu, kaṇṇāṭi, putakam (pottakam), kaṭiyāram, taṭṭu, kiṇṇam, cempu, pokkaṇam, peṭṭi, muṟam mutaliyaṉa.
17. iṭap peyar
akam, aṅṅāṭi (aṅkāṭi), iṭa (iṭam), iṟa (iṟavu), ula (uli), eḻuttup paḷḷi (paḷḷikkūṭam), kaṭal, kara (kari), kallaṟa (kallaṟi), kāṭu, kiṇaṟu, kiḻakku, kuṭi (=vīṭu), kuṭil, kuḻi, kuḷam, kōṭṭa (kōṭṭi), cuvar, tīvu, tuṟamukam (tuṟimukam), tekku, teru; tōṭṭam, nāṭu, paṭṭaṇam, pāṟa (pāṟi), puṟam, mala (mali), matilu, māḷika (māḷiki), muṟṟam, mūla (mūli), lōkam (ulakam), vaṭakku, vayal, vaḻi, vātil (vācal), vāṉam, vīṭu, kuṇṭu, (aṟi), cēri, illam, kaḻaṉi, kēṇi.
18. kālap peyar
kālam, camayam, nāḷ, nēram, pakkam (titi), pakal, pōḻ (pōḻtu), maṇṭalam, maṇi, mācam (mātam). rāttiri (irāttiri), rāvu (irā), ñāṉṉu (ñāṉṟu), mutaliyaṉa.
19. ciṉip peyar
itaḷ (itaḻ), ila (ili), īral, uṭal, eccil, ōla (ōli), kaṇṇu, kaḻuttu, kāmpu, kāy, kāl, ki, kuṭal, kuḷampu, kompu, ciraṭṭa (ciraṭṭi), ciṟaku, cekiḷ, cetumpal, cevi, cōra (cōri = arattam), cūca (cūci), taṇṭu, tala (tali), tala muṭi, taḷir, tuṭa (toṭi), tumpikki, tūval (tūvu), tōl, tōḷ, narampu, nāṭi, nār, nāvu, neññu (neñcu), paṭam, pallu, paḻam, pāḷa (pāḷi), pittam, pūvi, mayir, mukam, mula (muli), muḷ, muḷa, (muḷi), muḻaṅkālu, mīca (mīci), muṭṭu, mūkku, moṭṭu, rattam (arattam), vayiṟu, vāl, pittu, viral, vēr, alar.
20. palporuṭ peyar
ūñcal, iṭi, oli, meḻuku, poṭi, pēr, kūli, maṣi (mi), cumaṭu (cumi, puka (puki), maḻa (maḻi), cēṟu, vila (vili), veḷiccam, paṭam (pāṭam), accu, cāṇkam (cāṇam), kaṭam (kaṭaṉ), cūtu, tuṇa (tuṇi), paṭa (paṭi), veṭi, maṇ, maṇal, paṇam, tūṇ, nūl, kallu, cuṇṇāmpu, uttaram, viṭṭam, viṟaku, kaḻukkōl, vāri, ōṭu, paṭṭika (paṭṭiki), talayaṇa (taliyaṇi), pūṭṭu, tākkōl (tiṟavukōl), turumpu, cāmpal, tiri, kokka (kokki), paca (paci), caraṭu, kūṭu, kuññu (kuñcu), kantakam, curuṭṭu, cavam, pantam, pantu, pamparam, koṭi, veṇcāmaram, mattaḷam, veyil, vaḷam, cuṭar, nura (nuri).
21. kuṇappeyar
akalam, ayal, aḷavu, aḻukku, āḻam, iṭukku, iṉpam, uyaram, urappu, (= uṟuti), karippu, kuṟṟam, kippu, cūṭu, nīḷam, pacca (pacci), māṉam, vampu, vēkam, kaṭiṉam, civappu, veḷḷa (veḷḷi), naṉma, tiṉma (tīmi), paḻama, putuma, nīlam, karuppu, mēṉma, nāṇam, vallama mutaliyaṉa.
22. nōyp peyar
kāccal (kāyccal), kuṭalēṟṟam, kuṭaccal (kuṭiccal), kuru, cūla (cūli), pakkavātam mutaliyaṉa.
23. eṇṇup peyar
oṉṇu	patiṉoṉṉu	muppatu
raṇṭu	pantiraṇṭu	nālpatu
mūṉṉu	patimūṉṉu	ampatu	iraṇṭāyiram
nālu	patinālu	aṟupatu	mūvāyiram
añcu 	patiṉañcu	eḻupatu 	aiyāyiram
āṟu 	patiṉāṟu	eṇpatu	ompatiṉāyiram
ēḻu		patiṉēḻu	toṇṇūṟu	patiṉāyiram
eṭṭu	patiṉeṭṭu	nūṟu 	muppatiṉāyiram
ompatu	pattompatu	āyiram	nālpatiṉāyiram
pattu	irupatu	eṇṇāyiram	lakṣam (ilakkam)
kōṭi
kīḻilakkam : kāṇi, mākāṇi, arikkāl, kāl, ara (ari), mukkāl.
eṇṇaṭi uyartiṇip peyar : oruvar, iruvar, mūvar, nālvar, aivar, aṟuvar, eḻuvar, pantiruvar (paṉṉiruvar), patiṉālvar, nūṟṟuvar mutaliyaṉa.
24. toḻiṟ peyar
1.	mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar; aṭi, piṭi, veṭṭu, kaḷi, paṇa.
2.	mutaṉili tirinta toḻiṟpeyar; ūṇ, tīṉ, cūṭu, pōr.
3.	mutaṉili tirintu vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar; tavi-tākam, paṭi-pāṭam;
4.	mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭitta toḻiṟpeyar; āṭu-āṭṭu, pāṭu- pāṭṭu.
5.	mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭittu vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟ peyar; ēṟu-ēṟṟam, ōṭu - ōṭṭam.
6.	vikutipeṟṟa peyar.
	vikuti 	toḻiṟpeyar	vikuti	toḻiṟpeyar
	a(ai)	paka(paki)		pu		piṟappu
	al	tuppal		vu(u)		varavu
		viṟayal (viṟiyal)	ka(ki)	ceyka
	cal	pāccal(pāyccal)
	tam	piṭittal		vi		kēḷvi
	ti	poṟuti
7.	kālaṅkāṭṭun toḻiṟpeyar; ceytatu, ceyyuṉṉatu, ceyvatu.
8.	etirmaṟit toḻiṟpeyar; ceyyāyka, aṟiyāyka.
9.	pakuti vaḻakkaṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar; caṇṭi.
viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar
terinili : ceytavaṉ, ceyyuṉṉavaṉ, ceyvōṉ mutaliyaṉa
kuṟippu : valiyavaṉ, ceṟiyavaṉ, paḻiyatu, putiyatu mutaliyaṉa.
ii. viṉiccoṟkaḷ
cila mukkiya viṉikaḷ
akal, añcu, aṭi, aṭe (aṭi), amiḻ, ari, ara (ari), alaṟu, aḷa, ali (aḷi), aṟi, āku, āṭu, āḻ, āḷ, iṭaṟu, iṭi, iṭu, ira, iru, iruḷ, iṟaṅṅu (iṟaṅku), uṭu, uḻu, uṇar, utir, untu, umiḻ, uyir, uri, uḻu, uṟaṅṅu (uṟaṅku), eṭu, ettu (eytu), etir, eḻu, eḻutu, ēl, oli, ōmpu, ōṭu.
kaṭi, kacakku, katri (kattari), kaḻi, kaḻuku (kaḻuvu), kara (kari), kari 
(= aḻu), kaviḻ, kaṟa, kaṉa, kā, kāṇu, kāy, kiṭa, kuṭi, kuttu, keṭṭu (kaṭṭu), keṭu, kēḷ, koru, kottu, kol, kōṭṭāvi viṭu, kōr, kōlu, cāku, cār, ciri, cīntu, cuma, cuṭu, eruḷ, cuḻal, kuḻu, cettu (cetukku), cey, cel, coṟi, ñeḷi, (neḷi), taku, taṅṅu (taṅku), tavu, taṭṭu, taṇi, taḻuvu, taḷ, tā (tarika), tāḷi, tāṇṭu, tāḻ, tiṉ, tīr, tuṭe (tuṭi), tuṭaṅṅu (toṭaṅku), tuvarttu, tuṟa (tiṟa), tuppu (tummū), tuḷe (tuḷi), tē (tēy), toḻu, tōḷ, tōṉṉu (toṉṟu), naṭa, nakku, naṭu, naka (naki), naṭuṅku, nampu, naṉe (naṉi), nara (nari), nalku, nāṟu, nirattu, nil, nivir (nimir), niṟe (niṟi), nīṅṅu, nīntu, nīḷ, nīṟṟu, nuḻa (nuḻi), nūl, ney, nō, nōkku, nōl.
pake (paki), pari (paṭi), paḻu, paṟa, paṟi, paṟṟu, pāṭu, pāy, piraḷ (puraḷ), piri, piḷar, piṟa, pukaḻ, puku, puḷi, puṟappeṭu, peṟu, poṟu, pōku, maṟa, maṟu, māṟu, miku, meti (miti), mēy, rāku (arāvu), valikku (iḻu), vaḷa (vaḷi), vaṟaḷ, vaṟṟu, vā (varika), vāṅkupiṭi, viṭu, vita (viti), vil, viḷi, viḷa (viḷi), viṟa, (viṟi), vīḻ, vīḷ (mīḷ), vekka (vikka), veṭṭu veḷippeṭu vēku.
kuṟippu : maliyāḷa viṉikaḷellām ēvalākum pōtu perumpālum viyaṅkōḷ vaṭivukoḷḷum.
e.ṭu: cey - ceyyuka, tā - tarika.

tuṇiviṉikaḷ

āku eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi
iṟanta kālam :		ñāṉ āyi	=  nāṉ āṉēṉ.
				nī āyi	=  nī āṉāy?
				avaṉ āyi	=  avaṉ āṉāṉ.
nikaḻ kālam	:	ñāṉ ākuṉṉu	=  nāṉ irukkiṟēṉ.
				nī ākuṉṉu	=  nī irukkiṟāy.
				avaṉ ākuṉṉu = avaṉ irukkiṟāṉ.
etir kālam	:	ñāṉ ākum	=  nāṉ iruppēṉ.
				nī ākum	=  nī iruppāy.
				avaṉ ākum=  avaṉ iruppāṉ.
iru eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi (kalavikkālam)
i.kā. 	ñāṉ ceytiruṉṉu 		=  nāṉ ceytiruntēṉ.
			nī ceytiruṉṉu		=  nī ceyntiruntāy.
			avaṉ ceytiruṉṉu	=  avaṉ ceytiruntāṉ.
ni.kā.	ñāṉ ceytirukkuṉṉu		=  nāṉ ceytirukkiṟēṉ.
			nī ceytirukkuṉṉu	=  nī ceytirukkiṟāy.
			avaṉ ceytirukkuṉṉu 	= avaṉ ceytirukkiṟāṉ..
e.kā.	ñāṉ ceytirukkum		=  nāṉ ceytiruppēṉ.
			nī ceytirukkum		=  nī ceytiruppāy.
			ava ceytirukkum		=  avaṉ ceytiruppāṉ.
uṇṭu illi eṉṉum tuṇiviṉikaḷ
eṉikkuṇṭu = eṉakkuṇṭu 	eṉikkilla 	   = 	eṉakkilli
niṉakkuṇṭu = uṉakkuṇṭu	niṉakkilla 	   = 	uṉakkilli
avaṉuṇṭu = avaṉukkuṇṭu avaṉikkilla = 	avaṉukkilli
alla eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi
ñāṉ alla 	=  nāṉ allēṉ
nī alla	=  nī alli
avaṉ alla	=  avaṉ allaṉ
kuṟippu : tamiḻilum ulaka vaḻakkil alla eṉṉum palaviṉpāl vaṭivam vaḻuvāka irutiṇi yimpāl mūviṭaṅkaṭkum potuvāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
cila puṇarviṉikaḷ
aṭuttuvā, uṇṭāku, kāttiru, koṇṭuvā, paṟaññūvekka, vittiru, veḷippeṭu, koṇṭupō, tāṇupōka.
taṉviṉi piṟaviṉiyākum vaḻikaḷ
1. pakuti vali yiraṭṭal - e.ṭu: uruku - urukku. uṇṭāku - uṇṭākku, māṟu - māṟṟu.
2. pakuti (meli) valittal - e.ṭu: kalaṅṅu - kalakku.
3. vikuti peṟutal - e.ṭu: kāṇ - kāṇi (kāṇpi) nīḷ - nīṭṭu, kaṭi - kaṭippi, cuma - cumattu, kāy - kāccu (kāyccu).
kuṟippup peyareccaṅkaḷ
valiya (periya), nalla, ceṟiya (ciṟiya), paḻaya (paḻiya), putiya, cila, pala mutaliyaṉa.
kuṟippu : viṉi yeccaṅkaḷ - naṉṉāyi, muḻuvaṉ, vēkam, mutaliyaṉa.
iṭiccoṟkaḷ
1. vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ
1 ām vēṟṟumi 	makaṉ 	ñāṉ 	avaṉ 	vīṭu
2 ām vēṟṟumi	makaṉe	eṉṉe	avaṉe	vīṭṭiṉe
3 ām vēṟṟumi	makaṉāl,	eṉṉāl,	avaṉāl,	vīṭṭiṉāl.
		makaṉōṭu	eṉṉōṭu	avaṉōṭu	vīṭṭiṉōṭu
4 ām vēṟṟumi	makaṉṉu	eṉikku	avaṉṉu	vīṭṭiṉṉu
5 ām vēṟṟumi	makaṉil	eṉṉil	avaṉil	vīṭṭil
		niṉṉu	niṉṉu	niṉṉu	niṉṉu
6 ām vēṟṟumi	makaṉṟe	eṉṟē	avaṉṟe	vīṭṭiṉṟe
7 ām vēṟṟumi	makaṉil 	eṉṉil	avaṉil	vīṭṭil
8 ām vēṟṟumi	makaṉē	-	-	vīṭē
2. cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ - a, ā, i, ī
3. viṉā veḻuttukkaḷ - e, yā, ō
4. eṇṇiṭic col - um
5. uvama urupu - pōle, kaṇakkē;
6. pāl vikutikaḷ
ā. pā: aṉ, āṉ, āḷaṉ, āḷi, i, kāraṉ, oṉ, maṉ.
pe.pā: aḷ, āḷ, atti, acci, aci, āṭṭi, ātti, i, icci, oḷ, kāratti, mi.
pa.pā: ar, ār, o, kār, kārar, mar, mār
o.pā: atu, tu.
pal.pā: kaḷ, va.
7. cuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷ
appōḷ (appōḻtu), aviṭē (avviṭi), avviṭam, aṉṉu (aṉṟu), aṅṅaṉē (aṅṅaṉē), aṅṅu (aṅku), aṅṅōṭṭu (aṅkiṭṭu), iṅṅaṉamē ikarac cuṭṭum.
8. viṉāvaṭic coṟkaḷ
eppōḷ, eviṭē, eṉṉu (eṉṟu) mutaliyaṉa.
9. eṇmuṟi yoṭṭu
ām. e.ṭu. : oṉṉām, raṇṭām, pattām, patiṉeṭṭām, nūṟām.
toṭarccoṟkaḷ
muḻam iṭuka veyil tāḻuka, iṭavum valavum, aṭavukaḷ piḻekka, nalla aṭi, vayiṟṟiṉṉu aṭikka, colpaṭikku naṭakka, nāṭu kaṭattuka, nāṟṟam piṭikka, aṟimukam uṇṭākka, nāḷ pōkkuka, niṟaya uṇṭiṭṭu, aṟuti ceyka, nīntik karēṟuka, nūl ōṭṭuka, oṉṉiṉṉum ākā, paṭṭiṇi kiṭakkuka, paḷḷi koḷka, āñci nōkkuka, paḻakip pōyi, paḻi vāṅṅuka, āṇayiṭuka, pā viṭuka, pāḷayam iṟaṅṅuka, puṟamkāṭṭuka, uṟakkam piṭikka, pukaññu pōyi, puṭam vekka, puṭam iṭuka, vāyi pottuka, uḷḷattil paṟṟuka, maṉam poṟukka, vīṭu eṭukka, pottip piṭikka, oḻiccutarika (Vacate) pōyāṇṭu, oru nāḷu milla, kaṭam koḷka, pōkku varavu, kaṭam koṭukka, kaṭam oḻikka, pōrāte pōka, kuṟiyiṭuka, maḻa peyka, maḻa nilkka, kaṇakku tīrkka, māṟṟam ceyka, māṟṟu maruṉṉu, kappal iṟakka, kappal ēṟuka, mālayiṭuka, miṉakkeṭuka, kappam keṭṭuka, kappal ōṭṭuka, kāṭāyi kiṭakka, talamuṭi vīḻuka, muṭṭakkōḻi, muṟayiṭaka, muṟam tūṟṟuka, tī kāyuka, nilāvu kāyiṉṉu, mūkku cīntuka, mūrinivirka, mēḷam koṭṭuka, mēlkīḻāka, kāryam naṭakka, moṭṭa aṭikka, mōtirak ki, vayaṟṟuppāṭa, kūṭṭikkoṇṭu pōka, vala vīcaka, oruvaḻi kalpikka, vaḻiye pōyi, ki viṭuka, vaḻiviṭuka, vārikkoṭakka, uppu viḷayikka, viṭa koṭukka, viṭakoḷka, viṭavāṅṅuka, vila ēṟuka, tākam tīrkka, viḷakku vekka, vīṟu kāṭṭuka, veṟum vayiṟṟil, veḷḷa vīcaka, vēli aṭekka, vēli keṭṭuka, tāṅṅi paṟaka, tāṇu koṭukka, muṭṭekku ulāvuka, tikka muṭṭuka, nāveṭuttu paṟaka, uḷḷatu koṭukka, nōṉpu nōlkka, nōṉpu kākka, maṅkaḷam kūṟuka, maṇi taṭṭuka. matikeṭukka, maṟumukam nōkka, kāṭu maṟaññu pārkka, kūṭṭip paḻaka, miḻuṅṅip paṟaka, keṭṭi, nilkuṉṉa veḷḷam, mukattiṭ ṭaṭikka, kōṭṭāṉi iṭuka, kaṇṭiccu paṟaka, moḻiyum  moḻi kēṭum, cammaṇam kutti irikka, vaḻi piṟekka, cāṭi vīṇukaṭikka, cākātatu ellām tiṉṉuka, vāṭṭam piṭikka (oru pakkam cāyka), peṇṇiṉe vāḻikka (vāḻvikka), puṟattāka vaṇṭi keṭṭu, tīṉpaṇṭam, eṉṉeṉṉēkkum (eṉṟeṉ ṟikkum), piṟavik kuruṭaṉ, pāl kāccu, iṭaviṭāte kaḷa paṟi, tīppeṭṭi mutaliyaṉa.
pakirvup peyar
ōrōṉṉu (ovvoṉṟu), īraṇṭu (ivviraṇṭu), mummūṉṉu, naṉṉālu, ayyañcu, patuppattu (pappattu).
maraput toṭarkaḷ
akavum puṟavum nōkka, avaṉ tulaññu pōyi, atiṉṟe ippuṟavum appuṟavum nōkkātē, uruṭṭum piraṭṭum paṟaka, pottip potiññu vikka, eṉikku entu pōyi, kaṭikaṭi eṉṉu paṟaka, kaṇakkum kāryavum, kaṇṇu miḻikkuṉṉatiṉ mumpē, karakaṇṭavaṉ, mukam muṟiccu paṟaka, kaḷavum kayyumā yakappaṭuka, orunāḷ muṅkūṭṭi, kāṭu vāvā vīṭu pōpō eṉṉu paṟayuṉṉa kālam, kāṇiccuk koṭukka, niṉṟe mukattu mica muḷac ciṭṭillē, avaṉṟē kāryam kaḻiññu, itil kāryam oṉṉu milla, maṟṟoruttaṉ collappaṭṭikku naṭakkuṉṉavaṉ; raṇṭum keṭṭa, oru kiyāyirukka, kikaṇṭa, veṭṭā veḷiccam, cūcimuṉaye koṇṭu kuttu vāṉuḷḷa nilam koṭukkuṉṉilla, ūrum uṭalum illa, eṉṟe vīṭṭil eccilum kuppayum āyi, ivaṉ oru āṇi ākuṉṉu, oṉṉum iṟaṅṅāta camayam, untum taḷḷam (aṭipiṭi), nañcu tiṉṉa pōlē āyi, ēnti ēntikkoṇṭu naṭakka, kaṇṇima kūṭṭi yilla, untalum piṭiyumāka, karakāṇuka, uṭal ompatukāṇum viṟekka, raṇṭu kiyilum ciraṭṭapiṭiccu pōka.
vākkiyaṅkaḷ (coṟṟoṭarkaḷ)
ñāṉ ā āḷōṭu ī kutiraye vāṅki = nāṉ av āḷiṭam ik kutiriyi vāṅkiṉēṉ.
accaṉ puṟappeṭṭu pōya cēṣam oruttaṉ vannu eṉṟe cōṟu tiṉṉu = attaṉ (appaṉ) puṟappaṭṭup pōṉapiṉpu. oruttaṉ vantu eṉṉuṭiya cōṟṟit tiṉṟāṉ.
eṉṟē cerippu veṭippākkeṇam eṉṉu ñāṉ tivasan tōṟum kalpikkuṉṉeṉkilum, nī itu orikkalum naṉṉāyi ceyyāṟilla = eṉṉuṭiya ceruppit tuppuravākka vēṇṭumeṉṟu nāṉ nāḷtōṟum coṉṉālum nī iti orukālum naṉṟāyc ceykiṟatilli.
paḻamoḻikaḷ (paḻañcol - ma.)
akattiṭṭāl puṟattaṟiyām
ari (arici) eṟiññāl āyiram kākka.
aḷavu kaṭaṉṉāl amrutum nañcu.
ākum kālam ceytatu cākum kālam kāṇām.
āyiram mākāṇi aṟupattu raṇṭara.
ikkaraṉiṉṉu nōkkumpōl akkara pacca.
irimpūra kallum tēyum.
īr eṭuttāl pēṉ kūliyō.
kaṭalil kāyam kalakkiyatu pōle.
kāmattiṉṉu kaṇṇilla.
kāṟṟuḷḷa pōḷ tūṟṟaṇum.
kāryattiṉṉu kaḻuta kālum piṭikkēṇam.
kuraṅṅiṉṟe kayyil pūmāla kiṭṭiyatupōle.
kurakkuṉṉa nāyi kaṭikkayilla.
kōṭāte kāṇi koṭuttāl kōṭi koṭutta palam.
kōṭi kōṭi koṭuttāl kāṇi koṭutta palam.
tāḻē koytavaṉ ēṟē cumakkēṇam.
tīyillāte pukayuṇṭāka yilla.
nari nareccālum kaṭikkum.
nilāviṉe nōkki nāyi kurakkumpōle.
palatuḷḷi peru veḷḷam.
marattiṉṉu kāyi kaṉamō?
maruṉṉum viruṉṉum mūṉṉu nāḷ.
malarṉṉu kiṭaṉṉu tuppiyāl māṟattu viḻum.
mīṅkaṇṭam vēṇṭāta pūccayilla.
raṇṭu tōṇiyil kāl veccutu pōle.
tāḻa = aṭiyil. ēṟa = mikutiyāka.
oru kati
oru akrahārattil oru pirāmaṇaṉ pārttiruṉṉu. avaṉṉu makaṉ oṉṉē uṇṭāyiruṉṉu. oru nāḷ avaṉ oru vaḻikku yātra puṟappeṭṭu, makaṉeyum kūṭṭikkoṇṭu pōyi, vaḻiyil veccu ā ceṟukkaṉ accaṉe viḷiccu, ayyō! puli varuṉṉu eṉṉu uṟakkē nilaviḷiccu. accaṉ tiriññu nōkkiyapōḷ puliye kaṇṭilla. avar piṉṉēyum kuṟaya tūram pōyapōḷ vātavattil oru puli vaṉṉu ceṟukkaṉe piṭiccu, ā ceṟuk kaṉ vīṇṭum accaṉe viḷiccu, taṉṟe makaṉ mumpēpōlē kaḷivākka paṟayuṉṉu eṉṉu vicāriccu tiriññu nōkkiyilla. puli ceṟukkaṉe koṉṉu tiṉṉu.
itaṉ coṉmoḻipeyarppu
oru pārppaṉac cēriyil oru pārppāṉ vatintiruntāṉ. avaṉukku makaṉ oruvaṉē uṇṭāyiruntāṉ. oru nāḷ avaṉ oru vaḻippōkkup puṟappaṭṭu makaṉiyuṅ kūṭṭik koṇṭu pōṉāṉ. vaḻiyil vittu ac ciṟukkaṉ (ciṟuvaṉ) attaṉi (appaṉi) viḷittu, aiyō! puli varukiṟatu eṉṟu urakkak kattiṉāṉ. attaṉ tirumpi nōkkiya pōḻtu puliyik kaṇṭilli. avarkaḷ piṉṉēyum kuṟinta tolivu pōya pōḻtu uṇmiyil oru puli vantu ciṟukkaṉip piṭittatu. ac ciṟukkaṉ mīṇṭum attaṉi viḷittāṉ. taṉṉuṭiya makaṉ muṉpēpōla viḷiyāṭṭukkuc colkiṟāṉ eṉṟu eṇṇit tirumpip pārkkavilli. puli ciṟukkaṉik koṉṟu tiṉṟatu.
kuṟippu : ciṟukkaṉ (ā. pā.) - ciṟukki (pe. pā.) tamiḻil ciṟukkaṉ ceyyuḷ vaḻakku; ciṟukki ulaka vaḻakku kaḷi (ma.) viḷiyāṭṭu (ta.).  (ti.tā.)
maliyāḷamum tamiḻum
in nāvalan tēyattup paḻiya moḻikaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa, vaṭamoḻi, teṉmoḻi eṉṉum iruperu moḻikaḷuḷ aṭaṅkum. avaṟṟuḷ, vaṭamoḻic cārpiṉa vaṭintiyāvilum, teṉmoḻic cārpiṉa teṉṉintiyāvilum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. āriyam intiyāviṟkuṭ pukumuṉṉarē, tiraviṭam iṅku eṇṇaru nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka vaḻaṅki vantatāl, intiyāviṉ toṉmutumoḻi tiraviṭam eṉṉum teṉmoḻiyē. kumari mutal paṉimali (imayam) vari oru kālattil intiyā muḻutum teṉmoḻiyē vaḻaṅkivantateṉpataṟku, peluccit tāṉattilum vaṅkāḷattilum, muṟiyē, pirākuvi aracamakāl eṉṉum  tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅki varuvatum, vaṭanāṭṭu āriyamoḻi kaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi oru maruṅku tiraviṭamāyiruppatum. kumariyoṭu vaṭavimayat toru moḻivit tulakāṇṭa cēralātaṟku eṉṉum cilappatikāra vāḻttuk kāti urip pāṭṭumaṭi yeṭupputtoṭarum pōtiya cāṉṟām.
iṉi, kaṭikkaḻakak kālamvari vēṅkaṭattiṟkut teṟkuppaṭṭa nāvalnilam muḻuvatum tamiḻnāṭā yiruntami.
