poṉṉampalam, veḷḷiyampalam, ciṟṟampalam, pērampalam eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiyal niliyum  (tol. puḷḷi. 82)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāppaṭi veyil eṉṉum nili moḻi attuc cāriyi peṟṟum puṇarum puṇarcciyi, iṉṟu, veyilattuc ceṉṉu, veyilattup pōkarutu eṉa maliyāḷa nāṭṭu vaḻakkiltāṉ kāṇkiṉṟōm.
patiṟṟuppattu eṉṉum puṇarmoḻip peyarōṭotta muppatiṟṟuppattu, ampatiṟṟu nālu mutaliya iṟṟuc cāriyip puṇarccit toṭarmoḻikaḷum iṉṟu maliyāḷa nāṭṭiltāṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
cila maliyāḷa nāṭṭut teṉcoṟkaḷ muntu niliyiyē iṉṟum tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu:	maliyāḷam		tamiḻ
	ari		arici
	uḷḷu(uḷ)		uṇṭu
	nallu(nal)		naṉṟu
	el		elumpu
	parantu		paruntu
	pōḻ		pōḻtu
ari yeṟiññāl āyiram kākka, karayuṉṉa kuṭṭikkē pāluḷḷu, vampaṉōṭu vaḻutu nallu eṉpaṉa maliyāḷap paḻamoḻikaḷ.
puram = uyarvu. puriyuyar pākum eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (uri.4).
puram - param = mēl. coṟṟirivil ukaramutal akaramutalātal iyalpu.
e.ṭu. muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku. kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu. kuṭam - kaṭam.
parampu - paṟampu = (uyarnta) mali. paraṇ = mēliṭam. parantu - paruntu uyaramākap paṟakkum paṟavi. uyaravuyarap paṟantālum ūrkkuruvi paruntākumā? eṉṉum tamiḻp paḻamoḻiyum, paṇattiṉṉu mīte parantum paṟakkayilla eṉṉum maliyāḷap paḻañcollum iṅku nōkkattakkaṉa. parantu eṉṉum colliṉ iṭiyiluḷḷa akaram, īṟṟiluḷḷa ukaraccārpāl ukaramāyiṟṟu. itu uyiricivu māṟṟam (Harmonious sequence of vowels) eṉṉum neṟimuṟi yām. o.nō. parampu-parumpu. parāntu, pirāntu eṉṉum ulakavaḻakkum iṅku nōkkattakkaṉa.
pōḻ - pōḻtu - poḻutu - pōtu. pōḻtu - pōḻtam.
o.nō: vīḻ - vīḻtu - viḻutu.
tamiḻiluḷḷa cila ilakkaṇac colvakikaḷiṉ varaṉmuṟi. maliyāḷac coṟṟuṇi koṇṭē aṟiyamuṭikiṉṟatu.
e.ṭu.
1. nikaḻkāla iṭinili
kiṉṟu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla iṭinili, kunnu, unnu eṉṟu maliyāḷattilum. kun - un(uṉ) - n eṉṟu tamiḻilum tiriyum.
e.ṭu. ceykiṉṟāṉ - ceykunnāṉ - ceykunaṉ.
ceykunnāṉ - ceyyunnāṉ - ceyyunaṉ - ceynaṉ.
makiḻnaṉ, vāṇar (vāḻnar), āakuna (tol. 932), kaḷiyunar (kuṟaḷ. 879). pāṭunar, īkunar (puṟam. 235), pakarunar, ōcunar, tarunar (cilap. intira.) mutaliya nikaḻkāla viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarkaḷellām, mēṟkūṟiya vakiyil amintaviyē. kiṉṟu eṉṉum iyalnilikkum kun - un - n eṉṉum iṟutit tirinilikaṭkum uḷḷa toṭarpi. kunnu, unnu eṉṉum iṭittiri nilikaḷākiya maliyāḷa vaṭivukaḷē kāṭṭi niṟṟal kāṇka. iv nikaḻkāla iṭiniliyiṉ iyalpi virivāka viḷakkiṉām. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
2. etirmaṟi ēvalviṉi
ceyyātē eṉṉum etirmaṟi ēval allatu muṉṉili vilakkuviṉi. maliyāḷattil ceyyarutu eṉṉum vaṭivu koṇṭu niṟkum. tamiḻ vaṭivil viḷaṅkāta iyalpu maliyāḷa vaṭivil viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ceyyarutu eṉpatu ceyyaritu eṉpataṉ tiripu. ceyyaritu eṉpatu ceyya muṭiyātu eṉṉum poruḷatu. perumpālum muṭiyāmi allatu iyalāmip poruṭṭuc coṟkaḷē vilakku viṉiyā kiṉṟaṉa. ceyyakkūṭum, ceyyakkūṭātu, ceyyappaṭum, ceyyappaṭātu eṉṉum viṉikaḷ. uṭaṉpāṭṭu vaṭivil muṭitaṟporuḷiyum etirmaṟi vaṭivil muṭiyāmip poruḷi aṭiyākak koṇṭu vilakkupporuḷiyum, muṟiyē, uṇarttutal kāṇka. ceyyātē eṉṉum viṉi ceyyarutē eṉṉum ēkāravīṟṟu vaṭiviṉ tiripām. āyiṉ, ceyyātē eṉpatu tamiḻil orumikku maṭṭum urittā yirukka, ceyyarutu eṉpatu maliyāḷattil orumi paṉmi irumikkum potuvām. ceyyarutē eṉpatu maliyāḷattil ceyyarute eṉṟum niṟkum. iṉi, arutu eṉpatu maliyāḷattil tuṇiviṉiyāy maṭṭumaṉṟit taṉiviṉiyākavum varum.
e.ṭu: ī āḷkku vēṟē paṇi arutu = iv āṭku vēṟu vēli kūṭātu.
3. ikaravīṟṟu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccam
iṟantakāla viṉiyecca vaṭivuṭaṉ āl vikuti cērvatāl, orucār etirkāla viṉiyeccan tōṉṟum.
e.ṭu:	vantu + āl - vantāl
		kaṇṭu + āl - kaṇṭāl
		ceṉṟu + āl - ceṉṟāl
		kiṭṭi + āl - kiṭṭiyāl - kiṭṭiṉāl
yakaram cilaviṭattu nakaramāka māṟuvatu iyalpu.
o.nō: yāṉ - nāṉ, yamaṉ - namaṉ.
im muṟiyil, eḻutiyāl - eḻutiṉāl, ūtiyāl - ūtiṉāl, tīṇṭiyāl - tīṇṭiṉāl, kūṭiyāl - kūṭiṉāl, tōṉṟiyāl - tōṉṟiṉāl, tēṟiyāl - tēṟiṉāl eṉa yakara vuṭampaṭumey peṟṟa etirkāla viṉiyeccaṅkaḷi lellām. av yakaramey ṉakarameyyākat tiriyap peṟum. im muṟiyilēyē. āyāl - āṉāl, pōyāl - pōṉāl eṉavum varum. āyiṉ, āyiṉāl, pōyiṉāl eṅṅaṉam āyiṉaveṉiṉ āyi pōyi eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷē āl vikuti cērntu āyiyāl - āyiṉāl, pōyi yāl - pōyiṉāl eṉa muṟiyē tirintaṉa veṉka. itaṉāl iṉ eṉa ōr iṟantakāla viṭinili illi eṉpatum peṟṟām.
iṟantakāla viṉiyecca vaṭivukaḷē, mutaṟkālattil iṟṟi maliyāḷattiṟpōl muṟṟākavumiruntu piṉṉar muṟṟi vēṟupaṭuttuvāṉ vēṇṭi pālīṟu peṟṟaṉavātaliṉ, iṟantakāla muṟṟukaḷi uṟuppup pirittuk kāṭṭaṟku mutaṟkaṇ eccamum īṟumākavē pirittuk kōṭal vēṇṭum.
e.ṭu:	vantu + āṉ - vantāṉ
		kaṇṭu + āṉ - kaṇṭāṉ
		ceṉṟu + āṉ - ceṉṟāṉ
		kāṭṭi + āṉ - kāṭṭiyāṉ - kāṭṭiṉāṉ
		pōyi + āṉ - pōyiyāṉ - pōyiṉāṉ
		pōyi - pōy + āṉ - pōyāṉ - pōṉāṉ
iṅṅaṉam pirittuk kāṇumpōtu, nilimoḻikku ukara vuyirmey yīṟum ikara vuyirmey yīṟumaṉṟi iṉ īṟiṉmi kaṇṭu teḷika.
eḻutiyāl, kiṭṭiyāl eṉṉum yakara vuṭampaṭumey peṟṟa etirkāla viṉiyeccaṅkaḷum, āyi, pōyi eṉṉum ikaravīṟṟu iṟantakāla viṉi yeccaṅkaḷum, cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭukaḷil vaḻakku vīḻntu pōyiṉum, maliyāḷa(cēra) nāṭṭil iṉṟum vaḻakkāṟṟil iruntuvarukiṉṟaṉa. iviyē muntiya vaṭivukaḷām.
ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum  (tol. moḻi. 25)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
nāi - nāyi - nāy eṉpatupōṉṟē āi - āyi - āy eṉpatum eṉa aṟika.
iṉi, vaṭanāṭṭu moḻiyākiya intiyilum piṟa āriya moḻi kaḷilum eḻuvāyp peyariyum peyarp payaṉiliyiyum iṇikkum iru eṉṉum puṇarppuc coṟkum (copula), maliyāḷam aṭikōli yiruntirukkiṉṟatu eṉṉalām
e.ṭu: 	itu entākuṉṉu (entāṇu)?
		itu oru maramākuṉṉu (maramāṇu).
		yaḵ kiyā hi?  What is this?
		yaḵ pēṭ iA.	 This is a tree.
iṉi, moḻittuṟiyil maṭṭumaṉṟik kalittuṟiyilum paḻan tamiḻ marapu maliyāḷa nāṭṭilēyē pōṟṟappaṭṭu varuvatik kāṇalām. mātaṅkaḷi ōrip peyarāṟ kuṟippatum katakaḷi eṉṉuṅ kūttuvakiyum paḻantamiḻp paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷē.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, maliyāḷam vaṭamoḻi kalanta cēra nāṭṭuk koṭuntamiḻē eṉṟum, atu tamiḻttūymikkum tiraviṭac collārāycci moḻiyārāyccikkum peritum tuṇiyāvateṉṟum, atiluḷḷa teṉcoṟkaḷ kalantā laṉṟit tamiḻccoṟṟokuti nirampāteṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.  (centamiḻc celvi navampar 1955)
malivaki
aṟi ciṟupaṟi; murampu caraṭ pāṅkāṉa mēṭṭunilam; kāṭu kaṟpāṅkāṉa peruntiṭal; poṟṟi, poṟi, pocci, perumpāṟi; paṟampu ciṟu kuṉṟu; kuṉṟu kuṉṟam kōṭu ciṟumali; kuṟumpu araṇāṉa ciṟumali; mali maramaṭarntu vaḷamāṉa kuṉṟuk kūṭṭam; poruppu pakkamali; aṭukkam maliyaṭukku; vilaṅkal nāṭṭiṉ kuṟukkākavuḷḷa mali. viṇṭu viṇṇaḷāviyamali; ōṅkal uyarntamali; cilampu etirolik kum mali; vari mūṅkil vaḷarum mali. kuvaṭu, kōṭu malic cikaram; muṭi ucciccikaram; koṭumuṭi uccic cikaravucci. kavāṉ iru kuvaṭukaḷ cērumiṭam; veṟpu aṭimali; cāral maliyaṭi vāram  (col.48).
maṟanilikaḷ
maṟam vīram; tuṇivu viṉi muyaṟcik kēṟṟavīram; tuṇiccal nilimikku miñciya vīram; uraṉ allatu tiṭam allatu tiṇmi maṉavuṟuti; tiṭārikkam tiriyam; tiṇṇakkam neñcaḻuttam; taṟukaṇmi iṟappiṟkañcāta vīram.  (col. 58).
maṟam
maṟam eṉṉuṅ kalampakavuṟuppu, aracaṉ peṇ kēṭṭu viṭutta tirumukattik koṇarnta tūtaṉi nōkki, maṟavar eccarittum accuṟuttiyum viṭuppatākak kūṟum.  (ta.ti.11)
maṟamum pōrum
nāṭukāval naṉmi ceytal porutal ākiya mūvaki araca viṉikaḷuḷ, iṟutiyatu aracarkkuc ciṟantatātaliṉ, atu viṉiyeṉṉum potuccollāl vitantu kuṟikkappeṟuvatu marapu.
aracar pōr, āci, pātukāppu, paki, taṇṭaṉi, maṟam, aruḷ eṉṉum aṟuvakik kāraṇattāṟ piṟakkum.
maṇṇāci, peṇṇāci, poṉṉāci, pukaḻāci, viṇ ṇāci eṉa āci aivakippaṭum. taṟkāppu, nāṭṭuk kāppu, matakkāppu eṉap pātukāppu mūvakippaṭum. paḻitīrppu, paḻam paki, poṟāmi eṉap paki mūvakippaṭum. piṟavaracariṉ ikaḻccipaṟṟiyatum tiṟiyiṟāmi paṟṟiyatum paṭiyeṭuppup paṟṟiyatum eṉat taṇṭaṉi mūvakippaṭum. maṟam oṉṟē, atu kaṭu maṟam. taṉ kuṭikaḷ mītu koṇṭatum piṟar kuṭikaḷ mītu koṇṭa tum eṉa aruḷ iru vakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu, kaṭalkōḷ nērntaviṭattu vāḻ nilamillāk kuṭikaṭkup piṟar nāṭṭup pakutikaḷi veṉṟu taruvatu pōṉṟatu; piṉṉatu, koṭuṅkōl maṉṉar āṭciyiṉiṉṟu avar kuṭikaḷi mīṭpatu pōṉṟatu. iṉi, ōr aracaṉ iṟantapiṉ nērpiṟaṅkaṭi yillā viṭattu nikaḻum tāyappōrum uṇṭu. atu taṉṉurimi kāppatākaliṉ taṟkāppiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
aracar tam pakiyaracar tiṟattu naṭantukoḷḷum muṟi, iṉ col, pirippu, kūṭṭu, koṭi, uṭaṉpaṭikki, cantu, taṇṭaṉi, aciviṉmi, ākulam, araṭṭu, vañcaṉi, oḷivu, pukalaṭivu, maṟpōr, kalippōr eṉappatiṉintu tiṟantatu, pirippeṉpatu, pakiyaracariṉ tuṇivari avariṉiṉṟu, pirittu viṭuvatu. kūṭṭu eṉpatu, pakiyaracariṉ tuṇivarit tammoṭu kūṭṭik koḷvatum, piṟavaracarit tammoṭu kūṭṭik koḷ vatum, eṉa iruvakiyatu. cantu eṉpatu, irupaki yaracari mūṉṟām aracaṉ icittu vippatu. taṇṭaṉi eṉpatu, pōr, aciviṉmi yeṉpatu, pakiyip poruṭpaṭuttātu vāḷā viruttal. ākulam eṉpatu, āravāram. atu toṇṭimāṉ auviyārkkut taṉ paṭikkalak koṭ ṭilik kāṭṭiyatum, pōrkkaḷattil pakivar añcumāṟu paṭikaḷi aṇivakuttu niṟuttuvatum, pōṉṟatu, araṭṭu eṉpatu, neṭumoḻi allatu vañciṉam. vañcaṉiyeṉpatu, maṟintiruntu tākkuvatum, mūvēntarum pāriyiyum muttanātaṉ meypporuḷ nāyaṉā riyum vañcittuk koṉṟatum, pōṉṟatu. oḷivu eṉpatu, maliyiṟpōy oḷintu koḷvatu. pukalaṭivu eṉpatu, vēṟṟaraca ṉiṭam ceṉṟu aṭikkalam pukuvatu. maṟpōr eṉpatu, paṭi valiyāl vella viyalāta pōtu māṟṟaracaṉi maṟpōrāl velvatu. kalippōr eṉpatu, irāvaṇaṉ teṉṉāṭṭiṟ pukātavāṟu akattiyar pāṇṭiyaṉ cārpāka avaṉi iciyāṟ piṇittatu pōlvatu. paḻaṅkālattil, ōr aracaṉ taṉ paki vaṉip pōril vella muṭiyāviṭiṉ, avaṉi vēṟoru kaliyil velvatum ciṟupāṉmi nikaḻntu vantatu. piṟa veḷip paṭi.
paṇṭikkālattil, pōrviṉi, veṭci karanti vañci tumpi uḻiñi nocci kāñci eṉa eḻuvakip paṭṭiruntatu. avveḻuvakiyum, muṟiyē, veṭci karanti mutaliya eḻuvakip pūkkaḷ allatu pūmālikaḷ cūṭip porap paṭṭa miyāl, appeyar peṟṟaṉa. veṭci eṉpatu pakivar allatu vēṟṟaracar nāṭṭiluḷḷa ānirikaḷik kavarvatu. itu oru nāṭṭu maṟavar tāmē kavarvatum, tam aracaṉāl ēvappaṭṭuk kavarvatum, eṉa iruvaki. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu taṉṉuṟu toḻil eṉṟum, piṉṉatu maṉṉuṟu toḻil eṉṟum, kūṟap paṭum. karanti yeṉpatu kavarappaṭṭa ānirikaḷi mīṭṭal. vañciyeṉpatu, vēṟṟu nāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṭiyeṭuttuc cellal. tumpi yeṉpatu, oru veḷinilattiṟ porutal. uḻiñiyeṉ patu, ōr aracaṉ vēṟṟaracaṉ nakari muṟṟukiyiṭal, nocci yeṉpatu, muṟṟukiyiṭappaṭṭavaṉ taṉ nakarik kāttal. kāñci yeṉpatu, niliyāmi viḷaṅkit tōṉṟumpaṭi eṇṇiṟantavar virintu paṭumāṟu, irupaṭikaḷum ūṉṟip porutal.
pōr ceytaṟku, aracaṉ nāṭ pārttalum avaṉuṭiya maṟavar kuṟipārttalum marapu. aracaṉ peruṅkaṇiyi viṉavi nalla nāṭ kuṟittu, atil paṭiyoṭu pōrukkup puṟappaṭa vacatiyillā viṭiṉ, aṉṟu taṉ kuṭiyiyum vāḷiyum kōṭṭi vāyi lukku veḷiyē pōkkivippatu vaḻakkam. itu kuṭi nāṭ kōḷ eṉṟum vāḷnāṭkōḷ eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭum. ciṟupāṉ mi muraciyum iṅṅaṉam pōkkivippatuṇṭu. potuvāka, pōrukkup paraṇināḷ ciṟantatākak koḷḷappaṭum.
maṟavar kuṟipārttal, vāyppuḷ uṉṉam puḷviricci eṉa nālvakippaṭum. vāyppuḷ eṉpatu, taṟceyalākap piṟar vāyiṉiṉṟu kēṭkappeṟum maṅkalac col allatu naṟcol. uṉṉam eṉpatu, oru kuṟippiṭṭa uṉṉamaram taḻittuvariṉ tam aracaṉukku veṟṟi yeṉṟum, atu paṭṭuppōyiṉ avaṉukkut tōlvi yeṉṟum, paṭi maṟavar kuṟittukkoḷḷutal. puḷ eṉpatu, ānti kūki mutaliya paṟavikaḷ olikkun taramum kākam vallūṟu mutaliya paṟavikaḷ paṟakkun ticiyum paṟṟik kuṟittukkoḷḷum nimittam. viricci yeṉpatu. pōrmaṟavar pōyt taṅkiyirukkumiṭattil naḷḷiravil ōrttukkēṭkum vāṉaccol.
aracar tam makappēṟṟuk kālattil potuvāyp pōrukkuc celvatilli.
pōrukkuc celvataṟku iraṇṭoru nāṭku muṉpu, vaḷḷuvaṉ yāṉi mītēṟi muracaṟintu atip paṟṟit talinakarattārkku aṟivippāṉ. pōrukkup puṟappaṭumuṉ aracaṉ paṭimaṟavari ūkkuvittaṟ poruṭṭu avarkku oru ciṟanta viruntaḷippatu vaḻakkam. atu peruñcōṟṟunili eṉappaṭum. ataṉpiṉ, pōr maṟavarkku aṭiyāḷappūvum pōrppūvum paṭikkalaṅ kaḷum aracaṉāl vaḻaṅkappeṟum. paṭittalivarkkum ciṟanta maṟavarkkum iyaṟkip pūviṟkup patilākap poṟpū aḷikkap peṟuvatumuṇṭu.
aracaṉ tāṉum pōrukkuc celvatumuṇṭu; cellāmiyu muṇṭu; celvatē perumpāṉmi. aracaṉ taṉakku veṟṟi yeṉṟu muḻuvuṟutiyāyiruppiṉ, pakivar nāṭṭik kippaṟṟu muṉṉarē, atit taṉ iravalarkkum paṭit talivarkkum paṅkiṭṭuk koṭuttu viṭuvatum, ōrōviṭat tuṇṭu. atu koḷḷār tēeṅ kuṟitta koṟṟam eṉappaṭum, (tol. 1013).
pōrukkup puṟappaṭum aṉṟu pōrmuracukaḷ muḻakkappeṟum. pōrmaṟavar, kuḷittu vaṭṭuṭiyaṇintu paṭikkalantāṅkip pūccūṭi aṇivakuttu, koṟṟavikku narapaliyiṭṭu, neṭu moḻiyum vañciṉamuṅkūṟi yāravārittu, nakarukku valamākap puṟappaṭṭuc celvar. poṉṉulakap pēṟṟi virumpum cila maṟavar poṟṟakaṭṭi vāyiliṭṭuk koḷvatuṇṭeṉpatu, cintāmaṇiyāl teriya varukiṉṟatu, (778). pōrppaṭikaḷ paki nāṭṭūṭu cellumpōtu koḷḷiyaṭittuc celvatu vaḻakkam.
paṇṭikkālap pōrkkaruvikaḷ, eypaṭi, eṟipaṭi, kuttup paṭi, veṭṭuppaṭi, aṭipaṭi, taṭupaṭi eṉa aṟuvakiya. villampu eypaṭi; viṭṭēṟu eṟipaṭi; vēl īṭṭi kuntam mutaliyaṉa kuttuppaṭi; vāḷ veṭṭuppaṭi; maḻu taṇṭu mutaliyaṉa aṭipaṭi; kavacam kēṭakam mutaliyaṉa taṭupaṭi.
iṉi, erimaruntu kalveṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppatāl, ippōtu vēṭikkikkāka viṭappaṭum vāṇam, (akkiṉiyā tiram eṉṟu purāṇaṅ kūṟuvatu vāṇattip pōlum!) paḻaṅ kālattil uḻiñip pōril payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭirukkalām eṉa ūkikka iṭamuṇṭu.
paṭikaḷ pōrukkuc cellumpōtu, potuvāka, mutalāvatu kālāṭpaṭi yum, iraṇṭāvatu kutirippaṭiyum, mūṉṟāvatu tērppaṭiyum, nāṉkāvatu yāṉippaṭiyum cellum. ovvoru paṭiyilum cēṉikkaṇimakaṉ eṉṉum kaṇiyaṉ iruppāṉ. cēṉi muḻuvatiṟkum potuvāṉa kaṇiyaṉ cēṉip peruṅkaṇi eṉappaṭuvāṉ. taṉ nāṭṭil naṭakkum pōrāyiṉum, ayalnāṭṭil naṭakkum pōrāyiṉum, aracaṉ cellum pōtellām avaṉuṭaṉ pulavar pāṇar kūttar mutaliyōrum celvatu vaḻakkam. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉuṭaṉ vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟa nāṭaka makaḷir nūṟṟiruvarum, kuyiluvar (vāt tiyakkārar) iru nūṟṟeṇmarum, nakivēḻampar nūṟṟuvarum, āvar.
talinakarukkut tolivāṉa iṭattil naṭakkum pōruk kellām pācaṟi amittu muḷvēli kōlap peṟum. atu vēṉiṟkālattil amikkappeṟiṉ vēṉiṟ pācaṟiyeṉṟum, kuḷirkālattil amikkappeṟiṉ kūtaṟpācaṟi yeṉṟum, peyar peṟum. maṟṟak kālaṅkaḷil nīṇṭa nāṭ pōrkaḷ potuvāka nikaḻttap peṟuvatilli. vēṉiṟpōr kār toṭaṅkumuṉ allatu toṭaṅkiyavuṭaṉ muṭikkappaṭum. entap pōrukkum ittuṇikkālam eṉṉum yāppuṟavilli. pōrukkuc celluṅkālam paṭikaḷiṉ nilimiyiyum pōrk kaḷattiṉ toliviyum poṟuttatu. orunāṭ pōru muṇṭu; pallāṇṭup pōrumuṇṭu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ īrāṇṭeṭṭu mātamum, mutaṟkulōttuṅkaṉ īrāṇṭum, vaṭanāṭṭup pōriṟ celaviṭṭaṉar. pācaṟi pāṭivīṭu eṉavum paṭum.
pōr toṭaṅkumuṉ, pōrkkaḷatti yaṭutta vūrkaḷiluḷḷa ānirikaḷ, pārppaṉar, peṇṭir, makappeṟātōr, piṇiyāḷar, ciṟuvar, mutiyōr mutaliyōr avviṭattiṉiṉ ṟakaṉṟu pātukāvalāṉa iṭatti aṭiyum paṭi eccarikkap peṟuvar. pōrkkaḷattiṟkup paṟantali eṉṟu peyar.
pōr toṭaṅkumpōtu, yāṉippaṭi mikunta aracar tam yāṉikaḷi viṭṭup pakippaṭikaḷi yuḻakkac ceyvar. avvaccamayattiṟkēṟpa, caturam, nīḷcaturam, vil, vaḷiyam, vaṭṭam, arivaṭṭam, cakkaram, ciṟi viripaṟavi mutaliya vev vēṟu vaṭivil cēṉikaḷ amikkappaṭum. pōr neṭukalum muracukaḷ muḻaṅkum. pakiyaracaṉ taliyiyēṉum pakip paṭittalivaṉ taliyiyēṉum koṇṭuvantu kāṭṭiya porunaṉukku, aracaṉ peruṅkoṭi yaḷippaṉ. atu talimārāyam eṉappaṭum.
potuvāy, kari pari tēr kāl eṉṉum nālvakippaṭikaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟum taṉtaṉ iṉattuṭaṉēyē porum. aṅṅaṉamē paṭimaṟavar paṭittalivar aracar ākiya muttarattārum tattam tarattiṉaruṭaṉēyē poruvar. paṭiñar muṉpum paṭittalivar piṉpum aracar, iṟutiyum poruvatu iyalpu. ciṟupāṉmi paṭi mayakkam ēṟpaṭṭu immuṟi māṟiyum nikaḻum. palanāṭpōrāyiṉ, pakaltoṟum porutu irāt toṟum pācaṟiyil taṅkuvar.
pōriṭiyē, tōṟkum niliyilirukkum aracaṉ pōrkkaḷatti ṉiṉṟum pācaṟiyiṉiṉṟum tūtuviṭuppatum, māṟṟaracaṉ ati matittu uṭaṉpaṭikkik kiṇaṅkuvatum, matiyātu tūtaṉic ciṟi yiṭuvatum peṇ kōlam pūṇuvittu aṉuppuvatum uṇṭu.
āṭiyiḻantavaṉ, kuṭumi kulintavaṉ, āyutamiḻantavaṉ, pācaṟi pukuntavaṉ, tōṟṟōṭupavaṉ mutaliyōroṭu porutalum; puṇpaṭṭu viḻuntavaṉik kollutalum; paṭimaṭamākak karutappaṭṭaṉa.
pōr muṭivu, aracaṉuṭpaṭa aṉivarum porutu maṭital, īraracarum uṭaṉpaṭikki ceytal, ciṟi piṭikkappaṭṭa aracaṉ tiṟi koṭuttal, tōṟṟa aracaṉ ōṭippōtal, ākiya nālvaki nilikaḷuḷ oṉṟāyirukkum.
veṉṟa aracaṉ, vellappaṭṭa aracaṉi naṭparacaṉākki maṇa vuṟavu pūṇal, ciṟṟaracaṉākkit tiṟiyiṭuvittal, perun taṇṭam iṟuvittal, kāṭṭiṟkut turattip patilāḷiyamarttal, māṉakkēṭu ceytum ceyyātum ciṟiyiṭal, kollutal ākiya aṟuvakic ceyalkaḷuḷ oṉṟic ceyvaṉ. piṟkūṟap paṭṭa munnilikaḷilum, tōṟṟa aracaṉiṉ tēvimār veṉṟa aracaṉiṉ talinakariluḷḷa vēḷam eṉṉum ciṟikkōṭṭat til ciṟiyiṭappaṭuvatuṇṭu. karikālvaḷavaṉum yāṉikkaṭ cēymāntarañ cēralirumpoṟiyumpōl, ciṟiyiṉiṉṟu tappit tam urimiyiyum nāṭṭiyum mīḷap peṟṟārumuḷar.
pōr muṭintapiṉ, aracaṉ taṉ maṟavaruḷ kāyappaṭṭavarit tēṟṟalum pōṟṟalum, porutupaṭṭavariṉ makkaṭku naṉkoṭiyaḷittalum, vaḻakkam.
veṟṟiyaracaṉ tōṟṟa aracaṉoṭu naṭpuṟavu pūṇāviṭattu, avaṉ talinakarik koḷḷiyaṭittal, ataṉi eriyūṭṭutal, aṅkuḷḷa vayalaḻittal mutaliya aḻipput toḻilum; kaṭṭiṭa mum matilumiṭittuk kavaṭi vittutal, kaṭimaran taṭital, kāvaṟ poykiyi yāṉikaḷi viṭṭuk kalakkal, nāṭu nakar mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ peyari māṟṟal (3 ām kulōttuṅkaccōḻaṉ, pāṇṭi maṇṭalattiṟkuc cōḻa pāṇṭi maṇṭalam eṉṟum, maturikku muṭittali koṇṭa cōḻapuram eṉṟum, pāṇṭiyaṉatu ōlakka maṇṭapattiṟkuc cēra pāṇṭiyar tampirāṉ eṉṟum, peyar māṟṟiṉāṉ).
mutaliya avamāṉat toḻilum; ceytu perumi koḷvatu perum pāṉmi. koḷḷiyaṭikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷuḷ, karuvūlamum virutukaḷum yāṉi kutiriyum aracaṉic cērum; maṟṟavi maṟavaric cērum.
veṉṟa iṭattil, kalveṭṭal, veṟṟittūṇ nāṭṭal, peru maliyiruppiṉ atil taṉ ilacciṉiyip poṟittal, taṉ āṇi aṭippaṭumvari taṅkiyiruttal mutaliya ceyalkaḷi yum veṟṟiyaracaṉ mēṟkoḷvatuṇṭu. veṟṟiviḻā, iyalum pōtellām, veṉṟa aracaṉ talinakar vellap paṭṭa aracaṉ talinakar ākiya īriṭattum koṇṭāṭap peṟum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiyiṭattil atu tappātu nikaḻum. pōril nērnta kuṟṟaṅkaṭkuk kaḻuvāyāka, kōyiṟkum maṟiyōrkkum ciṟanta koṭikaḷ nikaḻttappeṟum.
pōriṟpaṭṭa paṭittalivarkkum ciṟanta maṟavarkkum, avaruṭiya ūrumpērum pōrum cīrum poṟikkappaṭṭa kal naṭappeṟum. atu naṭukal eṉṟum paṭṭavaṉ kuṟi eṉṟum aḻikkappeṟum. naṭukalli nīrppaṭiceytu ataṟku māli cūṭṭip paṭippatum, kōyileṭuttu viḻavayarvatum uṇṭu. (pa.ta.ā.)
maṟutali vaki
etir - Opposite
tirittal - Alteration
paki - Enimity
maṟutalippu - Denial
māṟṟumi allatu maṟutali - Contrariety
maṟuppu - Refutation
maṟi - Negation
etirmaṟi - Strong negation
māṟṟam - Transference
māṟṟu - Antidote
māṟu - Exchange
muraṇ - Contradiction
vilakku - Prohibition
vēṟṟumi - Difference
vēṟupāṭu -  Change.  (col. 41)
maṟuppu vaki
maṟuppu piṟar koḷkiyi allatu kūṟṟi maṟuttal (Refutation); kaṇṭaṉam piṟar koḷkiyiyāvatu kūṟṟiyāvatu kaṇṭittal (Censure): aṅkatam piṟar koḷkiyiyēṉum kūṟṟiyēṉum paḻittal (Satire).  (col : 60).
maṟuvīṭu (maruvīṭu)
maṇamāṉa piṉ, 3 ām, 5 ām, 7 ām, 9 ām nāṭkaḷuḷ oṉṟil, maṇamakkaḷ, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭantāl. maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭiṟkum, maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭantāl maṇa makaṉ vīṭṭiṟkumāma vīṭu māṟuvar. itu maṟuvīṭu (allatu maru vīṭu) pōtal eṉappaṭum. maṟu vīṭu maṇamakaḷ vīṭāyiṉ, aṅku maṇamakaṉukkuc ceyyappaṭum viruntu maru allatu maruvu eṉap peyar peṟum.  (ta.ti. 25)
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ māṇpu
tam vāḻnāḷ muḻutum tamakkeṉa vāḻātu tamiḻkkeṉavum tamiḻark keṉavum vāḻntu, āriyattāṟkuṟippuṇṭum ayaṟcoṟkaḷāl nerukkuṇṭum curukkuṇṭum akappaki varāl iḻippuṇṭum paḻippuṇṭum aṭiyōṭaḻintupōm niliyilirunta tamiḻit tam mukkaraṇattālum ollum vakiyāṟ cellum vaḻiyellām ōvātu pōṟṟikkāttu, āriyattiṉiṉṟu aṟavē mīṭṭu, pallavapuram potunilikkaḻakam niṟuviyum, aṟivukkaṭal ārāycciyitaḻ naṭattiyum, avakiḷiṟ coṟpoḻivāṟṟiyum, palvēṟu tuṟikaḷiṟ payaṉmikka nūlkaḷiyaṟṟiyum, teṉmaturit tīntamiḻi mīṇṭum tōṟṟu vittu, iṉi ivvulakuḷḷaḷavun toṭarum puttūḻiyit toṭaṅki vitta oppuyarvaṟṟa māperun tamiḻaṟiñar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkam
taṉittamiḻ eṉpatu, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ putitākat tōṟṟuvitta tum avaroṭu muṭintu pōvatumāṉa ōr ēṟpāṭaṉṟu. pāl eṅṅaṉam taṉippālākavē tōṉṟip piṉṉar viṟpaṉiyāḷariṉ pērāciyāl taṇṇīr kalakkappaṭumō, aṅṅaṉamē tamiḻum mutaṟkaṉ taṉittamiḻākavē tōṉṟip piṉṉar āriyariṉ poṟā miyāl vaṭacoṟkalakkappaṭṭatu; āṅkilar varumuṉ āṅkilac col kalavātiruntatu pōṉṟē, āriyar varumuṉ āriyac colluṅ kalavātiruntatu.
mutaliru kaḻakak kālattum, vēntar peyarkaḷum potumakkaḷ peyarkaḷum muḻuttūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷākavē yiruntaṉa.
e.ṭu.

potumakkaḷ : ātaṉ, pūtaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ, paṇṇaṉ, kāṭaṉ, kōṭaṉ, naṉṉaṉ, pāṉṉaṉ, nākaṉ, pēkaṉ, teṉṉavaṉ, maṉṉavaṉ, tēvaṉ, mūvaṉ.
vaḷḷuvaṉ, maḷḷuvaṉ, vēlaṉ, mūlaṉ, marutaṉ, virutaṉ, iṉṟu vaṭanāṭum teṉṉāṭum oṉṟeṉṉumpaṭi peyarkaḷ māṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
teṉcolvaḷaṅ kuṟinta mūvaki
1.	paḻampāṇṭi nāṭu muḻukippōṉamiyāl annāṭṭu ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ maṟintami.
2.	paṇṭum iṭiyum tamiḻilakkiyam iyaṟkiyāluñ ceyaṟkiyālum aḻiyuṇṭāmiyāl, avaṟṟiṉ ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ oḻintami.
3.	tamiḻar ayaṟcoṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉamiyāl avaṟṟiṟku nērāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭami.
tamiḻ taṉittu vaḻaṅkātu eṉṟu cilar karututaṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ
(1) āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭami. (2) vaṭa moḻic ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷi vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉṟu tavaṟākak karutuki. (2) putuccoṟkaḷi vēṇṭiya aḷavu puṉintu koḷḷalām eṉṉum aṟiviṉmi.
maṉ (1)
maṉ - manu (i.vē.)
muṉ - maṉ = karutum āṟām aṟivuḷḷa māntaṉ. maṉpatu = makkaḷiṉam.
maṉpatu maṟukkat tuṉpaṅ kaḷivōṉ  (paripā. 15:52)
maṉpatu - maṉpati = 1. makkaḷiṉam.
maṉpati kākkuniṉ purimi  (puṟam. 210)
2. makkaṭ kūṭṭam, paṭi.
maṉpati peyara (patiṟṟup. 77:3)
OS, OHG man, OE, man (n), E mar, Goth manna.  (va.va. 225)
maṉ muṉṉutal = karututal, eṇṇutal, cūḻtal. AS munan = to think. muṉ - maṉpa = pakuttaṟivum matinuṭpamuṅ koṇṭu ciṟappākak karuti yaṟiyum māntaṉ. maṉpati = makkaṭ kūṭṭam.
āṟaṟi vatuvē yavaṟṟoṭu maṉaṉē  (tol. 152. 6)
makkaḷ tāmē yāṟaṟi vuyirē  (tol 153)
maṉ - manu (va.), ṭīnu ayan, ṭīḷu, ṭīḻaḻu ayan, nu ayan, ḻuṭivā. ayanyē.  (ti.ma.751)
maṉ (2)
uṉ - uṉṉal = vi. 1. karututal. uṉṉalē tiyāṉam (kantapu. tirunakara. 81). 2. niṉittal. pe. 1. karutuki (piṅ.) uṉṉal kālē ūṉṟal ariyē muṟuttal mukkāl viṭuttal oṉṟē. 2. maṉam (piṅ.).
ma. uṉṉu, ka. uṉṉicu.
uṉṉalar = karutār, pakivar.
uṉ - uṉṉam = 1. karuttu. (tivā.). 2. ūḻkam, (tiyāṉam), akattiyāṉ paḷḷiyi paḷḷiyi yuṉṉañ ceyta (tēvā. 847: 2). 3. maṉam (tivā.). 4. karutuṅkuṟi, kuṟiyākak koḷḷum maram. ōṭā uṭalvēn taṭukkiyavuṉṉa niliyum (tol. puṟat. 5).
uṉ - uṉṉi. uṉṉittal = ūḻkutal (tiyāṉittal). kaṇṇa pirāṉ kaḻal vāḻttumi ṉuṉṉittē (tiv. tiruvāy. 4:6:9).
uṉṉi = ūḻkutaṟkuriya poruḷ. ñālamuṉṉiyik kāṇṭu nāṅkūrilē (tiv. periyati. 10:1:3).
uṉṉippu = 1. kavaṉippu. avaṉ uṉṉippāyk kēṭkiṟāṉ. 2. uyttuṇarvu (ūkippu.). 3. tappāk kuṟippu iruṭṭilum uṉṉippāka eṭuttuviṭṭāṉ (W). 4. matik kūrmi (W).
uṉ - muṉ = maṉakkuṟippu. muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal (tol. kaḷa. 36).
ka. muṉṉu.
muṉ - muṉṉam = 1. karuttu. muṉṉa mukattiṉuṇarntu (puṟanā. 3). 2. kuṟippu. muṉṉattā ṉoṉṟu kuṟittāypōṟ kāṭṭiṉi (kalit. 61). 3. kuṟippup poruḷ muṉṉavilakku (taṇṭi. 42). 4. im moḻi collutaṟku uriyārum kēṭṭaṟku uriyārum iṉṉār eṉap paṭippōr aṟiyumāṟu ceyyappaṭum ceyyuḷuṟuppu. muṉṉam poruḷē tuṟivaki eṉāa (tol. cey.1). 5. maṉam (tivā.).
muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam = karutum pulam.
muṉ - maṉ = cūḻum mantiram. (piṅ.).
maṉṉutal = karututal, cūḻtal.
maṉ + tiram = mantiram. o.nō: maṉ + tu = maṉṟu - mantu - manti.
mantiram = 1. tiṇṇiya eṇṇam (piṅ.), maṉavuṟuti. 2. cūḻvu (ālōcaṉi) (piṅ.). 3. araciyal paṟṟiya aracaṉ allatu amiccaṉ cūḻvu. 4. amiccar kuḻu, mantirikaḷ cavi. maṉṉavaṉ taṉakku nāyēṉ mantirattuḷḷēṉ (kamparā. urukkāṭ. 31). 4. maṟimoḻi.
niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa.  (tol. cey. 176)
4. tirumūlar tirumantiram pōṉṟa nūl, 5. civapōṟṟi (civāyanama.) eṉpatupōṉṟa uruppōṭum tirumoḻi. 6. tiruvinteḻutti luḷḷa uyirnāṭi yeḻuttu, ci. nāyōṭṭu mantiram nātaṉirup piṭam (tiruman. 2672). 7. vaciya mantiram. 10. cāvippu mantiram. 11. pēy mantiram. 12. māyam.
mantiram - va. mantra (mantra).
mantirakkalappi = mantira maṉṟāṭṭiṟkuriya paṇṭaṅkaḷ. māṉurimaṭiyu mantirak kalappiyum (peruṅ. uñcik. 36 : 226).
mantirakkāraṉ = mantirañ ceypavaṉ (W.).
mantirakkiḻavar = cūḻvukkuḻuvār. mantirak kiḻavari varukeṉ ṟēviṉāṉ (kamparā. ayōt. mantira. 4).
mantirakkūṟi = pūcippaṭṭu. (W.).
mantirakkōṭṭi = cūḻvukkuḻu. maṉamuṇak kiḷanta mantirak kōṭṭiyuḷ (peruṅ. uñcik. 36 : 277).
mantirakkōṭi = mantira vāḻttoṭu maṇamakaḷ peṟum puṭivi. mantirak kōṭi yuṭutti (tiv. nāyc. 6 : 3).
mantirakkali = mantira nūl.
mantirakkāvi = ceṅkāvi tōytta tuṟaviyuṭi. (W).
mantirac cuṟṟam = mantirak kiḻavar. mantirac cuṟṟamoṭu maṉṉaṉum virumpi (maṇi. 28 : 184).
mantiraccāli = aracaṉ amiccaroṭu cūḻvuceyyum taṉi maṇṭapam. māṭakkōyil mantiracāli cērntāṉ (cūḷā. kumāra. 41).
mantirattōḻi = taliviyiṉ aṇukkap pāṅki. yāppiyā yiṉiyeṉu mantirat tōḻiyoṭu (peruṅ. makata. 9:54).
mantiratteyvam = mantirattiṟkuriya teyvam.
mantira nāyakaṉ = mantirat teyvam.
mantirap paṭalam = aracaṉ cūḻvu ceyvatik kūṟum pāviyap (kāviyap) pakuti.
mantirap poruttam = mantiram ōtupavaṉukkum mantirattiṟku muḷḷa poruttam.
mantirapporuḷ = mantirac ceyti, mantirap poruḷ vayiṉ āa kunavum (tol. ecca. 53).
mantira patam = mantirip patavi.
mantiram paṇṇutal = 1. mantiram ōtutal. 2. pēy mantirañ ceytal. 3. māyañ ceytal.
mantiramāli = tirumūlar tirumantiram. ñāṉam mutal nāṉku malar naṟṟiruman tiramāli (periyapu. tirumūla. 26).
mantiramintu = ainteḻuttu mantiram. mantiramintu ... ataṉcevic ceppukiṉṟāṉ (cīvaka. 945).
mantirar = amiccar. (cūṭā).
mantiravāḷ = 1. mantira āṟṟal vāynta vāḷ. (W.). 2. aracaṉ vāḷ. (W.).
mantiravāḷi = mantirakkāraṉ.
mantiravōli = aracaṉ tirumukam. mantira vōli pōkkiya vaṇṇamum (peruṅ. makata. 25 : 39).
mantiram - mantiri.
mantirittal = ce. kuṉṟāvi. 1. cūḻtal (ālōcittal). makaṭpēci mantirittu (tiv. nāyc. 6:3). 2. mantirattāl ūḻkutal. mantirip pār maṉattuḷāṉi (tēvā. 590:4). 3. mantira valimiyāl etiriyi aṭakkutal allatu piṟarik kaṭṭuppaṭuttutal. 4. mantirattāl nōy nīkkutal. 5. mantiram ōtik kuḻiyaṭittal. 6. mantira āṟṟaluṇṭākkutal. nīri mantirittuk koṭuttāṉ.
ce.ku.vi. mantiram ōtutal. mantirittal eṉṉum viṉiccol vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
mantiri = pe. 1. amiccaṉ. paḻuteṇṇu mantiriyiṉ (kuṟaḷ. 639). 2. varuṅkālac ceyti colvōṉ. mantirik kaḻaku varumporu ḷurittal (veṟṟi vēṟki. 8) mantiri = va. mantriṉ (mantrin).
tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻil mantiram iruntateṉpatum, vāymoḻi eṉpatu pōṉṟē mantiram eṉpatum teṉcol eṉpatum, teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
cilar, vāymoḻi yeṉṟoru col tamiḻiliruppatāl, mantiram eṉpatu vaṭacolleṉak karutuvar. tamiḻ muṉ moḻiyum iyaṉmoḻiyum vaḷamoḻiyum vaḻaṅku moḻiyu māku meṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi piṉmoḻiyum tirimoḻiyum kalavi moḻiyum ilakkiya naṭi moḻiyumākumeṉṟum vēṟupāṭu avar aṟital vēṇṭum.
aimperuṅ kuḻuviyum eṇ pērāyattiyum tuṇik koṇṭa ceṅkōṟ kōvaracu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē tōṉṟi viṭṭataṉālum, ak kālattu aracaṉ teyvattōṭoppak karutap paṭṭataṉālum,aracaṉatu kaṭṭaḷiyi eḻutuvōr tirumantira vōli yeṉṟum, avariṉ talivar tirumantira vōli nāyakam eṉṟum, toṉṟu toṭṭuc collappaṭṭu vantaṉar. āriyar varukikku muntiya taṉit tamiḻilakkiyam aṉittum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, paṇṭi vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷi iṉṟu kalveṭṭukkaḷilēyē kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu.
mantiram eṉṉuñ colliṟku maṉ eṉpatu mūlamāka vaṭamoḻi akara mutalikaḷiṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppataṉālēyē mantiram eṉpatu vaṭacollāki viṭātu. maṉ eṉpatu muṉ eṉpataṉ tiripeṉṟu mēlē viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷami kāṇka.
uṉ - muṉ - muṉam - maṉam - va. maṉa - L. mens. maṉa - maṉacu - maṉatu eṉṉum tiripu tamiḻukku vēṇṭiyatilli. āṅkilatti luḷḷa mind eṉṉum colliṟku men eṉpatē mūlamākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu amikkap paṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. o.nō: uram - va. ura. 
kiḷēṉ (Klein), tam āṅkilac coṟpiṟappiyal akara mutaliyil, maṉattik kuṟikkum āṅkilac colliṉ iṉac coṟkaḷiyum mūlattiyum toṭarpuḷḷa tiricoṟkaḷiyum piṉvarumāṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟār.
E. mind. n. ME mind mynd. minde, munde, fr. OE. gemynd. ‘memory, remembrance’, rel. to OHG. gimunt, Goth. gamunds, of s.m., OS. minna OHG. minna, OFris. MDu., MHG., G mine (orig. ‘memory, loving memory’), ON. minni. ‘mind’, Dan. minde, ‘memory, mind’, Goth muns, ‘thought’, munan, ‘to remember’, fr. I. - E base men. ‘to think, remember, have one’s mind aroused, apply oneself to’, whence also OI. matih matih (for mantis),  ‘thought, opinion’, manas, ‘spirit, passion’, manyate#,‘thinks’ munih, ‘inspired’, Toch, Amna, thought, Arm.i-manam ‘I understand’, Gk. menos ‘desire, ardor, spirit, passion’, memona, I remember, wish, long, yearn, strive’, mentor, ‘adviser, mnasuai, to remember, mimneskein, ‘to remind, call to mind’, mnesis, ‘remembrance, memory’, meesios, pertining to memory, amnesia, forgetfulness, mneme. remembrance, memory, mnemon, ‘mindful, remembering’, memonikos, ‘of memory, having a good memory’, minesuai, ‘to be mad’, mania, ‘madness, frenzy’, mantis, ‘seer’, manteia, ‘oracel, divination’, matos, in automatos, ‘acting of one’s own will (compounded of autos, ‘self’ and mntos, ‘thinging’), L. mens, gen. mentis (for I. - E. mntis), ‘mind, understanding, reason’, menini, meminisse, ‘to remember’, menere, monere, ‘to remind, warn, advise, instruct’, monstrun an evil omen, portent, mouster (for mone strom, lit. ‘that which serves as a warning’ fr. morere), monstrere, ‘to show, point out, indicate’, Lith. mintis, ‘thought, idea’, utmintis, ‘rememberance’, OSlav. mineti, to believe, think’, pameti, ‘rememberance’, Russ. pamjat, ‘memory’, OIr. domoiniur, ‘I believe, think’.

kiḷēṉ, mati, man eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷi maṉ eṉṉuñ colloṭu toṭarpu paṭuttiyuḷḷār. aḵtoṉṟu tavira maṟṟavi yellām cariyē. avar kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu, maṉam eṉpatu tūya teṉcol leṉṟum, ataṉ mūlamāṉa maṉ eṉpatē mind eṉpataṟkum ataṉ iṉac coṟkaṭkum mūla meṉpatum, teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
ōraṟivuyirukku mēmpaṭṭa uyiriṉaṅkaṭkellām karuttup pulaṉ iruppatāl, man = thinking animal eṉpatu poruntātu.
Mind eṉṉuñ col varalāṟṟil, AS gemynd, memory, mind, thought (where the prefixed ge makes no difference); Formed (with the usual vowel - change of u to y) from AS.) munan, to think eṉṟu kīṟṟu (Skeat) tam āṅkilac coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟikkiṉṟār.
munan eṉpatil mum mutaṉili; an nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccavīṟu muṉ(ṉu) vāṉ - muṉṉāṉ - muṉaṉ. vāṉ ni. kā. vi. e. īṟu. vāṉ - āṉ - aṉ, āṉ eṉpatē maliyāḷa ni. kā. vi. e. īṟāyiruttal kāṇka.

maṉac ceyalkaḷ
muṉṉutal karuttōṭum karuttillāmalum oṉṟi uḷattiṟ koḷḷutal (To Think) uḷḷutal oṉṟi ūkkattōṭu muṉṉutal; uṉṉittal oṉṟi muṉṉik kaṇṭu piṭikka muyalutal (to guess); uṇartal pulaṉ vaḻiyālaṉṟi nēraṭiyāka uḷḷattāl aṟital; aṟital pulaṉ vaḻiyāka uḷḷattāṟ kāṇutal; ukattal oṉṟic ciṟappāka virumputal (to choose); ūkittal kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷil oṉṟi yoṉṟālaṟital (to infer); eṇṇutal; yōcittal; ōrtal oṉṟi allatu porunta vuṇartal; ōytal nuṇuki āytal : uṉṉutal tiyāṉittal; kaṇṇutal kavaṉamāy muṉṉippārttal (to consider).
maṉac ceyalkaḷ
kaṇittal kaṇakkiṭṭuk kaṇṭupiṭittal (to calculate); karututal caṟṟu viruppattōṭu muṉṉutal (to intend); cūḻtal ālōcittal; terital oṉṟaṉ taṉmi yaṟital; terittal terinteṭuttal (to select); teḷital aiyam akalutal allatu kalakkam nīṅkutal; tēṟutal uṟuti koḷḷutal; namputal; tērtal parīṭcittal, āynteṭuttal; niṉittal ñāpakam koḷḷutal (to remember); matittal nitāṉittal; māṉittal muṉṉiyaḷattal; tīrmāṉittal muṉṉiyaḷantu muṭivu ceytal; ucāvutal uriyāṭi ālōcittal (Discussion); koḷḷutal oṉṟi namputal; uṭkoḷḷutal uḷḷattiṟ koḷḷutal.  (col. 57).

māṇavaṉ teṉcollā? vaṭacollā?
teṉcollā vaṭacollā eṉṟu aiyuṟappaṭum coṟkaṭku, kīḻkkāṇum nilimikaḷiṟ pala vottiruppiṉ teṉcol leṉṟum, irāviṭiṉ vaṭacolleṉṟum tuṇiyalām.
1. 	vērum vērpporuḷum tamiḻiliruttal.
2. 	kiḷiccoṟkaḷum vaḻiccoṟkaḷum, tamiḻil maṭṭumāyiṉum tamiḻilum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷilumāyiṉum, ciṟu peruṅ kulamākavō kuṭumpamākavō iruttal.
3. 	caṅkanūlkaḷil āṭcipeṟṟiruttal, allatu toṉṟu toṭṭa ulakavaḻakkil vaḻaṅkivaral.
4. 	tamiḻeḻuttukaḷāl (tamiḻolikaḷāl) amintiruttal.
5. 	nāṭṭuppuṟattuk kallāmakkaḷāl perumpāṉmiyākavō ciṟap pākavō vaḻaṅkappeṟal.
6. 	(aiyuṟappaṭum collāl) kuṟikkappeṟum poruḷ allatu karuttut tamiḻnāṭṭil allatu tamiḻar uḷḷattil toṉṟutoṭṭē (aḵtāvatu āriyar varumuṉ piruntē) iruntu varal.
7. 	colvaṭivu allatu collamiti tamiḻukkēṟṟal.
iṉi, māṇavaṉ eṉṉum collukkuc ciṟuvaṉ eṉpatē vērp poruḷām. makkaḷ āciriyaṉiṭattuk kaṟkum paruvam ciṟuparuva mātalāl, ciṟuvaṉik kuṟikkum collē kaṟpōṉiyum kuṟittatu. iṉṟum, piḷḷikaḷ eṉṟum, paḷḷip piḷḷikaḷ eṉṟum māṇavar aḻikkap peṟutal kāṇka.
āṅkilattilum, ciṟuvaṉik kuṟikkum cila coṟkaḷ māṇa vaṉiyuṅ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
Pupil. n. (L. pupillus, pupilla, dim.s. of pupas, a boy; pupa, a girl.) 1. A young person of either sex under the care of an instructor or tutor; a scholar; a disciple.  - The Imperial Dcictionary, p. 573.
Pedagogue. n. (Gk. pidagogus - pis, pidos, a child and ago, to lead) A teacher of children; one whose occupation is to instruct young children; a school master now used generally by way of contempt  (Ibid.p.396).

māṇ - māṇavaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ, kaṟpōṉ.
māṇavaṉ - māṇavakaṉ = iḷiñaṉ, kaṟpōṉ, maṇavātāṉ, ciṟuvaṉ, aṟivīṉaṉ.
yāḻppāṇa akarātiyil, māṇavakaṉ eṉṉum collukku 8 mutal 16 āṇṭukkuṭpaṭṭa ciṟuvaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
potuvākak kalvikaṟkum paruvam maṇattiṟku muntiyatāta lālum, maṇapparuvam āṭavaṉukkup patiṉāṟāṇṭeṉṟu akapporuḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvatālum, 8 āṇṭu mutal 16 āṇṭuvarippaṭṭa iḷiñaṉi māṇavakaṉ eṉṟu aḻippatu tamiḻmarapiṟkē mikap poruttamām.  iṉṟum,  vacatiyuḷḷa āṭa varkkellām patiṉāṟām āṇṭilēyē maṇam nikaḻkiṉṟatu. āriyamaṇam eṭṭaṉuḷ oṉṟāṉa piramam eṉpatu. nāṟpat teṭṭiyāṇṭu piramacariyaṅ kāttavaṉukkup paṉṉīrāṭṭip paruvattāḷāyp pūppu eytiyavaḷip peyarttu iraṇṭām pūppu eytāmuṉ aṇikalaṉaṇintu tāṉamākak koṭuppatu (tol. poruḷ.92, uri) tamiḻaṉ 48 āṇṭuvari maṇavā tiruttal mika aritākum. veppanāṭṭār muntiyum taṭpa nāṭṭār pintiyum maṇattal iyaṟki viḷivākum. iṉṟum, mēṉāṭṭār perumpālum nāṟpatiyāṇṭu nirampiya piṉṉarē  maṇakkiṉṟaṉar. intiya āriyariṉ muṉṉōr ātiyil vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkumuḷḷa kuḷirnāṭukaḷiṟ kuṭiyiruntatiṉāl, avarkku nāṟpatiyāṇṭu maṇavātirukka muṭintatu. tamiḻarkkō in nili aṉṟu milli: iṉṟumilli ātalāl, iḷaṅkaṟṟuc colliyi māṇavakaṉ eṉpatu tamiḻvaḻakkēyākum.
māṇavakam = kalvi (cintāmaṇi nikaṇṭu)
māṇavaṉ - (māṇakaṉ) - (māṇakkaṉ) - māṇākkaṉ.
māṇavi māṇākki eṉpaṉa peṇpāl vaṭivaṅkaḷ.
māṇavaṉ māṇavakaṉ eṉṉum īr āṇ peyarum vaṭa moḻi yil, muṟiyē, māṇava māṇavaka eṉa īṟukeṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉ ṟaṉa. māṇavikā eṉpatu vaṭamoḻip peṇpāṟ peyar. māṇākkaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāl vaṭivamum māṇavi māṇākki eṉṉum peṇpāl vaṭivaṅkaḷum māṇavaṉ eṉpataṟku aṭivaḻiyāṉa koṭivaḻic coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyi lilli. ṉakaravīṟṟut tamiḻp peyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ perumpālum īṟukeṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu iyalpu.

mutaliya pakutikaḷum, muṟkāṭṭiya potuppāyirap pakutiyum, āciriya vāyiṟ kalviyi uṇarttutal kāṇka. āciriya māṇavar āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭikkālattē tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntatiṉāl avarik kuṟittaṟkuc coṟkaḷum paḻantamiḻil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. kaṟpōṉ, koḷvōṉ, kōḷāḷaṉ, kēṭpōṉ mutaliya māṇavaṉ peyarkaḷum: kaṟpippōṉ, nuvalvōṉ, urippōṉ mutaliya āciriyaṉ peyarkaḷum: cūttiraṅkaḷilum ceyyuḷkaḷilum mōṉiyetukik kēṟṟa vāṟu amikkappeṟum potuc coṟkaḷēyaṉṟi, marapu neṟip paṭṭa ciṟappuc coṟkaḷalla.
kaṟṟuccolli eṉpatu iṭikkālatteḻunta toṭarmoḻip peyar. māṇavaṉ eṉṉum colliṉ amippum tamiḻmuṟip paṭṭatē. māṇ, pakuti: avaṉ, vikuti. ūṉavaṉ, kāṉavaṉ, vāṉavaṉ, mīṉavaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka. ivaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷi oru kāl ūṉam, kāṉam mutaliya makara vīṟṟu vaṭivaṅ kaḷākak koḷḷiṉum, āṉavaṉ, kōṉavaṉ mutaliya peyar kaḷiṉ pakutikaḷi aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷa muṭiyāmi kāṇka.

māttiri (1)
māttal aḷattal. ivviṉi iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
mā eṉpatu palvēṟu aḷavu kuṟitta col.
mā + aṉam = māṉam = aḷavu. paṭi (mēli vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku).
mā + tiram = māttiram = aḷavu. avaṉ eṉakku emmāttiram eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku.
itaṉ vili emmāttiram (emmāttam) eṉpatu mēli vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku.
vuviṉaṉ muyaṅku māttiram  (kalit.47. 22).  (ti.mā.241)
mā + tiri = māttiri (maruntaḷavu allatu eḻut toliyaḷavu)
mā eṉpatu toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum tamiḻkkīḻvāy eṇṇup peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ataṉ aḷavu (ka/20) (1/20) mā (ma.),  māvu (te.).
arimā, orumā, orumāvari (orumāri), irumā, mummā, nāṉmā, mākāṇi eṉpaṉa avvaḷavāl ēṟpaṭṭa eṇṇup peyarkaḷ.
oru vēliyil irupatil oṉṟāṉa nila aḷavumā eṉappaṭum.
māniṟi villatum paṉṉāṭkākum  (puṟam.184)  (ti.ma.241)
ōr eṭiyil irupatil oṉṟāṉa niṟiyum mā eṉappaṭum (tol 170 uri). iṅṅaṉam palvēṟu aḷavu kuṟitta mā eṉṉum mutaṉilit toḻilākupeyar. āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa kumarināṭṭu muḻut tūymit tamiḻccol. ātalāl, irukkuvētattil mā. mātrā eṉṉum coṟkaḷ āḷappaṭṭi ruttal nōkkimayaṅkaṟka. mā eṉṉum mutaṉili pōṉṟē, ataṉiṉṟu tirinta māttiram, māttiri eṉṟa coṟkaḷum tūya tamiḻ eṉa aṟika. iṉṟum atu māttiram, kēṭṭa māttirattil māttirikkōl (variyiṭṭa aḷavukōl) eṉappotu vaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
aḷapu, māttiri eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷum tamiḻiṉ colvaḷat tiyē kāṭṭum.māttiri yiṉṟi naṭakkumēl nālaṭi. 242) ti.ma.24 eṉpataṉāl māttiri eṉpataṉ aṭippaṭipporuḷ aḷavu eṉpatē eṉṟu aṟintu koḷka. māttiram - mātra (va.). māttiri - mātrā (va.).

vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlamum ituvē.  (va.va:230)
māntaṉ cerukkaṭakkam
iv vulakiluḷḷa uyiriṉat tokutiyuḷ, māntaṉ nākarikap paṇpāṭṭu vaḷarccikkēṟṟa matiyuṭimipaṟṟi uyarntavaṉē. ataṉāl, avaṉ taṉṉi uyartiṇi eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭatu uttikkum poruntuvatē. āyiṉum avaṉ taṉṉi iṟappa matittu, iyalātu eṉṉum col eṉ akaramutaliyil illiyeṉṟum, eṇperuñ cittukkaḷi aṭivēṉ eṉṟum, iṟivaṉē illi yeṉṟum, ulaka muḻuvatiyum orē kuṇṭil oḻittuviṭuvēṉ eṉṟum, tarukkiyum cerukkiyum taṭṭukkeṭṭut taṭumāṟik keṭāvaṇṇam, avaṉ avin taṭaṅki aṟivupeṟum vakiyil, aḵṟiṇiyuyirkaṭkum ciṟcila pēṟukaḷi otta aḷavilum uyarnta aḷavilum iṟivaṉ aruḷiyiruppatu mikamikak kavaṉikkattakkatu.
1. nīṭuvāḻvu
māntaṉ ivvulakil aṭiyum pēṟukaḷuḷ nīṭuvāḻvum oṉṟām. iṉpac ciṟappu ataṉ mikutiyi maṭṭumiṉṟi nīṭciyiyum taḻuvum. uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṉ vāḻnāṭ pērelliyi nōkkiṉ, iṉam uyara vuyara vāḻnāḷ kuṟukuvatik kāṇalām.
uyiriṉam			vāḻnāṭ pērelli (āṇṭu)
māntaṉ				100
yāṉi				100
āmi				120
mutali			300
timiṅkilam			500
br«ku«(American red-wood)		4000
kalipōrṉiyāviluḷḷa oru  marattiṉ (sherman) akavi aiyāyiram āṇṭu vari matikkappeṟukiṉṟatu.
maṉitarkku vayatu nūṟalla tilli.
eṉṟu kapilar kūṟiṉārēṉum, ikkālattil aṟupatāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa tellām iṟivaṉāl aḷikkappeṟum nīṭṭip peṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. nūṟāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavarum orōvoruvar āṅkāṅkiruppatu uṇmi yēṉum, atu arukiya vaḻakkātaliṉ kaṇakkiṟ koḷḷappaṭuvataṉṟu. ākavē, aṟupatāṇṭu vāṇarum ik kālattu nīṭuvāḻiyarām.
acuvattāmaṉ, māvali, (makāpali) mutaliya eḻuvarum eṉṟum vāḻiyar eṉṟum, mārkkaṇṭēyaṉ eṉṟum patiṉāṟāṭṭi yiḷiñaṉ eṉṟum, kūṟuvatellām poruḷoṭu puṇarāp puṉmoḻiyeṉa vilakkuka.
iṉi, mutupaḻaṅkāla makkaḷ paṉṉūṟāṇṭu vāḻntiruntatāka maṟinūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉavē yeṉiṉ, ataṟkēṟpa aḵṟiṇi yuyirikaḷiṉ vāḻnāḷum nīṇṭiruntateṉak kūṟi viṭukka.
2. vaṉmi.
timiṅkilamum yāṉiyum oṭṭakamum parumikkum, arimāvum vēṅkiyum kāṇṭāvum valimikkum, ciṟantavi. uṭaṟ parumi, uṟuppuram ākiya iraṇṭiṉālum vaṉmiyuṇṭām, iv viraṇṭumaṟṟa pāmpum curamaṇṭalamum pōṉṟa ciṟṟuyi rikaḷ naccuṟuppiṉāl vaṉmi peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. pāmpik kaṇ ṭāṟ paṭiyum naṭuṅkum, curamaṇṭalam kaṭittāṟ paramaṇ ṭalam, araṇi tīṇṭiṉāl maraṇam (uyiri - pirāṇi)
iṟṟi māntaṉ tumukki (tuppākki)yoṭu vēṭṭikkuc celliṉum, kuṇṭu tappiṉāṟ koṭuvilaṅkāl kollap paṭuvatu tiṇṇam.
iṉi, parumiyum valimiyum peṟṟa irutiṇi yuyirikaḷiyum eḷitiṟ kollattakka ūṉak kaṇṇiṟkut teriyāta, nuṇṇiya ulaṇṭukaḷum (germs) uḷḷaṉa.
3. aḻaku
iyaṅkutiṇi (caṅkamam) uyirikaḷuḷ, cila paṟaviyiṉaṅkaḷ aḻakiṟkuc ciṟantavi. mayilum kiḷiyum aḻakiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ aḻakiya peṇṭirkku uvamiyāku peyarāka ilakkiyattil vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. pālit tīviluḷḷa viṇṇakap paṟavikaḷ (paradise birds) vaṇṇat tālum vaṇṇikka muṭiyāta aḻaku vāyntavi.
nilittiṇi (tāvaram) uyirikaḷuḷḷum oru cila marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ uṟuppukaḷum kaṇkavar kaviṉ koṇṭa vi. ataṉālēyē, āṭi yaṇikaḷiyum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷiyum taṭṭumuṭṭu kaḷiyum aṇi ceyyum ōviyam. perumpālum ili vaṭivākavum malar vaṭivākavum irukkiṉṟatu. ili mukap pim pūṇ eṉṟār kumarakuruparar. pūvārāṭi eṉṟār ulōccaṉār (puṟam.274). pūkkaṉintu eṉṟār muṭattāmakkaṇṇiyār (poruna.82).
4. nāṟṟamiṉmi
māntaṉ nāḷtoṟum mūvēḷiyum muḻukik kuḷittālum cavarkkāramiṭṭut tēyttālum, cāntamum cuṇṇamum pūciṉālum viyarvi nāṟṟam avaṉiviṭṭu vilakuvatilli. pala vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavikaḷum paṉṉāṭ kuḷiyātiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ uṭalam tī nāṟṟam taruvatilli.
5. eccilaṉmi
kalattil allatu iliyil paṭikkappaṭṭa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷ māntaṉ kipaṭiṉum eccām. āyiṉ aṇiluṅ kiḷiyum kaṭitta kāy kaṉikaḷum, īyum eṟumpum moytta uṇṭivakikaḷum, nāy kuviya vēṭṭiyuyirikaḷum evarkkum ēṟpām.
..... ................... ..... ............ nāy koṇṭāl
pārppārum tiṉpar uṭumpu  (36)
eṉpatu paḻamoḻic ceyyuḷ.
iṉi, māntaṉ kipaṭiṉum virintu keṭum kaṇṇuṟiyum (macāliyum)kaṟivakikaḷum, kōḻi, kuruvi mutaliya cila paṟavi kaḷum āṭu, māṭu mutaliya cila vilaṅkukaḷum vāy vippiṉum keṭuvatilli.
6. tīṭṭaṉmi
aracaṉ iṟappiṉum iḻavu kēṭkac celpavarkkut tīṭṭām. āyiṉ, cetta vilaṅkukaḷiyum paṟavikaḷiyum pārppiṉ evarukkum tīṭṭilli.
7. kaḻiporuḷ tūymi
māṭṭiṉ, ciṟappāka āviṉ, ciṟunīrum cāṇamum nilattiyum taḷattiyum tuppuravākkum karuvikaḷāka maṭṭumaṉṟi, irupāl makkaṭkum tīṭṭuk kaḻikkum tirupporuḷkaḷākavum, ittēyattil toṉṟutoṭṭu āḷappeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
pulampaṉiṉ (āṉmāviṉ) mummalattiyum pōkkuvatākak karuti, civaneṟiyārāl mēṉimuḻutum pūcappeṟum tiṟunīṟu, āviṉ eruccāmpalē. iṉi, civaṉaṭiyār maṭṭumaṉṟic civaperumāṉum tirunīṟu pūcuvatākac collappeṟum. pūcuvatum tirunīṟu eṉṟār māṇikkavācakar (tiruccāḻal). āviṉ pāl, tayir, ney, ciṟunīr, cāṇam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ kalaviyākiya āṉintu (pañcakavviyam) civaṉ kōyilkaḷil teyvaccilit tirumuḻukkiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttap peṟukiṉṟatu. tirumāl neṟip peṇṭir cilar piḷḷippēṟṟut tīṭṭuk kaḻikka āṉintu uṭkoḷvatākavum collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
kālañceṉṟa tuṭicikiḻār a. citamparaṉār āviṉ pāl, tayir, mōr, veṇṇey, ney ākiya aintaṉ kalaviyē āṉintu eṉpar. itu uttikkup poruttamē: āyiṉ iṟṟi vaḻakkililli.
8. oḻuṅku
māntaṉ tōṉṟi aintilakkam āṇṭiṟku mēlāyiṉum iṉṉum pala nāṭṭu māntariṭik kūṭṭuṟavum oḻuṅkum tōṉṟavilli.
nīril vāḻum iṟāvum mīṉum kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāka iyaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. nilattil vāḻum ōnāyum māṉum pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷ manti mantiyāka mēyntu tattam cēkki tirumpukiṉṟaṉa. nilattilum vāṉilum iyaṅkum veṭṭukkiḷikaḷum kuruvikaḷum paṭi paṭiyākap paṟantiṟaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. cila veṭṭukkiḷip paṭikaḷ veyil maṟiyumaḷavu ilakkak kaṇakkāṉa kiḷikaḷ ceṟintavi.
oru tēṉ kūṭṭiluḷḷa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa allatu āyirak kaṇakkāṉa tēṉīkkaḷ oru tāyum pala āṇum kaḻipala peṇṇum cērnta oru kuṭumpamāy iruntu tattam vēliyi oḻuṅkākac ceytu varukiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟumikku eṭuttukkāṭṭākac collappeṟum ciṟṟeṟumpum, ilakkakkaṇakkilum kōṭikkaṇakkilum oru vaḷikkuḷ kuṭiyiruntu ekkāraṇattiyiṭṭum ēṉi vaḷiyeṟumpu kaḷōṭu mōtāmal, ūṇirukkumiṭatti oṟṟālaṟintu talimiyum, muṟkāvalum piṟkāvalum amittuk koṇṭu, ovvoṉṟākavō ciṟcilavākavō oḻuṅkāṉa varici yākac ceṉṟu, iḻukkaviyalātati irunta viṭattum iḻukka viyalvati vaḷikku iḻuttuc ceṉṟum oṟṟumiyākap pakuttuṇṭu toṉṟutoṭṭu naṭāttivarum uyariya kuṭiyaracu, mēṉāṭṭārum nāṇit talikuṉiyattakka viyattaku ceytiyām.
nāy mukattiyuṭiya oruvakik kuraṅkiṉam (baboon) vēlip pakuppum āṭciyoḻuṅkum uṭiyateṉa nūlkaḷ kūṟum.
9. paṇpāṭu
māntariṭik kāṇṭaṟkariya pala paṇpāṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷ aḵṟiṇi yuyirkaḷiṭattuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kuruvi, kākam, puṟā mutaliya paṟavikaḷ orumaṉi maṇatti uṟutiyākak kaṭippiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟil, makaṉṟil eṉṉum paṟavikaḷ iṇipiriyātu vāḻntu oṉṟiyoṉṟu pirivāṟ ṟāmal uṭaṉiṟakkum uṇmik kātalaṉa.
kavarimāṉ eṉṉum tipēttiyak kāṭṭerumi, taṉ uṭam piṉiṉṟu oru mayir vīḻiṉum uyirviṭattakka taliyāya māṉa muṭiyatu.
mayirnīppiṉ vāḻāk kavarimā aṉṉār
uyirnīppar māṉam variṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 969)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
kākam ōriṭattu uṇavik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ taṉ iṉatti aḻittu uṭaṉuṇkiṉṟatu.
kākki karavā karantuṇṇum ākkamum
aṉṉanī rārkkē yuḷa  (kuṟaḷ. 572)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
naṉṟi maṟappatu naṉṟaṉṟu naṉṟalla
taṉṟē maṟappatu naṉṟu  (kuṟaḷ. 108)
koṉṟaṉṉa iṉṉā ceyiṉum avar ceyta
oṉṟunaṉ ṟuḷḷak keṭum  (kuṟaḷ.109)
eṉṉum iru kuṟaḷkaṭkum ilakkāyatu nāyiṉam oṉṟē.
yāṉi yaṉiyavar naṇporīi nāyaṉiyār
kēṇmi keḻīik koḷal vēṇṭum - yāṉi
aṟintaṟintum pākaṉiyē kollum eṟintavēl
meyyatā vālkuḻikkum nāy  (nālaṭiyār. 213)
eṉṉum uṇmi eṉṟum eṅkum kāṇalām.
kaṭuṅkaṭ kēḻal iṭampaṭa vīḻnteṉa
aṉṟavaṇ uṇṇā tāki vaḻināḷ
perumali viṭarakam pulampa vēṭṭeḻun
tiruṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal nalvalam paṭukkum 
puli... ...  (190)
eṉṉum puṟappakuti, puli pacippiṉum iṭappakkam viḻunta vilaṅki uṇṇāk kōṭpāṭuṭiyatu eṉat terivikkum.
karuṅkaṭ ṭākkali perumpiṟi tuṟṟeṉak
kimmi yuyyāk kāmar manti
kallā vaṉpaṟaḻ kiḷimutaṟ cērtti
ōṅkuvari yaṭukkattup pāyntuyir cekukkum
cāral... ... ...  (99)
eṉṉum kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷ, oru mantiyiṉ taliyāya kātaliyum, ataṉ kuṭṭiyip pēṇum poṟuppuṇarcci yiyum, ak kuṭṭiyi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa piṟa kuraṅkukaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟa vaṉpiyum, oruṅkē uṇarttum.
10. matinuṭpam.
vilaṅkukaṭkuḷ nari valakkārattil (tantirattil) ciṟantateṉpatu marapuk kūṟṟu. valakkāram matinuṭpak kūṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
kuṟavi mīṉ, eli, mūṭṭippūcci mutaliya uyiriṉaṅkaḷ māntar kikkut tappuvataṟkuk kiyāḷum virakukaḷ (upāyaṅkaḷ) avaṟṟiṉ matinuṭpattic ciṟappak kāṭṭum.
11. pulanuṇukkam
pāmpu ciṟappāka nalla pāmpu, kūrṅkaṭ pulaṉālum mōppat tiṉālum ellā āṭkaḷiyum eḷitāy aṭiyāḷaṅ kaṇṭukoḷkiṉṟatu. ataṉāl, oruvaṉāl aṭiyuṇṭu tappiya nākam piṉṉar avaṉik kaṇṭu koṉṟuviṭum eṉpatu ulaka nampikki.
maliyāḷa nāṭṭu illaṅkaḷil paṭippiṉālum pūciyiṉālum pēṇappeṟum nākaṅkaḷ, tammip pēṇuvōr iruṇṭa iravil tam pakkamākac celliṉum, avariyum avaroṭu kūṭa varum piṟariyum kaṭippatilli eṉṟu, ceytittāṭkaḷ aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa.
pāmpu nuṇṇiya cevip pulaṉum uṭiyatu. aravam aravam aṟiyum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. iṭiyōci nākattiṟku iṉṉātatu. 
kaḻukiṉam neṭuntolivil kiṭakkum viṭakkūṉiyum mukarntaṟiyum āṟṟaluṭiyatu.
vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉa acuṇam eṉṉum vilaṅku, iṉṉicikkuc ciṟanta eḵku ceviyuṭiya teṉṟum, ataṉi vēṭṭiyāṭupavar ataṉarukē maṟiviliruntu iṉiya yāḻi iyakki atu iṉpuṟṟu mayaṅki niṟkumpōtu tiṭumeṉak kaṭumpaṟi yaṟiya, atu uṭaṉē iṟantuviṭumeṉṟum, ilakkiyam kūṟum.
yāṉi nīṭiya niṉivāṟṟaluṭiyatu.
12. viṉittiṟam
vāṉkuruvi yiṉkūṭu vallarakku tolkaṟiyāṉ
tēṉcilampi yāvarkkum ceyyaritāl - yāmperitum
vallōrumē yeṉṟu valimicola vēṇṭāṅkāṇ
ellārkkum ovvoṉ ṟeḷitu
eṉṉum auviyār pāṭaloṉṟē cila aḵṟiṇi yuyirikaḷiṉ viṉittiṟattik kāṭṭap pōtumāṉatu.
13. payaṉpāṭu
uṇavu, maruntu, karuvi, viṉittuṇi, kāṭciyiṉpam, kāval, āṭiyaṇi, naṟuviri, uṟiyuḷ, taṭṭumuṭṭu mutaliya palvēṟu vakiyil iyaṅkutiṇi, nilittiṇi.,  ākiya irupāl aḵṟiṇi yuyirikaḷum, uṭampu muḻumiyum maṭṭumaṉṟi uṟuppuṟuppākavum, māntarkkup payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
māṭu celvampōṟ payaṉpaṭuvatāl, māṭu eṉpatē celvattiṟkup peyarāyiṟṟu, (pecuniary eṉṟa āṅkilac collum ikkaruttatē - L. peccv = cattle).
14. paṉṉā ḷuṇavēṟṟal
oṭṭakam oru mātattiṟku vēṇṭum uṇavi oruṅkē uṭkoḷḷa vallatu.
orunā ḷuṇavi oḻiyeṉṟāl oḻiyāy
irunāḷuk kēleṉṟāl ēlāy - orunāḷum
eṉnō vaṟiyā iṭumpikūr eṉvayiṟē
uṉṉōṭu vāḻtal aritu
eṉṟu pulampiṉār auviyār.
15. nīṭuṇṇāmi
oṭṭakam paṉṉāḷuṇavi oruṅkuṭ koḷḷalil nīṭuṇṇāmiyum aṭaṅkum.
mūṭṭippūcci mummātam uṇṇā tuyir piḻikkum.
16. piṟantavuṭaṉ iyaṅkal
cila paṟavik kuñcukaḷum vilaṅkuk kuṭṭikaḷum piṟantavuṭaṉ iyaṅka vallaṉa.
17. mūviṭa vāḻkki
cila ūruyirikaḷ nilattilum, nīrilum, cila paṟavikaḷ nilam nīr vāṉ ākiya mūviṭattilum iyaṅka vallaṉa.
18. veṭṭa veṭṭat taḷirttal
ellā nilittiṇi uyirikaḷum veṭṭa veṭṭat taḷirkkum. vātamaṭakki pōṉṟa cila marattiṉ kiḷikaḷ, kāynta piṉpum maḻi peyyiṉ taḷirkkum.
aṭṭi, pūrāṉ pōṉṟa pūccikaḷ naṭuvil veṭṭuṇṭa piṉpum piḻikkum.
19. kāppu
cila marañceṭi koṭikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ uṟuppukkaḷum nōyi ṉiṉṟum pēyiṉiṉṟum kākkum tiṟattaṉavāyk koḷḷap peṟum. mūvēntariṉ aṭiyāḷap pūkkaḷum, pōrppūkkaḷum, kaṭimaraṅkaḷum ittakiyaṉa. vēppili yum veṇciṟu kaṭukum pēykkāppeṉappaṭum.
narikkompu mikunta poṅki (atirṣṭatti) uṇṭu paṇṇum eṉpatu potu makkaḷ nampikki.
20. teyvam
cila vilaṅku paṟavikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. kaluḻaṉum (karuṭaṉ) nallapāmpum iṉṟum uyiruḷḷa niliyil toḻappeṟu kiṉṟaṉa.
naṉmip pēṟum tīṅkaccamum teyvat toṭarpum itaṟkuk kāraṇamām.
iṟutiyiṟ kūṟiya iraṇṭum mūṭappaḻakkamē yāyiṉum perumpālum māntark killāta ciṟappik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cilar māntarkkup pēcuntiṟaṉ piṟavuyirikaṭ killāta peruñ ciṟappeṉpar. cila kiḷikaḷum pūvikaḷum kaṟṟukkoṭutta aḷavilāvatu pēcuntiṟaṉuṭiyaṉa.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, māntaṉ ekkāraṇattiyiṭṭum cerukkaṭi yātu. muṭicārnta maṉṉarum muṭivi loru piṭi cāmparākum uṇmiyi niṉintu, iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭiyaṭintu uyvāṉāka.  (teṉmoḻi 1959.)
māntaṉ tōṉṟiya vaki
uyiriṉaṅkaḷ, aṭimaṭṭattiliruntu uccinili vari, paṭi muṟiyāyt tāḻnta iṉattiliruntu uyarnta iṉamāṉa oṉṟiṉoṉṟu tōṉṟi vaḷarntaṉa veṉṟu, nilanūlār (Geologists) uyirnūlār (Biologists), māntaṉūlār (Anthropolo gists) mutaliya aṟiviyal nūlārāl nampappaṭiṉum, kaṭavuḷ nampikkiyil uṟittu niṟkum perumpāṉ makkaḷāl oppukkoḷḷap peṟavilli.
iṅṅaṉam, māntaṉ tōṟṟam paṟṟit tiripākkam (Evolution) kaṭavuṭpaṭippu (Divine Creation) eṉa iruvēṟu koḷkikaḷ uḷa.
tiripākkak koḷkiyarum, tiripākkam iṟivaṉāl nikaḻttap peṟṟateṉṟu koḷḷum nampumatattārum (Theists), atu iyaṟkiyāy nikaḻntateṉṟu koḷḷum nampāmatattārum (Atheists), eṉa iru cārār.
kuraṅku vakikaḷuḷ kāṭṭu māntaṉ (Orang-outang), kurakku māntaṉ (Pithecanthropos) mutaliya māntaṟ pōlikaḷ (Anthropoids) tōṟṟattil māntaṉi ōraḷavu ottiruppiṉum tōl meṉmi, mūḷi vaḷarcci, pēccuttiṟaṉ, nakiyaḻuki meyppāṭukaḷ mutaliya māntaṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷiṉṟi vilaṅku niliyilēyē iruttalāl, kurakkiṉam uṭalamippil māntaṉukkaṭutta nili yiluḷḷa vilaṅkiṉam eṉṟu koḷvatallatu, kuraṅkiṉiṉṟu māntaṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu koḷvatu poruntātu. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyiṉaṅkaṭkum vaṭivoppumiyaṉṟip piṟapput toṭarpu koḷvataṟkiṭamilli. kālantōṟum uyiriṉaṅkaḷ vēṟupaṭṭum uḷḷaṉa. palvēṟu nilappaṭikaḷiṟ kāṇappeṟum elumpuk kūṭukaḷum kalluṭampukaḷum (Fossils) tāḻnta iṉaṅkaḷik kīḻum uyarnta iṉaṅkaḷi mēlumākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉiṉ, atu iṟivaṉ uyiriṉaṅkaḷi ēṟuvari ciyiṟ paṭittāṉeṉṟu koḷvataṟkaṉṟit tiripākkak koḷkikkuc cāṉṟākātu.
māntaṉ tumukki (gun) pōṉṟa poṟiyiyaṟ paṭikkalaṅkaḷi ākkik koḷḷu muṉpum, avaṉ tōṉṟu muṉpum, irunta kimmalikaḷum, paṉiyum teṉṉiyum pōṉṟa pāmpu pallikaḷum erumiyaḷavāṉa peru vāvalkaḷum, valimimik kiruntum vaḷarcciyiṉṟiyum eñcātum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
ākavē, ēṉiyiṉaṅkaḷip pōṉṟē māntaṉiyum iṟivaṉ paṭittāṉeṉṟu koḷvatē cālap poruttamām.
māntaṉ tōṟṟam paṟṟiyum, oru vayiṟ piṟappu (Monogeny), palavayiṟ piṟappu, (Polygeny) eṉa iruvēṟu koḷki yuḷa.
varalāṟṟiṟ piṉṉukkuc cellac cella māntar toki ciṟuttup pōvatālum, iṉṟu makkaṭṭoki mikkuḷḷa pala tīvukaḷum nilappakutikaḷum oru kālattil māntaṉ kuṭiyirup piṉṟi yiruntamiyālum, taṭpaveppa niliyiṉālēyē makkaḷ niṟam vēṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟuvataṉālum, ennāṭṭu āṭavaṉum en nāṭṭup peṇṭoṭum kūṭi vāḻvataṉālum, evarum ennāṭṭu moḻiyiyuṅ kaṟkavum ennāṭṭu nākarikattiyum mēṟkoḷḷavum iyal vataṉālum, pala nāṭṭup paḻakkavaḻak kaṅkaḷ ottiruppatālum, palamoḻikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpuṭimiyālum, moḻikaḷ tiripāluṅ kalappālumē oṉṟu palavāyp peruki vantiruppataṉālum, makkaḷuṟaviṉiṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭa piḷḷikaḷ oru moḻiyum pēcāmiyālum, palanāṭṭu aṟiñar karuttu oṉṟu paṭuvatālum, maṉpati muḻuvatum anākarika niliyiliruntē nākarikatti yaṭintiruppataṉālum, orē peṟṟōriṇiyiṉiṉṟē makkaḷellām tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. itu varalāṟu taḻuviya muṭipē yaṉṟic camayak koḷkiyaṉṟu.
māntaṉ nilikaḷ
1. tēva māntaṉ (Homo Divinus).
2. iyaṟki māntaṉ (Homo Naturalis).
3. aṟivuṭi māntaṉ (Homo Sapiens)
tēva māntaṉ eṉṉum niliyik kiṟittava icalām mataṅkaḷē koḷkiṉṟaṉa. civaṉiya (civa) māliya (viṇava) camaṇa putta mataṅkaḷ koḷvaṉa ēṉiyiraṇṭumē. avviraṇṭiyum avi taḷiyuyir eṉa oṉṟākavē koḷkiṉṟaṉa.
tēva māntaṉ ulakil vāḻntatākac collappaṭiṉum, naṭi yuṭi moḻi mutaliyavaṟṟil ulakiyal māntaṉiṉṟum vēṟu paṭṭa vaṉātalāl, avaṉ ceyti īṇṭārāyccik kuriyataṉṟu.
ulaka muḻutum paraviya iyaṟki māntar mutaṟkaṇ vilaṅkāṇṭi (Savage) niliyilēyē iruntaṉar. piṉṉar avaruṭ palar arinākarika nili yaṭintaṉar. ataṉ piṉṉar aṟivuṭi māntar eṉṉum nākarika māntar tōṉṟiṉar. ātalāl, ulaka māntar iṉṟu im mūvaki niliyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, nākarika māntar toṭarpiṉāl vilaṅkāṇṭiyār mikac ciṟupāṉmiyarākiyuḷḷaṉar. vilaṅkāṇṭi nilikkum ari nākarikattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa niliyi anākarikam (Barbarism) eṉalām.
mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiya aṟivuṭi māntaṉ tamiḻaṉē. paṇ pāṭṭiyal māntaṉūlum avaṉ tōṟṟattōṭēyē toṭaṅku kiṉṟatu.
māntaṉ piripu naṭuvam (Centre of Diffusion of Man)
māntaṉiṉam kumarikkaṇṭattiṉiṉṟē ñālattiṉ pal vēṟiṭaṅ kaṭkum pirintupōyiruttal vēṇṭum.
itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ
1. 	iṟṟiyulakil muntiyal māntarāyuḷḷa āttirēliyarum teṉṉāppirikkarum kumarikkaṇṭattic cērntavarē. teṉṉamerikkarum kumarikkaṇṭattiṉiṉṟē ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
2. 	kumarikkaṇṭam naṇṇilakkōṭṭic cārnta ñālanaṭu viṭam.
3.	tamiḻ colvaḷarcciyiṉ  aivēṟu nilikaḷik kāṭṭi niṟṟalāl, acinilik kālattil cīṉarum maṅkōliyarum koḷuvunilik kālattil ūral. āltēkka moḻiyiṉarum, pakucoṉṉilik kālattil mēliyāriyarum, pirintu pōyirukkalāmeṉṟu koḷḷap pōtiya iṭamuṇṭu. āttirēliyarum āppirikkarum moḻit toṭakkaniliyil tām irunta iṭattilēyē taṅkiviṭṭaṉar. tiraviṭar nēr vaṭakkē toṭarntu ceṉṟu centamiḻik koṭun tamiḻākavum koccit tamiḻākavum citittuk koṇṭavar.
4.	paṇṭi yekipatiya varalāṟṟi ārāynta villiyar tūvarṭṭu (Villiers Stuart) eṉpavar, airōppiyariṉ muṉṉōruṭ perumpālār āciyāviṉiṉṟu pāpel mantepu (Babel Mandeb) eṉṉum nīriṇippu (Strits) vaḻiyāka āppirikkak kariyēṟi ettiyōppiyāviṟ cila kālan taṅkiyapiṉ, ekipatu nāṭṭu nīlāṟṟu veḷinilat tiṟkupparaviṉareṉṟum, piṉpu aṅkiruntu naṇṇilak kaṭal kaṭantu airōppāviṟ kuṭiyēṟi āriyarāka māṟiṉareṉṟum, ekipatiyak kuṟunila maṉṉarellāriyum mutaṉ mutalāka oṉṟāka iṇittu ōraracāṭci niṟuviya meṉecu (Menes) vēntaṉum teṟkattiyāṉēyeṉṟum, ekipatiya moḻi airōppiya moḻikaṭkellām mūlamāṉa acinili moḻiyeṉṟum, atu camaṟkirutattiṟku mūvāyiram āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭateṉṟum, tam ōr ekipatiya araciyiṉ paḷḷi paṭip paṭamāṭam (The Funeral Tent of An-Fgyptian Queen) eṉṉum varalāṟṟārāycci nūliṉ iṟutiyiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
	muṟkālattil arapikkaṭaṟparappu nilamāyiruntatiṉāl kumarikkaṇṭat tiṉar nilavaḻiyāka vaṭa āppirikkāviṟkuc ceṉṟiruttal kūṭum.
5.	nōvā kālattup peruveḷḷattiṉ piṉ makkaḷ kiḻak kiṉiṉṟu vantārkaḷeṉṟu tirumaṟi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
	makkaḷ kiḻakkēyiruntu vaḻic celkiyil ciṉeyār nāṭṭilē camanilattik kaṇṭu aṅkē kuṭiyiruntārkaḷ. (mutaṟ pottakam, 11:2.)
6. 	nōvā kālattiṟku muṉpē airōppāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ veḷḷiyarākiviṭṭaṉar.
	tēva putalvar māntar makaḷiri mikunta aḻakiyarākak kaṇṭu, avarkaḷiṭamiruntu taṅkaṭkup peṇkaḷit terintu koṇṭārkaḷ.  (tirumaṟi, mu.po., 6:2)	akkālattu makkaḷ veḷḷiyarit tēvareṉṟu karutiṉar.
annāṭkaḷiṟ pāril arakkar iruntaṉar.  (tirumaṟi, mu. bgh., 6:4)
	arakkar eṉappaṭṭavar āppirikkarā yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
7.	aivēṟu allatu aṟuvēṟu moḻinilikaḷil, māntariṉat tār vevvēṟu ticinōkkip pirintu ceṉṟu vevvēṟu moḻiyiṉarākavum niṟattiṉarākavum iṉattiṉarākavum māṟutaṟku ēṟṟavāṟu, mikap paḻamiyāṉa tāynilap pakuti kumarikkaṇṭam oṉṟē.
8. 	tamiḻccoṟkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻar paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum paṇṭi yulakameṅkum paraviyiruntirukkiṉṟaṉa.
9. 	nāṭu muḻuvatum muḻukumāṟu nikaḻnta oru peru veḷḷak kati, ovvoru nāṭṭilum vevvēṟu vakiyiṟ collap peṟukiṉṟatu.
	orukāl, ikkati, paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭit taṉṉuṭ koṇṭa kumarikkaṇṭak kaṭalkōḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟik kuṟittatāka irukkalām.
	tamiḻnāṭṭuk kaṭalkōḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟik kūṟum catāpata pirāmaṇam eṉṉum vētakkāla vaṭanūl, oru kaṭal kōḷukkut tappiya aracaṉit tirāviṭapati eṉṟu avaṉ pēḻi taṅkiya iṭatti vaṭamali (kuṭamali) eṉṟum kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
	nāṭṭiluḷḷa vilaṅku paṟavikaḷellām ovvoriṇi uḷḷaṭaṅku māṟu, māperu marakkalatti 4500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ ceyyakkūṭiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉ oruvaṉē,
10.	tamiḻariṉ eḻunāṭ kiḻami toṉṟutoṭṭu nākarika nāṭukaḷi lellām vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu.
māntaṉ piṟantakam
teṉṉilam
mānta iṉaṅkaḷiṉ koṭivaḻiyum potuppaṭiyāṉa iṭamāṟṟaṅkaḷum paṟṟiya karutukōḷ:- māntaṉiṉ muntiyal iruppiṭam iṉṟu intumāvāriyil mūḻkiyuḷḷa oru kaṇṭam eṉṟum, atu iṉṟuḷḷapaṭi āciyāviṉ teṉ kariyi neṭukalum aṭuttu (perumpālum iruntirukkak kūṭiyapaṭi ataṉoṭu cilaviṭaṅkaḷil iṇintum), kiḻakkil appāli intiyāvum (Further India) caṇṭāttīvukaḷum variyum, mēṟkil maṭakācukkarum āppirikkāviṉ teṉ kīḻkkariyum variyum, paraviyiruntateṉṟum, karutuvikkum pala cūḻnilikaḷ (ciṟappākak kālakkaṇakkiyal uṇmikaḷ) uḷḷaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷum nilittiṇiyum paṟṟiya pala ñālanūluṇmikaḷ, attakiya teṉṉintiyak kaṇṭamoṉṟu muṉṉirunta teṉpatip perituṅ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkaṇṭattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriyaṉa vāyirunta muntiyaṟ pāluṇṇikaḷāl, atu ilemuriyā (Lemuria) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. ati mutaṟkāla māntaṉiṉ uṟiviṭamākak koḷvōmāyiṉ, mānta iṉaṅkaḷ iṭam peyarntu āṅkāṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyirukkum tiṇiyiyaṟ pātīṭu eḷitāy viḷaṅkiviṭum. (M.A.M.P. pak. 33, aṭikkuṟippu (2).
mānta iṉavārāycci, vaṭapākattilum naṇṇilak kaṭaṟ kariyilum iṉṟuvāḻum mānta iṉaṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr, teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyākattāṉ avviṭaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟiruntār eṉpatu, evvakiyilum naṭantirukkak kūṭāta ceytiyaṉṟu eṉpatik kāṭṭum. intiyak kīḻkariyiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa māntaṉelumpuk kūṭukaṭkum aṭiyāḷaṅkaṭkum uriyakālam, iṉṉateṉṟu tīrmāṉikkappaṭāta tāyiruppiṉum, potuvākak kaṇikkappaṭum varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟṟum appāṟpaṭṭa tākum. (M.A.M.. pak. 111).
ituvari ulakiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa māntaṉ elumpuk kūṭukaḷuḷ, cālittīvil (Java)  1891-il tūpāyicu eṉpavarāl eṭukkappaṭṭataṟkuriya nimirnta kurakku māntaṉ (Pithecan thropos Erectus) kālam ki.mu. 5,00,000 eṉṟu kaṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. 1961-il teṉṉāppirikkāvil taṅkaṉiyikkāvil pērā. ilīkki (Prof. Leakey) eṉṉum āṅkila māntaṉūlaṟiñarāṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷa īrelumpuk kūṭukaḷuḷ, oṉṟaṟkuriya neṟṟuṭippāṉ (Nut cracker Man or Sinjanthropos Boisi) iṟṟikku 6,00,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum, iṉṉoṉṟaṟ kuriya, iṉṉum peyariṭappaṭāta, naṉimika muntiya māntaṉ, kuṟinta pakkam 17,50,660 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum, kaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iti amerikka māntaṉūṟ pērāciriyar cilar maṟuttuḷḷaṉar. uṇmi eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, cālit tīviyum teṉṉāppirikkāviyum taṉṉuṭaṉ iṇittuk koṇṭirunta perunāṭē kumarik kaṇṭa mātaliṉ, annilatti lēyē māntaṉ tōṉṟiṉā ṉeṉṟum, attōṟṟam ki.mu. 5,00,000 āṇṭukaṭku muntiya teṉṟum, maṟuppaccamiṉṟik kūṟalām.
uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṉ iṭampeyarvum pātīṭum paṟṟiya atikārattil, ñālattiṉ mēṟparappil aṭikkaṭi māṟikkoṇṭirukkum nīr nilappātīṭṭik kuṟikkumpōtu, ekkēl, intumāvāri oru kālattil cantāttīvukaḷiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki, āciyāviṉ teṉkari vaḻiyāy āppirikkāviṉ kīḻkkarivarikkum paraviyirunta oru kaṇṭamāyiruntatu. kiḷēṟṟar ippaḻaṅkālap peruṅkaṇṭattiṟku, atilvatinta kuraṅkotta uyiri paṟṟi ilemuriyā eṉṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟār. . ikkaṇṭam māntaṉiṉ piṟantakamāyiruntirukkak kūṭumātaliṉ, mika mutaṉmiyāṉatākum.”  (C.T.S.I. Vol. 1,  pak.20) eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
nilaviyal varalāṟ ṟārāyccikkut terintavari, iññālattiṉ toṉmutu paḻampakutiyāyiruntatu, teṉmāvāriyil mūḻkip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭamē. yōvāṉ iṅkilāntu (John England) eṉṉum ārāycciyāḷar, kōṭiyāṇṭu kaṭkumuṉ, orukāl ataṟkum munti, oru peruṅkaṇṭam āppirikkāviyum intiyāviyum iṇittiruntatu. eṉpar.
iṟṟit tamiḻakattilum, nīlamali, āṉimali, paḻaṉimali, ēlamali, cēravariyaṉ (cērvarāyaṉ) mali ākiyavaṟṟiṉ pāṟivaki eḻupatu kōṭiyāṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟiya teṉa, nilanūlārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar.
muntiyal māntaṉiṉ vāḻviṟ kēṟṟa palvēṟu nilimikaḷi nōkkiṉ, iññālattiṉ naṭuviṭamē niṟivuṟṟa māntaṉ piṟantakamāyiruntirukka muṭiyumeṉpatu pulaṉākum. attakiya iṭam kumarikkaṇṭamē. naṇṇilakkōṭu (Equator) ataṉū ṭēyē celvatit tiṇippaṭattiṟ (Map) kāṇka. 
mutaṉili māntaṉiṉ mēṉi muḻuvatiyum mūṭiyirunta kōri mayir utirvataṟkum meṉmiyaṭivataṟkum, veppanāṭṭu vāḻkkiyē ēṟṟatākum.
airōppāviṟkum āppirikkāviṟkum iṭiyē naṇṇilak kaṭal (Mediterranean Sea) eṉṟu peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu, uṇmiyil irukaṇṭattiṭik kaṭalēyaṉṟi naṇñālak kaṭalaṉmi aṟika.
mutaṟkāla māntaṉ kāṭṭuvilaṅkāṇṭiyākavum anākari kaṉākavumiruntu, taṉ vāḻkkikku iyaṟki viḷivukaḷiyē cārntiruntataṉāl, avaṉukkēṟṟa peruvaḷanāṭu kumarik kaṇṭamē.
ētēṉ (Eden) tōṭṭam eṉpatu palvakik kaṉimaraṅkaḷ niṟinta vaḷanāṭṭiyē kuṟikkum. ētēṉ eṉpatu iṉpam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum epirēyac col. pālum tēṉum ōṭum kāṉāṉ tēcameṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappaṭṭālum, mēliyāciyāviṟku atu ciṟantatēyaṉṟi ñālattiṟ ciṟanta nāṭākātu. naṇṇilakkaṭal oru kālattil nērē kiḻakkunōkki nīṇṭu amitimāvāriyiṟ (Pacific Ocean) kalantiruntataṉāl, aṉṟu kāṉāṉum ētēṉ tōṭṭam iruntatākac collap paṭum mecoppottēmiyāvum, kaṭalaṭiyil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṟku muṉpu mutaṟkālattilum ētēṉ tōṭṭa mirunta iṭam nilappakutiyākavē iruntateṉṟu koḷḷiṉum, atu kumarināṭṭiṉum vaḷañ ciṟantatākak koḷḷamuṭiyātu. ativaḷappaṭuttiya nālāṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa aipirāttu (Euphretes) paḵṟuḷi pōṟ pēriyāṟaṉṟu. aṅkuḷḷa malikaḷuḷ oṉṟum kumaripōṟ paṉmaliyaṭukkamaṉṟu. iṭiyiṭi vaṟaṇṭa vemmaṇaṟpālikaḷum pala vuḷa.
piṟanāṭukaḷiṟpōl eṉṟum vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭu kotikkum pāḻ maṇaṟ pālivaṉamākiya iyaṟkinilam, tamiḻakattil evviṭattum iruntatilli. iṅkuḷḷa pāliyellām, mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyum mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil nīrnilivaṟṟi nilittiṇi(tāvaram) paṭṭu nilaṅkāynta kuṟuṅkāla niliyiṉavē. kōṭimāṟi māripeytapiṉ, appālinilam pulpūṇṭum marañceṭikoṭikaḷum taḷirttu muṉpōl mulliyuṅkuṟiñciyumāka māṟiviṭum. iṅṅaṉam pāliyiṉ niliyillā nili nōkkiyē, ataṉi nīkki ñālatti nāṉilam eṉṟaṉar paṇṭit tamiḻaṟiñar.
itaṉāl, paṇṭittamiḻakam īṭiṇiyaṟṟa peruvaḷa nāṭā yiruntateṉ pataṟku eḷḷaḷavum iḻukkilliyeṉka.
māmaṉ
māmaṉ - māmā1, māmaka
ammi - ammāṉ = ammiyiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ.
attaṉ (tanti) - atti = attaṉiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ.
ammāṉ maṉivi attiyeṉṟum, atti kaṇavaṉ ammāṉ eṉṟum, aḻikkap peṟuvar. oruvaṉ ammāṉ makaḷiyum atti makaḷiyum, orutti ammāṉ makaṉiyum atti makaṉiyum maṇappatu muṟi marapu.
iṅṅaṉam neruṅkiya uṟavil maṇamakaṉō maṇamakaḷō illātapōtum, ayalil maṇamakaṉō maṇamakaḷō celvat tilum aḻakilum piṟavaṟṟilum ciṟantirukkumpōtum, ayalil maṇavuṟavu koḷvatu vaḻakkam.  (va.va.)
makaṉiṉ maṉiviyiyum makaḷiṉ kaṇavaṉiyum makkaḷip pōṟ karutavēṇṭumeṉṟum, avarkaḷum kaṇavaṉ taṉ maṉivi peṟṟōriyum maṉivi taṉ kaṇavaṉ peṟṟōriyum taṉtaṉ contap peṟṟōrip pōṟ karutavēṇṭumeṉṟum, paṇṭit tamiḻar tīrmāṉittē, makaṉ maṉiviyi marumakaḷ (maruviya makaḷ) eṉṟum makaḷ kaṇavaṉi marumakaṉ (maruviya makaṉ) eṉṟum aḻittaṉar. maruvutal = poruntutal, kuṭumpattoṭu kalattal, iṉamākat taḻuvutal.
ayalil maṇavuṟavu koṇṭapōtu, maṉiviyiṉ tantiyiyum kaṇavaṉiṉ tantiyiyum maruvammāṉ (maruviya ammāṉ) eṉṟu aḻittirukkalām. maruvammāṉ - māmāṉ.  (va.va.)
maṇavuṟavu koṇṭa ammāṉum maruvammāṉum muṟiyaḷavil orē niliyiliruppatāl, peṇkoṭutta allatu peṇ koṇṭa ammāṉum māmaṉ eṉap peṟṟāṉ. ataṉpiṉ maṇavuṟavu koḷḷāta ammāṉum appeyarp peṟṟāṉ.
māmāṉ makaḷē  (tiv. tiruppā.9).
	māmāṉ - māmaṉ. ma. māmaṉ, te. māma.
māmaṉu marukaṉum pōlu maṉpiṉ  (cīva. 43).
cila kulattilum kuṭumpattilum orutti taṉ ammāṉi maṇantu koḷḷum vaḻakkamiruppatāl, kaṇavaṉi māmaṉ eṉṟaḻikkum nilimiyum nērntatu. oruvaṉ taṉ akki allatu taṅki makaḷi maṇappatum ituvē.  (va.va.).
māmaṉ - māmi = 1. ammāṉ maṉivi.
aṉpuṭiya māmaṉu māmiyunī  (tēvā. 1228 :1).
2. atti.
3. maṉivi allatu kaṇavaṉ tāy.
ciṟakku māmiyar mūvarkkum  (kamparā. cūḷā.33).
ammāṉ māmi - ammāmi (āriyap pārppaṉa vaḻakku) = 
1. ammāṉ maṉivi.
2. māmaṉ maṉivi.
ammāmi taṉvīvuṅ kēṭṭāyō tōḻi  (cilap.29. tēvantiyaraṟṟu).  
(va.va.)
vaṭamoḻiyil māmi eṉṉum peṇpāl vaṭivilli.
māma (māmaka) eṉṉum āṇpāṟ coṟku mama (eṉatu) eṉṉum taṉmi yorumip peyariṉ 6-ām vēṟṟumiyaṭiyi mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar. māma = eṉakkuriyavaṉ.
 “ayajaayaja, a.  (fr. mama, lit. ‘belonging to mine’) dear friend, uncle (only in voc.sg. as a term of affection among animals in fables), Pancat” eṉṟu mā. vi.a. (pak. 810) kūṟutal kāṇka.  (va.va: 231-232)
māyki = mayakkam, mayakkak kātal.
māy - māyi = 1. maṟivu. 2. māyakkali.
māyiyi ṉoḷitta maṇimē kalitaṉi  (maṇi. 18:155)
3. maṟippāṟṟal (tirōtāṉa catti).
4. poyttōṟṟam, 5. poyttōṟṟa ulakam.
tōṉṟikkeṭu māyākāri yatti meyyeṉanī kaṇṭaṉiyē  (aruṭpā, 1, neñcaṟi. 527).
6. aimpūtamūlam. pūtalaya mākiṉṟa māyimuta leṉpar cilar  (tāyu.paripūraṇā.6).
o.nō: cāy - cāyi - va. cāyā (uyajalayaja).  (va.va:232).
māyi
māyi - māyā  (i.vē.)
māḷ - māy
māytal = maṟital, maṟantupōtal, iṟattal, aḻital.
māy - māyam = 1. tiṭumaṟivu. 2. mayakkam. 3. niliyiṉmi.
eṉmāya vākki yitaṉuṭ pukku  (tiv.tiruvāy.10:7:3).
4. poy. vanta kiḻavaṉi māyañ ceppi  (tol.po.114).
5. pācāṅku. 6. ēmāṟṟu (vañcaṉi).
māya makaḷir muyakku  (kuṟaḷ. 918).
7. poyt tōṟṟam (māyi).
varuntiṭa māyañceytu nikumpali maruṅku pukkāṉ  (kamparā.māyā cīti. 96).
8. māyakkali.  (va.va:)
mārāyam
mārāyam eṉpatu, ceyaṟkariya ceyta amiccarum paṭit talivarum maṟavarum pulavarum piṟarum mārāyaṉākiya vēntaṉāṟ peṟuñ ciṟappu. atu mārāyam peṟṟa neṭumoḻi yākum eṉṟu vañcit tiṇittuṟiyākat tolkāppiyap puṟattiṇiyiyalil (81) kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itaṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar piṉvarumāṟu uri kūṟiyuḷḷār.
mārāyam peṟṟa neṭumoḻiyālum - vēntaṉāṟ ciṟappeytiya vataṉāl tāṉēyāyiṉum piṟarēyāyiṉum kūṟum mīkkūṟṟuc collum.
ciṟappāvaṉa:- ēṉāti kāviti mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum nāṭum ūrum mutaliyaṉavum peṟutalumām. muṟkūṟiyatu paṭivēṇṭiya vāṟu ceyka eṉṟatu: iḵtu ap paṭikku oruvaṉit tali vaṉākki avaṉ kūṟiyavē ceyka ap paṭi eṉṟu variyaṟi ceytatu.
pōrkkaṭa lāṟṟum puravittērp palpaṭikkuk
kārkkaṭal peṟṟa kariyaṉṟō - pōrkkelān
tāṉāti yākiya tārvēntaṉ mōtirañcēr
ēṉātip paṭṭat tivaṉ
itu piṟar kūṟiya neṭumoḻi.
tuṭiyeṟiyum puliya
eṟikōl koḷḷum iḻiciṉ
kāla māriyiṉ ampu tippiṉum
vayaṟ keṇṭiyiṉ vēl piṟaḻiṉum
polampuṉi yōṭi aṇṇal yāṉi
ilaṅkuvāṉ maruppiṉ nutimaṭut tūṉṟiṉum
ōṭal cellāp pīṭuṭi yāḷar
neṭunīrp poyki piṟaḻiya  vāḷi
nelluṭi neṭunakark kūṭṭumutaṟ puraḷum
taṇṇaṭi peṟutal yāvatu paṭiṉē
mācil makaḷir maṉṟal naṉṟum
uyarnili yulakattu nukarpa ataṉāl
vampa vēntaṉ tāṉi
impar niṉṟuṅ kāṇṭirō varavē  (puṟam.287)
itu taṇṇaṭi peṟukiṉṟatu.
taṇṇaṭi - nāṭu, marutanilattūr.
vēntuviṭu toḻiliṉ paṭiyuṅ kaṇṇiyum
vāyntaṉar eṉpa avarpeṟum poruḷē  (marapiyal.83)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟuvataṟkēṟpa, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli mārāya vañci eṉṉun tuṟikkuk kūṟum,
nērāram pūṇṭa neṭuttaki nērkaḻalāṉ
cērār muṉinōkkik kaṇcivappap - pōrār
naṟavēy kamaḻteriyal naṇṇā reṟinta
maṟavē lilimukanta mārpu
eṉṉum veṇpā, pōrmaṟavar muttāraṅkaḷi mārāyamākap peṟum ceytiyik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
iṉi pakiyaracaṉ taliyiyō avaṉuṭiya paṭittalivaṉ taliyiyō koṇṭuvantu koṭutta maṟavaṉukku vēntaṉ ceyyum ciṟappu talimārāyam eṉṉum kāñcittiṇit tuṟiyākap puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pakivaṉiṉ taliyik koṇṭu vantu koṭuppataṟkuc ceyta mārāyam talimārāyam.
ataṟkuriya veṇpā.
uvaṉtali yeṉṉum uṟaḻviṉṟi yoṉṉār
ivaṉtaliyeṉ ṟētta viyalum - avaṉtali
tantāṟku nalkal viyappō kiḷantētti
vantārk kuvantīyum vāḻvu.
eṉpatu itu taruvāṉatu peruvāḻviṟkēṟṟa peruñ cel vattik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
iṉi, nāṭum ūrum āramum celvamum mutaliya uṭamikaḷik koṭuppatuṭaṉ, mārāyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭattiyum arumpaṇi yāṟṟiya amiccar paṭit talivar karumattalivar mutali yōrkku vēntaṉ mārāyamākak koṭuppatu vaḻakkam.
pañcavaṉ mārāyaṉ ....... koṅkāḷvāṉ eṉṟu kalveṭṭil varutal kāṇka. arivarttaṉa pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ amiccarākiya māṇikkavācakar pirāmaṇarā yiruntatiṉāl, teṉṉavaṉ piramarāyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭatti mārāyamākap peṟṟār. murañciyūrilirunta muṭinākar marapic cērnta pulavar oruvar muṭinākarāyar eṉṉum paṭṭam peṟṟār pōlum!
paṇṭit tamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta aracar, kuṟunila maṉṉar eṉṉum ciṟṟaracarum perunila maṉṉar eṉṉum pēraracarumāka iru tiṟattiṉar. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar mūvarum pēraracar: piṟaṟellām ciṟṟaracar. piṉṉavar vēḷir eṉavum peyar peṟuvar.
aracaṉ eṉṉum col aricaṉ, ariyaṉ eṉṟum muṟiyē tirintu vaḻaṅkum. ariyaṉ eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟikkap peṟumpōtu, pēraracaṉ māvariyaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. māvariyaṉ eṉpatu mārāyaṉ eṉa maruvum. mārāyaṉ ceyyum ciṟappu mārāyam eṉappaṭṭatu.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, ēṉāti kāviti mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum nāṭum ūrum mutaliyaṉavum peṟutalumām eṉṟurittu mārāyam eṉpatu eṉṉateṉṟu viḷakkiṉārē yoḻiya, mārāyam eṉṉuñ colliṟkup poruḷ kūṟiṉā rallar. avar oru moḻi nūlatikāriyumallar: avar kālattil moḻiyārāycciyum illi. ākavē, mārāyam eṉpatu, mārāyaṉāl ceyyappaṭum ciṟappeṉṟē poruḷpaṭuvatākum. ac ciṟappu celvakkoṭi paṭṭamaḷippu eṉa iru vakippaṭum. potuvāka paṭit talivarkku ēṉātippaṭṭamum, amiccarukkuk kāvitip paṭṭamum, vaṇikarkku eṭṭip paṭṭamum vaḻaṅkap peṟum.
ēṉāti nallutaṭaṉ, ēṉāti tirukkiḷḷi, ēṉāti nāyaṉār (civaṉaṭiyār aṟupattumūvaruḷ oruvar) mutaliya pala ēṉātip paṭṭattiṉar peyarkaḷ ilakkiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kāviti eṉṉum colliṟkut tivākarattil mantiri yeṉṉum poruḷum. cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭil kaṇakkar eṉṉum poruḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. eṭṭi kāvitiyarkkuk koṭukkappaṭum poṟpū, eṭṭippū, kāvitippū eṉṟum, nāṭu allatu ūr eṭṭip puravu kāvitippuravu eṉṟum, muṟiyē peyar peṟum vaṇika mātar ivviru paṭṭaṅkaḷiyum peṟṟatākak kūṟum eṭṭi kāvitip paṭṭan tāṅkiya mayiliyaṉ mātar eṉṉum peruṅ katikkūṟṟu ettakiya teṉṟu tiṭṭamāyt teriyavilli. orukāl kaṇavar paṭṭaṅkaḷi avarkaḷum tāṅki vantaṉar pōlum! ēṉātikkuk koṭukkappaṭum aṭiyāḷam mōtiramākum. atu ēṉāti mōtiram eṉappaṭum.
vēntaṉāṟ peṟum peruñciṟappu makiḻccikkum pārāṭṭiṟkum uriya naṟceyti yātalāl, mārāyam eṉṉum colliṟku makiḻcci, pārāṭṭu naṟceyti mutaliya poruḷkaḷun tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa.
māli
māli - mālā
mul - muḷ - muḷku. muḷkutal = muyaṅkutal.
iḷamuli mukiḻceya muḷkiya  (kalit. 125).
muḷ - (muy) - muya - muyaṅku - muyakku - muyakkam.
muyaṅku - mayaṅku - mayakku - mayakkam.

mayalilaṅkut tuyar  (tēvā. 121:2).
ma. mayyal, te. mayala, ka. mayamu.

mayaṅku - macaṅku. mayal - mayaṉṟi - mayaṇṭi - macaṇṭi.
toṭip poruḷil kalattaṟ karuttum, antivēḷip poru ḷil kalattaṟ karuttoṭu kalakkak karuttum, amintuḷḷaṉa eṉa aṟika.
kaṇ teriyātu kalaṅkuvatum irāttaṅka iṭamiṉṟik kalaṅkuvatum kātalar oruvariyoruvar viṭṭup pirintu kalaṅkuvatum, mālikkāla mayakka vakikaḷām, itaṉiyē maruṇmāli eṉṉum ilakkiya vaḻakku uṇarttum.

2. māṭam pōṉṟa kōyil.
uttara kōcamaṅki ........... māḷiki pāṭi  (tiruvāca.16:3).
vaṭavar mālikā eṉṉum colli mālā eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpupaṭutti, mēṉmāṭiyuḷḷa vīṭu eṉṟu poruḷurippar. malaraṭukkup pōṉṟa māṭiyaṭukku eṉpatu avar karuttup pōlum! “ a white - washed upeer - storied house” eṉṉum poruḷi mā.vi. a. eṅṅaṉ peṟṟatō, aṟikilam.  (va.va:234).
māṉam
māṉam - māṉa = perumi (Greatness).
mal - malli = perumi. mallic celva vaṭamoḻi maṟi vāṇar  (tiv.tiruvāy. 8:9:8).
mal - maṉ. maṉṉutal = mikutal. vitavātaṉa maṉṉē.  (naṉ.165).
maṉ = mikutaṟ kuṟippu. ciṟiyōṉ peṟiṉatu ciṟantataṉṟu maṉṉē  (puṟam. 75).
maṉ = 1. perumi. 2. periyōṉ, talivaṉ.
maṉṉuyir nīttavēliṉ  (pu.ve. 4:23,koḷu).
3. kaṇavaṉ. 4. aracaṉ. maṉ - maṉṉaṉ.
maṉ - māṉ - māṉam = perumi.
pukaḻu māṉamu meṭuttuvaṟ puṟuttalum  (tol.po.41).
māṉam - māṉa = matippu (Honour)
mā + aṉam = māṉam = aḷavīṭu, aḷavu, aḷavi, matippu.
mā. vi. a.vum ceṉṉip pa. ka. ka. ta. a . vum ivviru colli yum oru collākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.  (va.va.234 - 235).
māṉiyam
māṉiyam = māṉya
māṉam - māṉi. māṉittal = aḷattal, matittal, perumip paṭuttutal, taṉṉi mika matittuc cerukkutal.
o.nō: tīrmāṉam = muṭivu. tīrmāṉittal = muṭivu ceytal, muṭipu koḷḷutal.
māṉam - māṉi = aḷakkuṅ karuvi, taṉmāṉamuḷḷavaṉ, cerukkaṉ.
māṉi - māṉiyam = pulavariyum maṟavariyum matittuc ceyyum ciṟappu, avarkkaḷikkum muṟṟūṭṭu allatu iṟiyili (carva māṉiyam), kōyilkaṭkum aṟaccālikaṭkum viṭum aṟappuṟam (iṟiyilinilam).
māṉiyam - māṉipam. ivvaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyililli.
nilamāṉiyam (Endowment of land) eṉṉum poruḷum mā.vi.a.viṟ kuṟikkap peṟavilli.  (va.va:235).
miti
miti - mrut (d.).
mitittal = pātatti mēlvittal, pātattāl tēyttal.  (va.va.235)
mīṉ
mīṉ : miṉ - mīṉ = miṉṉum veṇṇiṟamīṉ, miṉṉum veḷḷi allatu vāṉcuṭar.
veḷḷāṭṭiṉ peyar ataṉ iṉamāṉa kārāṭṭiṟkum cev vāṭṭiṟkum potuppeyarāka vaḻaṅkiṉāṟpōla, miṉṟum keṇṭi mīṉ peyar ataṉ iṉamāṉa piṟavakikaṭkum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟa teṉka.
mīṉ - mīṉam (perumīṉ) - mīṉa (va.).  (ti.ma. : 752).
mīṉam
mīṉam - mīṉa
mīṉ - mīṉam (tivā.). am perumip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu, mīṉ eṉṉum vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyililli. vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlam mī1 = kuṟi, ciṟu, aḻi; vaḻitappu, vaḻivilaku; mīṟu, kalakkaṭi, māṟṟu, tirivatu (cañcarippatu) eṉṟu mūlap poruḷ kūṟuvar. ciṟuttaṟ poruḷil mī eṉpatu miṉ (min) eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉ tiripākat terikiṉṟatu.  (va.va. 235-236).
mukkuḻiyāṭṭam viḷiyāṭṭu
iruvarum avarkku mēṟpaṭṭavarum, periya elumiccaṅ kāyaḷa vāṉa iruppukkuṇṭu āḷukkoṉṟu vittuk koṇṭu, pappattuk kacam iṭippaṭṭa mukkuḻikaḷil eṟintāṭuvatu, mukkuḻiyāṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
itu perumpāṉmi pakalilum ciṟupāṉmi nila viravilum āṭappeṟum. tirucci vaṭṭārattār itic ciṟappāka āṭuvar.  (ta.nā.vi.)
muk kuṟṟam
ātaṉiṉ (āṉmāviṉ) allatu māntaṉiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷi yellām mūṉṟāka aṭakki, avaṟṟi āci, ciṉam, aṟiyāmi eṉa muṟippaṭuttik kūṟiṉar nam muṉṉōr. avaṟṟiyē,
kāmam vekuḷi mayakkam ivi mūṉṟaṉ
nāmaṅ keṭakkeṭu nōy  (kuṟaḷ. 360)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. kāmam. āci; vekuḷi, ciṉam; mayakkam, aṟiyāmi, vekuḷi, ciṉam eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ collā yiṉum, vēkum neruppip pōla vemmi mikka ciṉamē vekuḷi eṉa aṟika.
kuṇameṉṉuṅ kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟār vekuḷi
kaṇamēyuṅ kātta laritu  (kuṟaḷ. 29)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷi nōkkuka.
potuvāka, aṟiyāmiyē tuṉpattiṟkellām mūlamākac collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. oru poruḷit tavaṟākap payaṉpaṭuttu vatālum, oru koṭiya uyiriyiṭam allatu poṟiyiṭam allatu iyaṟkiyiṭam akappaṭṭuk koḷvatālum, oru tīya poruḷi nallateṉṟu nukarvatālum, tuṉpuṟuvatu meyyē. āyiṉ, avaṟṟi aṇṭāmalum nukarāmalum irunta viṭattiṟ pātukāppāka viruppiṉ, perumpālum tuṉpattiṟkut tappik koḷḷalām. oru poruḷit tēṭic ceṉṟu atu kiṭiyāk kāl tuṉpuṟuvataṟku, atu paṟṟiya aṟivum ōraḷavu karaṇiyamām. ākavē, uṇmiyil tuṉpattiṟku mūlak karaṇiyamāyiruppatu āciyēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷi viḷi vikkum nalviṉi tīviṉikaḷic ceyvippatum, oruvaṉuṭiya āciyē yaṉṟi aṟiyāmiyaṉṟu. ataṉālēyē,
avāveṉpa vellā vuyirkkumeñ ñāṉṟum
tavāap piṟappīṉum vittu  (kuṟaḷ. 361)
avāvillārk killākun tuṉpa maḵtuṇṭēl
tavāatu mēṉmēl varum  (kuṟaḷ. 368)
iṉpa miṭiyaṟā tīṇṭu mavāveṉṉun 
tuṉpattuḷ tuṉpaṅ keṭiṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 369)
ārā viyaṟki yavānīppi ṉanniliyē
pērā viyaṟki tarum.  (kuṟaḷ. 370)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. avāvaṟuttal eṉṉum atikārattit tuṟavaṟaviyaliṉ iṟutiyil vittiruppatum kavaṉikkattakkatām.
kāmam allatu āci tuṉpattiṟku mūlak karaṇiyamāyiruppata ṉālēyē atu muṉvikkappaṭṭatu. virumpiyatoṉṟu kiṭi yāmiyāl allatu veṟuppāṉa toṉṟi iṉṉoruvaṉ ceytataṉāl, ciṉam allatu vekuḷi piṟappatu iyalpē. āyiṉ, kāmattiṟkum vekuḷikkum iṭi nilam (middle ground) iruppata ṉāl. vekuḷi eṉpatu kāmam eṉpataṉ etirccollē (contrary term) yaṉṟi maṟutalic col (contradictory term) aṉṟu virumpāta poruḷmē lellām oruvaṉukku veṟuppuṇṭākātu. am maṉappāṉmi notumal nili yottatē. kāmattoṭu etirnilit toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatāl, vekuḷi ataṉpiṉ vikkappaṭṭatu.
mayakkam eṉṟatu aṟiyāmiyē aṟivu muṟṟaṟivum ciṟṟaṟivum eṉa iruvakippaṭṭiruppatu pōṉṟē. aṟiyāmiyum muṟṟaṟiyāmiyum ciṟṟaṟiyāmiyum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. oṉṟippaṟṟi oṉṟum teriyāmi muṟṟaṟiyāmi. oṉṟi iṉṉoṉṟākap piṟaḻa vuṇartal ciṟṟaṟiyāmi oṉṟi atuvō ituvō eṉa mayaṅkal; aṟiviṟkum aṟiyāmikkum iṭippaṭṭa aiyanili. ciṟṟaṟiyāmi tiripu eṉṟum, muṟṟaṟivu teḷivu eṉṟum, collappeṟum. kāma vekuḷi mayakkam eṉṉum mūṉṟiyum vaṭavar aintāka virippar.
kuṟṟaṅka ḷintāvaṉa : avicci, akaṅkāram, avā, viḻivu, veṟuppeṉpaṉa; ivaṟṟi vaṭanūlār pañcak kilēcameṉpar eṉṟu 38 ām kuṟaḷuriyilum.
anātiyāya avicciyum, atupaṟṟi yāṉeṉa matikkum akaṅkāramum, atupaṟṟi eṉakkitu vēṇṭumeṉṉum avāvum, atu paṟṟi ap poruṭkaṭ cellumāciyum, atupaṟṟi ataṉ maṟutalikkaṭ celluṅ kōpamumeṉa vaṭanūlār kuṟṟa minteṉṟār. ivar avaṟṟuḷ akaṅkāram aviccik kaṇṇum avāvutal ācikkaṇṇu maṭaṅkutalāṉ, mūṉṟeṉṟār. eṉṟu 360 ām kuṟaḷuriyilum, parimēlaḻakar kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
akaṅkāram eṉṉum col, vaṭamoḻiyil viṉi mutaṉili yiṉṟi aham-kāra eṉa niṉṟu, nāṉ eṉṉum akappaṟṟi yuṇarttu meṉṟum, tamiḻil akaṅkari eṉṉum mutaṉiliyaṭip piṟantu akaṅkarippu allatu cerukku eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum toḻiṟpeyar allatu toḻiṟ paṇpup peyarākumeṉṟum. vēṟupāṭaṟika. akam, maṉam, karittal, kaṭuttal allatu mikutal. akam + kari = akaṅkari - akaṅkarippu. akaṅkāram vaṭa moḻi yil. aham - kāra eṉpatu eṉatu eṉṉum puṟappaṟṟi yuṇarttum mamakāra eṉpataṉ maṟutalic collām. tamiḻil attakiya niliyiṉmiyi nōkkuka. itaṉāl irucollum vevvē ṟeṉavum, vaṭivoppumiyiṉālum ārāycciyiṉmi yālum tamiḻarāl aṟiyātum, vaṭavarāl aṟintum, oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu mayakkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum aṟika.
iṉi, āriyac cārpāṉa civaṉiyak koṇmuṭipil (civa cittān tattil), āṇavam, māyi, kāmiyam eṉak kūṟiyirukkum mum malap peyarum, kāma vekuḷi mayakkam eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tirippē.
āṇavam eṉpatu āṇ eṉṉum aṭippiṟantu akaṅkāratti allatu vekuḷiyik kuṟippatu; aṇu eṉṉum aṭippiṟantu aṟiyāmiyik kuṟippataṉṟu. mayakkam eṉpatē aṟiyāmiyi (uyiroṭu niliyāṉa toṭarpaṟṟa uṭampi nāṉ eṉa uṇarum tiripuṇarcciyi) uṇarttum. aimpūtaṅ kaṭkum mūlamāṉa māyi vēṟu; piṟaḻvuṇarcciyik kuṟikkum mayakkam vēṟu, māy + ai = māyi (māynta allatu maṟinta nili), o. nō: cāy + ai = cāyi = cāyā (va.). māyi - māyā (va.) kāmam - kāmiyam = viruppamāṉavi. kārmiya eṉpataṉ tiripākak koṇṭu iruvaki viṉiyeṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruntātu.
tiruvaḷḷuvar illaṟattālum vīṭupēṟuṇṭeṉak kūṟiyirutta lālum civaṉaṭiyār palar illaṟattil niṉṟē vīṭupeṟṟatākap periya purāṇaṅ kūṟutalālum nukarcciyiṉālum nalviṉi yālum paḻantīviṉi pōkkappaṭu mātalālum tīviṉi kalavātu iṟivaḻipāṭṭōṭu kūṭiya nalviṉit tokutiyum piṟavikkuk karaṇiyamām eṉpatu uttikkup poruntāmiyālum, iv vulakiṟ piṟantu vaḷarntu kaṟṟut tuṟantu oru viṉiyum ceyyā mal vīṭupeṟalāmeṉpatu iyalāta tātalālum, nalviṉi tīviṉikaḷ mēṟkuṟittavāṟu tūlamāc ceyyappaṭum poḻutu ākāmiyam eṉṟum piṉ cūkkumamāya nilipeṟṟu niṟkumpoḻutu cañcitam eṉṟum, piṉ iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷāy vantu payaṉpaṭumpoḻutu pirārattam eṉṟum peyar peṟum pirārattamē ūḻ eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu nalviṉi tīviṉi eṉa iruvakippaṭṭu viriyāl eṇṇiṟantaṉavāy niṟṟaliṉ caṭamāyum palavāyum uḷḷa ivi kāriyam, eṉpatum ataṉāl ivi tōṟṟamum aḻivum uṭiyaṉa eṉpatum, ataṉāl mūla kaṉmamē kāraṇa kaṉmamāyt tōṟṟak kēṭiṉṟi niṟkum eṉpatum. peṟappaṭum. eṉṟu aṟappuravaḷākam (tarumiyā tīṉam) veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa cittāntat teḷiviyal eṉṉum koṇmuṭiput teḷiviyal kūṟuvatu (pak. 160) aṟivārāycci mikka ik kālattiṟ kēṟkātu.
ākāmiyam, cañcitam, pirārattam eṉṉum mūṉṟiyum muṟiyē etirvu, iṟappu, nikaḻvu eṉat tūya tamiḻiṟ kūṟalām.
mukaṭi
kūri mukaṭṭil taṅkuvatākak karutappaṭuvatāl mūtēvi mukaṭi eṉappaṭṭāḷ. iṉi mukaṭu eṉṉum coṟkuppāḻ eṉṉum poruḷ iruppatāl pāḻāṉa nilimiyuṇṭu paṇṇupavaḷ mukaṭi eṉṟumām avaḷatu pēyt taṉmiyālum vaṟumip pañcattaṉmiyālum avaḷukkuk karumi niṟam koḷḷap paṭṭatu.  (kuṟaḷ. 617).
mukam
mukam - muka (kh)- i.vē.
mukam = muṉpakkam, taliyiṉ muṉpakkam, mukattiṉ muṉ nīṇṭuḷḷa mūkku, nuṉi, toṭakkam, muṉpu, mutaṉmi. ivi yellām muṉmik karuttip potuvākak koṇṭavi.
ipporuḷkaṭkellām aṭippaṭi tōṉṟaṟ karuttē. iyalpāṉa tōṟṟamum iyakkamum muṉṉōkkiyē nikaḻvatāl, oru poruḷ tōṉṟumpōtu ataṉ muṉpuṟamē teriyum.
mukañ ceytal = 1. tōṉṟutal.
mukañcey kāriki (peruṅ. uñcik. 35 : 49)

mukam = 1. tōṟṟam. cuḷimukak kaḷiṟaṉṉāṉ (cīvaka. 298)
2. nōkku. pukumukam purital (tol. po. 261).
muku = mukiḻ. mukiḻtal = arumputal. mukiḻttal = tōṉṟutal.
mūvakiyulaku mukiḻttaṉṉa muṟiyē  (aiṅkuṟu. kaṭavuḷ).

arumputal = tōṉṟutal. mukiḻttal = tōṟṟuvittal, īṉutal. muku eṉṉum aṭipōṉṟē, uku, nuku, puku eṉṉum aṭikaḷum tōṉṟaṟkaruttuc coṟkaḷit tōṟṟu vittuḷḷaṉa.  (va.va.)

mukam - mukar - mukari = mūkkum vāyum cērnta pakuti.

mukaṉi - mōṉi = mutaleḻuttu ottu varuki.  (va.va.)
mukam - muka. mukattal = mūkkāl nukartal. muka - mō - mōppu - mōppam. mukam - mukar. mukartal = mōttal.
vaṭamoḻiyil muka eṉṉuñ coṟku mūlamilli.
vaṭavar accoṟku mukam eṉpativiṭa vāy eṉpatiyē ciṟappup poruḷākak koḷvatāl, piṉvarumāṟu poruntap poyttalāka moḻip poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatumuṇṭu.
muka = mu + ka (kha). ka = kaṉ (tōṇṭu).

itu oru nakiyāṭṭuc ceytiyāka iruppatoṭu, kaṉ (khan) eṉṉum collum teṉcollākavē yiruttal kāṇka.
