muṭṭu
muṭṭu - mit (th) - i.vē.
muṭṭutal = etirppaṭutal, poruntutal, mōtutal.  (va.va. 238)
muṇṭam
muṇṭam - muṇṭa
(muḷ) - moḷ - moṇ - maṇ. maṇṇutal = maḻittal. intak katti maṇṇātu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. moṇ - moṇṇaṉ = vaḻukkit taliyaṉ.
vaṉkaṇṇar moṇṇari  (tēvā. 705 : 4)
moṇ - moṇṇi = vaḻukki. kūrmiyiṉmi.
moṇṇiyaṉ = aṟivu maḻuṅkiyavaṉ. moṇṇi - maṇṇi = 1. kūrmaḻukkam.
maṇṇi veṇkōṭṭuc ciṟukaṇ yāṉi  (akam. 24).
2. mūṭaṉ.
immaṇiyi niṉmaṇiyoṭu kalakkum maṇṇitā ṉuḷaṉō  (mēruman. 303).
(moḷ) - moḻu - moḻaccu. moḻaccutal = maḻittal.
moḻu - maḻu - maḻi. maḻittal = moṭṭiyaṭittal, tali vaṟaṇṭutal.  (va.va.)
maḻittalum nīṭṭalum vēṇṭāvām  (kuṟaḷ. 280)
maḻittali = moṭṭittali. koymmaḻit taliyoṭu kimmiyuṟa (puṟam. 261).
maḻi - vaḻi. mukam vaḻittal, nākku vaḻittal eṉpaṉa ulaka vaḻakku.
vaḻi - vaḻal. vaḻalutal = mayir nīṅkuvatupōl tōlurital.
vaḻal - vaḻaṟṟu (pi.vi.). vaḻal - vaḻaṉṟu - varaṉṟu - vaṟaṇṭu.
vaḻaṉṟutal = tōlurital. varaṉṟutal = vaḻittu vārutal.
maṇivaraṉṟi vīḻumaruvi  (nālaṭi. 369)
vaṟaṇṭutal = vaḻittal. tali vaṟaṇṭutal eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku. kaḷivaṟaṇṭi, pātāḷa vaṟaṇṭi eṉṉum karuvikaḷik karutuka.
moḻu - moḻuppu - mōppu - mōppi = muṇṭi. te. mōppi, ka. mōppu.  (va.va.)
moḻu - moḻukku. moḻukkutal = vaḻitta talipōṟ camaṉākumāṟu uḻuta nilattiṟ parampaṭittal.
moḻukku - moḻukkaṉ = vēlippāṭillātu vaḻukkiyāṉa aṇikalam.
moḻukkaṉ - moḻuṅkaṉ - maḻuṅkaṉ = vēlippāṭillāta aṇi.
moḻu - maḻu. maḻumaṭṭi, maḻukūḻi (vāl muṟṟumaṟṟatu) eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka.
maḻu - maḻuku. maḻukutal = moṭṭiyātal, maḻuṅkutal.
nutimuka maḻukiya ..... veṇkōṭṭu  (akam. 24)
maḻuku - maḻukku ..... maḻukkam = moṭṭi, kūriṉmi.
maḻuku - maḻuṅku. maḻuṅkutal = 1. kūrnīṅki moṭṭiyātal.
nutimaḻuṅkiya  (puṟam. 4).
1. matikkūrmi kuṟital
utikkiṉṟa puttiyum maḻuṅkiṭum  (tiruvēṅ. cata. 15)
moḻu - mōḻal = moṭṭi mūñciyuḷḷa paṉṟi (piṅ.).
moḻu - mōḻi = 1. moṭṭi. 2. kompilā māṭu.
ēḻiyik kaṇṭāl mōḻiyum pāyum  (paḻamoḻi)
3. marattiṉ aṭimuṇṭam. 4. maṭami.
5. noy cērāta kañci.
moṭṭik kūḻ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
mōḻimukam = paṉṟi.
muḷ - muṇ - muṇṭu - muṇṭam = 1. maḻitta tali.
caṭiyumuṇṭamuñ cikiyum  (aric. pu. vivāka. 2).
2. vaḻukkittali.
3. ammaṇam.
muṇṭakkaṭṭi = mayirillāt talipōl āṭiyillā vuṭampu.
4. taliyillā vuṭampu.
muṇṭam - muṇṭaṉ = 1. maḻitta taliyaṉ.
kaṇṭarāy muṇṭarāki  (tēvā. 269: 3).
2. amaṇaṉ. 3. mañcikaṉ (nāvitaṉ).
muṇṭaṉ - muṇṭi (pe. pā.) = talimaḻitta kimpeṇ, kimpeṇ.
muṇṭi - muṇṭicci (iraṭṭip pe.pā.)
muṇṭu - muṇṭi. muṇṭittal = taliyi moṭṭiyaṭittal.
talimuṇṭikku moṭṭari  (tēvā. 423 : 4)
muṇṭi = maḻitta taliyaṉ, mañcikaṉ.
muṇṭi + aṉam = muṇṭaṉam (talimaḻikki).
moḷ - moṭṭu = moṭṭi, kūriṉmi. moṭṭampu (piṅ.).
moṭṭu - moṭṭam. moṭṭantali = moṭṭittali.
moṭṭan taliyiṟ paṭṭaṅkaṭṭiyāḷa vantāṉō?
moṭṭu - moṭṭi. ma. moṭṭu, te. mottu (dd).
moṭṭit taṉmiyik kuṟikkum muṇṭam eṉṉum collum, uruṭciyik kuṟikkum muṇṭam eṉṉum collum vevvēṟeṉa aṟika.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum muṇṭ (mu|n|d) eṉṉum mūlam ceyaṟki yeṉpatu teḷivu.
“mun|d| (prob. artificial, to serve as the supposed source of the words below), cl, I. P. to cut (khan|d|ane = chidi), Dha#tup. ix, 40; to crush, grind, ix, 38 (v.l. for mut|); cl. I. A#. to, cleanse’ or to sink’ or ‘to shave”  eṉṟu mā. vi. a. kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va. 239-241).
muttam
muttam - muktā
muḷ - muṭṭu - muttu = 1. uruṇṭi vaṭivāyirukkum ciṟu poruḷ.
2. toṇ (nava) maṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
mulli mukimuṟuvaṉ mutteṉṟu  (nālaṭi. 45)
3. āmaṇakku viti.
muttirukkuṅ kompacikkum  (kāḷamēkam)
4. amminōyk koppuḷam
5. kaṇṇīrt tuḷi.
parumuttuṟiyum  (cīvaka. 1518)
āmaṇakku muttu (koṭṭi muttu), kurukkumuttu, puḷiya muttu, vēppamuttu eṉpaṉa ōraḷavu uruṇṭi vaṭivāṉa vitikaḷ.  (va.va.)
muttuccampā, muttuccōḷam, muttumātuḷi eṉpaṉavumattakiyaṉavē.
muttukkuḷippu maṉṉārkuṭāk kaṭalil toṉṟutoṭṭu nikaḻntu varukiṉṟatu. muttūrkkūṟṟam eṉpatu pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ kīḻkarip pakuti. urōma nāṭṭiṟku ēṟṟumatiyāṉa pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muttu varalāṟṟup pukaḻ peṟṟatu.
muttu - muttam. am perumip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu. 
vaṭavar, muktā eṉṉum colli muc (c) eṉṉum viṉi mutaṉiliyoṭu toṭarpupaṭutti, cippiyiṉiṉṟu viṭutali peṟṟatu eṉṟu poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatu ettuṇi vēṭikkiyāṉatu! muc = viṭu, viṭutali cey.  (va.va. 241-242)
muttiri
muttiri - mutrā (d)
mukam - mukar = aracaṉatu muka allatu taliyuruvam poṟitta muttiri. per, muhar.
muka + tiram = mōtiram, muttiriyiṭṭa viralaṇi.
muttiri mōtiram eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
mukattiram - (muttiram) - muttiri. o.nō : māttiram - māttiri.
vaṭavar mutra (makiḻcciyāṉa) eṉṉuñ colli mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar.  (va.va.242)
muttumāri
ammik koppuḷam muttup pōṉṟu iruttalāl ati mutteṉṟē aḻippar. ataṉāl, mārikku muttumāriyammaṉ eṉṟorupeyar vaḻakkum uṇṭu.  (col. 9)
mutalivaki
mutali - (Gavia) teṉṉāciyāvil perumpāṉmiyāka uḷḷatu.
iṭaṅkar - (Crocodile) vaṭa āppirikkāvil perumpāṉmiyāka uḷḷatu.
karām - (Allegator) teṉ amerikkāvil perumpāṉmiyāka uḷḷatu. (col. 40)
mutaṟṟāy moḻiyiṉ iyalpukaḷ
ulakamoḻikaḷ pallāyirakkaṇakkākap paravikkiṭappiṉum. avaṟṟuk kellām ōraḷavil mūlamāka allatu mutalāka oru perun tāymoḻi yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu. moḻi nūlāṟ pōnta muṭipām.
makkaḷellām oru tāy vayiṟṟiṉar eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭā viṭiṉum. māntaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭam oṉṟē eṉpatu māntaṉū lāl teriya varutalāl, ulakamoḻikaṭkellām ātiyil ētēṉumoru toṭarpiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
moḻikaḷ yāvum ciṟṟaḷavākavum pēraḷavākavum tirintu koṇṭē yiruppatāl, avaṟṟukkuḷḷa toṭarpu mutaṟkālat tiliruntatupōl ikkālattil teḷivāyilli. āyiṉum, āḻntu ārāyuṅkāl, ap paḻantoṭarpik kaṇṭaṟitaṟkuc cila cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
itupōtuḷḷa moḻikaḷuḷ, mutaṟṟāymoḻiyākak karutappaṭak kūṭiyavi. vaṭamoḻi (camaṟkirutam), teṉmoḻi (tamiḻ) eṉṉum iraṇṭē ivaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻi yoṉṟē mēṉāṭ ṭārāl virivākavum viḷakkamākavum aṟiyap paṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻit tamiḻarum cevviyāyaṟintārallar. ataṉāl, vaṭamoḻi yoṉṟiyē, mutaṟṟāymoḻiyāyiruntirukkalām eṉa mēṉāṭṭār karuti varuvatil, yātoru kuṟṟamum tappeṇṇamu milli.
oru pōlikkoḷkiyi allatu poykkoḷkiyi ettuṇip pēr koṇṭiruppiṉum, atu nīṭittu nillātu, uṇmi eṉṟikkēṉum veḷip paṭuvatu tiṇṇam.
mutaṟṟāymoḻikku, ataṟkuriya cila ciṟappiyalpukaḷum nilimi kaḷum uṇṭu. avi, vaṭamoḻi, teṉmoḻi eṉṉum iraṇṭuḷ etaṟkuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu pārppōmāyiṉ, mutaṟṟāy moḻiyik kaṇṭuviṭalām.
mutaṟṟāy moḻiyiṉ iyalpukaḷ
1.	mikap paḻimiyāṉatā yiruttal
2.	perumpālum ellā moḻikkum potuvāṉa eḷiya olikaḷiyē koṇṭiruttal.
3.	kūṭṭuvari(camyuktākṣaram), meymmutal, ka ca ta pa meykaḷiṉ piṉ vēṟṟu mey varavu(vēṟṟunili meymmayakkam), valliṉa meyyīṟu mutaliyaṉa villātiruttal.
4.	muṉṉoṭṭukaḷi (upacarkkaṅkaḷi) mikutiyākak koḷḷāmi.
5.	iṭukuṟippeya rillātiruttal.
6.	ēṟattāḻa ellāc coṟkaṭkum vērpporuḷ uṇarttal.
7.	pala moḻikaṭkum aṭippaṭiccol vaḻaṅki yiruttal.
8.	paṉmoḻip potuccoṟkaḷiṉ mūla vaṭivattik koṇṭiruttal.
9.	colvaḷamuṭimi.
10.	neṭuṅkālam kaḻiyiṉum mikac ciṟitē tirital.
11.	iyaṟkiyāṉa muṟiyil vaḷarntiruttal.
12.	iyaṉmoḻiyāyaṉṟit tirimoḻiyā yillātiruttal.
13.	poruḷpaṟṟiyaṉṟi īṟupaṟṟip pāluṇarttāmi.
14.	poruṭpākupāṭu ceyvatil eḷiya muṟiyit taḻuviyiruttal.
15.	mutaṟkāla makkaḷiṉ eḷiya karuttukaḷik koṇṭiruttal.
		mutaṟṟāymoḻiyiṉ nilimikaḷ



1. 	māntaṉ tōṉṟiyirukkakkūṭiya iṭattil tōṉṟiyiruttal.
2.	ulakap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭṭiruttal.
iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa iyalpukaḷum nilimikaḷum irumoḻi kaḷuḷḷum etaṟkuriyaṉaveṉṟu viṉaviṉ, tamiḻukkēyuriyaṉa veṉṟu pakivarum viṭiyiṟuppar, pakuttaṟivum naṭuniliyum uṭiyarāyiṉ,
............................................................ nērniṉṟu
kākki veḷiteṉpār eṉcolār tāykkoli
cālpuṭit teṉpāru muṇṭu
ātalāl, vēṇṭumeṉṟu colpavar etiyum colvar.
ōṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu
taṉnēr ilāta tamiḻ.
eṉṟu paḻaṅkālattilum tamiḻiṉ tāymiyippaṟṟit tamiḻaṟi ñarkku nalluṇarcciyiruntatu. kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷākat toṭarntuvarum kulappiriviṉiyiṉāl, tamiḻarkkuṇṭāṉa tāḻvuṇarcciyiṉālēyē, tamiḻiṉ talimiyi oppuk koḷḷum tuṇivilli. it tuṇiviṉmiyē tamiḻiṉ vaḷarccikkup peritun taṭiyāyuḷḷatu. āyiṉum tamiḻar tam pakuttaṟivip payaṉpaṭutti iṉimēlāyiṉum uṇmiyaṟiyavum tāymoḻiyip pēṇavum talippaṭuvārāka,
ettuṇi vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻiṟ kalantiruppiṉum, avi eṭṭuṇiyum tamiḻukku vēṇṭiyavalla. vēṇṭumāyiṉ, tamiḻ taṉṉiṭattuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷi muṟṟum vilakkuvatu maṭṭu maṉṟu; vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟiyē taḻittōṅkutalum kūṭum eṉṟu kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār kūṟiyatu muṟṟum uṇmiyē.
tamiḻccoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉ, avaṟṟit tamiḻc coṟkaḷeṉṟu koḷvatallatu vaṭacoṟka ḷeṉṟu mayaṅkuvatu, pakuttaṟiviṟkup poruntātu.
kali eṉṉum tamiḻccol, vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkuva tiṉāl maṭṭum vaṭacollākiviṭātu. kali eṉpatu, kal eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyāyp piṟanta toḻiṟpeyar; nil-nili, vil-vili, kol-koli eṉpaṉapōla.
iṉi, kaṟṟēṉ kaṟkiṟēṉ kaṟpēṉ; kaṟṟēm kaṟkiṉṟēm kaṟpēm kaṟṟāy kaṟkiṉṟāy kaṟpāy; kaṟṟīr kaṟkiṉṟīr kaṟpīr; kaṟṟāṉ kaṟkiṉṟāṉ kaṟpāṉ; kaṟṟāḷ kaṟkiṉṟāḷ kaṟpāḷ; kaṟṟār kaṟkiṉṟār kaṟpār; kaṟṟatu kaṟkiṉṟatu kaṟkum; kaṟṟaṉa kaṟkiṉṟaṉa kaṟkum eṉa irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭaṅkaḷilum puṭi peyarn tatu kal eṉṉum viṉi.
vaṭamoḻiyilō pakutiyumiṉṟip puṭipeyarcciyumiṉṟik kalā eṉṉum col taṉittē niṟkum. mēlum, kalā eṉṉum col lukku vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum pakutipporuḷ, pirivu allatu pakuti eṉpatē matiyiṉ pirivik kuṟikkum kali eṉṉum col lukku ip poruḷ poruntumēyaṉṟik kalviyiṉ pirivik kuṟikkum kali eṉṉum collukkup poruntātu. porun tuvatākat tōṉṟiṉum uṇmiyākātu. poruttam vēṟu; uṇmi vēṟu.
kalleṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu, kaṟṟal, kaṟki, kalvi, kali, kaṟpu mutaliya pala moḻippeyarkaḷ tōṉṟum. iviyellām pakutip poruḷil oṉṟāyiruppiṉum, vikutipporuḷil vēṟeṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum. poruḷ tiriyumpōtu collum uṭaṉ tiriyavēṇṭum eṉṉum collākka neṟimuṟipaṟṟi, vevvēṟu vikutikaḷ pakutiyōṭu cērntu vevvēṟu nuṇporuḷi yuṇarttum. vikuti puṇarva tellām poruḷi vēṟu paṭuttaṟkē pakutipporuḷ vikuti kūṭa ōraḷavu poruḷi vēṟupaṭuttattāṉ ceyyum. av vēṟupāṭu mika nuṇṇitāyiruppatāl, atik kavaṉippatilli oppil pōli eṉappaṭuvatilum eṅṅaṉam ciṟitu oppuṇṭō, aṅṅaṉamē pakutipporuḷ vikuti eṉpatilum ciṟitu poruḷ vēṟupāṭṭil iṭamuṇṭu.
ovvoru collukkum mutaṟporuḷ vaḻipporuḷ eṉa iruvakip poruḷuṇṭu. kāraṇac coṟkaḷilellām mutaṟporuḷ vērpporuḷiyē paṟṟiyirukkum. tamiḻiṟ kāraṇac collaṉṟi iṭukuṟic collilli. vaḻipporuḷ kāraṇampaṟṟiyu mirukkalām; āṭci paṟṟiyu mirukkalām. āṭcipporuḷ vērpporuḷit taḻuvavēṇṭuvatilli. nīrniliyil makkaḷ kūṭum iṭattiṟkut tuṟi eṉṟu peyar. tuṟutal = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal, tuṟuvatu tuṟi (tuṟu + ai). ic col nilattilum makkaḷ kūṭum iṭattiyum, akapporuḷ nāṭakattil talivaṉ talivi mutaliyōr oruvarōṭoruvar allatu oruvarōṭu piṟar kūṭum iṭattiyum, aṅṅaṉam kūṭi oruvar kūṟum kūṟṟiyum, ak kūṟṟip pāṭum oruvakic ceyyuḷiyum, ovvōr iṭamum allatu kūṟṟum allatu ceyyuḷum ovvoru vakiyil ovvoru pirivi yuṇarttu talāl oṉṟaṉ piriviyum (Department, Branch) muṟiyē kuṟikkum. ivaṟṟuḷ pirivu eṉpatu vaḻipporuḷēyaṉṟi mutaṟ poru ḷaṉṟu. tuṟi eṉṉum colliṉ vēr kūṭṭattik kuṟikkumē yaṉṟi ataṉ maṟutaliyāṉa pirivik kuṟikkātu. iṅṅaṉamē kali eṉṉum colliṉ vaḻipporuḷum pakuti eṉpatākum. ovvoru kaliyum kalviyiṉ oru tuṟiyāy allatu pakutiyāyiruppatāl, kali eṉṉum collukkup pakuti eṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu. kalvittuṟi pakuti eṉṉum iru poruḷkaḷuḷ, muntiṉatu muṉṉatēyaṉṟip piṉṉataṉṟu. kal eṉṉum viṉippakutikkuk kalipporuḷ uriyatē yaṉṟip pakuti allatu pirivupporuḷ uriyataṉṟu. ātalāl, vaṭamoḻi yiṟ kūṟappaṭum pakutipporuḷum tamiḻpporuḷi aṭip paṭiyāyk koṇṭatē.
kali eṉṉum collukkup pakuti eṉṉum poruḷ oruvakiyiṟ poruntiṟṟēṉum, mīṉam (mīṉ + am) eṉṉuñ collukkuc cañcarippatu eṉṉum poruḷ oruciṟitum poruntātu. miṉṉuvatu mīṉ eṉpatu mikat teḷivākavum iyalpākavumirukka, ati vilakkiviṭṭuc cañcarippatu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvatu. mīṉam eṉṉum teṉcolli vaṭacollākak kāṭṭa vēṇṭiyē. iṅṅaṉam poruntap puḷukalum poruntāp puḷu kalum ellām, tamiḻariṉ pētimi kāraṇamāka eḻupaviyē. ātalāl, pakuttaṟivu paṭitta tamiḻar yāvarum iṉimēlāyi ṉum pētimi viṭṭu mētimi mēṟkoḷka.  (centamiḻc celvi caṉavari 194)
mutircci vakikaḷ
paḻuttal mā vāḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kāy mutircci
muṟṟal curi pūcaṇi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kāy mutircci
neṟṟu tēṅkāy pīrkku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kāy mutircci
viḷiccal nel cōḷam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ katir mutircci  (col. 68)
muracam
muracam - muraja
muruḷ - muruṭu = 1. peruṅkuṟaṭu. 2. muṭiccuḷḷa marakkaṇu. 
3. uruṇṭa marakkaṭṭi.
vaṉparāy muruṭokkumeṉ cinti  (tiruvāca. 23 : 4)
4. paṟippotu (piṅ.).
5. peru mattaḷam. muruṭatirntaṉa (cilap. maṅkala.).
muruṭu - (murucu) - muracu. iṭiyeṉa muḻaṅku muraciṉ (puṟam. 17).
muracu - muracam = perumuracu. tiṇpiṇi muraca miṭippulat tiraṅka (puṟam. 288).
vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamilli. “fr. mura + ja?” eṉṟu viṉavukiṉṟatu mā.vi.a.  (va.va. 242).
muruku mutaṉmi
muruku mutaṉmi yeṉpatu murukaṉiṉ mutaṉmi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu. mutaṉmi eṉṉuñcol muṉmi, talimi yeṉa iruporuḷ tarum.
1. muruku eṉṉuñ col varalāṟu 
ul - mul - muṉ - muṉi = (kaṉṟu), yāṉikkaṉṟu, muṉiyuṭik kavaḷam pōla (naṟ. 360).
muṉ L. min = Small, minimus = Smallest.
iv vilattīṉ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu, mini (e.g. minibus, minicale), miniature, minify, minim, minimal, minimalize, minimus, minor, minute mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ piṟakkum
mul - muḷ - muḷi = 1. tōṉṟuvatu, tōṉṟiyatu, 2. iḷami. muḷiyami tiṅkaḷ (kamparā. kumpaka. 16) 3. marakkaṉṟu. ataṉṟāḷ vaḻiyē muḷiyōṅkupu (cīvaka. 223) ma. muḷa. tu. muḷe. ka. moḷe. te. moḷa.
muḷi - muḷiyāṉ = ciṟukuḻanti, inta muḷiyāṉ pēcci yār kēṭkiṟatu. (u.va.).
muḷ - maḷ - maḷḷaṉ = 1. iḷiñaṉ. poruviṟal maḷḷa (tirumuru. 262). 2. kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻvōṉ. (cūṭā). 3. maṟavaṉ. vīraṉ. kaḷampuku maḷḷar (kalit. 306).
maḷ - maḻa = 1. iḷami. maḻavuṅ kuḻavum iḷamip poruḷa. (tol. uri 14) 2. kuḻanti. aḻumaḻap pōlum (tirukkō. 147). 
maḻa - maḻavu. (piṅ.).
maḻavu - maḻavaṉ = 1. iḷiñaṉ. maḻavartama ṉiyaṉa maṇa voli (kamparā. nāṭṭup. 50). 2. maṟavaṉ. maḻavar peruma (puṟanā. 96).
maḻa - maḻal - maḻali = 1. iḷami. perumaḻali veḷḷēṟṟiṉar (tēvā. 579) 2. kuḻantikaḷiṉ tiruntāc col. tam makkaḷ maḻalic coṟ kēḷātavar (kuṟaḷ. 66) 3. meṉmoḻi, maḻalivāyiṉ muṟuvaṟ ṟiyalāḷ (cīvaka. 181).
maḻal - maḻaṟu. maḻaṟutal = meṉmiyātal, maḻaṟu tēṉ moḻiyārkaḷ (tiv. tiruvāy. 6:2:5).
maḻa - māḻi = 1. iḷami. māḻi maṭamāṉ piṇiyiyal veṉṟāy (kali. 131). 2. aḻaku. māḻinōkkoṉṟum māṭṭēṉ miṉē (tiv. tiruvāy. 2:4:10). 3. pētimi. māḻi meṉṉōkki (tirukkōvi. 61).
maḻa - maka = 1. iḷami. (yāḻ. aka). 2. piḷḷi man timmaka (cīvaka. 18).
3. makaṉ allatu makaḷ. maka muṟi taṭuppa (malipaṭu. 185) ka.ma.ka.
maka - makavu = 1. kuḻanti. makavumuli varuṭa (kamparā. tila. 13) 
2.makaṉ.koṇṭatōrmakaviṉāci(aric.pu.mayāṉa.20). 3. kuraṅkuk kuṭṭi. (tol. mara. 14). ka. makavu (g).
maka-makaṉ. ka. makam (g) maka - makaḷ. ma.ka. - makaḷ (g).
“mac (mak). a Gaelic word signifying son, and prefixed to many surnames, as Mac Donald, Mac Grigor & c. It is synonymous with son fem, in names of teutonic origin. It is allied with Goth. magus. a son. magattes (G. magd, a mid)”. The Imperial Dictionary of the English Language (Vol. III, p. 91).
muḷ - (mur) - muruku = 1. iḷami. (tivā). 2. aḻaku. (piṅ.). 3. murukaṉ. aruṅkaṭi vēlaṉ murukoṭu vaḷii (maturik . 611) 4. teyvam. muruku meyppaṭṭa pulitti pōla (puṟanā. 259). 5. vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭṭu murukayarntu vanta mutuvāy vēlaṉ (kuṟun. 362). 6. murukaṉ tiruviḻā. murukayar pāṇiyum (cūḷā. nāṭ. 7).
muruku - murukaṉ = 1. kaṭṭiḷamiyōṉ. (tivā). 2. murukatēvaṉ.
3. vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭṭāḷaṉ. mutiyāḷōṭu murukaṉi muṟiyiṟ kūvi (kan. pu. vaḷḷi. 155).
iḷamikkuriya paṇpu aḻakum maṟamum, mutaṟkālak kuṟiñcinilavāṇar piṟkālap pāli nilavāṇar pōṉṟē kaṭu maṟavarāyiruntaṉar. ataṉāl tam teyvam iḷamiyum maṟamum aḻakum uṭiyōṉ eṉṟu karuti, murukaṉ eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. aḻakuk karuttiṉum iḷamik karuttē muntiyatu āyiṉ, tiru.vi.ka. tam murukaṉ allatu aḻaku eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyil aḻakuk karuttiyē muntiyatāka allatu mutaṉmiyāṉatākak koṇṭār.
muru - muruntu = 1. koḻuntu (caṅ. aka.). iḷantaḷir. (aru. ni.).
2. teṉṉi paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ veṇkuruttu (cūṭā.).
muru - muṟu - muṟi - taḷir. muṟimēṉi (kuṟaḷ. 1113).
muṟi - maṟi = vilaṅkiṉkuṭṭi. pārppum - maṟiyum eṉṟu - iḷamip peyarē (tol. mara. 1).
marañceṭi koṭikaḷ aṭarntum aṭikkaṭi maḻi poḻintum iruṇṭum kuḷirntum irunta kuṟiñciyil, oḷi peṟavum kuḷir pōkkavum maṭṭumaṉṟi, kiḻaṅku cuṭavum koṭuvilaṅkit turattavum, neruppu iṉṟiyamiyātatā yiruntatiṉāl, avvap pōtu maraṅkaḷ uraci iyaṟkiyākat tōṉṟum neruppit tam teyva veḷippāṭākak karuti, kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ murukaṉukkuc cēyōṉ (civantavaṉ) eṉṟu peyariṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. taṅkaḷippōl vēṟpaṭi yuṭiyōṉ eṉṟum, kuṟiñcinilak kaṭappamāli yaṇivōṉeṉṟum karuti vēlaṉeṉṟum kaṭampaṉeṉṟum peyariṭṭaṉar. aracaṉ, vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ īṟukuṉṟi aracu, vēntu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟu, cēyōṉ, murukaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, muṟiyē, cēy, muruku eṉa ilakkiyattil vaḻaṅkum.
2. murukaṉ muṉmi (vaṇakka nili)
mutaṟkālat tiṇinilit teyva nūṟpāvil cēyōṉ mēyamivari ulakamum eṉṟu tolkāppiyar murukaṉiyē kuṟiñci nilat teyvamākak kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar.
kumarināṭṭil, mutaṟkālattil, murukaṉ kuṟiñci nilattiṟkē ciṟappāka vuriya teyvamāka, aṟuvaṭiyum tirumaṇamum pōṉṟa iṉpak kālattilum, nōyum pañcamum pōṉṟa tuṉpak kālattilum, avvap pōtu immip payaṉ nōkkiyē, maturi vīraṉum elliyammaṉum pōla vaṇaṅkappaṭṭāṉ.
piṟkālattil aintiṇi vāḻkki yēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, veṟiyāṭum vēlaṉālum, kuṟi kūṟum kuṟattiyālum muruka vaṇakkam marutanilattil pukuntatu.
3. civaṉ piṉmi (mata nili)
maruta nilattu makkaṭ perukkālum nākarika vaḷarcciyālum, uḻavu, vāṇikam, kāval, kalvi eṉṉum nālvakit toḻilum, vēḷāḷar, vaṇikar, aracar, antaṇar eṉṉum nālvakit toḻil vakuppārum tōṉṟiyapiṉ, maṟumi nōkkum mata vuṇarcciyum ēṟpaṭṭu, kuṟiñci nilaccēyōṉ vaṇakkat tiṉiṉṟu civamatamum, mulli nila māyōṉ vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu tirumāl matamum tōṉṟiṉa.
āyiṉ, civaṉ cēyōṉiṉum vēṟupaṭṭavaṉeṉṟum, iḷami nīṅkiya naṭup paruvattāṉeṉṟum, civi allatu malimakaḷ eṉṉum tēviyuṭiyaṉeṉṟum, kāḷiyūrtiyaṉeṉṟum, tilli maṉṟāṭi yeṉṟum, vīṭṭiṉpan tarupava ṉeṉṟum, maṟikaḷāṟ pōṟṟavum meyp poruḷ nūlkaḷāl āyavum paṭupavaṉeṉṟum, niliyāṉa karuttukaḷ eḻuntuviṭṭaṉa.
āriyap pūcakar murukaṉi makaṉākavum civaṉit tantiyākavum iruvariyum āriyat teyvaṅkaḷākavum kāṭṭi, pala toṉmaṅ kaḷiyum (purāṇaṅkaḷiyum) tīṭṭi viṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl at teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvat tōṟṟamum vaḻipāṭṭumuṟiyum mika māṟiviṭṭaṉa.
cēy eṉṉuñ colliṟku murukaṉ kuḻanti, eṉṟu iru poruḷiruppatum, iḷiñaṉik kuṟikkum kumaraṉ eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyil kumāra eṉṟu tirintu makaṉik kuṟippatum. toṉmikarkkut (purāṇikarkkut) tuṇiyāyiṉa.
4. cēyōṉukkum civaṉukkumuḷḷa oṟṟumi
civaṉuñ civiyum peṟṟōr pōṉṟum, murukaṉum vaḷḷiyum makaṉum marumakaḷum pōṉṟum, tōṟṟamaḷippiṉum, muruka vaḻi pāṭṭil taṉip paṟṟuḷḷavar, murukaṉuñ civaṉum oṉṟē yeṉṟu koḷḷaṟkēṟṟa oṟṟumic cāṉṟukaḷiyuṅ kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
1.	iruvarukkum neruppup pūta aṭippaṭiyil, cēyōṉ eṉṟum civaṉ allatu ceyyavaṉ (ceyyōṉ) eṉṟum peyar tōṉṟiyuḷḷami. itaṉāl civāyanama eṉpatu murukaṉukkum ēṟkum.
2.	iruvarukkum malikkōyilkaḷ ciṟappirukkiyā yirukki.
3.	iruvar ūrtikaḷum paṭikkalaṅkaḷum malaraṇikaḷum kuṟiñci nilattiṟ kuriyaṉavāyiruttal.
4.	iruvarkkum mukaṅ kaṇ kikaḷ muppatāy muṭiki.
5.	murukaṉum vīṭaḷippavaṉākat tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi kūṟal.
6.	iruvariyum nāṉmuka tirumālarkku mēlavarākak katikaḷ viḷampal.
7.	civaṉiyak koṇmuṭiyum (cittāntamum) meyp poruṭ kōviyum murukaṉukkum ēṟṟal.
8.	iruvarumakattiyaṟkut tamiḻāciriyarākac collappaṭutal.
9.	vaḷḷi kuṟiñci nilat tēviyātalāl, malimakaḷ eṉṉum peyar avaḷukkum poruntum. malimakaḷ = malittēvi.
10.	mulli nilattu māyōṉ eṉṉum vaṇakkanilit teyvam matanilit teyvamāka (peruntēvaṉāka) māṟiyapōtu, murukaṉuñ civaṉum pōṉṟē vēṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭavilli.
5. murukaṉ kavarcci
iyaṟki vaḷañ ciṟanta kuṟiñci nilattil, iḷamiyum aḻakum iṇinta murukaṉum vaḷḷiyum, aracaruṅ kāṇa virumpum aḻakiya āṇmayil mītamarntu. aṭiyārkku aḷikkum kaṇkavar kaviṉ kāṭciyum, kāvaṭi nōṉpiṉ māṭciyum, murukanēyarkku muḻumaṉak kavarcciyeṉpatic colla vēṇṭuvatilli.
6. murukaṉ mutaṉmi
iṉi, murukaṉē civaṉukku ōṅkārap poruḷi uṇarttiṉāṉ eṉṟu civaṉiyarum oppuk koḷḷuñ ceyti. makavē māntaṉiṉ tanti (The child is father to the man) eṉṉum paḻamoḻik karuttiyuṇartti murukaṉiṉ mutaṉmiyi nāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
kāntam (kāntam) uḷḷiṭṭa patiṉeṇ purāṇaṅkaḷip puṉinta viyācar kālam ki.mu. 1000 allatu 1200. tamiḻakattil muruka vaṇakkan tōṉṟiyatu ki.mu. 1,00,000.
tamiḻnilap paṟavikaḷuḷ veṟṟi kāṇumvari vīṟu maṟap pōriṭuvatu kuṟiñci nilak kōḻic cēval oṉṟē. ataṉāl, kuṟiñcinila maṟavar tam maṟatteyva murukaṉukkuk kōḻic cēvalik koṭic ciṉṉamākkiṉar. itiyuṇarātu kālañ ceṉṟa kē.ē.nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār, murukaṉ eṉṉum peyar cēvalik kuṟikkum murkā eṉṉum pāracīkac colli ṉiṉṟu tirintatākak karutiyatu. ettuṇi iḻitakavāṉatu eṉpatip pakuttaṟivuḷḷa naṭuniliyaṟiñar kaṇṭukoḷka.  (naṉṉīrāṭṭu naṟumalar, kuṉṟakkuṭi, 1973)
mul1 (iḷamik karuttuvēr)
iḷami eṉpatu ōr uyiriyiṉ piṟappiṟkum muḻu vaḷarccikkum iṭippaṭṭa nilimi allatu kālam. atu piṟanta nili yiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki, puṉiṟṟiḷami, ciṟṟiḷami, pēriḷami eṉa munnilippaṭṭatākum.
piṟappeṉiṉum tōṟṟameṉiṉum okkum.
iyaṟkiyāṉa tōṟṟam allatu iyakkamellām muṉṉōk kiyē nikaḻtalāl, tōṟṟak karuttil muṉṉōkkal allatu mukaṅkāṭṭaṟ karuttu iraṇṭaṟak kalantuḷḷatu.
iṉi, iḷamip paruvattilēyē uruvac ciṟumiyum uṭal meṉmiyum aḻaku niṟivum maṟavuṇarcciyum valimi mikutiyum potuvāka amintiruttalāl, iḷamik karuttiṟ ciṟumi meṉmi aḻaku maṟam valimi mutaliya karuttukaḷum tōṉṟum.
mul - muṉ - muṉi = yāṉik kaṉṟu. muṉiyuṭik kavaṉam pōl (naṟ. 360).
muṉ - muṉṉi = mulli nilattil iyaṟkiyāka viḷiyum oru ciṟu payaṟu.
o.nō: L. Min, small. itaṉiṉṟē. minify, minim, minimum, minish, minor, minute mutaliya pala coṟkaḷ tirintuḷḷatākat terikiṉṟatu.
mul - muḷ - muḷi = 1. vittiṉiṉṟu  muḷitta ciṟu veḷippāṭu. vittiya veṇmuḷi (aiṅkuṟu. 29). 2. iḷami. muḷiyami tiṅkaḷ (kamparā. kumpaka. 10), 3. marakkaṉṟu. ataṉṟāḷ vaḻiyē muḷiyōṅkupu (cīvaka. 223). 4. ciṟupiḷḷi. 5. makaṉ. (piṅ.).
ma. muḷa. tu. muḷe. ka. moḷe. te. molakka.
muḷittal = 1. muḷi tōṉṟutal. oṉṟāy muḷitteḻuntu (tiruvāca. 10 : 8). 2. katiravaṉ tōṉṟutal kāli ñāyiṟu katirvirittu muḷippa (maṇi. 8 : 18).
ma. muḷi. ka. moḷe. te. molatcu.
muḷi - muḷiyāṉ = ciṟukuḻanti. inta muḷiyāṉ pēcci yār kēṭkiṟatu (W).
muḷ - muḷu - mucu - mūcu = piñcu. palāmucu. (u.va.). x.neh.: uḷu - ucu. ḷa - ca. pōlittiripu.
muḷ - muṭṭu. muṭṭuk kurumpi = ciṟu teṉṉam piñcu. allatu paṉam piñcu.
muṭṭu - moṭṭu = arumpu. moṭṭarumalar (tiruvāca. 29 : 8).
moṭṭu - moṭṭi = maṇamākāta iḷiñaṉ (W.) moṭṭip piyaṉ (u.va.).
muḷu - muḻu - muku - mukiḻ. o.nō: toḻu - toku.
mukiḻttal = (ce.ku.vi.) 1. arumputal. arumaṇi mukiḻttavēpō liḷaṅkatir muliyum (cīvaka. 551). 2. tōṉṟutal. mūvaki yulakum mukiḻttaṉa muṟiyē (ainkuṟu. kaṭavuḷ). kūmpum malarpōṟ kuvital allatu mūṭutal. makavukaṇ mukiḻppa (kallā. 7).
(ce.kuṉṟāvi.). 1. īṉutal. amararātiyari mukiḻttu (vināyakapu. 8:154).
2. tōṟṟuvittal. aṟputa mukiḻttār (kāñcippu. paṉṉiru. 163).
ka. mukuḷ (g).
mukiḻ = 1. arumpu. kuṟumukiḻa vāyiṉuṅ kaḷḷimēṟ kin nīṭṭār (nālaṭi. 262). 2. kumiḻi. peyaṟuḷi mukiḻeṉa (kalit. 56).
mukiḻ - mukiḻam = malarum paruvattup pērarumpu (catu.).
ya. mukula.
mukiḻ - mukiḻi, mukiḻittal = mukiḻttal.
mukiḻi - mukiḻitam = mukiḻtam = arumpal, arumpu. poṉṉiṉ. mukiḻitam viḷittu (kuṟṟā. tala. nāṭṭuc. 9).
mukiḻ - mukiḷ. mukiḻam - mukiḷam. mukiḻitam - mukiḷitam.
mukiḷ - mukuḷ - mukuḷam = 1. arumpu. paṅkaya mukuḷan taṉṉik koṅkiyāp paṭitta (tivālavā. 4 : 14). 2. oru kiyiṉ aintu viralum nimirntu nuḷi poruntik kūmpi niṟkum iṇiyā viṇikki vaki. (cilap. 3 : 18), uri).
va. mukula.
mukuḷam - mukuṭam = maṇimuṭi. mukuṭamum peruñ cēṉiyum (pārata. kuru. 14). 2. muṭiyuṟuppu aintaṉuḷ oṉṟu. (tivā.).
mukuṭam - va. mukkuṭṭa.
mukuṭam - makuṭam = 1. maṇimuṭi. arakkaṉṟaṉ makuṭam (kamparā. mutaṟpō. 246.). 2. tērk kuppam. 3. ōliccuvaṭiyiṉ maṇi muṭiccuk koṇṭi.
makuṭam - va. makkuṭṭa.
pērā. parō tam camaṟkiruta moḻi eṉṉum nūliṉ iṟutiyil, mukuṭam (makuṭam) eṉpatu tiraviṭac colleṉṟu kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.
mukiḷ - mukuḷ - mukuḷi. muḷittal = kavital. muruḷikkum ... aravintam (taṇṭi. 62).
muku - muki = arumpu. muki mokkuḷuḷḷatu nāṟṟam pōl (kuṟaḷ. 1274).
mukital = arumputal.
muku - moku - mokku = pū moṭṭu. 2. moṭṭup pōṟ ceyyappaṭum ōviya vēlippāṭu. 3. nilattiṉmēl iṭum pūkkōlam.
ka. mokku (gg) te. mokka. (gg).
mokku - mokkuḷ = 1. malarum paruvattup pērarumpu. mukimokkuḷuḷḷatu nāṟṟam pōla (kuṟaḷ. 1274). 2. nīrkkumiḻi. paṭumaḻi mokkuḷiṉ (nālaṭi. 27). ka. mukutal. (g).
mokkuḷ - mokkuḷi. mokkuḷittal = 1. kumiḻiyuṇṭātal. (w). 2. tiraḷutal. (yāḻ. aka.).
ka. mukkuḷicu.

ka. makam (g).
o.nō: mac (mak). A. Gaelic word signifying so, and prefixed to many surnames, as Mac Donald, Mac Grigor, & c. It is synonymous with Son in tentonic origin ... and with mab or Map (shortened into Ab or Ap) in Webh names. It is allied to Goth. magus, a son fen, magaths (G. magd, a mid.).  (The Imperial Dictionary of the English Language.)

mul2 (muṉmik karuttuvēr)
tōṟṟak karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa iḷamik karuttu vērc colliṉ uṭaṉilik karuttukkaḷ, ciṟumi meṉmi aḻaku maṟam valimi eṉpaṉa.
tōṟṟamum iḷamiyum ōruyiriyiṉ vāḻkkiyil muṟpaṭṭa nili kaḷātalālum, tōṟṟameṉpatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟi ṉiṉṟu muṉ varutalāta lālum, tōṟṟak karuttiṉ vaḻinilik karuttu muṉmiyeṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum.
muṉmi yeṉpatu kālamuṉ iṭamuṉ eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. kāla muṉmi mutaṉmiyiyum mutumiyiyum kuṟikkum. mutumi mutircciyil muṟṟum.

meḻuku - meḻukku = 1. cāṇattāl meḻukuki. pularvataṉ muṉṉalakiṭṭu meḻukku miṭṭu (tēvā. 727:3). 2. cāṇam. tiruvalakum tirumeḻukkum tōṇṭiyuṅ koṇṭu (periyapu. tirunāvuk. 68). 3. mēṟ pūccup poruḷ. vēriyiṉ meḻukkārtta meṉpū nilattu (cīvaka. 129). 4. arakku. 5. piciṉ.
ma. meḻukku.
meḻukku - meḻukkam = cāṇattāl meḻukiya iṭam.malaraṇi meḻukka mēṟi (paṭṭiṉap. 248).
meḻukkuttuṇi = 1. meḻuku pūciṉa tuṇi. 2. nīrkkāpputtuṇi. 3. nilakkari neyyiṭṭa pōrvi (Tarpulin).
meḻukkūṭṭutal = mēṟpūcciṭutal.
meḻukiṭutal = 1. nūliṉmēl meḻuku pūcutal. 2. cāṇamiṭṭu nilatti allatu tiṇṇiyit tuppuravu ceytal. 3. paṭimi vārkka meḻukiṉāṟ karukkaṭṭutal.
meḻukukkaḷimpu = ciraṅkiṟkiṭum kaḷimpu maruntuvaki.
meḻuku kaṭṭutal = paṭimi vārkka meḻukiṉāṟ karukkaṭṭutal.
meḻuku kaṭṭi vārttal = meḻuku karuvil urukkiṉa māḻikaḷi (ulōkaṅkaḷi) vārttup paṭimi yamittal.
meḻuku cāṇi = 1. meḻukāl tuṭitta urikal. 2. meḻukuṅ karumaṇaluṅ kalantu ceyta cāṇik kal (W.).
meḻuku cāttutal = paṭimi vārkka meḻukiṉāṟ karukkaṭṭutal. 
ciṟparkaḷāṉ meḻuku cātti  (tiruvālavā. 45:2).
meḻuku cīli = meḻukkut tuṇi.
meḻuku cērvi = meḻukuk kaḷimpu (W.).
meḻukuttaṇṭu = meḻukut tiri (yāḻ. aka).
meḻukuttiri = meḻuku tiri.
meḻukuttuṇi = meḻukkut tuṇi.
meḻuku patam = kāyccappaṭṭa maruntellām cērntu meḻukupōl tiraṇṭuvarum patam.
meḻuku paṉiyaṉ = amminōy vaki (yāḻ. aka).
meḻuku pākal = pākalvaki (mū.a).
meḻuku pāḷam = meḻuku takaṭu.
meḻuku pīrkku = pīrkkuvaki (u.va.).
meḻuku pūccu =paṭimimēl meḻuku pūciyeṭukkum accu.
meḻuku pommi = 1. meḻukiṉāṟ ceyta viḷiyāṭṭup paṭimi. 2. meyvaruttan tāṅkamuṭiyātava - ṉ-ḷ.
ka. mēṉatupompe (b.).
meḻuku pōṭutal = marappaṇṭaṅkaṭkup paḷapaḷappēṟṟa meḻukik kāyccip pūcutal (W.).
meḻukumaṇ = karukkaṭṭum pacimaṇ (W).
meḻuku muṭṭam = meḻuku pāḷam (iṭavaḻakku).
meḻukeṇṇey = marappaṇṭaṅkaṭku merukiṭa utavum meḻuku cērtta pūcceṇṇey (i.va.).
meḻukeḻututal = tuṇiyil accaṭikka meḻukāl uruva meḻututal (i.va.).
kavaṉippu
la ḷa ḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟu iṉavolikaḷuḷ, muntiyatu lakaramē la tiraṇṭu ḷakaramum. ḷa tiraṇṭu ḻakaramum ākum.
e.ṭu. kāl-kāḷ-kāḻ (karumi).
lakaram nēraṭiyāy ḻakaramāvatu muṇṭu.
e.ṭu: māl - maḻi.
iṉi, ittakiya coṟṟirivukaḷil, iṭippaṭṭa ḷakaraccol iṟantupaṭṭa teṉiṉumām.
mul4 (poruntaṟ karuttuvēr)
poruntaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu ottal, muṭṭutal, cērtal, kūṭutal, maṇattal, kalattal, porutal mutaliya karuttukaḷum; ottaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu, aḷaviṭutal, matittal, cerukkutal mutaliya karuttukaḷum; cērtaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu tiraḷutal, paruttal, virital, mikutal, ceḻittal, aḻakātal mutaliya karuttukaḷum; paruttaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu mottamātal, valuttal mutaliya karuttukaḷum; kalattaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu kalakkam, mayakkam mutaliya karuttukaḷum; mayakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu iruṭci, karumi mutaliya karuttukaḷum vaḻinilik karuttukaḷākap piṟakkum. ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟu mēṟkoṇ ṭum cila kiḷik karuttukaḷ tōṉṟum.
mul-mulli = kuttaki (yāḻp). mullikkāraṉ = kuttakik kāraṉ (yāḻp).
oru poruḷ muḻuvatiyum kuṟitta kālattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttac ceytukoḷḷum oppantam kuttaki.
mulli - molli = 1. mēḻam (parutta cemmaṟiyāṭṭuk kaṭā). 2. mēḻavōri (cūṭā. uḷ. 9).
molliyiṟ pōṭutal = kuṭumpap potuc celaviṟkuk koṭuttal.
mol - molu. molu molu veṉal = īkkaḷ moyttaṟ kuṟippu.
mol - molōr. molōreṉal = ciṟumīṉ kūṭṭam nīrṭṭattil tuḷḷivarum olik kuṟippu.
x.neh.: ara. mulākāt = cantippu.
in. milṉā = cantikki, cantikka : mil = canti (mutaṉili).
mul - mal = 1. parumi (yāḻp). 2. vaḷam. maṟṟuṉṟu māmalariṭṭu (tirukkō. 178). 3. valimi (piṅ.). 4.maṟpōr. mallalit teḻuntu vīṅki (cīvaka. 268) 5. mallaṉ. malloṭu kañcaṉun tuñca veṉṟa maṇivaṇṇaṉ (tiv. periyati. 11:2:3). 6. kaṇṇapirāṉ malla ṉāy vāṇācuraṉi veṉṟāṭiya kūttu. (piṅ.).
pala aṇukkaḷ allatu uṟuppukaḷ allatu poruḷkaḷ poruntuvatāl (oṉṟu cērvatāl) tiraṭci allatu parumi uṇṭākum. o.nō: cērē tiraṭci (tol. uri. 65). mikutiyāl vaḷamum parumiyāl valimiyum uṇṭākum. iruvar allatu iru paṭikaḷ porunti allatu kūṭip poruvatē pōr. poru (poruntu) - pōr. kala-kalām. kalakam. kikalattal eṉṉum vaḻakkiyum nōkkuka.
mal-mallal = 1. mikuti. 2. vaḷam. mallal vaḷaṉē (tol. uri. 7). 3. celvam. mallaṟkēṇ maṉṉuka (paripā. 11:121). 4. valimi. mallaṉ maḻaviṭi yūrntāṟku (cilap. 17, koḷu. 3). 5. polivu (cūṭā). 6. aḻaku. mallaṟṟaṉ ṉiṟamoṉṟil (tirukkō. 58, pērā).
mal-mali. malital = 1. ottal. kaviki māmatik kaṭavuḷi maliya (kantapu. cūraṉaraciruk. 9). 2. etirttal (cūṭā.). 3. pakittu māṟupaṭutal. ikaṉmalin teḻunta pōtil (cīvaka. 747). 4. pōrāṭutal. tatta mataṅkaḷē yamivatāka varaṟṟi malintaṉar (tiruvāca. 4:53).
ka. male.
malittal = 1. māṟupaṭutal. maṭaṅkaliṟ cīṟi malitteḻuntār (pu.ve. 3:24). 2. porutal. vēntaṉōṭu ... malittaṉi yeṉpatu (puṟam. 36). 3. varuttutal. maḷḷar malittal pōkiya cilittār mārpa (puṟam. 10).

malku - malkā - mallā. mallāttal = 1. malarntāṟpōl mukam mēṉōkkik kiṭattal. narakattiṉ mallākkat taḷḷi (tiruman. 199). 2. tōṟṟuppōtal (u.va.).

mukam malar pōṉṟateṉpatum, mukam mēṉōkkik kiṭattal moṭṭu virinta niliyiyum mukaṅ kuppuṟak kiṭattal malarnta pūk kuvinta niliyiyum okkumeṉpatum karuttu.

maṉ - maṉṟu = 1. avi. ampalam. 2. tillip poṉṉampalam. teṉṟilli maṉṟiṉu ḷāṭiṉi pōṟṟi (tiruvāca. 4 : 92). 3. aṟaṅ kūṟavi. neṭumaṉṟil vaḷaṉuṇṭu (kamparā. mūlapala. 145). 4. āṉkaṇam. maṉṟāṭi colla (tiruvālavā. 52 : 5. aṭikkuṟippu). 5. āṉtoḻu. āṉkaṇam ... maṉṟuniri pukutara (kuṟiñcip. 218). 6. marattaṭit tiṇṇip potuviṭam. maṉṟum potiyilum (tañcivā. 34). 7. makkaḷ kūṭum nāṟcanti. maṉṟilē taṉṉuṭiya vaṭivaḻaki muṟṟūṭṭāka aṉupavippikku mavaṉi (īṭu. 3:6:3) 8. maṇam. maṉṟalar ceḻuntuḷavu (kamparā. tiruvava. 24).

kaṭali nōṉpika ḷoṭiyā vuḷḷamoṭu

14. maṇam (piṅ.).
am oru perumipporuṭ piṉṉoṭṭātalāl, maṉṟam eṉpatu ilakkaṇa neṟippaṭi perumaṉṟik kuṟikkum.
maṉṟu - maṉṟakam = ampalam. maṉṟakattē nampi māṭa meṭuttatu (tiruman. 148).
maṉṟu - maṉṟal = 1. tamiḻat tirumaṇam. 2. irupāṟ kūṭṭam. maṟiyōr tēettu maṉṟa leṭṭaṉuḷ (tol. kaḷavu. 1), 3. puṇarcci. maṉṟaṉ māmayil (cīvaka. 2171). 4. neṭunteru. maṉṟal varuñcēṉi (kuṟṟā. kuṟa. 73:2). maṇam (piṅ.).
maṉṟu-mantu = 1. āṉkaṇam. 2. āṉtoḻu. 3. eruminiri. 4. nīlamalit tuṭavar kuṭiyiruppu.
e.ṭu: oṟṟikkal mantu (Ootacamund).
tuṭavarkku eruminiri yōmpalē piḻippuvaḻiyātalāl. avar kuṭiyiruppu mantu eṉappaṭṭatu. āṭṭup paṭṭikaḷum māṭṭup paṭṭikaḷum irunta vūrkaḷ. paṭṭiyeṉṟē peyarīṟu peṟṟiruttali nōkkuka.
orē peruṅkal irunta mantu oṟṟikkal mantu eṉappaṭṭatu. teluṅku nāṭṭil oru pakuti oru kal nāṭu allatu ōrāṅkal (Warangal) nāṭu eṉap peyar peṟṟiruttalik kāṇka.
āṅkilar mutaṉmutal nīlamaliyil aṭinta iṭam oṟṟikkal mantu. ataṉāl atu ciṟappup peṟṟatu. ataṉ peyariṉ āṅkila vaṭivi yoṭṭi. utakamaṇṭalam eṉṉum peyari amit tum koṇṭaṉar vaṭamoḻi veṟiyar. ataṉ marūuvāṉa utaki eṉpataṟkut talimāṟāka, oṟṟaki eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatē tamiḻneṟik kēṟṟatām.
mantu - manti = 1. kālnaṭik kūṭṭam. āṭumāṭukaḷ manti mantiyāy mēykiṉṟaṉa (u.va.). 2. ūriṉ potu mēyccaliṭam, mantiveḷi (u.va.). 3. ūriṭip potu veḷiyiṭam. 4. āṭumāṭu mēykkum kulattāruḷ oru pirivup peyar.
te. mantu. ka. mante.
maṉ - maṉi = 1. palar kūṭi allatu nilittu vatiyum vīṭu. cīrkeḻu vaḷamaṉi tiḷittu (cīvaka. 828). 2. vīṭu kaṭṭutaṟkariya veṟunilam. oru maṉi 2400 caturaaṭi (u.va.). 3. allum pakalum vīṭṭil vatiyum maṉivi. piṟaṉmaṉi nōkkāta pērāṇmi (kuṟaḷ. 148). 4. vīṭṭil vatiyum kuṭumpam. 5. tuṟavukku etirāṉa ilvāḻkki. maṉittakka māṇpuṭiyaḷāki (kuṟaḷ. 51). 6. cūtāṭṭiṟku allatu kaṇiyattiṟku variyum aṟi.
ma. maṉa. ka. maṉe.
o.nō: E. manor, f. ME. f. AF. maner, OF. manoir, f.L. manere, remin.

mantiram - va. mantira.
makkaḷ uṟiviṭattiyum arimāk kukiyiyum kutirik kūṭattiyum kuṟikkum mantiram eṉṉum col oṉṟē. ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akaramutali, kutiric cāliyik kuṟikkum mantiram eṉṉum colli vēṟeṉa mayaṅkip pirittuk kūṟiyatu vaḻuvākum. makkaḷ paṭukki, vilaṅkiṉ paṭukki, paṟavik kūṭu ākiya mūṉṟiyum,  cēkki yeṉṉum orē col potuvākak kuṟittal kāṇka.
cūḻviṉiyik kuṟikkum mantiram eṉṉum col vēṟu. atu amippil ottiruppiṉum. ataṉ mutaṉiliyāṉa maṉ eṉṉum col muṉ eṉpataṉ tiripākum. muṉṉutal = karututal. cūḻtal. maṉ + tiram - maṉṟiram - mantiram - va. mantra. maṉ = mantiram. (piṅ.) itu mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarum toḻilāku peyarumākum.

(iṭi.) 1. aḷavu kuṟittuvarum toḻiṟpeyarīṟu. e.ṭu. peṟu māṉam. 2. nili kuṟittuvarum toḻiṟpeyarīṟu. e.ṭu. kaṭṭu māṉam.
o.nō: “moon (A. Sax. mona, the moon(masc.); cog. O. Fris. mona, Goth. mena, Ice. mani, Dan. maane, D. maan, OHG. mane, (the Mod. G. mena, moon, is a derivative like E. month). Lith. menu, Gr. mene, Per. ma. Skt. mas, all meaning the moon; from a root ma, to measure; the moon was early adopted as a measure of time, hence the name.”  - The Imperial Dictionary.

pala poruḷkaḷ oṉṟucērvatāl, kuḻampalum kuḻappamum kalakkamum mayakkamum uṇṭākum. nīrum maṇṇum cēriṉ kalaṅkal allatu kaluḻi yeṉappaṭuvatum, nīrum piṟa poruḷum cērntu tiṇṇamāyiṉ kuḻampu allatu kuḻampal eṉap paṭuvatum, pakalum iravum mayaṅkum (kalakkum) vēḷi macaṅkal (mayaṅkal) eṉappaṭuvatum kāṇka.

muṭṭattaṉam = mūṭattaṉmi (W.).
muṭṭāṭṭam = (W.). 1. muṭṭāḷ taṉmi. 2. aṟiyāmiyā luṇṭākuñ cerukku. avaṉ muṭṭāṭṭa māṭukiṟāṉ. (u.va.).
muṭṭāḷ1 (muṭṭu + āḷ) = mūṭaṉ. muṭṭā ḷarakkar (tiruppu. 141). ma. muṭṭāḷ.
muṭṭāḷ2 = muṭṭum (muṭṭit tāṅkum) āḷ, tāṅkum uruvam, aṇikattiṉ (vākaṉattiṉ) kīḻ atit tāṅkuvatu pōl vikkum paṭimi. ka. muṭṭāḷ.
muṭṭarici = naṉṟāy vēkāta (vēkkāṭu kuṉṟiya) arici (u.va.).
muṭṭam = 1. poruntiya pakkam, pakkaccarivu. naḷimali muṭṭamum (peruṅ. vattava. 2:43). 2. palar kūṭi vāḻum ūr (cūṭā.). kuraṅkaṇiṉ muṭṭam (civaṉ kōyil).
o.nō: muṭṭā = 1. ūrkkiḻami, kuṟunila āṭci. 2. cottu (w.).
muṭṭā - miṭṭā = 1. nāṭṭup pakuti, vaṭṭam 2. ūrkkiḻami, kuṟunila āṭci. 3. cottu. 4. urimi.
miṭṭā - in. miṭṭā (mittha). 
muṭṭutal = kūṭutal, poruntutal.
o.nō: OE. mitan, OS. motian, ON. moeta, Goth. (ga) motjan, E. meet. 
OE. motian, E. moot, assembly.
E. wikenagemot = Anglo - Saxon national council or parliament. OE. witena. wise mens. (ge) mot, meeting.
muṭṭāṭṭam = muṭṭuki.
muṭṭu - o.nō: E. butt, meet end to end.
muṭṭu = tāṅkal. o.nō: ME. buttress, OF. bouterez, mod. F. bouteret, f. bouter, E. buttress f. butt.
OF. abutex (but, end), end on, E. abut, border upon, abutment, a lateral support.
muṭṭukkaṭṭutal = 1. kaṭṭi mutaliyavaṟṟit tāṅkalākak koṭuttal. 2. muḻaṅkālik kikaḷāṟ kaṭṭutal. muṭṭuk kaṭṭi uṭkārātē (u.va.). 3. elli kaṭṭutal (yāḻp.). 4. vaḻiyaṭittal (w.).
muṭṭukkaṭṭiyāṭutal = poykkāṟ kaṭṭiyil niṉṟu naṭattal.
muṭṭukkāl, muṭṭukkoṭuttal, muṭṭuc caṭṭam, muṭṭuc cuvar mutaliya toṭarccoṟkaḷ tāṅkal koṭuttaliyum.
muṭṭukkaṭṭi, muṭṭuttoyvu (mūccuviṭa muṭiyāmi), muṭṭuppāṭu mutaliya toṭarccoṟkaḷ taṭipaṇṇutaliyum; 
muṭṭumuṭuku. muṭṭiṭi mutaliya toṭarccoṟkaḷ vaḻiyiṭuk kattiyum; 
muṭṭuccīli, muṭṭup paṭṭiṉi, muṭṭup pānti mutaliya toṭarccoṟkaḷ tīṭṭuppāṭṭiyum;
muṭṭukkāl taṭṭutal. muṭṭukkuṭṭu. muṭṭuk kuttutal (muḻaṅkālmēl niṟṟal), muṭṭuppiṭippu, muṭṭuvali, muṭṭuvīkkam mutaliya toṭarccoṟkaḷ muḻaṅkāl viṉiyiyum uṇarttum.
muṭṭutal = neruṅkutal, iṭukutal.
muṭṭu - muṭṭi = 1. viraṟ poruttut teriyumpaṭi muṭakkiya ki. 2. muṭṭikkiyāṟ kuttuṅ kuttu. muṭṭikaḷ paṭappaṭa (pārata. vēttira. 56). 3. nāl viralkaḷi yiṟuka muṭakki avaṟṟiṉmītu kaṭṭi virali muṟukap piṭikkum iṇiyā viṉikki. (cilap. 3: 18, uri). 4. kippiṭiyaḷavu. muṭṭi māttira mēṉum (cētupu. irāmatī. 3). 5. piṭiyaḷavāka iṭum aiyam (picci). muṭṭipukum pārppār (taṉippā.). 6. paṭikkalam piṭikkum vaki. tuyya pācupatat toṭiyu muṭṭiyum (pārata. aruccuṉaṉṟava. 129). 7. oruvar tam kiyuḷ maṟittati iṉṉoruvar tāmāka aṟintu kūṟuṅ kali : 8. tiruviḻāvil ūrellit teyvattiṟkum paṭikkuñ cōṟu. 9. muḻaṅkāṟ cippi allatu poruttu.
muṭṭutal eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ tali allatu kaṭi iṉṉoru poruḷmēṟ paṭutalātalāl, muṭṭum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu muṭivu allatu ellip poruḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu.
ellikkaṟuppaṉ (karuppaṉ). ellippiṭāri, elliyammaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, ciṟṟūrvāṇariṉ ellit teyva vaṇakkattik kuṟikkum.
muṭṭi - va. muṣṭi.
muṭṭikkāl = 1. muḻaṅkāṟ cippi allatu poruttu. 2. muṭṭikkāṟ piṉṉal. (W.).
muṭṭikkāl taṭṭutal = muḻaṅkāṟ cippikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uracutal. muṭṭikkālaṉ = muṭṭikaḷ taṭṭumpaṭi naṭappavaṉ (W.). muṭṭikkāṟ kaḻuti = piṉṉaṅkāl muṭṭikaḷ taṭṭumpaṭi naṭakkuṅ kaḻuti.
muṭṭiccaṇṭi = muṭṭikkippōr (W). muṭṭippicci = piṭiyarici yiyam.
muṭṭiyaṭittal = 1. muṭṭic caṇṭiyiṭutal. 2. muṭṭip pōr ceytal pōl varuntit tiṇṭāṭutal. vēlikku muṭṭiyaṭikkiṟāṉ (u.va.).
muṭṭikkāl eṉpatu pōṉṟē muṭṭikki eṉpatum poruttiyē ciṟappākak kuṟikkum. muṉṉatu kāṟporuttum piṉṉatu viraṟporuttum ākum.
muḷ - mūḷ. mūḷutal = 1. poruntutal. 2. tīppaṟṟutal 3. ciṉaṅkiḷamputal.                  4. mikutal. 5. muṉital. mutalaṟa muṭikka mūṇṭāṉ (kamparā. māyācī. 96).
mūḷ - mūṭṭu (pi.vi). mūṭṭutal = 1. poruttutal. 2. iṇittal. icittal. kālkoṭut telumpu mūṭṭi (tēvā. 631 : 3). 3. tittal. intak kōṇiyi mūṭṭu (u.va.). 4. tīppaṟṟa vittal. mūṭṭiya tī (nālaṭi. 224). 5. tī. mūṭṭuvatupōṟ ciṉamūṭṭutal. 6. paki mūṭṭic caṇṭikkut tūṇṭutal. iruvarukkum naṉṟāy mūṭṭiviṭṭu viṭṭāṉ (u.va.). 7. oṉṟic celuttutal. kaṭukupu katirmūṭṭi (kalit. 8).
ma. mūṭṭuka. te. mūṭṭiñcu (Muttintsu).
mūṭṭu = 1. uṭal. aṇi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ poruttu. kavacattiyu mūṭṭaṟuttāṉ (kamparā. caṭāyuva. 113). 2. cantippu (yāḻ. aka.). 3. tiyal. 4. kaṭṭu. vaṉṟāṇ micippiṇitta vallikaḷiṉ mūṭṭaṟuttu (kantapu. mīṭcip. 8). 5. kaṭṭappaṭṭatu (W.). 6. kutirik kaṭivāḷam (piṅ.). 7. maṉaveḻucci. 8. vāḻiyilik kaṟṟi. mūṭṭumūṭṭāyp pirintupōy viṭṭatu (u.va.). 9. pottakak kaṭṭaṭap pakuti.
k., te. mūṭṭu. ka. mūṭṭi.
mūṭṭu - mūṭṭi = 1. poṭṭaṇam. intat tuṇikaḷiyum mūṭṭiyil vittuk kaṭṭu (u.va.). 2. poti. arici mūṭṭikaḷi yellām vaṇṭiyilēṟṟik koṇṭupōy viṭṭārkaḷ. 3. aṟupatu paṭṭaṇampaṭi koṇṭa oru kōṇiyaḷavu. 4. perumpoy. avaṉ mūṭṭi yaḷakkiṟāṉ.
ma. mūṭṭa. te. mūṭṭa. tu. mūṭṭē.
mūṭṭi - mūṭi = 1. poti. potimūṭip pōrēṟi (paṭṭiṉap. 137). 2. mikac ceṟinta kūlakkōṭṭi kaṭunteṟṟu mūṭi (puṟam. 285). 3. kūlakkutir. kaḻunteṟṟu mūṭiyiṉ (poruna. 245).
mūṭṭu - māṭṭu. māṭṭutal = 1. iṇittal. ampiṉi māṭṭi yeṉṉē (kamparā. nikumpali. 96) 3. cerukutal. ciṟupoṟi māṭṭiya peruṅkal laṭāar (naṟ. 19). 2. tokuttal. aṭup piṉiṉ māṭṭu milaṅkakil (kāñcippu. nakara. 73). 4. maṉattiṟ koḷḷutal coṉmāli yīrintu māṭṭiya cinti (tiruvici. karuvū. 8:10). 5. tīppaṟṟavittal. viṟakiṟ ... centī māṭṭi (ciṟupāṇ. 156). 6. viḷakkuk koḷuttutal. neypeytu māṭṭiya cuṭar (kuṟun. 398). 7. aṭittal. viṉmuṟiya māṭ ṭāṉō (taṉippā.). naṉṟāy māṭṭu māṭṭeṉṟu māṭṭi viṭṭāṉ (u.va.). 8. payaṉpaṭuttutal. vaḷḷaṟāṉ valla vellā māṭṭiṉaṉ (cīvaka. 1274).

mūṭṭu - mūṭṭam = 1. ulimukam (yāḻ. aka.). 2. cokkappaṉi (W.). 3. viṟaku (yāḻ. aka.). 4. kammak karuvi vaki (yāḻ. aka.).
mūṭṭu - muṭṭāṉ - muṭṭāṉ = aṇiyātu vikkum neruppu mūṭṭam.
muḷ - muy - muya - muyaku - muyaṅku. muyaṅkutal = 1. poruntutal. muliyu mārpu muyaṅkaṇi mayaṅka (paripā. 6:20). 2. taḻuvutal. muyaṅki kikaḷi yūkka (kuṟaḷ. 1238). 3. puṇartal. aṟaṉillāṉ piya muyaṅkiyuḻi (kalit. 144).                        4. ceytal. maṇaviṉi muyaṅka lilleṉṟu (cūḷā. tūtu. 100).
muyaṅkik koḷḷutal = kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāka vāḻtal. (w.).
muyaṅku - muyakku = taḻuvuki. vaḷiyiṭi pōḻap paṭāa muyakku (kuṟaḷ. 1108). muṉpu mātavap payatti ṉālavaṇ muyakkamar vār (taṇikippu. nāṭṭup. 48).
muyakku - muyakkam = 1. taḻuvuki. poruṭpeṇṭir poymmi muyakkam (kuṟaḷ. 913). 2. puṇarcci. muyakkam peṟṟavaḻi (aiṅ. 93. uri). 3. toṭarpu. āṇavattiṉ muyakkamaṟṟu (taṇikippu. nanti. 110).
muyaṅku - mayaṅku mayaṅkutal = 1. neruṅkutal. 2. kalattal. 3. ottal. kāriruḷ mayaṅkumaṇi mēṉiyaṉ (paripā. 15:50). 4. kikalattal. tamiḻtali mayaṅkiya taliyālaṅ kāṉattu (puṟam. 19). 5. mayaṅkiṉa vāyppuḷum (pu.ve.10 ciṟap. 11) 6. aṟivu keṭutal pulāṉ mayaṅkāṇ (ēlā.2). 7. maruḷutal. 8. vēṟṟuvaya mātal. 9. veṟi koḷḷutal. tāmayaṅki yākkattuṭ ṭūṅki ... vāḻ nāḷip pōkkuvār (nālaṭi. 327). 10. māṟupaṭutal. mēṉiyu muḷḷamu mayaṅkāt tēvar (kallā. 82:30). 11. niliyaḻital āḷpavar kalakkuṟa mayaṅkiya naṉṉāṭṭu (maṇimē. 23 : 104). 12. varuntutal. muyaṅkal viṭāa viviyeṉa mayaṅki (akam. 26). 13. tākkuṇṇutal. kāloṭu mayaṅkiya kaliḻkaṭa leṉa (paripā. 8 : 31). 14. aiyuṟutal. 15. tayaṅkutal. avarip pōyp pārppataṟku mayaṅkukiṟāṉ (u.va.). 16. uṇarcci yiḻattal.
mayaṅkicic koccakam = uṟuppukkaḷ mayaṅkiyum mikkum kuṟintum varum koccakak kali.
muyakku - mayakku = 1. pōrceyki. irumpuli mayakkuṟṟa (kalit. 48). 2. kalakkam. kaṉāmayak kuṟṟēṉ (maṇimē. 11. 104). 3. maruḷac ceyyuñ ceyki. māya mayakku mayakkē (tiv. tiruvāy. 8:7:3).
mayakkutal = 1. kalattal. pāṟpey puṉkan tēṉōṭu mayakki (puṟam. 34). 2. cērttal. uyireṉun tirimayakki (tēvā. 1189:4). 3. maṉaṅ kuḻampac ceytal. kuṟaḷi pēci mayakki viṭiṉum (nālaṭi. 189). 4. maruḷac ceytal. māya mayakku mayakkā ṉeṉṉi vañcittu (tiv. tiruvāy. 8:7:4). 5. vēṟṟuvayap paṭuttutal. (kamparā. irāvaṇaṉkō. 51). 6. nili keṭuttal. vaḷḷiyāy koṭimayakki (akam. 6). 7. citittal. erumi katiroṭu mayakkum (aiṅ. 99). 8. uṇarcci yiḻakkac ceytal. matakariyi yuṟṟari neritteṉa mayakki ... tukittāṉ (kamparā. makuṭa. 5).
muyakkam - mayakkam = 1. kalappu. vaṭacoṉ mayakkamum varuvaṉa puṇartti (kallā. 62 : 18). 2. oru vēṟṟumi yurupu maṟṟoru vēṟṟumip poruḷiṟ kalattal. vēṟṟumi mayakkam. (naṉ. 317, uri). 3. eḻuttup puṇarcci. uṭaṉili meymmayakkam. vēṟṟunili meymmayakkam.

maṭṭakkōl, maṭṭaṅ kaṭṭutal, maṭṭac cuvar, maṭṭañ ceytal, maṭṭattaram, maṭṭan taṭṭutal, maṭṭanūl, maṭṭappalaki, maṭṭappā (moṭṭi māṭi), maṭṭappoṉ, maṭṭam pārttal, maṭṭam piṭittal, maṭṭam pōṭutal, maṭṭa vēlik kāraṉ, maṭṭa viḻippuḷi, maṭṭavēli mutaliya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷi nōkkuka.
iḷamaṭṭam = 1. kuṟumaṭṭak kutiri. 2. iḷamparuvattōṉ. iḷamaṭṭam - iḷavaṭṭam = iḷiñaṉ.
maṭṭu - maṭṭi = 1. (tāḻnta matiniliyuḷḷa) mūṭaṉ aṟiyāta ... mūṭamaṭṭi (tiruppu. 195). 2. parumpaṭi. 3. maṭṭikkāri. 4. makku. 5. oḻuṅkiṉmi. 6. maṭṭi vāḻi (nāñ).
maṭṭittāḷ. maṭṭittiyal, maṭṭippuṭivi, maṭṭivēli mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
bj., ka. maṭṭi (maddi).
maṭṭu - maṭṭi = 1. payaṉaṟṟa - vaṉ - vaḷ - tu. inta maṭṭik kiṟutta tellām pōtum (viṟaliviṭu. 889). 2. maṭṭamāṉa nel. 3. maṭṭamāṉa arici.
maṭṭuk kaṭṭutal. maṭṭukku miñcutal. maṭṭuk kōṇam, maṭṭukkōl, maṭṭut tapputal, maṭṭut tiṭṭam, maṭṭuppaṭutal, maṭṭup piṭittal, maṭṭu matippu (maṭṭu mariyāti), maṭṭu matiyam, maṭṭu maruṅku mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
muttutal (muṭṭutal) = poruntutal, ottal.
muttu (muṭṭu) - mattu (maṭṭu) = aḷavu.
mattu - matti. mattittal = aḷaviṭutal.
matti - mati. matittal = 1. aḷaviṭutal. maṇ viḻintu vāḻnāḷ matiyāmi (tirikaṭu. 29). 2. karututal. āṭali matittāṉ (kantapu. tiruviḷi. 1). 3. poruṭpaṭuttutal. maṇṇāḷvāṉ matittumirēṉ (tiruvāca 5:12) 4. ūḻkutal (tiyāṉittal). nantiyaṅ kuravaṉi matippām (vināyakapu. kaṭavuḷ. 13). 5. tuṇital. tērmaṇik kuraleṉa vivaṇmatikkumaṉ (kalit. 126:7).
mati - va. mati. miti.
OE., OS metan., OHG. mezzan, ON meta, Goth. metan, E. mete, to measure.
mati = 1. matippu. nīṇmatik kulicam (iraku. yākap 92). 2. iyaṟki yaṟivu. matinuṭpa nūlō ṭuṭiyārkku (kuṟaḷ. 636). 3. pakuttaṟivu. matiyili maṭaneñcē (tivāca. 5 33). 4. aṟiyum pulaṉ (intellect) 5. aṟivuṭimi. matimi cālā maruṭki nāṉkē (tol. meyp. 7).
mati - matam = 1. matittaṟintu kiyāḷum nūl neṟimuṟi.
eḻuvaki matamē uṭaṉpaṭal maṟuttal
piṟartam matamēṟ koṇṭu kaḷivē
tāa ṉāṭṭit taṉātu niṟuppē
iruvar māṟukōḷ orutali tuṇivē
piṟarnūṟ kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭal ēṉip
piṟitoṭu paṭāaṉ taṉmataṅ koḷalē.  (naṉ. potup. 11)
2. ammi yimmi yummi(maṟumi) nilikaḷi matittaṟintu kaṭippiṭikkuṅ koḷki. e.ṭu.
civamatam, tirumāl matam, kaṭavuṇ matam.
muttutal = poruntutal, kalattal.
muttu - mattu = kalappu, kalakkam, mayakkam, mayakkañ ceyyum pittam. mayakkan tarum kaḷ, tīṅkaḷḷākiya tēṉ, pitta muṇṭākkum ūmatti.
mattu - maṭṭu = 1. kaḷ. veppuṭiya maṭṭuṇṭu (puṟam. 24). 2. tēṉ. maṭṭuvā yaviḻanta taṇṭār (cīvaka. 1145). maṭṭuvār kuḻalammi = malimakaḷiṉ peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. 3. iṉcāṟu. karuppumaṭṭu vāy maṭuttu (tiruvāca. 5:80). 4. kāmak kuṭippu. maṭṭuṭi maṇamakaḷ (cīvaka. 98). 5. maṭṭu vikkuñ cāṭi maṭṭuvāy tiṟappavum (puṟam. 113). 6. (tēṉiṟkun tīṅkaḷḷiṟku muriya) naṟumaṇam. maṭṭu nīṟoṭum (iraku. irakuvuṟ. 23).
mattu - mattam = 1. mayakkam. mattamām piṇinōykku (tēvā. 426 : 3). 2. veṟi, kaḷippu. mattak kariyuriyōṉ (tirukkō. 388). 3. yāṉi matam (tirukkō. 388, uri) 4. cerukku (uri. ni.) 5. kōṭṭi (pittiyam). matta maṉattoṭu māliva ṉeṉṉa (tiruvāca. 5:3). 6. karuvūmatti mattanaṉ māmalarum matiyum vaḷar (tēvā. 923:8).
mattam - va. matta.
mattu - matu = 1. kaḷ. matumaṟin tuṇṭōr makiḻcci pōla (tol. poruḷ. 114. pa. 495). 2. tēṉ. matuviṉ kuṭaṅkaḷum (cilap. 25:38). 3. iluppippū mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu kāycci yiṟakkum veṟinīr (caṅ. aka.). 4. amutam (caṅ. aka.). 5. iṉimi (aka.). 6. parācam (makarantam) (caṅ. aka.).  7. atimaturam (mali).
va. matu. (madhu), OE. meodu, MLG. mede. OHG. metu, ON. mjathr, E mead, alcoholic liquor of fermented honey and water. Gk methu, wine. L mel. honey. 
mattam - mattaṉ. 1. matimayakka maṭintavaṉ. iṟantaṉar pōla vīḻnta mattari (pārata. niri. 102). 2. pittaṉ. mattaṉēṉ peṟumāya malamāya (tāyu. poṉṉi. 35). 3. pērūkka muḷḷavaṉ. pittariṉ mayaṅki mattariṉ matittu (tiruvāca. 153). 4. koḻup puḷḷavaṉ. mattaṉi rāvaṇaṉ kotittāṉ (irāmanā. uyut. 44).
mattaṉ - va. matta.
mattu - matti - mati = mayakkañ ceyyum tiṅkaḷ. ip poruḷ vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
o.nō: L. luna, moon. L. lunaticus - E. lunatic, insane person. E. lunacy, insanity.
paṇṭik kālattil. nilavoḷiyāṟ kōṭṭiyuṇṭāvatāka oru karuttu ulaka muḻutum paraviyiruntatu.
matu - matam = 1. kaḷ(matu). 2. matukkaḷippu (malipaṭu. 173, uri). 3. tēṉ ... mataṅkamaḻ kōti (cīvaka. 2584). 4. veḷi (caṅ. aka.). 5. makiḻcci. kātali colliṟ piṟakku muyarmatam (nāṉmaṇi. 7). 6. cerukku. pōretirn tēṟṟār matuki matantapa (paripā. 18 1). 7. valimi. paṭimatañ cāmpa votuṅki (paripā. 4:18). 8. kāma vēṭkimikuti (tirukkō. 69. uri). 9. yāṉikkaṭam. matanīr (cīvaka. 2485). 10. nilavaḷam (W.). 11. kācaṟi (katūri) naṟumi. māliyuñ cāntu matamu miḻikaḷum (paripā. 10 92). 12. kaṉmatam. (tilava. tila. 125). 13. vīriyam (yāḻ. aka.). 14. mikuti (yāḻ aka.). 15. perumi (yāḻ. aka.).
matu - matuki = 1. valimi. aṉimatukiyar kol (kuṟun. 290). 2. aṟivu. vāṉuyar matuki vāṭṭum (cīvaka. 664).
matu - matuṅku.
matuṅkutal = iṉimiyātal.
matuṅkiya vārkaṉi  (tiruman. 2914)
matam - mataṉ = 1. kalakkam (yāḻ. aka.). 2. maṭami. mataṉuṭi nōṉṟāḷ (tirumuruku. 7 uri). 3. cerukku mataṉuṭi nōṉṟāḷ (paṭṭiṉap 278). 4. valimi, mataṉuṭi muḻavuttōḷ (puṟam. 50). 5. maṉaveḻucci. mataṉuṭi nōṉṟāḷ (puṟam. 75). 6. aḻaku (piṅ.). 7. māṭcimi (piṅ.). 8. mikuti (yāḻ. aka).
mataṉ - mataṉam = 1. tēṉī (yāḻ. aka.) 2. tēṉmeḻuku (yāḻ. aka.). 3. marukkāri (mali.). 4. perumitam (yāḻ. aka.). 5. kāmam (piṅ.). 6. puṇarcci viruppam (piṅ.). 7. iḷavēṉil (yāḻ. aka.). 8. oru cāmpal niṟak kaṭalmīṉ. 9. oru veṇṇiṟak kaṭalmīṉ.
matam - matar. matarttal = 1. kaḷittal. matupparukip paruvāḷi niṉṟu matarkku maruṅkelām (kamparā. nāṭṭup. 24). 2. mataṅkoḷḷutal. matar viṭiyiṟ cīṟi (pu. ve. 714). 3. cerukkutal (W.). 4. marañceṭikaḷ maṭṭiṟku miñcik koḻuttal. 5. ceḻittal. 6. mikutal. matarari maḻikkaṇ (cīvaka. 2803).
matar = 1. yāṉi matanīr (nāmatīpa. 207). 2. cerukku. arimatar maḻik kaṇṇīr (kalit. 77). 3. makiḻcci (tivā.). 4. mikuti (piṅ.). 5. vīram (aru.ni.). 
matar - matarvu. 1. ācip perukkam (yāḻ. aka.). 2. uḷḷakkaḷippu (piṅ.). 3. iṟumāppu. 4. valimi (cūṭā.). 5. ceḻippu 6. aḻaku (cūṭā.). 7. mikuti (tivā.).
matar - matarvi = 1. mayakkam. matarvi veṅkatir parappu kiṭanteṉa (cīvaka. 322). 2. kaḷippu (piṅ.) 3. cerukku. matarvi nōkkamum (kantapu. teyvayā. 64). 4. ceḻippu. matarvik kompu (cūḷā. nakara. 25).
matarvu - matavu = valimi. matavunaṭi nallāṉ (akam. 14).
matavu - mata. matattal = 1. mayaṅkutal (W.). 2. kaḷittal. 3. cerukkutal. 4. koḻuttal. 5. kāma mikutal. 6. mataṅkoḷḷutal. mikavum matattu matam poḻintu (ñāṉavā. tiruvā. 47).
mata - va. mata.
mata = 1. mikuti. 2. valimi. 3. aḻaku. 4. maṭami. matavē maṭaṉum valiyu mākum (tol. uri. 79).
matavu - mataku - mataṅku - matakku - matakkam.
o.nō: mayaṅku - mayakku - mayakkam.
matakkam = pēruṇṭi, kuṭi, kañcā mutaliyavaṟṟā luṇṭākum mayakkam. āṟu matakkattiṉā lallavā eṅkaḷi viḻuṅkāmal viṭṭiṭṭatu. (avivē. kati). 2. kaḷippu (yāḻ. aka.).
matamatappu = 1. ceḻippu (tiruvirut. 9, viyā. pa. 71). 2. timir (W.). 3. uṇarc yiṉmi (W.).
